### [1] [What Stops Recursive Self-Improvement in Robotics? Lessons from 123 Rounds of Agentic Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31760)
*Jiaming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究构建了一个可自主诊断机器人失败、编写或安装技能并在仿真中测试的智能体，经过123轮改进后发现：它能自主获得新能力，但改进未能累积，目标任务始终未成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索机器人能否像代码智能体改进软件那样，通过观察失败并自主迭代来提升能力，并厘清此类系统为何难以取得持续进展。

Method: 构建自主改进系统，使其识别能力缺口、编写新技能或寻找并部署外部模型，在仿真中测试每次改动后继续迭代；在家居操作任务上运行123轮，并分析改进过程与评估机制。

Result: 系统曾自主发现目标不在视野内，进而请求、调试并部署主动观察模型，形成可用的搜索技能。但尽管单项改动不断通过测试，系统始终未能完成将调味品放到冰箱顶层的目标任务。研究将障碍归纳为三类：感知模块难以识别“顶层”等关系概念，促使系统不断增加无效几何规则；长技能链使学习和测试偏向最先失败的步骤，后续技能难以得到改进；评估框架、薄弱测试及误导性记忆使智能体优化了被测指标，而非真正的任务表现。

Conclusion: 机器人自主改进的瓶颈未必在改进智能体本身，也可能来自感知能力、技能链结构和评估框架。研究据此提出了配套可证伪实验的系统设计建议，强调应改进关系理解、覆盖后续步骤的学习与测试，以及更可靠的评估和记忆机制。

Abstract: Can a robot improve itself the way coding agents now improve software? We built an agentic system to find out. It watches a robot fail, works out which capability is missing, writes new skills or finds and installs external models, tests every change in simulation, and repeats, with no human writing robot code. We ran it for 123 improvement rounds on household manipulation tasks. This report describes what we learned. The good news is that the agent can discover capabilities on its own: noticing that its targets were out of view, it asked for an active-viewing model, debugged it, and deployed a working search skill. The bad news is that its improvements did not add up. Changes kept passing their tests, yet the target task, putting condiments on the top shelf of a fridge, never succeeded. We found that the agent was rarely the bottleneck. Three things around it were. First, chained perception modules do not understand relations. Segmenters such as SAM 3 find shelves but not "the top shelf", so the agent filled the gap with ever more geometric rules that never converged, when what it needed was a different kind of model. Second, skill chains lock learning onto the first step. Long tasks mostly fail early, so evidence and fixes pile up there, and later skills are rarely reached, tested, or improved. Third, what the agent learns is decided by the harness. The agent optimized exactly what the evaluator measured, including where it was wrong, and weak tests and misleading memory turned activity into a standstill. We distill these lessons into concrete recommendations for building robot systems that improve themselves, each paired with an experiment that could prove it wrong.

</details>


### [2] [An Empirical Study on What Matters for Viewpoint-Generalizable Policies in Visual Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32762)
*Mino Nakura,Sriram Krishna,Yufei Wang,Shubham Tulsiani,Zackory Erickson,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现，保留密集视觉 token 并让动作头参与几何推理，可提升视觉模仿学习策略对不同视角的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模仿学习可用于训练多任务机器人操作策略，但现有策略容易受摄像机视角变化影响，限制了其在多样环境中的部署。

Method: 开展受控实证研究，比较不同设计选择对跨视角泛化的影响，并在摄像机位姿范围广泛的模拟任务上评估策略。

Result: 保留密集视觉 token、使动作头参与几何推理的设计，在模拟任务中能更好地维持不同视角下的表现；采用这些设计训练的策略还可在随机摄像机配置下实现从模拟到真实世界的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 视觉表征的密集程度与动作模块中的几何推理，是增强机器人操作策略视角泛化和迁移能力的关键设计因素。

Abstract: Visual imitation learning is a promising approach to training robot manipulation policies capable of completing a wide variety of tasks. However, policies today remain brittle to viewpoint perturbations, making deployment in diverse environments a challenge. We present a controlled empirical study of which design choices allow visuomotor policies to generalize across viewpoints. We find that viewpoint generalization improves when dense visual tokens are retained and the action head participates in geometric reasoning. On a suite of simulated tasks that span a wide range of camera poses, we show that these design choices yield a policy that remains performant across viewpoints. As a practical consequence, a policy trained with these design choices also transfers zero-shot from simulation to the real world under random camera configurations.

</details>


### [3] [MORPH: Self-Organising Multi-Robot Task Allocation via Neuroplasticity-Inspired Adaptive Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32745)
*Xuezhi Niu,Didem Gürdür Broo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MORPH 是一种无需训练、任务模型或竞价机制的多机器人任务分配框架，通过在线可塑性规则学习机器人间的定向偏好，在仓储基准中兼顾吞吐量、协调效率与分布变化适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人任务分配方法往往依赖先验任务知识、效用函数、成本矩阵或离线训练策略，因此在缺少先验信息或任务分布变化时容易失效。研究旨在实现能从运行反馈中在线学习并适应变化的协作机制。

Method: MORPH维护一个有向成对偏好矩阵，并根据任务共现与完成反馈应用四类局部可塑性规则：突触可塑性、稳态可塑性、结构可塑性和元可塑性。分配决策依据学习到的 AGV—Picker 偏好，而非固定距离规则；框架不需要任务模型、竞价或离线训练。

Result: 在 TA-RWARE 仓储基准（8—24 个智能体、4 张地图、每回合 800 步、5 个随机种子）上，N=24 时 MORPH 达到全连接方法吞吐量的 110%，协调链路仅为潜在链路的 21%，且链路效率优势随规模扩大而单调增长。面对空间任务分布变化，其性能退化幅度比基于邻近度的方法低 3 倍；学习到的偏好与曼哈顿距离不相关。消融实验显示四类可塑性规则均有可测贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明，局部、在线的可塑性规则能够在无需显式任务知识的情况下形成有效的全局分配行为，并适应任务分布变化。系统还自发呈现跨类型偏好占优和偏好逐步稀疏化等特征；作者据此将 MORPH 定位为该 MRTA 分类下首个在线学习定向成对分配偏好的方法。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocation (MRTA) in dynamic environments faces a fundamental tension: effective coordination requires learned structure, but that structure must adapt when conditions change. Existing methods resolve this by assuming prior task knowledge, a utility function, a cost matrix, or a trained policy making them brittle when deployed without such knowledge or when task distributions shift. We present MORPH(Multi-agent Online Rewiring through Plasticity-guided Hierarchy), a training-free MRTA framework where global allocation quality emerges from 4 local plasticity rules (synaptic, homeostatic, structural, and metaplasticity) applied to a directed pairwise preference matrix updated from runtime co-occurrence and task-completion feedback. MORPH requires no task model, no bid computation, and no offline training; response decisions use learned AGV-to-Picker preferences rather than a fixed proximity rule. Within the Gerkey-Mataric MRTA taxonomy, MORPH is the first method in the single-task, single-robot, instantaneous-assignment class to learn directed pairwise allocation preferences online. Evaluated on the TA-RWARE warehouse benchmark (8-24 agents, 4 maps, 800 steps per episode, 5 seeds), MORPH achieves 110% of all-to-all throughput at N=24 while using only 21% of possible coordination links as an efficiency advantage that grows monotonically with fleet size. Under spatial task distribution shift, MORPH degrades 3x less than proximity-based methods while its learned preferences remain uncorrelated with Manhattan distance. Systematic ablation confirms all four plasticity rules contribute measurably. Two allocation properties emerge without programming: cross-type preference dominance and progressive preference sparsification, mirroring the developmental refinement of biological neural circuits. Learned preferences are driven by task co-occurrence history, not spatial proximity.

</details>


### [4] [SEES: A Self-Evolving Embodied System via Failure-Guided VLA Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32698)
*Ziwen Li,Hanlue Zhang,Zhenyang Ren,Tianyu Huang,Runqi Lin,Haoyu Wang,Zhengqing Gao,Yandong Guo,Fakhri Karray,Tongliang Liu,Chris Russell,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEES 通过从执行失败中自动识别瓶颈，并在仿真中进行在线强化学习，逐步提升视觉—语言—动作策略的长程任务能力，无需额外专家示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略在长程任务中仍不可靠，原因之一是训练数据偏重成本较低的单阶段操作；单项原子技能薄弱可能导致多阶段任务失败。依赖专家定位瓶颈并补充示范，成本较高且部署后难以实施。

Method: SEES 将长程任务拆解为原子任务，并路由至对应的技能族策略；每个技能族中的相关原子技能共享一个 VLA 适配器。系统监测执行结果，自动定位高频失败技能，再通过恢复已遇到的状态、利用大语言模型生成任务专属成功标准，在仿真中构造定制强化学习任务，并在线更新共享适配器。

Result: 实验表明，SEES 可与不同 VLA 主干模型集成，并逐步提升其长程任务表现；在未见任务上也观察到持续改进，显示出超越进化训练设置的迁移能力。

Conclusion: SEES 提供了一种无需新增专家示范的自我进化方案：通过失败驱动的瓶颈定位和仿真强化学习，促进相关原子技能间的正向迁移与多轮累积改进。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) policies demonstrate promising generalization across diverse short-horizon tasks. However, they remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks, partly because the large-scale training data is biased toward single-stage manipulation tasks that are cheaper to demonstrate. A single weak atomic skill can cause failures across multiple multi-stage tasks. To address such failures, existing methods often require experts to identify the bottleneck and provide additional demonstrations, making the improvement costly and potentially impractical after deployment. To this end, we present a Self-Evolving Embodied System (SEES) that learns from failures and improves the VLA policy without additional expert demonstrations. SEES decomposes long-horizon tasks into atomic tasks and routes them to corresponding family policies. Each family consists of related atomic skills that share one VLA adapter. During execution, the system automatically monitors atomic-task outcomes to identify the most frequently failing atomic skills as the current bottlenecks. To overcome these bottlenecks, SEES constructs tailored RL tasks in simulation by restoring previously encountered states and generating task-specific success criteria with an LLM. Online RL updates the shared family adapters to promote positive transfer among related atomic skills and cumulative improvement across evolution rounds. Extensive experiments show that SEES can be integrated with different VLA backbones to progressively improve their long-horizon performance. We also observe continued improvement on unseen tasks, providing evidence of transfer beyond the evolution settings.

</details>


### [5] [PF-RL: Progress Field Reinforcement Learning via Goal-Conditioned Value Geometry for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32634)
*Yunpeng Qing,Yilun Kong,Sixu Lin,Ming Zhou,Yiming Fei,Shuang Luo,Yixiao Chi,Haoming Gu,Jingyuan Liu,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Progress Field Reinforcement Learning（PF-RL），在预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）特征上学习结构化、目标条件化的任务进度表示，并将其转化为稠密奖励，以改进长时程操作任务中的策略优化与信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化微调主要依赖稀疏的任务级结果，难以判断中间状态和动作对最终目标的贡献，尤其不利于长时程操作。现有进度感知方法通常将任务进度建模为单一标量，缺乏对中间观测与任务目标之间结构关系的刻画，限制了转移级信用分配效果。

Method: PF-RL在预训练VLA特征之上引入轻量级共享Progress Field头，将当前状态表示与目标表示映射到紧凑的进度空间。该空间中的几何距离用于构造目标条件价值，并结合时间一致性和目标结构等辅助目标约束表示几何。相邻转移的价值变化被转换为稠密进度优势，用于离线策略改进和在线强化微调。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0以及真实世界双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，PF-RL相较于强监督微调、强化微调方法和进度感知基线，能够持续提升策略性能。摘要未报告具体数值，因此无法进一步比较提升幅度。

Conclusion: PF-RL通过学习结构化的目标条件化进度场，将中间状态与最终目标的关系显式融入价值建模，为长时程VLA操作提供了更细粒度的稠密信用分配机制，并展现出在离线和在线策略优化中的通用有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning~(RFT) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) policies, yet sparse task-level outcomes provide limited credit for intermediate transitions, especially in long-horizon manipulation. A natural approach is to model intermediate task progress and use it as dense feedback for policy improvement. Despite their architectural differences, existing progress-aware methods commonly formulate task progress as an explicit scalar prediction, providing limited structure for modeling how intermediate observations relate to the task goal, which may hinder effective transition-level credit assignment. We introduce Progress Field Reinforcement Learning (PF-RL), which learns a structured goal-conditioned progress representation over pretrained VLA features and converts it into dense credit for policy optimization. A lightweight shared Progress Field head maps current and goal representations into a compact progress space, where geometric distance induces goal-conditioned value, while complementary temporal and goal-structure objectives shape the learned geometry. Transition-level value changes naturally yield dense progress advantages, enabling fine-grained credit assignment for both offline policy improvement and online reinforcement fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin2.0, and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that PF-RL consistently improves policy performance over strong supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement fine-tuning, and progress-aware baselines.

</details>


### [6] [World SLAM Model: Joint World Modeling for SLAM and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32626)
*Minghui Qin,Yijun Yuan,Weicheng Zheng,Kenan Li,Weibang Wang,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Anmin Liu,Yicheng Yao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出World SLAM Model（WSM），将SLAM从传统的上游定位与建图模块转变为导航系统内部的世界状态维护机制，通过持续状态更新、持久记忆和后端误差优化，实现面向长时程导航的视觉预测、运动估计与稠密几何建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法通常将SLAM仅作为提供位姿、地图或视觉令牌的前置模块，难以在交互过程中持续维护一致的空间状态，也难以有效处理累积误差。本文旨在将SLAM的核心机制直接融入下游导航，使导航决策能够受益于持续的世界建模和几何优化。

Method: WSM接收当前观测与导航目标，预测未来视觉状态，并联合估计相机运动和稠密几何。模型通过持久记忆保存并增量更新世界状态，同时利用后端优化修正累积误差；新观测到来后持续更新空间状态，并以此生成动作和执行闭环导航。训练采用端到端的导航与SLAM联合目标。

Result: 实验表明，WSM在保持较强SLAM精度的同时，能够提升导航性能，说明将SLAM式状态维护和误差 refinement 机制内置于导航模型具有实际收益。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线、指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: WSM验证了SLAM不仅可以作为导航的前置感知模块，也可以作为长时程世界建模与具身交互的内在机制。通过统一维护空间状态、预测未来视觉信息并进行几何 refinement，该框架有望增强导航系统的一致性、闭环能力和长期决策性能。

Abstract: We introduce World SLAM Model (WSM), a unified framework that brings the SLAM paradigm directly into downstream navigation. Rather than treating SLAM merely as an upstream module that provides poses, maps or tokens, WSM adopts its core mechanisms, including incremental state updates with persistent memory and backend refinement of accumulated errors, to maintain a consistent world state during interaction. Given the current observation and a navigation goal, WSM predicts future visual states and jointly estimates their camera motion and dense geometry, grounding visual prediction in an evolving spatial world state. This spatial state is continuously updated as new observations arrive and provides the basis for action generation and closed-loop navigation. WSM is trained end-to-end with a joint navigation--SLAM objective, enabling downstream navigation to benefit directly from SLAM-style state maintenance and refinement while preserving accurate geometric estimation. Experiments demonstrate improved navigation performance together with strong SLAM accuracy, highlighting the potential of SLAM as an intrinsic mechanism for long-horizon world modeling and embodied interaction.

</details>


### [7] [RECAST: Recasting Vision-Language Semantics into an Actionable Cost Map for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32595)
*Incheol Cho,Jintae Park,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee,Jaegul Choo,Seokha Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RECAST 将视觉语言模型（VLM）的场景推理与视觉基础模型的空间定位结合，生成可执行代价图，以提升机器人导航的成功率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式导航模型在训练分布之外容易失效；VLM虽能理解场景，但其判断往往缺乏空间落地，也难以与具体动作对齐。

Method: VLM根据机器人前视图和用户指令，判断可通行表面、风险物体、优选方向及可穿越间隙；视觉基础模型将相关判断定位到图像中，并融合为紧凑的可执行代价图。代价图用于条件化轨迹解码器，并对候选轨迹评分。为应对VLM推理与实时画面之间的延迟，系统结合VLM评估的枢轴帧代价图与当前帧重新计算的地形、碰撞代价图。

Result: 相较于最强基线，RECAST在仿真和真实四足机器人上的成功率分别提高13.3和31.4个百分点，碰撞率分别降低9.6和14.3个百分点；其碰撞率在所有对比方法中最低。

Conclusion: 将高层场景推理空间化为代价图，并结合历史判断与当前观测，可有效提升机器人导航的成功率和安全性。

Abstract: Safe and robust robot navigation across diverse environments requires a high-level understanding of complex scenes and the ability to carry it into stable motion. Recent works tackle this with learning-based models trained at scale and with approaches built on vision-language models (VLMs). However, learning-based models break down outside their training distribution, while VLM-based approaches bring that understanding but rarely ground it in the scene or align the action with it. To address these limitations, we present RECAST, a robot navigation framework that combines the reasoning of a VLM with the spatial grounding of vision foundation models to build an Actionable Cost map. Given the robot's front view and the user's instruction, we first decompose the scene with the VLM, judging which surfaces are traversable, which objects pose a risk, which heading to prefer, and which gaps are passable. Vision foundation models then ground these surfaces and objects in the image, and all four judgments are spatially recast into one compact cost map. This map both conditions the trajectory decoders and scores their proposals to select the one to execute. As the VLM's answers trail the live scene, both steps draw on cost maps from two points in time: the pivot frame the VLM judged, which carries all four judgments, and the current frame, whose terrain and collision costs are rebuilt from the current image. RECAST improves success over the strongest prior method by 13.3 points in simulation and 31.4 points on a real quadruped, and reduces the collision rate relative to it by 9.6 and 14.3 points, reaching the lowest collision rate among all methods. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [8] [Think Fast, Plan Selectively: Adaptive Deliberation for Efficient Data-Driven MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32591)
*Yi Xian Goh,Sze Jue Yang,Hao Luan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-TD-MPC 根据状态需求，在快速策略执行与测试时规划之间自适应切换，在保持任务表现与鲁棒性的同时提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动模型预测控制需要逐步采样并评估大量候选轨迹，计算开销限制了其实时控制频率。论文关注的核心问题是：是否每个决策都需要同等程度的规划计算？

Method: 提出轻量级 Fast-TD-MPC 框架，借鉴快速直觉处理与慢速审慎推理的双过程思路，在快速策略执行和测试时规划之间自适应路由；在更需要仔细决策的状态下调用规划。

Result: 在 103 项连续控制任务中，Fast-TD-MPC 达到有竞争力的任务表现，推理速度最高提升约 4 倍。面对外部扰动时，系统会选择性地回退至规划，鲁棒性与原始规划器相当。

Conclusion: 自适应分配计算资源可降低模型预测控制的推理成本，同时保留其任务性能与抗扰能力，为实时连续控制提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) combines learned world models with online trajectory optimization, achieving strong performance in continuous control. However, the per-step cost of sampling and evaluating hundreds of candidate trajectories restricts deployment to control frequencies well below what real-time robotics demands. Motivated by the dual-process theory of human cognition, which distinguishes between fast, intuitive processing (System 1) and slower, deliberative reasoning (System 2), we ask whether every decision requires the same degree of computational deliberation. We propose Fast-TD-MPC, a lightweight framework that adaptively routes between fast policy execution and test-time planning, reserving costly deliberation for states where it is most needed. Fast-TD-MPC delivers competitive task performance across 103 continuous control tasks while achieving up to ~4x faster inference. Under external disturbances, Fast-TD-MPC selectively falls back to planning, maintaining robustness comparable to the original planner.

</details>


### [9] [Assisting for Open-Ended Tasks: Goal-Oriented Shared Autonomy as a Particle Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32576)
*Mengxue Fu,Ethan Xu,Sam Iyer-Singh,Yinlong Dai,Michael Hagenow,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向开放式、动态变化目标的共享自主框架，通过粒子滤波在线推断和生成用户目标，并据此提供辅助操作。实验表明，该方法可减少遥操作时间并提升用户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自主方法通常预先假设有限且固定的候选目标集合，难以适应非结构化辅助场景中不断出现或变化的用户意图。因此，研究旨在实现无需预定义完整目标集合的开放式目标推断与辅助。

Method: 将目标导向共享自主建模为粒子滤波过程，其中粒子表示候选用户目标。转移模型根据交互进展动态提出新目标，用户动作则实时更新目标信念。系统结合视觉 grounding 和大语言模型生成与上下文相关的语义目标，利用低层技能基元生成目标条件辅助，并依据用户纠正优化技能。

Result: 在包含12名参与者的用户研究中，参与者完成了多种桌面操作任务，并与该方法及先进共享自主基线进行比较。结果显示，该粒子滤波方法减少了用户的遥操作时间，并提高了用户满意度。

Conclusion: 该框架能够突破固定目标集合的限制，为开放式、动态目标环境中的共享自主提供可行方案。结合基础模型后，系统具备在线目标发现、目标条件辅助和基于用户反馈改进技能的能力。

Abstract: A common approach for shared autonomy blends human inputs with autonomous assistance based on the human's likely goal. However, most existing approaches assume that a static set of possible goals is known a priori, which limits the use of such methods in unstructured assistive settings. We instead investigate how to enable shared autonomy with open-ended and dynamically changing goals. We formulate goal-oriented shared autonomy as a particle filter in which particles represent candidate human goals. Unlike conventional approaches with a fixed goal set, our transition model dynamically proposes new candidate goals as the interaction evolves, and human actions update the belief over these goals in real time. We instantiate this framework with foundation models (e.g., vision grounding and large language models) that propose context-relevant semantic goals, generate goal-conditioned assistance from low-level skill primitives, and refine those skills from human corrections. We assess our approach through a user study where 12 participants perform a variety of tabletop manipulation tasks with our method and state-of-the-art shared autonomy baselines. The results show that our particle filter-based approach reduces the amount of time users spend teleoperating the system and improves user satisfaction. User study videos: this https URL

</details>


### [10] [GlowTact: Simple and Compact Vision-Based Tactile Sensing with High Sensitivity and Spatial Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32471)
*Yuxiang Ma,Megha Tippur,Pengfei Ye,Sandra Q. Liu,Haonan Chen,Francis Richard Cottrell,Edward Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GlowTact 是一种紧凑型视觉触觉传感器，可通过原始图像直接呈现接触压力分布，适用于人形机器人的指尖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉触觉传感器的结构较难简化，也不易适配人形指尖的尺寸与设计约束；同时，显式重建接触几何会增加处理复杂度。

Method: 采用压力响应式传感机制，以单色、非定向光照观察接触情况，使触觉图像直接反映压力分布，无需显式几何重建；传感器结合耐用的丁腈膜与简化光学结构。

Result: 实验展示了克级接触检测、准确的法向力估计，以及对精细接触几何的重建能力，包括 M1 螺纹。该设计兼具较高灵敏度与丰富的空间细节。

Conclusion: GlowTact 提供了一种结构简单、紧凑且可定制的触觉感知方案，有望用于人形机器人的指尖，并支持丰富的接触信息获取。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTS) provide rich contact information for robotic manipulation, but existing designs can be hard to simplify and adapt to the size and constraints of humanoid fingertips. We introduce \textbf{GlowTact}, a pressure-responsive vision-based tactile sensing mechanism that directly visualizes contact pressure. \textbf{GlowTact} requires only single-color, non-directional illumination, and the raw tactile image directly represents the pressure distribution without explicit geometry reconstruction. This simple sensing principle enables compact, customizable tactile sensors while preserving high sensitivity and rich spatial detail. We demonstrate gram-scale contact detection, accurate normal-force estimation, and reconstruction of fine contact geometry, including M1 screw threads. These results establish \textbf{GlowTact} as a practical new sensing technology for compact humanoid fingertips, combining a durable nitrile membrane and simple optical design with sensitive and information-rich tactile perception.

</details>


### [11] [DRAM: Delta-rule Recurrent Associative Memory for Robot Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32453)
*Xinyu Zhao,Yixiang Shan,Tao Yang,Runyu Lei,Yiming Zhao,Jiaxin Fan,Zongbao Feng,Peng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAM 是一种可插接的固定容量记忆模块，为预训练机器人策略提供长时记忆，无需修改或重新训练策略主干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作依赖历史信息，但现有策略通常只利用当前观测或短时间窗口。延长输入窗口会增加推理成本，而依赖预定义语义特征的方法泛化受限，且可能需要重新训练主干。

Method: DRAM 使用带门控的 delta-rule 线性注意力维护固定容量的关联记忆，并通过改进的更新方式并行处理每帧内的全部 token。其与架构无关的读出模块将历史上下文融入动作预测；训练时冻结预训练策略主干，仅针对任务训练记忆模块和动作专家。

Result: 实验表明，DRAM 在多种预训练策略上均优于短上下文基线及其他紧凑记忆设计。

Conclusion: DRAM 可作为后置式记忆模块，为不同架构的冻结策略提供有效长时记忆，且无需改动或重新训练策略主干。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is inherently history-dependent, yet most pretrained robotic policies condition on only the current observation or a short temporal window. Equipping such policies with long-term memory remains challenging: existing approaches either feed the backbone multi-frame observation windows, which substantially increase inference cost, or rely on pre-defined semantic features, which limit task generality and may also require the retraining of the backbone to adapt to the memory. We introduce DRAM (Delta-rule Recurrent Associative Memory), a plug-and-play memory module that can be attached to a wide range of pretrained robotic policies, endowing them with long-horizon memory without architectural modification or backbone retraining, requiring only task-specific post-training of the memory module and action expert. DRAM maintains a fixed-size associative memory using gated delta-rule linear attention, with a modified update that incorporates all tokens within each frame in parallel. An architecture-agnostic readout integrates historical context into action prediction across different policy architectures. Experiments show that DRAM consistently improves frozen pretrained policies over short-context baselines and alternative compact memory designs, validating its effectiveness as a fixed-size, post-hoc memory module trained with the backbone frozen.

</details>


### [12] [RE-0: Verified Recursive Improvement of Embodied Code-as-Policy Agents through Local On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32416)
*Jiawei Zhang,Xiangrong Zhang,Rui Song,Huanbin Zhou,Chengye Song,Hongzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RE-0 与 RE-OPD 通过验证教师在具体失败情境中的纠正是否有效，提升代码策略智能体，并将经验证的改进迁移至独立学生策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法通常把教师的完整行为视为监督目标，即使教师在某些状态下失败，也可能错误分配训练信号。

Method: RE-0 针对学生自身的失败历史请求教师提供局部纠正，并在环境中检验纠正是否带来真实收益。RE-OPD 将反事实验证有效的干预用于在线策略蒸馏，按局部收益加权，并把改进投射回独立学生策略；理论上还给出每轮学生收益的下界，误差取决于验证与投射过程。

Result: 在多项代码策略具身任务中，RE-0 提升了教师辅助执行效果和独立学生表现，并能泛化至新机器人与新场景。

Conclusion: 以环境验证筛选并量化教师干预，可避免盲目模仿不可靠教师，使监督位置、教师贡献权重与学生策略共同迭代改进。

Abstract: Code-as-Policy agents accomplish long-horizon embodied tasks by generating and executing code, yet continually improving them with teachers that are stronger but not globally reliable remains a key challenge. Existing distillation methods typically treat the teacher's complete behavior as the supervision target and thus misassign training credit on states where the teacher fails. We propose RE-0, a recursively verified policy improvement framework: rather than assuming that the teacher globally outperforms the student, RE-0 requests local corrections from the teacher on the student's own failure histories and checks in the environment whether each correction is genuinely beneficial; verified corrections yield immediate improvement. Building on this, we propose RE-OPD, which turns verified interventions into supervision for on-policy distillation. Only counterfactually verified teacher interventions provide distribution-level supervision, weighted by their measured local benefit, and the improvement they induce is projected back into the standalone student, so both where supervision is applied and how much credit the teacher receives co-evolve with the student policy. We further prove that the student's per-round gain is lower-bounded by its verified intervention gain up to verification and projection error terms. Experiments across multiple Code-as-Policy embodied tasks show that RE-0 improves both teacher-assisted execution and the standalone student, and generalizes to novel robots and scenes.

</details>


### [13] [Proactive Motion Planning for Human-Robot Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32354)
*Elena Basei,Edoardo Lamon,Matteo Saveriano,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将基于图的深度学习人体运动预测与机械臂动态路径规划结合，实现面向人机协作的主动避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态或仅响应当前位置的规划难以应对人体运动带来的动态碰撞风险，因此需要预测人体未来运动并及时调整机器人轨迹。

Method: 采用基于图的深度学习模型预测人体运动，并将预测结果融入规划框架；框架使用静态路网和时变A*算法，动态调整UR5e机械臂的轨迹。

Result: 摘要称该方法改善了人机交互，并通过运动预测与自适应轨迹规划实现主动碰撞规避；未提供定量指标或实验细节。

Conclusion: 将人体运动预测整合进动态规划，有望提升协作环境中的机器人安全性与主动响应能力；其效果仍需结合具体实验数据评估。

Abstract: This abstract addresses the incorporation of human motion prediction into proactive and dynamic human-aware motion planning, with the goal of enabling safe collaboration between humans and robots. A deep learning, graph-based model is used to forecast human motion and is integrated into a planning framework. This framework employs a static roadmap along with a time-variant A* algorithm to modify the trajectory of a UR5e manipulator. This method greatly improves human-robot interaction and enables proactive collision avoidance by combining precise motion forecasts with adaptive trajectory planning.

</details>


### [14] [Uranus: Building the Next-Generation Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24815)
*Wenkang Qin,Yukun Zhou,Noah Shen,Jisong Cai,Dongxiao Mao,Baicheng Li,Yue Zhang,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uranus 是一种数据驱动的机器人模拟器，可根据在线输入的关节位置轨迹，持续生成多视角视觉观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人交互成本高，而传统模拟器的构建需要大量人工投入，因此需要可扩展的模拟工具支持数据生成、策略训练、评估与安全迭代。

Method: 采用以关节轨迹为条件的自回归扩散模型，在线接收未来关节位置轨迹，并逐步生成潜在帧；每个潜在帧对应四张 RGB 图像。系统支持无固定时域的连续 rollout，并提供适用于不同机器人形态与相机配置的统一控制接口。

Result: 经推理优化后，Uranus 的生成速度达到 24 FPS。论文在分布内和分布外数据上进行了定量与定性评估，并报告了系统当前的局限性。

Conclusion: Uranus 展示了以数据驱动方式构建低延迟、可扩展机器人模拟器的可行性；作者公开代码与模型权重，供社区使用和进一步研究。

Abstract: Scalable simulation is essential for robot data generation, policy training, evaluation, and safe iteration, yet real-world interaction is costly and conventional simulators require labor-intensive construction. We present Uranus, a data-driven robot simulator built around a joint-trajectory-conditioned autoregressive diffusion model. Uranus offers three key capabilities: (1) streaming, open-ended rollout, which receives future joint-position trajectories online and autoregressively generates one latent frame per step, corresponding to four RGB frames, without a fixed horizon; (2) low-latency generation, achieving 24 FPS after inference optimization; and (3) scalable, extensible robot control, providing a unified interface for synchronized multi-view generation across diverse robot embodiments and camera configurations. We conduct comprehensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data, providing an objective assessment of Uranus and clearly identifying its current limitations. We release the code and model weights to empower the community with practical tools and insights.

</details>


### [15] [DexRoam: Learning Mobile Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Whole-Body Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35761)
*Rui Zhou,Yibo Yuan,Junkai Zhao,Fangyuan Zhao,Xiaoguang Zhao,Shanghang Zhang,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexRoam 将连续、耦合的全身人类示范迁移到机器人动作空间，用于学习移动式双手灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动式双手灵巧操作需要协调移动、全身动作与手指灵巧性，机器人示范采集成本高。既有迁移方法常简化人类动作，丢失任务所需的细粒度耦合结构。

Method: 使用消费级 VR 头显和头戴式双目相机，构建无需外部相机或动作追踪器的人类示范采集系统；通过具身、动作语义和时间三个阶段进行对齐，将人类动作映射至机器人动作空间，并与机器人示范共同用于标准视觉—语言—动作（VLA）策略训练。

Result: 真实机器人实验显示，人类示范在不同训练范式和 VLA 骨干网络上均提升策略表现：GR00T N1.7 的平均成功率从 29% 提升至 56%，pi0.5 从 32% 提升至 57%。使用一半机器人示范时，加入人类示范仍可达到仅用机器人示范训练的表现。消融实验表明，三个对齐阶段均有必要。

Conclusion: DexRoam 表明，在保留细粒度、连续全身动作结构的前提下，人类示范能够有效扩展移动式全身操作的训练数据，并提升策略学习效果。

Abstract: Mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation requires continuous coordination of locomotion, whole-body motion, and finger-level dexterity within a single trajectory, creating a severe robot demonstration bottleneck. Egocentric human demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, but prior approaches ease the transfer by simplifying human motion, discarding exactly the fine-grained, coupled structure such tasks depend on. We present DexRoam, a complete system for learning mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation from human demonstrations, in which whole-body motion remains continuous and coupled throughout the human-to-robot transfer process. To enable scalable collection of whole-body human manipulation demonstrations, we develop a tracker-free capture system using only a consumer VR headset and a head-mounted stereo camera, without external cameras or motion trackers. We then perform three explicit alignment stages---embodiment, action-semantic, and temporal---to map captured motion into the robot action space, preserving fine-grained whole-body motion and allowing human and robot demonstrations to be jointly learned by standard VLA policies. Real-world experiments with different VLA backbones show that human demonstrations consistently improve policy learning across training paradigms, raising average success from 29% to 56% on GR00T N1.7 and from 32% to 57% on pi0.5, while matching robot-only training with half the robot demonstrations. Ablations confirm that each alignment stage is necessary. These results highlight the potential of human demonstrations for scalable whole-body mobile manipulation with preserved fine-grained motion structure.

</details>


### [16] [RoboCompiler: Graph-Native Compilation of Closed-Chain Robots for Consistent Modeling, Control, and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35717)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Joongheon Kim,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCompiler 将机器人机构图编译为共享的机械接口，统一处理闭环约束、运动、执行器映射与动力学，并支持接触环境下的仿真和控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环机构、耦合执行器和变化接触要求配置、运动、力与动力学模型保持一致；分别为控制和仿真重建这些接口，容易造成闭环约束与驱动映射不一致。

Method: 从包含刚体、关节、坐标系、惯性参数和执行器端口的规范机构图出发，构建闭环路径及解析残差雅可比；通过秩检验的延拓与修正求解可行配置。采用切空间提升映射独立速度，并通过成对端口映射保持虚功一致；进一步加入约束曲率修正，以计算加速度和投影刚体动力学。借助按环路局部计算、自动生成雅可比和依赖感知复用，实现闭环输入变化时的局部更新。

Result: 在 Komatsu 挖掘机、Unitree Go2、Franka Panda、Kangaroo 和六支链 UPS Stewart 平台上评估了物理闭环与任务约束。高精度约束动力学计算及独立 Pinocchio 校验支持其机械一致性；MuJoCo 与 Isaac Sim/PhysX 测试展示了接触条件下的任务执行和模型复用。对 Kangaroo，在动力学与控制保持不变时，残差及雅可比评估耗时降低 96.7%，闭环运行总耗时降低 66.8%。

Conclusion: 该框架将机构图转化为统一且可复用的机械接口，兼顾闭环一致性、动力学正确性和局部计算效率；实验结果表明其适用于多种机器人平台及原生接触仿真。

Abstract: Robots with kinematic loops, coupled actuators, and changing contacts require consistent models of configuration, motion, force, and dynamics. Yet these interfaces are often reconstructed separately for control and simulation, making closure and actuation consistency difficult to maintain. This paper presents RoboCompiler, a graph-native framework that compiles a canonical mechanism graph into a shared mechanical interface. From bodies, joints, frames, inertias, and actuator ports, it constructs closure paths and analytic residual Jacobians, then assembles feasible configurations through rank-checked continuation and correction. A tangent lift maps independent velocities to full robot and task motion, while paired actuator-port maps preserve virtual work. A constraint-curvature correction extends the reduction to accelerations and projected rigid-body dynamics, including floating-base and support modes. Cycle-local evaluation, generated Jacobians, and dependency-aware reuse enable localized updates when closure inputs change. We evaluate physical loops and task-induced constraints on a Komatsu excavator, Unitree Go2, Franka Panda, Kangaroo, and a six-UPS Stewart platform. High-precision constrained-dynamics and independent Pinocchio checks confirm mechanical consistency; MuJoCo and Isaac Sim/PhysX executions demonstrate task performance and model reuse under native contact. For Kangaroo, compilation reduces residual-and-Jacobian evaluation time by 96.7% and closed-loop rollout wall time by 66.8%, with dynamics and control held fixed.

</details>


### [17] [Humanoid Loco-Manipulation With Discrete VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35709)
*Wenxin Shao,Siqi Chai,Kun Li,Kerou Zhang,Xinzhou Jiang,Wei Xu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Holo-M 是一种面向人形机器人全身移动操作的离散视觉-语言-动作模型，在 SIMPLE 基准的通才与专才评测中均取得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散 VLA 模型主要面向机械臂，难以应对人形机器人腿、躯干、手臂和手部构成的高维异构动作空间，以及相应的训练和实时推理需求。

Method: 提出统一动作分词器，将动作空间划分为末端执行器、身体、手部和运动学四类，并扩展语言模型词表以纳入动作 token，从而支持多种机器人形态与数据来源的训练。推理时采用分组离散扩散解码生成各身体部位的动作 token，避免逐 token 自回归解码。

Result: 在 SIMPLE 人形机器人移动操作基准上，Holo-M 在通才和专才评测中均获得最高成功率，并显著领先第二名。

Conclusion: Holo-M 展示了利用语言模型原生词表和分组离散扩散解码处理人形机器人全身动作的潜力；论文称将公开代码和模型权重。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models using discrete action tokens have proven effective for controling robotic arms on manipulation tasks. For a humanoid, however, the whole-body action space -- legs, torso, arms, and hands -- is far higher-dimensional and heterogeneous, raising tokenization, training, and real-time inference challenges that the previous VLA models do not address. We present Holo-M, to our knowledge the first discrete VLA model for humanoid loco-manipulation that intrinsically exploits the language model by extending its vocabulary with action tokens. In this model, we devise a unified action tokenizer that decomposes the humanoid action space into four body-part-specific tokenizers -- end-effector, body, hand, and kinematics -- enabling training across drastically different embodiments and data sources, including humanoid teleoperation, ego-centric human video, and simulation. By extending the language model's vocabulary with these action tokens, we avoid the knowledge-insulation problem inherent to the models that use separate continuous action experts. To meet real-time control requirements, we decode each body part's action tokens through grouped discrete diffusion decoding, rather than using autoregression on the action tokens. We have conducted extensive experiments on the SIMPLE humanoid loco-manipulation benchmark, in which Holo-M achieves the highest success rates in both the generalist and specialist evaluations, leading the second best by significant margins. We will release all the code and model weights.

</details>


### [18] [LQR-ArUco Fusion: Robust Hierarchical Control for Navigation and Asymmetric Manipulation in Two-Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35700)
*Anupam Chatterjee,Arpita Kumari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于双轮倒立摆机器人的分层控制框架，兼顾精准导航、扰动抑制与负载操作时的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双轮平台持续进行平衡调整，导致里程计难以支持精确导航；侧装机械臂举升负载还会产生未驱动的横滚力矩，在不平地形上尤其容易引发失稳。

Method: 采用双回路架构：机载视觉系统追踪上方 ArUco 标记，提供全局航点导航以减轻里程计漂移；低延迟机载控制回路结合惯性与编码器数据，实时抑制物理扰动。

Result: 实物实验表明，机器人能够准确导航、抵御减速带造成的瞬态冲击、适应动态跷跷板坡道，并在狭窄轮距下稳定搬运负载。

Conclusion: 将高延迟的全局视觉导航与低延迟的机载稳定控制相结合，可同时缓解导航漂移和负载操作引起的动态失稳。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical control framework to address severe dynamic instabilities and navigational drift that arise when a two-wheeled inverted pendulum (TWIP) robot attempts asymmetric object manipulation. While two-wheeled platforms are highly manoeuvrable, their constant balancing adjustments make onboard odometry highly unreliable for precise navigation. Furthermore, the addition of a side-mounted robotic arm introduces unactuated lateral roll moments when a payload is lifted, a challenge heavily compounded on uneven terrain. To solve these coupled problems, our architecture divides the workload. An offboard vision system tracks overhead ArUco markers to provide high-latency global waypoint navigation, bypassing odometry drift. Simultaneously, a low-latency onboard control loop rejects active physical disturbances using inertial and encoder data. In our physical experiments, this dual-loop approach enabled the custom-built robot to navigate accurately, reject transient impacts from speed bumps, adapt to a dynamic seesaw ramp, and carry a payload securely without falling over its narrow wheelbase.

</details>


### [19] [Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35690)
*Puming Jiang,Tianrun Hu,Haozhe Du,Yibo Li,Zhiwei Xue,Xinhu Li,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APPL 将技能策略的结构先验同时用于策略训练和技能组合接口，提升机器人在新情境中的技能泛化与未见任务组合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少量示范学习面临两类相互依赖的泛化：组合已有技能以完成新任务，以及让单个技能策略适用于新情境。传统接口（如名称、指令或符号操作）未表达策略适用条件，导致组合过程难以利用技能内部结构。

Method: 构建 Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning（APPL）：构造智能体将完整示范切分为可复用技能，为每项技能提出多个结构先验，并据此训练和验证对应策略；运行时智能体依据这些先验描述的接口选择策略并组合技能，以实现新任务目标。

Result: 在 MetaWorld 和长时程 ManiSkill 任务上，APPL 提升了分布外技能泛化，并能完成此前未见的技能组合。消融结果显示，移除接口信息会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 将训练时使用的结构假设显式作为技能与组合模块之间的接口，有助于弥合技能学习和技能组合之间的信息缺口。

Abstract: Robots that learn from a few demonstrations often require two forms of generalization. Compositional generalization recombines skills to solve new tasks, and skill generalization lets the learned policy behind each skill work in new situations. The two depend on each other, yet information is lost between composition and the skills it calls. Where a skill works is determined by the structure its policy is trained with, while composition sees the skill only through a separate description, such as a name, an instruction, or a symbolic operator, that omits this structure. Our key idea is to use each policy's structural prior as part of the interface between composition and the skill. A structural prior states what a behavior depends on, for example that a grasp depends only on the gripper's pose relative to the object. Built into training, it shapes where the policy generalizes; stated in language, it tells composition where the policy applies. We instantiate this idea in Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning (APPL). A construction agent segments complete demonstrations into reusable skills, proposes several structural priors for each skill, and trains and verifies one policy per prior. A runtime agent then selects among these prior-specific policies and composes them toward new task goals using their interfaces. Across MetaWorld and long-horizon ManiSkill tasks, APPL improves out-of-distribution skill generalization and enables previously unseen skill compositions; ablating the interface information substantially reduces performance. These results support the use of training-time structural assumptions as a bridge between skill learning and skill composition.

</details>


### [20] [MM-ABC: Towards Generalist Mobile Manipulation via Seeing, Coordinating and Imagining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35652)
*Qiwei Liang,Guangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Zikuan Xiao,Jinxuan Lu,Yifan Xie,Renjing Xu,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MM-ABC 是面向移动操作的基础模型，通过空间感知、臂-底盘协同及训练期未来监督，提升多场景机器人任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作要求机器人在自身持续运动时准确感知空间，并协调机械臂与移动底盘。现有方法虽增强几何建模或预测能力，但常将两类动作分开处理，缺少高效协同的表示与机制。

Method: MM-ABC 使用稀疏多层级视觉语言模型特征进行空间感知；训练期间引入未来分支，以世界想象和几何意图提供额外监督；通过 MM-APT 的掩码联合注意力协同操作与移动两条动作流，并采用 clean-action x-prediction。模型在涵盖 17 种机器人形态的 12 个数据集上预训练，数据超过 5,000 小时、40 万个 episodes。

Result: MM-ABC 在 EBench 上成功率为 44.71%，RoboCasa365 为 61.2%，LIBERO 为 99.1%，LIBERO-Plus（无扰动训练）为 82.8%；五项真实世界任务的平均成功率为 83%。在 RoboCasa365 composite-seen 任务上，将 clean-action prediction 换为 velocity prediction 后，成功率从 32.8% 降至 29.2%；移除未来监督或多层级条件会造成更大幅度下降。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，面向空间感知的多层级特征、训练期未来监督，以及机械臂与底盘动作流的协同设计，共同支持了 MM-ABC 在模拟基准和真实机器人任务中的表现。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation extends robot interaction beyond a fixed kinematic workspace by making the reachable region itself controllable. This flexibility introduces two central challenges: spatially grounded perception under continuous ego-motion and coordinated control of heterogeneous arm and base actions. Existing approaches strengthen geometry through explicit 3D representations or predictive world models, and often decouple mobility and manipulation into separate action streams. We argue that effective mobile manipulation requires not only decoupling, but also representations that support efficient cross-stream collaboration. We present MM-ABC, a foundation model built around Seeing, Coordinating, and Imagining Arm-Base Collaboration. MM-ABC combines sparse multi-level VLM features for spatial perception; a training-only future branch that uses world imagination and geometric intent as extra supervision, strengthening perception and manipulation-intent prediction and improving the overall learning signal; and MM-APT, which coordinates separate manipulation and mobility streams through masked joint attention and clean-action x-prediction. In controlled ablations, replacing clean-action prediction with velocity prediction lowers success on RoboCasa365 composite-seen tasks from 32.8% to 29.2%, and removing future supervision or multilevel conditioning causes larger drops. We pretrain MM-ABC on 5,000+ hours of heterogeneous robot data spanning 400K+ episodes, 12 datasets, and 17 embodiments. Experiments cover EBench, RoboCasa365, ManiSkill-HAB, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world mobile manipulation. MM-ABC achieves 44.71% success on EBench, 61.2% on RoboCasa365, 99.1% on LIBERO, 82.8% on LIBERO-Plus without perturbation training, and 83% mean success on five real-world tasks.

</details>


### [21] [Denoising Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35651)
*Yuhao Zhang,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于 D4orm 扩散去噪框架，构建多种多机器人轨迹规划器，并在仿真与真实机器人实验中验证其效率、可靠性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人轨迹规划具有非凸、多模态和高维等特点，计算难度大；现有方法在规划速度、可靠性及不同资源条件下的适应性方面仍有局限。

Method: 以 D4orm 为基础，通过大规模并行采样和迭代去噪，优化候选控制轨迹的形变，生成满足运动学与动力学约束、且避免冲突的轨迹。进一步设计解耦式规划器、带反馈控制的在线滚动时域规划器，以及适用于资源受限场景的分布式规划器。

Result: 在二维和三维环境中，针对差速及全向机器人进行的评估显示，D4orm 方法相较 MPPI 等采样优化方法及一种学习式扩散模型方法，能更快、更可靠地找到高质量解。实验还展示了其在十架真实四旋翼上的零样本部署、对一百个仿真机器人的冲突协调，以及六台地面机器人上的全机载分布式长期运行。

Conclusion: 研究表明，扩散去噪可作为一种高效、可靠且可扩展的多机器人协调框架，并能通过不同规划架构适应多样化的运行需求。

Abstract: Multi-robot trajectory planning is a fundamental problem in multi-robot coordination but remains computationally challenging due to its nonconvex, multimodal, and high-dimensional nature. This work builds upon D4orm, a dynamics-aware diffusion-denoising framework, and develops a family of planning architectures for diverse operational requirements. Unlike conventional numerical optimization methods, D4orm employs sampling-based optimization to generate solution trajectories through massively parallel sampling, leveraging modern computing architectures such as GPUs. Its diffusion-denoising structure iteratively optimizes \textit{deformations} to candidate control trajectories, providing an efficient and versatile paradigm for generating kinodynamically feasible and conflict-free trajectories. Using D4orm as the building block for advanced planners, we present a decoupled planner for improved scalability, an online receding-horizon planner with feedback control, and a distributed planner for resource-constrained settings. Evaluations with differential-drive and holonomic robots in 2D and 3D environments demonstrate that D4orm-based approaches find high-quality solutions faster and more reliably than other sampling-based optimization methods, such as MPPI, as well as a learned diffusion-model-based method. We further demonstrate zero-shot deployment on ten real quadrotors with obstacles, large-scale deconfliction with 100 simulated robots, and fully onboard distributed `lifelong' operation with six ground robots. Overall, these results establish diffusion denoising as a scalable and reliable framework for multi-robot coordination. Code and video: this https URL

</details>


### [22] [CollisionSplatting: Collision-Aware Motion Planning in 3DGS Scenes with Image-Conditioned Objectives and Adjustable Conservatism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35619)
*R. Khorrambakht,Joaquim Ortiz-Haro,Stephan Weiss,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CollisionSplatting 直接在标准 3D 高斯泼溅场景中进行快速碰撞评估，将几何安全约束与图像目标结合，支持实时视觉引导规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何规划会丢失场景的视觉细节，而学习式视觉模型通常缺乏几何可解释性，且计算效率不足。

Method: 提出一种可调保守程度、受概率思想启发的距离度量，并将其用于 GPU 加速的 MPPI 和 RRT 规划器；结合学习得到的图像条件奖励函数，实现碰撞感知代价与图像空间目标的联合优化。

Result: 碰撞分类性能与代表性基线相当或更好，同时显著提高碰撞检测吞吐量并降低显存使用；还在真实世界的视觉引导导航与操作任务中进行了验证。

Conclusion: 3D 高斯泼溅可作为丰富视觉感知与实时运动规划之间的实用桥梁，CollisionSplatting 则为直接基于该表示开展高效、可解释的规划提供了模块化方案。

Abstract: Incorporating dense visual information into motion planning remains challenging, as geometric planners rely on abstracted scene representations that discard visual richness, while learned visual models often lack geometric interpretability and computational efficiency. This paper introduces CollisionSplatting, a simple, modular, GPU-accelerated, probability-inspired distance metric with tunable conservatism that operates directly on standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes. When combined with learned image-conditioned reward functions, this metric enables joint geometric and visual planning by unifying collision-aware costs with image-space objectives. We integrate the metric into GPU-accelerated Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) and Rapidly-Exploring Random Tree (RRT) planners, and show on-par or better collision-classification performance compared to representative baselines while achieving substantially higher collision-checking throughput and significantly lower VRAM usage. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our metric in real-world vision-guided navigation and manipulation tasks, highlighting 3DGS as a practical bridge between rich perception and real-time motion planning.

</details>


### [23] [F4R: Failure-Driven Recognition, Reconstruction, Refinement, and Redeployment for Continual Robot Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35575)
*Zhuoyuan Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Tianle Liu,Yihua Ren,Peng Yu,Chen Bai,Ziheng Zhang,Yufei Jia,Jindou Jia,Yuhang Zhang,Xinrui Zhang,Shang Yujing,Yuxiang Chen,Chuhao Zhou,Tiancai Wang,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出F4R，一种由失败驱动的真实世界—仿真—真实闭环学习框架，将机器人真实执行中的失败转化为针对性的策略改进。在四项操作任务上，F4R达到93.75%的分布内成功率和90.0%的分布外成功率，在不收集额外真实纠正示范的情况下，较预算匹配的Targeted BC基线在OOD条件下提升18.75个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉—语言—动作模型受限于专家示范覆盖不足，以及对物理交互理解不充分。通过收集新的真实世界失败示范进行修正虽然有效，但成本高、效率低、存在安全风险且难以规模化。因此，需要一种能够利用真实失败信息、减少额外真实数据采集并持续改进策略的方法。

Method: F4R首先利用智能体从真实执行轨迹中自动识别并诊断失败；随后将每个失败重建为交互式、以物体为中心的桌面环境，保留与任务相关的空间和物理条件。策略通过失败条件化的仿真—真实协同训练进行初步优化，再在重建环境中执行针对性强化学习。改进后的策略重新部署到真实环境，新产生的失败被持续反馈至下一轮重建和学习，形成闭环。

Result: 在四项真实世界机器人操作任务上的评估显示，F4R取得93.75%的分布内成功率和90.0%的分布外成功率。与预算匹配的Targeted BC基线相比，F4R在OOD条件下成功率提高18.75个百分点，且无需额外收集真实世界纠正示范。

Conclusion: F4R证明了将真实失败转化为可交互仿真环境并进行定向策略优化的有效性。该方法能够以较低的真实数据和采集成本提升策略的泛化能力，尤其适合处理分布外场景及复杂物理交互，但其效果仍依赖失败诊断质量和环境重建的真实性。

Abstract: The real-world performance of current vision-language-action models is fundamentally constrained by the limited coverage of expert demonstrations and their insufficient understanding of physical interactions. A common remedy is to collect additional real-world demonstrations of newly encountered failures. However, this process is costly, inefficient, potentially unsafe, and difficult to scale. To address this challenge, we propose Failure for Rising (F4R), a failure-driven real-to-sim-to-real closed-loop learning framework that converts real-world failures into targeted policy improvement. F4R first uses an agent to automatically identify and diagnose failures from rollouts. It reconstructs each failure as an interactive, object-centric table-top environment that preserves the task-relevant spatial and physical conditions. The policy is then refined through failure-conditioned sim-real co-training followed by targeted reinforcement learning in the reconstructed environments. The improved policy is subsequently redeployed, while newly observed failures are continuously fed back into the next reconstruction and learning cycle. Real-world evaluations on four manipulation tasks show that F4R achieves 93.75% In-Distribution and 90.0% Out-of-Distribution (OOD) success, outperforming the budget-matched Targeted BC baseline by 18.75 percentage points under OOD conditions without collecting additional real-world corrective demonstrations.

</details>


### [24] [EdgeVLN: Runtime-Aware Deployment Ready Quantized Vision Language Navigation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35570)
*Rithvik Jonna,Man Namgung,Aakash Gurram,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EdgeVLN 将量化的 StreamVLN 与轻量停止预测模块 LATTE 结合，并通过面向设备的运行时和记忆令牌剪枝，实现适用于 Jetson Orin NX 的视觉语言导航部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航模型通常面向计算资源充足的平台；仅进行模型压缩，无法证明模型在边缘设备的内存、延迟和能耗约束下仍能保持导航性能。

Method: 评估 StreamVLN 的多种权重量化精度与推理运行时，并在其释放的资源预算内复用骨干网络隐状态，加入 LATTE 预测停止动作验证器排名，无需额外视觉编码器或骨干前向计算。模型通过 VLN 驱动重建流式上下文并在设备端剪枝记忆令牌；实验覆盖 R2R VLN-CE val-unseen 的 1,839 个回合，并在 Jetson Orin NX 16 GB 上测量延迟、能耗与常驻内存。

Result: LATTE 在部署的 4-bit 模型上取得 58.02% 成功率，为所测方案最高，且相较 BF16 基线每步仅增加 0.013 秒延迟。不同 4-bit 格式的成功率近似，但能耗因执行路径相差 36.8 倍。只有 VLN 驱动下的 IQ4 NL 满足设备内存约束：常驻内存为 11.35 GB；与存储流式 BF16 相比，速度提高 20.8 倍、能耗降低 13.3 倍。INT2 则导致性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 边缘端 VLN 部署不仅取决于量化精度，也取决于运行时、上下文管理与记忆令牌剪枝。实验表明，IQ4 NL 与专用驱动及 LATTE 的组合能在该硬件上兼顾导航性能和资源约束。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) models perform well but target compute-rich platforms, limiting deployment on memory- and power-constrained robotic edge devices. Compression alone does not establish whether a VLN model fits the memory, latency, and energy budgets of an edge platform while preserving navigation behavior. We introduce EdgeVLN, a runtime-aware, deployment-ready quantized VLN model that closes this gap. EdgeVLN combines a quantized StreamVLN model with Latent Trajectory Termination Extractor (LATTE), a lightweight causal transformer that improves real-time stopping by predicting a Stop Action verifier rank. Both execute through our this http URL VLN driver, which reconstructs streaming context and prunes memory tokens on-board. We characterize a pretrained StreamVLN backbone across weight quantization from 8 to 2 bits and multiple inference runtimes to identify a feasible operating point. LATTE reuses backbone hidden states within the budget freed by quantization, requiring neither a second vision encoder nor an additional backbone forward pass. We evaluate six backbone precisions and seven candidate stop heads on BF16 and IQ4 NL across all 1,839 R2R VLN-CE val-unseen episodes. We measure success rate (SR) in simulation and latency, energy, and resident memory on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 16 GB. LATTE achieves our highest SR, 58.02 percent on the deployed 4-bit model, exceeding the BF16 baseline with only 0.013 s additional latency per navigation step. Four-bit formats achieve nearly identical SR, but step energy varies 36.8 times by execution path. Only IQ4 NL under our VLN driver fits the board, using 11.35 GB resident memory while running 20.8 times faster and using 13.3 times less energy than storage-streamed BF16. INT2 collapses. Runtime selection, memory-token pruning, and quantization are essential for efficient edge deployment.

</details>


### [25] [Inspection-SPARS: Task-Oriented Sparse Roadmaps for Inspection Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35516)
*Adir Morgan,Oren Salzman,Kiril Solovey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Inspection-SPARS，一种面向检查任务的路线图稀疏化方法，在保留 POI 覆盖、连通性与路径质量保证的同时，大幅压缩路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集路线图包含丰富的观测视角和运动捷径，通常能支持高质量检查路线，但其庞大的组合搜索空间使现有图检查规划（GIP）求解器难以在实际时间预算内找到优质解。既有稀疏化方法未充分考虑检查任务需求，或虽保证 POI 覆盖，却不保证检查路线质量。

Method: 将 SPARS 框架从通用几何准则扩展为面向检查任务的准则，设计检查感知的顶点接纳机制，将 POI 覆盖与连通性、路径质量共同作为稀疏化标准，并提供相对于密集路线图的覆盖和路径质量保证。

Result: 在真实感三维环境实验中，Inspection-SPARS 将顶点和边数量压缩至原来的约八分之一至四分之一，同时保留 POI 覆盖；GIP 求解器生成的路线长度最多比基于密集路线图或当前先进检查路线图得到的路线短 25%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，路线图稀疏化可以显式适配检查任务，在降低规划搜索负担的同时维持覆盖与解质量保证，从而提升检查规划的实际效率和效果。

Abstract: Inspection planning seeks a minimum-length collision-free robot tour that observes a given set of points of interest (POIs). Sampling-based methods reduce this continuous problem to a graph inspection planning (GIP) problem over a discrete roadmap, which is then solved using combinatorial solvers. Dense roadmaps capture diverse inspection viewpoints and motion shortcuts, and thus admit higher-quality solutions, but they induce large combinatorial search spaces on which state-of-the-art GIP solvers struggle to find good solutions within practical time budgets. Roadmap sparsification---restructuring a dense roadmap into a compact representation that preserves connectivity and path lengths---can alleviate this burden. However, existing sparsification approaches are either agnostic to the underlying inspection task, or strive to ensure coverage of the POIs without accounting for the quality of the resulting inspection plan. We present Inspection-SPARS, which is, to our knowledge, the first inspection-roadmap sparsifier with POI coverage and path-quality guarantees relative to the dense roadmap. To this end, we generalize the SPARS framework, a popular task-agnostic sparsifier, from purely geometric criteria to task-oriented ones, introducing an inspection-aware vertex admission mechanism that treats POI coverage as a first-class sparsification criterion alongside connectivity and path quality. Experiments in realistic 3D environments show that Inspection-SPARS reduces vertex and edge counts by 4-8x while preserving coverage, allowing the GIP solver to compute tours up to 25% shorter than with the dense roadmap or state-of-the-art inspection roadmap. More broadly, Inspection-SPARS shows that sparsification can be made task-aware without sacrificing guarantees on solution quality.

</details>


### [26] [Terrain-Aware Autonomous Planetary Exploration for Exteroceptive-Proprioceptive Mapping with Quadruped Scouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35493)
*Alberto Sanchez-Delgado,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向月球类环境的四足机器人地形感知自主探索框架，融合视觉几何信息与本体感知交互信息，支持地图构建、目标选择和避碰导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知且崎岖的地形会带来通行风险与能耗不确定性。仅依赖几何地图难以完整反映机器人与地面的实际交互，因此需要结合外感知和本体感知来评估地形并规划探索。

Method: 机载RGB-D相机生成以机器人为中心的高程图，估计几何可通行性并构造导航代价；本体测量提供补充性的地形交互线索。系统将局部地图增量配准为全局多层表示，由探索模块选择未探索的兴趣区域目标，再利用地图与代价层进行避碰导航。

Result: NVIDIA Isaac Sim仿真显示，机器人能够自主探索并扩展地图，同时建立地形几何与机器人—地形交互信息的空间关联。利用探索所得信息进行后续导航时，平均运输成本（CoT）低于初次探索阶段。

Conclusion: 多源感知与分层地图可支持四足机器人在未知崎岖环境中的自主探索，并有助于降低后续导航能耗。当前结果基于仿真，摘要未提供实地验证或定量提升幅度。

Abstract: Autonomous planetary exploration requires robots to navigate unknown, uneven terrain while assessing risk, traversability, and energetic cost. Quadruped scouts are well suited for this task because they can traverse irregular surfaces and gather mobility-relevant information during locomotion. This paper presents a terrain-aware exploration framework that combines exteroceptive and proprioceptive mapping for a quadruped robot in lunar-like environments. An onboard RGB-D camera builds robot-centered elevation maps, estimates geometric traversability, and derives navigation costs for autonomous planning. In parallel, proprioceptive measurements provide interaction-aware terrain cues that complement geometry-based assessment. Local maps are incrementally registered into a global multi-layer representation, which is used by an exploration module to select targets in unexplored regions of interest. The targets are reached by an autonomous navigation system that guides collision-aware motion using the available map and cost layers. Simulation results on NVIDIA Isaac Sim show autonomous exploration, map expansion, and spatial association between terrain geometry and robot-terrain interaction. Subsequent navigation using this information exhibits lower average Cost of Transport (CoT) than initial exploration.

</details>


### [27] [ForVis: An In-Field Dataset and Benchmark for VIO Using Under-Canopy UAV Flights in Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35482)
*Arman Kiani,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Jeffrey Eiyike,Aaron Weiskittel,Prabuddha Chakraborty,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForVis 是用于评估森林环境中无人机视觉惯性 SLAM（VI-SLAM）的实地数据集与基准，比较了七种开源系统及两种传感器配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林中的运动、光照变化、重复植被和振动会影响定位估计，而真实森林飞行条件下的 VI-SLAM 评测仍然困难。

Method: 采集十二次飞行数据，覆盖林间空地、树冠上方和树冠下方等条件；使用 Intel RealSense D435i 与 OAK-D Pro Wide 同步记录图像、惯性及飞控数据。数据总计包含 563.8 秒飞行和 1096.8 米轨迹，并对七种开源 VI-SLAM 系统进行了 504 次运行评测。

Result: 七种方法在 OAK-D Pro 上的中位轨迹误差均低于在 D435i 上的误差；传感器选择对轨迹误差的影响大于不同算法之间的差异。

Conclusion: ForVis 为森林环境中的无人机 VI-SLAM 提供了实地评测资源，可用于比较系统的速度、精度与鲁棒性；摘要结果也表明，传感器配置是影响定位表现的重要因素。

Abstract: Visual-inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for UAVs remains difficult to evaluate in real forest environments, where motion, illumination changes, repetitive vegetation, and vibration can all affect estimation. We present ForVis, an in-field dataset and benchmark for evaluating VI-SLAM during UAV flight in forest environments. The dataset contains twelve flights across open meadow, above-canopy, and under-canopy conditions in each environment. In total, it provides 563.8s of flight over 1096.8m of trajectory, recorded simultaneously with an Intel RealSense D435i and an OAK-D Pro Wide together with inertial and flight-controller data. We benchmark seven open-source VI-SLAM systems over 504 runs. The results show that sensor choice has a larger effect on trajectory error than the spread between algorithms: all seven methods achieve lower median error on the OAK-D Pro than on the D435i. ForVis is intended to support evaluation of speed, accuracy and robustness for VI-SLAM in challenging forest flight.

</details>


### [28] [Robot Tool Design from Scratch via Behavior-Aware Hierarchical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35479)
*Yinghan Chen,Xiyao Tian,Yizan Dai,Yuyang Li,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HOT 框架，从任务目标出发联合设计工具结构、形状与动作，并在仿真和真实机器人上验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人工具设计方法通常预先规定或生成工具结构，只优化形状和动作，未将结构纳入物理优化过程。研究旨在根据期望的物理效果，从零开始设计工具。

Method: HOT 采用分层优化：上层通过 BASS 搜索离散工具结构；下层进行物理优化，评估任务表现，并将里程碑进展作为行为证据反馈给结构搜索，最终联合优化工具形状与动作。

Result: 在四项具有不同物理功能的工具使用任务中，HOT 仅评估最多包含 5600 万种结构的搜索空间中的一小部分，即发现具备功能的结构。随后进行的几何优化降低了所有任务的损失且保持成功，并产生了功能上可解释的形变。3D 打印后的工具使用仿真中生成的动作，在真实机器人上完成了全部任务。

Conclusion: 将工具结构、形状和动作共同纳入任务驱动的物理优化，可从期望效果而非已知工具目录出发设计工具，为实现更开放式的工具制造提供了进展。

Abstract: The ability to design a tool for a task marks a level of intelligence beyond merely understanding, selecting, or using one. Existing methods for robotic tool design typically optimize a tool's continuous shape and action within a structure that is prescribed or generated beforehand, so the structure itself stays outside the physical optimization loop. We study task-driven tool design from scratch, where tool structure, shape, and action are all derived from the desired physical outcome. Here we show that the three elements can be designed jointly by HOT, a hierarchical optimization whose upper level searches over discrete tool structures with BASS, while lower-level physical optimization evaluates their task behavior and returns milestone progress as behavioral evidence for the search, ultimately providing jointly optimized shape and action. On four tool-use tasks with distinct physical functions, HOT discovers functional structures after evaluating only a small fraction of search spaces containing up to 56 million structures, and the subsequent refinement of their geometry lowers the task loss on all tasks while preserving success, through deformations that are functionally interpretable. Once 3D printed, the tools accomplish all tasks on a real robot with the actions found in simulation. Designing tools from required physical effects, rather than a catalog of known tools, is a step toward the open-ended tool making seen in humans and animals.

</details>


### [29] [CoBrush: A Hierarchical Planning Framework for Human-Robot Co-Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35476)
*Dantong Qin,Yike Guo,Qinlin Liu,Alessandro Bozzon,Pan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoBrush 是一种分层框架，使机器人能够根据不断变化的人类意图，在实体画布上持续开展多轮协同绘画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考图驱动或反应式绘画系统多针对单次绘制或草图补全进行优化，难以维持多轮协作的一致性，也不擅长逐步创作内容丰富的场景。

Method: 将多轮协同绘画拆分为语义意图推断、空间定位和笔画级控制三个环节，支持在真实丙烯画布上逐步发展场景；通过人机绘画会话、压力测试和用户研究进行评估。

Result: 相较于单轮基线，CoBrush 在语义对齐、空间推进稳定性以及用户感知的机器人动作合理性方面表现更好。摘要未提供具体数值或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 结构化的多阶段推理有助于提升交互绘画的连贯性与稳健性，并支持逐步创作内容丰富的实体艺术作品。

Abstract: Embodied co-painting requires a robot to repeatedly update a shared physical canvas while human intent evolves over interaction. Existing reference-driven painters or reactive assistants are typically optimized for single-shot rendering or sketch completion, limiting their ability to sustain coherent multi-round collaboration or to construct complex, content-rich scenes over time. We present CoBrush, a hierarchical framework that formulates multi-round co-painting as a coordinated semantic, spatial, and execution process. By separating high-level intent inference from spatial grounding and stroke-level control, the system supports progressive scene development on real acrylic canvases. We evaluate the framework through real human-robot painting sessions, stress tests, and user studies. Compared to single-turn baselines, our approach achieves stronger semantic alignment, more stable spatial progression, and higher perceived plausibility of robot actions. These results demonstrate that structured multi-stage reasoning improves the coherence and robustness of interactive painting and supports the progressive development of content-rich physical artworks.

</details>


### [30] [Rethinking Causal Action Tokenization with Conditional Annealing in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35469)
*Chenyu Zhang,Yuhang Cao,Daru Du,Yingxi Lu,Jing Shao,Ruoqu Chen,Jiajun Liu,Liu Cao,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CATok 将动作离散化建模为因果生成过程，使动作 token 更适配自回归视觉—语言—动作模型，并提升重建、推理与任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作 tokenizer 侧重压缩，生成的表示与自回归模型的语义结构不匹配，限制了动作重建和 VLA 系统的效率与性能。

Method: CATok 通过条件退火逐步推进 flow matching；每个 token 依赖此前 token，并编码特定噪声水平下的残差，形成由粗到细的因果表示。随后，基于多模态扩散 Transformer 的 token 条件 flow-matching 解码器，将离散 token 还原为连续动作片段。

Result: 在三个仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务中，CATok 在重建保真度—压缩率权衡及推理效率方面优于现有方法，并提升 VLA 任务成功率与训练效率。

Conclusion: CATok 以因果结构化的离散瓶颈连接高层语义推理与低层动作执行，为高性能、可扩展的纯自回归 VLA 系统提供了一种方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a scalable path to robot learning, yet existing action tokenizers treat tokenization as a compression problem, producing representations that are semantically misaligned with the autoregressive backbone. We propose CATok, a causal action tokenizer that reframes tokenization as a causally structured generative process. CATok introduces a conditional annealing mechanism that extracts action tokens by progressively annealing a flow-matching process: each token is conditioned on all preceding tokens and encodes the residual reconstruction signal at a specific noise level, establishing a coarse-to-fine causal token space whose generative semantics are structurally aligned with autoregressive modeling. A token-conditioned flow-matching decoder built on Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (MMDiT) reconstructs continuous action chunks from these discrete tokens with the precision of hybrid diffusion-head architectures. This discrete bottleneck enforces knowledge insulation by design, cleanly separating high-level semantic reasoning from low-level motor execution without requiring explicit attention masking. Extensive evaluations across three simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that CATok consistently surpasses existing tokenization methods in both reconstruction fidelity-compression tradeoff and inference efficiency, while improving VLA task success rate and training efficiency, establishing a high-performance, scalable foundation for purely autoregressive VLA systems.

</details>


### [31] [Uni-VLaT: Whole-Body Tactile Adaptation of VLA Policies for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35450)
*Zihao Wang,Shutong Liu,Siqi Zheng,Liu Cao,Ruoqi Chen,Rundong Liu,Yanchao Yang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-VLaT 将全身触觉与视觉、语言和动作策略结合，并通过预测未来多模态表征提升机器人接触丰富任务的控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉和本体感觉难以充分识别被遮挡的接触区域；预设位置的稀疏力或力矩传感也无法保留全身接触的空间分布信息。

Method: 为视觉-语言-动作策略加入触觉通路。训练触觉潜在状态以生成动作，并预测未来触觉、本体感觉和视觉表征，从而形成以触觉为基础的多模态上下文。

Result: 在涵盖触觉触发移动、持续身体接触、人机接触和移动操作的五项真实机器人任务中，平均成功率为75%；比无触觉基线高43个百分点，比无预测监督的触觉基线高7个百分点。两种预训练策略骨干上，桌面清扫任务均提升30个百分点，背部轻拍行走任务提升85至90个百分点。消融实验表明，情境化触觉预测和绝对未来目标对性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 预测式触觉学习能够有效扩展预训练视觉-语言-动作策略，使其适用于全身物理交互任务。

Abstract: Physical contact often determines how a humanoid should respond during loco-manipulation, yet vision and proprioception alone are often insufficient to characterize physical interaction, especially when the contact region is occluded. Unlike sparse force or torque measurements at predefined regions, distributed tactile sensing preserves spatially resolved contact patterns across the robot body. We therefore study how to integrate such whole-body tactile information into vision-language-action (VLA) policies for contact-rich control. Our approach, Uni-VLaT, introduces a tactile pathway whose latent state is trained not only for action generation, but also to predict future tactile, proprioceptive, and visual representations. This predictive objective builds a tactile-anchored multimodal context, encouraging a more structured understanding of the physical world. We evaluate Uni-VLaT on five real-robot tasks covering tactile-triggered locomotion, sustained physical interaction, human-robot contact, and loco-manipulation. Uni-VLaT achieves a 75% average success rate, outperforming a baseline without tactile input by 43 points and a tactile-input baseline without predictive supervision by 7 points. Across two pretrained VLA backbones, our method improves Table Sweeping by 30 points on both backbones and Back-Tap Walking by 85-90 points. Ablations further show that contextualized tactile prediction and absolute future targets are critical to performance. These results indicate that predictive tactile learning provides an effective route for extending pretrained VLA policies to whole-body physical interaction.

</details>


### [32] [Revision, Not Restart: Revisable Visual Plans for Closed-Loop World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35439)
*Pengyiang Liu,Junbo Niu,Wenhao Zheng,Xinchen Chen,Canyu Li,Zhongyue Shi,Jiahao Xie,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可根据执行反馈修订视觉未来预测的时序规划方法 RTP，在两个基准上取得较高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型利用预测的视觉未来来引导机器人动作，但实际执行反馈可能使部分预测失效，同时原有任务结构仍有价值。需要一种机制，既保留有用规划信息，又能根据最新观测进行调整。

Method: RTP 将视觉未来作为持续的动作条件，并通过学习得到的修订桥，从视觉生成过程中保存的中间状态继续生成，适应当前观测。视觉与动作监督将修订结果连接到后续控制；时间感知历史提供观测证据，自适应策略则在保留原预测、桥接修订和基于新噪声重新规划之间作出选择，再解码下一步动作。

Result: 在 RoboMME 和 RMBench 上，RTP 的任务平均成功率分别为 48.6% 和 84.8%。匹配对比支持学习式续接的有效性；检查点来源与动作前缀的估计影响为正，但证据精度相对有限。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于反馈修订视觉规划并将其用于闭环控制，有助于提升任务执行表现；学习式续接获得了对照实验支持，而部分因素的效果仍需更精确验证。

Abstract: World-action models use predicted visual futures to condition robot actions, yet execution feedback can invalidate parts of a prediction while leaving its task structure useful. We propose Revisable Temporal Planning (RTP), which maintains the visual future as a persistent action condition and revises it after feedback. Its central mechanism is a learned revision bridge: it resumes an intermediate state saved during visual generation and adapts its continuation to current observations. Visual and action supervision connect this revision to subsequent control. Time-aware history supplies observed evidence, and an adaptive policy selects retention, bridge revision, or fresh replanning from new noise before decoding the next action. On RoboMME and RMBench, RTP achieves task-averaged success rates of 48.6% and 84.8%, respectively. Matched comparisons support learned continuation; estimated checkpoint-source and action-prefix effects are positive but less precisely resolved. These results connect feedback-driven visual-plan revision to closed-loop task performance. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [33] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents: From Digital Programs to Physical-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35432)
*Hongcheng Gao,Jingjing Zhou,Zelin Zheng,Shijia Ge,Jay Zhu,Yazhe Wang,Jianshu Zeng,Xuan Shangguan,Di Wu,Lingyu He,Zhiqi Jia,Sihang Wu,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Physical Coding：将任务状态与执行过程显式表示为代码，并通过工具调用、反馈验证和程序修订提升机器人任务的泛化与长程执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA/WAM 将任务要求、环境条件、执行进度和失败恢复隐含在动作序列中，因此对布局或视角变化敏感，指令泛化和过程检查也较困难。论文借鉴数字编程智能体的工具使用与反馈修订机制，探索可检查、可调整、可复用的物理任务表示。

Method: 提出两类代码表示：Code as World 编码物体、关系、约束和进度；Code as Policy 组织规划、验证、恢复与执行。基于此构建 HexaAnything，调用感知、规划和控制工具（包括 VLA/WAM 策略），根据外部反馈进行决策，并将验证过的执行轨迹转化为数据和记忆，以支持工具、框架及模型的迭代改进。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 上，HexaAnything 相较 XR-1 VLA 提高了 Composite-Unseen 和整体成功率；经 Harness 训练的 HexaModel 在所有数据划分上优于基础模型。该系统还在 PhyBench 和 AgileX 双臂机器人上自主完成物理实验及多数桌面任务，部分任务用时优于已发表结果。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式代码轨迹有助于机器人执行经验的复用，并可能使模型逐步内化物理执行能力。论文展示了数据、模型和工具的自我演进潜力；未来方向包括将能力内化至模型权重、自主改进架构与表示，以及拓展至制造和科学应用。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action (WAM) models map observations and instructions directly to robot actions. This directness ties a policy to training: minor layout or viewpoint changes cause failure, and instructions generalize poorly. The root cause lies in representation: task requirements, conditions, progress, and failure recovery are implicitly encoded in action sequences, making them difficult to inspect or revise. Digital coding agents offer a precedent: LLMs call tools, verify results, and revise from feedback as executable code. The same working pattern of explicit state, manageable execution, and revisable procedures underlies generalization and long-horizon execution in the physical world, letting physical experience return as reusable programs, memory, or evidence. We propose Physical Coding, representing task state and execution as code. Code as World records objects, relations, constraints, and progress; Code as Policy organizes planning, verification, recovery, and execution. We build HexaAnything, which calls perception, planning, and control tools, including VLA/WAM policies, and makes in-the-loop decisions from external feedback. Verified traces become data and memory, enabling evolution from tools and Harness to model weights, architectures, and ultimately hardware and task design. On RoboCasa365, HexaAnything improves Composite-Unseen and overall success over XR-1 VLA, and its Harness-trained HexaModel beats the base on every split, indicating code traces internalize physical execution. On PhyBench and a dual-arm AgileX robot, the agent autonomously completes physics experiments and most tabletop tasks, often faster than published results. We observe data, model, and tool self-evolution; future work targets weight internalization, autonomous redesign of architectures, languages, representations, and tasks, and deployment in manufacturing and science.

</details>


### [34] [Memory in the Sky: Low-Altitude Question Answering with Multi-Agent Memory Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35431)
*Chengyang Li,Yujie Wan,Shuai Wang,Kejiang Ye,Weijie Yuan,Boyu Zhou,Yik-Chung Wu,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出面向低空问答（LAQA）的记忆质量评估与资源分配框架，通过优化无人机记忆选择和传输功率提升问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资源分配方法侧重感知、通信、控制或计算指标，难以衡量无人机记忆对长期问答任务的实际价值；同时，问答流水线内部机制可能无法访问。

Method: 提出生成式对抗考试（GAE），利用前向模拟和考试得分评估候选记忆的检索质量及其下游问答价值。在此基础上构建记忆中心框架 MemCen，联合选择无人机并分配传输功率，以通信约束下的记忆质量最大化为目标；针对噪声受限场景推导质量感知的截断注水功率分配律，并设计 PSO 与 L2M 求解器。

Result: MemCen 在 CARLA Town04 和 Town05 的静态与动态通信条件下分别取得 92.4% 和 84.0% 的问答准确率；在真实世界 PMAS 基准上达到 88.5%。无人机与机器狗协同演示进一步展示了所获记忆对环境理解和导航的实用价值。

Conclusion: 研究将下游问答价值纳入无人机记忆管理与通信资源分配，为黑箱问答系统中的记忆优化提供了可操作的评估指标和方法，并通过模拟及真实实验验证其有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies low-altitude question answering (LAQA), in which distributed unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) memories are aggregated at a ground server to answer questions about observations over a long horizon. Unlike conventional resource allocation based on sensing, communication, control, or computation metrics, LAQA requires an explicit measure of memory value. We propose a generative adversarial exam (GAE) that uses forward simulation to evaluate memory retrieval and exam scores to quantify memory quality. This enables the downstream QA value of candidate memories to be measured and optimized without accessing the internal mechanisms of the black-box captioning, retrieval, and reasoning pipeline. Building on this metric, we develop a memory-centric (MemCen) framework that jointly selects UAVs and allocates transmit power to maximize memory quality under communication constraints. In the noise-limited regime, we derive a QoM-aware capped water-filling law that explicitly connects task utility with physical-layer power allocation. We further develop penalty successive optimization (PSO) and learning to memorize (L2M) solvers. MemCen achieves QA accuracies of 92.4% and 84.0% in CARLA Town04 and Town05 under static and dynamic communication conditions, respectively. In real-world experiments, MemCen achieves 88.5% QA accuracy on the panoramic multi-agent system (PMAS) benchmark. Finally, UAV-to-robot-dog demonstrations further validate the practical utility of the acquired memories for environmental understanding and navigation.

</details>
