During static rolling, the proposed controller maintained task-space tracking accuracy comparable to a conventional fixed-frame impedance controller while reducing the maximum compressive interaction force from $\mathbf{31.56}$~N to $\mathbf{20.09}$~N, corresponding to a $\mathbf{36.3\%}$ reduction. These results demonstrate the potential of ultrasound images as direct contact feedback for safe and accurate robot-assisted ultrasound imaging.

</details>


### [74] [RoboIRGBench: Benchmarking Implicit Referential Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34384)
*Aernaer Akelijiang,Jiannan Li,Zhineng Chen,Jingjing Chen,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboIRG-Bench，用于评估视觉—语言—动作模型从语言与感知上下文中推断隐含指代对象的能力；实验发现模型在隐含指代任务中的表现明显弱于显式指令任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准通常假设指令明确指出目标，但真实交流常通过上下文隐含表达对象、数量或关系。评估并提升模型对这类隐含指代的理解，是可靠遵循指令的重要前提。

Method: 作者将隐含指代落地定义为一项独立能力，并基于 RoboMME 构建 RoboIRG-Bench，包含由 11 项任务扩展出的 40 个变体，覆盖直接、推理介导、空间和上下文指代四类挑战。基准用于评估采用不同记忆机制的代表性视觉—语言—动作模型；研究还在 Franka Research 3 机械臂上进行真实操作验证。

Result: 模型在显式指令下表现良好，但当目标信息需从上下文中恢复时，性能会显著下降；推理介导和空间指代尤其困难。使用外部视觉—语言模型的系统相对更稳健，但仍存在明显失败，替换为更强的外部模型也无法消除差距。真实机器人实验同样观察到这一差距，错误既表现为指代对象识别错误，也会导致后续执行失败。

Conclusion: 隐含指代落地是机器人指令遵循中独立且尚未充分研究的能力。研究结果表明，提升可靠性需要模型更稳健地整合语言、感知、推理与动作信息。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet existing benchmarks typically assume that task-relevant information is explicitly specified in the instruction. In practice, however, humans frequently refer to objects, quantities, and relations implicitly, requiring robots to recover the intended target from linguistic and perceptual context. We study this capability as Implicit Referential Grounding (IRG) and introduce RoboIRG-Bench, a manipulation benchmark designed to systematically evaluate it. Built upon RoboMME, RoboIRG-Bench contains 40 variants derived from 11 tasks and covers four challenges, including direct, reasoning-mediated, spatial, and contextual referential grounding. As IRG often requires retaining and retrieving previously established context, we evaluate representative VLAs spanning different memory mechanisms. Our evaluation reveals a noticeable referential robustness gap. Models that perform well under explicit instructions can degrade sharply when the same task-relevant information must be recovered from context. Reasoning-mediated and spatial references are particularly challenging, while models using external VLMs show greater robustness but still exhibit significant failures. Moreover, replacing the external VLM with a stronger model does not eliminate these gaps. We further validate these findings on a Franka Research 3 robot arm, where the gap persists under real-world manipulation and manifests as both incorrect referent grounding and downstream execution failures. These results establish IRG as a distinct and underexplored capability for reliable robotic instruction following and highlight the need for VLAs that can robustly integrate language, perception, reasoning, and action.

</details>


### [75] [FutureDuet: Decoupling Observation Access from Future Supervision in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34362)
*Jie Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Junqi Liu,Weichen Zhang,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Jingyan Jiang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FutureDuet，一种为主相机与腕部相机设计差异化未来监督目标的World Action Model，在保持双视角控制信息的同时提升精细交互任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM通常将主相机和腕部相机融合为单一视觉流，并使用相同的未来视频预测目标。然而，主相机主要反映场景级任务进展，腕部相机则受到末端运动、视角变化和自遮挡影响，二者具有不同的视觉动态与预测需求，因此统一监督会限制表示学习和控制性能。

Method: FutureDuet保留主视角和腕部视角，并分别构建Task State与Interaction State。主视角使用未来RGB预测任务演化，同时通过交互掩码和机器人骨架强化对任务对象及机器人运动的监督；腕部视角采用未来潜变量预测，以建模短时交互变化而避免像素级重建。ActionDiT联合读取两类状态生成动作。所有辅助预测模块仅在训练阶段使用，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在RoboTwin50上，FutureDuet取得94.2%的干净场景成功率和94.1%的随机化场景成功率；在LIBERO上达到99.2%的平均成功率。对于六个需要精确交互的RoboTwin50任务，相较Fast-WAM，干净与随机化设置下的平均提升分别为9.2%和12.8%。受控实验表明，分离腕部视觉路径以及为两种视角设计独立未来监督均带来互补收益。

Conclusion: FutureDuet证明，依据不同相机视角的视觉动态定制未来监督，比对所有视角使用统一视频目标更有效。主视角负责场景级任务进展建模，腕部视角负责局部交互变化建模，二者结合能够显著提升机器人，尤其是精细操作任务中的控制性能，同时不增加部署阶段的计算开销。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot action generation with future visual supervision. Existing WAMs commonly fuse main and wrist observations into one visual stream and train both with the same future-video objective, despite their different visual dynamics. A stable main camera reveals scene-level task evolution, whereas wrist cameras move with the end effector, mixing local interaction changes with viewpoint shifts and self-occlusion. These contrasting predictive demands suggest that the two views may benefit from different future objectives. We introduce FutureDuet, which retains both views for control, while allowing each visual stream to receive a different future objective. For the main view, future RGB models task evolution, while interaction masks and robot skeletons focus supervision on task objects and robot motion. For the wrist stream, future latent prediction models short-horizon interaction changes without requiring pixel-level reconstruction. ActionDiT jointly reads the resulting Task State and Interaction State, combining scene-level progress with close-range interaction evidence. All auxiliary prediction modules are training-only, adding no inference overhead. FutureDuet achieves 94.2% clean and 94.1% randomized success on RoboTwin50 and 99.2% average success on LIBERO. The improvements are most pronounced on six RoboTwin50 tasks that require precise interaction, averaging gains of 9.2% and 12.8% over Fast-WAM in clean and randomized settings. Controlled studies further show complementary gains from separating the wrist pathway and designing future supervision separately for the two views.

</details>


### [76] [Predictive Semantic Safety: From Visual Physical Reasoning to Safety-Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34356)
*Taekyung Kim,Salem Fradi,Yanning Dai,Mateusz Ostaszewski,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测语义安全框架（PSS），利用视觉语言模型预测未来物理事件，并据此约束机器人控制；在仿真测试中，其安全回合率显著高于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据机器人当前周围的几何信息，无法识别接触、支撑失效等物理交互引发的未来风险，因此传统安全过滤可能遗漏潜在危险。

Method: 视觉语言模型预测物理事件及其发生时间，或直接预测物体位移；显式运动模型将事件假设转化为物体轨迹。随后使用分割保形预测联合校准多个物体与多个未来时刻的位置误差，并以几何形状边界估计物体占据区域。框架评估预设备份机动是否与该区域冲突，再生成输入仿射约束，在机器人动力学和输入限制下尽量修正名义控制，同时维持备份机动可行性。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 中使用 Unitree Go1 测试了设施坠落、冲击导致支撑丧失及接触传播等场景。PSS 的安全回合率为 99.3%，而仅使用当前障碍物几何信息的备份控制障碍函数基线为 43.3%。

Conclusion: 将视觉物理推理、统计校准和备份控制安全过滤结合，能够使机器人控制考虑由交互引发的未来占据风险；仿真结果显示该方法较当前几何信息基线具有更高的安全性。

Abstract: Physical interactions can create future hazards that are not apparent from the robot's current geometric surroundings. We present a framework termed Predictive Semantic Safety (PSS), which connects visual physical reasoning to backup-based safety filtering. A vision-language model (VLM) predicts physical events and their timing or directly predicts object displacements. An explicit motion model converts event hypotheses into object trajectories. Split conformal prediction calibrates position errors jointly across specified objects, observation times, and future times; geometric shape bounds convert the resulting position regions into predicted object occupancy. PSS evaluates a prescribed backup maneuver against this occupancy and derives input-affine constraints for minimally modifying the nominal input while preserving backup feasibility under the robot dynamics and input limits. MuJoCo experiments with a Unitree Go1 consider falling fixtures, impact-driven support loss, and contact propagation. PSS achieves a safe episode rate of 99.3%, compared with 43.3% for a Backup Control Barrier Function baseline that only uses current obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [77] [When World Models Lie: Adaptive Safety Analysis Under Wrong Imaginations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34300)
*John Cao,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应保序推断的潜空间安全滤波器，利用实际观测到的世界模型预测误差构造在线不确定性集合，并通过悲观优化提高安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型存在偏差、失准和高置信错误，基于其动力学学习的Hamilton–Jacobi安全价值函数可能继承这些误差，导致安全估计过度乐观。现有方法依赖集成模型分歧或价值目标残差，但这些信号在模型预测偏离观测时仍可能较小。

Method: 利用预测潜状态与由观测推断的潜状态之间的差异，采用自适应保序推断在线构造不确定性半径及集合；随后在该集合内对学习到的安全价值函数进行最小化，以获得对模型误差更稳健的悲观安全评估。方法在模型准确时保持较低保守性，在观测揭示模型失配时自动提高谨慎程度，并给出自适应不确定性半径的有限时间覆盖保证。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，该方法相较现有潜空间安全滤波器显著减少失败，同时保持任务完成能力。摘要未提供具体的失败率、覆盖率或任务性能数值。

Conclusion: 直接利用观测揭示的世界模型误差，比依赖间接不确定性信号更适合校准潜空间安全推理。所提出方法在安全性、保守性和任务完成之间实现了较好的平衡，并具备理论覆盖保证。

Abstract: World models offer a powerful substrate for safety reasoning in high-dimensional robotic systems, but they are also fallible: their predictions can be biased, miscalibrated, or confidently wrong. This creates a central challenge for latent-space safety filters, which often learn Hamilton-Jacobi safety value functions on the dynamics of a world model. If the world model is incorrect, the resulting value function can inherit its errors and produce overconfident safety estimates. Existing latent safety filters often rely on auxiliary signals such as ensemble disagreement or value-target consistency residuals for adaptation, but these signals can remain small even when the world model's predictions deviate from observations. We propose an adaptive latent safety filter that calibrates safety reasoning using directly observed world-model error. Our method uses Adaptive Conformal Inference to construct online uncertainty sets from discrepancies between predicted and observation-inferred latent states, then evaluates safety pessimistically by minimizing the learned value function over these sets. This allows the filter to remain minimally conservative when the world model is accurate, while becoming more cautious when observations reveal model mismatch. We provide a finite-time coverage guarantee for the adaptive uncertainty radius. Through simulation and hardware experiments, we show that our method significantly reduces failures relative to state-of-the-art latent safety filters while preserving task completion.

</details>


### [78] [TLC-DiT: Task-Aligned Local Visual Conditioning for Robust Multitask Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34297)
*Xianbo Cai,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Zihang Wang,Yijun Lu,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TLC-DiT在多任务机器人操作策略中引入显式、任务引导的局部视觉特征图，在不改变扩散目标和动作生成流程的情况下提升了任务成功率、视觉扰动鲁棒性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件机器人策略通常将任务相关的局部视觉证据隐含在视觉骨干网络或注意力层中，导致策略难以检查，并且在相机、背景和传感器噪声变化下容易性能下降。因此，论文旨在构建一个显式且与任务对齐的局部视觉通道。

Method: 提出TLC-DiT，作为Multitask Diffusion Transformer策略的插件式扩展。针对每个相机视角，使用冻结的DINOv2提取图像块特征，并通过CLIP任务嵌入进行FiLM调制；随后利用轻量级CoordConv CNN适配器将特征细化为空间连续的局部特征图。该特征图与原始全局图像、语言、关节状态和扩散时间步条件进行拼接，同时保持原有扩散目标和动作生成过程不变。

Result: 在LIBERO上，TLC-DiT平均成功率达到93.5%，高于Multitask DiT的86.5%和SmolVLA的79.25%。在LIBERO-plus上，总成功率由54.07%提升至57.24%，且在相机、背景和传感器噪声变化下提升更明显。在真实双臂任务中，Teabag Putting完成率由44%提升至89%，而Match Box Opening性能基本保持不变。特征图可视化显示，模型能够在不同视角和视觉扰动下关注任务相关区域。

Conclusion: 显式的任务引导局部视觉通道能够有效增强多任务机器人策略的性能、鲁棒性和可解释性。TLC-DiT以较小的结构改动兼容现有扩散策略，并通过可视化特征图提供了直接检查视觉依据的途径。

Abstract: Language-conditioned robot policies have made clear progress in multitask manipulation, but task-relevant local visual evidence usually stays hidden inside a visual backbone or attention layers. This leaves the policy difficult to inspect and fragile under visual change, two symptoms of a missing explicit, task-aligned local visual channel. We present TLC-DiT, a plug-in extension of the Multitask Diffusion Transformer (DiT) policy that adds explicit task-guided local visual feature maps without changing the diffusion objective or the action-generation process. For each camera view, frozen DINOv2 patch features are modulated by the CLIP task embedding through FiLM and refined by a lightweight CoordConv CNN adapter into smooth spatial maps, which are concatenated with the original global image, language, joint-state, and timestep conditions. On LIBERO, TLC-DiT reaches a 93.5% average success rate, compared with 86.5% for Multitask DiT and 79.25% for SmolVLA. On LIBERO-plus, the total success rate improves from 54.07% to 57.24%, with larger gains under camera, background, and sensor-noise changes. In real-world bimanual tasks, TLC-DiT raises Teabag Putting completion from 44% to 89% while maintaining comparable Match Box Opening performance. Feature-map visualizations confirm that the model attends to task-relevant regions across views and perturbations, providing a direct way to inspect the visual evidence.

</details>


### [79] [NavHarness: Towards Lifelong Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34276)
*Xunyi Zhao,Jian Zhou,Sihao Lin,Gengze Zhou,Zerui Li,Xinyu Yan,Jiajun Liu,Anton van den Hengel,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavHarness 是一种无需训练的具身导航框架，将记忆处理纳入导航流程，并在跨任务、跨会话场景中利用和修正既有经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次导航任务中的多模态推理能力不断提升，但长期导航还要求智能体依赖可能不完整或与新观察冲突的地图和历史记录。研究旨在解决经验如何跨任务保存、校验并有效复用的问题。

Method: 导航过程中，智能体通过多轮交互结合地图、任务记录和房屋知识，并根据当前观察核验信息、记录修正以辅助行动。框架将经验保留到后续新会话或失败恢复尝试中，并通过结果验证和任务结束摘要支持经验复用；研究还比较了结构化恢复交接与等长度摘要，并评估跨房屋经验整合。

Result: 在 GOAT-Bench 上，相较于仅使用上下文的独立会话，NavHarness 的 s-SR 分别为 Astra 提升 18.6 个百分点、为 Opus 5 提升 22.6 个百分点。使用 SLAM 估计位姿时，GPT-6 Astra 在 GOAT-Bench 上达到 83.7 s-SR 和 36.9 e-SR，在 IR2R-CE 上达到 85.9 s-SR。结构化恢复交接优于等长度摘要；跨房屋整合经验也比仅保留地图和任务记录更有助于导航。

Conclusion: 长期导航的进步不仅取决于单任务能力，还取决于连续推理会话能否有效承接、核验和整合先前经验。

Abstract: Frontier models can now perform well on individual embodied navigation tasks through multi-round multimodal reasoning with simple tools. Across successive tasks, however, an agent must also rely on an evolving map and earlier search records, both of which may be incomplete or conflict with new observations. We present NavHarness, a training-free embodied harness towards lifelong navigation that makes memory processing part of the navigation loop. During navigation, its multi-round agentic session draws on maps, task records, and house knowledge, checking them against observations and recording corrections to guide its actions. NavHarness preserves this experience across fresh conversations for new tasks or recovery attempts, while outcome verification and run-end summaries support its later reuse. On GOAT-Bench, NavHarness improves s-SR over context-only independent sessions by 18.6 points with Astra and 22.6 with Opus 5. Using SLAM-estimated poses, NavHarness with GPT-6 Astra achieves state-of-the-art task success of 83.7 s-SR with 36.9 e-SR on GOAT-Bench and 85.9 s-SR on IR2R-CE. To understand these gains, we examine how experience is carried between sessions and find that structured recovery handovers outperform length-matched summaries. In extended deployments across houses, consolidation improves navigation beyond retaining maps and task records, with case studies showing how agents use earlier experience to interpret new goals, investigate unresolved questions, and resume failed searches. We suggest that progress towards lifelong navigation depends on how successive reasoning sessions build on prior experience, alongside improvements in single-task capability.

</details>


### [80] [Bayesian Active Learning for Intent Disambiguation in Interactive Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34270)
*Huao Li,Carson Sobolewski,Augustinos Saravanos,William Tan,John Karigiannis,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于贝叶斯主动学习的机器人指令澄清方法，将用户意图表示为带语义落地的信号时序逻辑（STL）任务规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言指令常含糊或不完整；若完全依赖大语言模型生成多轮对话，可能产生系统性失误，影响机器人对意图的理解与执行。

Method: 利用大语言模型生成候选STL规范并将有区分度的对比转化为自然语言问题；通过贝叶斯优化估计用户意图的不确定性，并选择信息增益最大的澄清问题。推断收敛后，将规范交给形式化规划器生成可验证的机器人轨迹。

Result: 在四类仿真与真实任务领域中，该方法总体上比大语言模型基线取得更高的任务满足度，并减少澄清轮次；同时帮助较小模型缩小与较大型推理模型的性能差距。

Conclusion: 将澄清建模为针对形式化任务规范的主动学习，可提升交互式机器人规划的效率与可靠性，并结合大语言模型的语言能力和贝叶斯方法的不确定性管理。

Abstract: Interactive robot planning requires robots to infer and execute human intentions from natural language instructions that are often ambiguous, incomplete, or underspecified. Although large language models (LLMs) provide a powerful interface for clarification, relying on the generative model to drive an multi-turn conversation can introduce systematic failures. We propose a Bayesian framework that treats clarification as an active learning problem over grounded Signal Temporal Logic (STL) task specifications. Our method uses LLMs to initialize candidate formal specifications and translate informative contrasts into natural-language clarification questions, while Bayesian optimization maintains uncertainty estimation over user intent and selects queries that maximize information gain. After convergence, the inferred STL specification is passed to a formal planner to synthesize a verifiable robot trajectory. Across four simulated and real-world task domains, our approach generally achieves higher task satisfaction and requires fewer clarification rounds than LLM baselines, while helping smaller models close the performance gap against larger reasoning models.

</details>


### [81] [SAGE: Symbolic Action-Gating and Editing for LLM Task Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34268)
*Trung Minh Bui,JongSul Moon,YoungOuk Kim,Quang-Ngoc Phung,Se-Woong Jun,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE通过零模型调用的符号动作门控与局部计划修补，提升具身智能体的执行安全性和故障恢复效率；在较难的多目标组合任务上，相比基线取得明显更高的目标完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM具身规划通常缺少执行前的环境约束检查；常用基准又趋于饱和，难以区分方法优劣。执行中出现失败时，整体重规划还可能带来较高的模型调用成本。

Method: SAGE使用两项轻量机制：以符号规则检查动作前置条件，并在违规时拦截动作、给出类型化原因；仅重新生成失败子目标之后的计划后缀，保留已完成及未受影响的部分。系统还结合初始与在线记忆，并采用留一检索评测协议，在五种开放权重模型和75项AI2-THOR任务上评估；另测试注入故障后的恢复、执行安全门控及验证器可迁移性。

Result: 标准基准上，52%的实例已可轻易完成，目标完整度趋于饱和，SAGE与强层级基线持平。较难的多目标组合任务中，SAGE在四种模型上的完整度领先0.06至0.23。注入执行故障后，其恢复可靠性与整体重规划相当，但LLM调用减少至后者的约三分之一至五分之二。符号门控使所有受测规划器的模拟器报告步成功率均有提升，最高增加0.11；门控耗时约0.008毫秒/计划。验证器在ALFWorld留出数据上的可迁移性为0.89。

Conclusion: 结果表明，低成本的符号运行时检查与局部修补可互补：前者在执行前拦截不安全动作，后者以较少模型调用实现故障恢复。SAGE在饱和基准上的优势有限，但在更具区分度的组合任务中表现突出；其边缘设备复现和跨环境验证结果支持实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now the default cognitive core of embodied household agents, yet the plans they emit are rarely checked against a grounded model of the environment before execution, and the task-success they report is often measured on benchmarks so saturated that no method can be separated from another. We present SAGE (Symbolic Action-Gating and Editing), a single-LLM planner built from two lightweight mechanisms: a domain-agnostic symbolic gate (~250 lines of Python, zero tokens, $O(|\pi|)$) that blocks precondition-violating actions with typed reasons as a runtime safety monitor, and a local edit that regenerates only the failed sub-goal's suffix, keeping completed and untouched work intact; a hybrid seed+live memory store supports cold-start coverage. We evaluate under a leak-free protocol (leave-one-out retrieval) over five open-weight models and a 75-task AI2-THOR benchmark. On the standard benchmark goal-completeness saturates (52% of instances trivially solved) and SAGE ties strong hierarchical baselines. On a harder, method-agnostic multi-goal composition, SAGE's completeness lead re-emerges large (+0.06 to +0.23 across four models). Under injected mid-execution failures, SAGE recovers as reliably as whole-plan replanners at 2.4-3.3x fewer LLM calls. As a verify-before-execute gate, the symbolic monitor blocks unsafe actions before actuation and raises simulator-reported step-success for every planner tested (up to +0.11), a signal the verifier never sees (non-circular). Because the gate calls no model (0.008 ms/plan), it is a safety layer that runs essentially free on the edge: SAGE planning reproduces its quality on a Jetson AGX Orin, where small-model verification helps most. We release the benchmark, the leak-free protocol, the recovery and safety-gate harnesses, and a verifier-portability study (auto-induced on ALFWorld, 0.89 held-out).

</details>


### [82] [RoboICL: Embodied In-Context Learning with GPT-6 Astra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34261)
*Fangcheng Liu,Yeqing Shen,Anda Cheng,Weishi Mi,Chao Tang,Chenyuan Liu,Yushun Xiang,Tingguang Li,Yong-Lu Li,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboICL通过组织示范样例与交互记忆，提升通用视觉语言模型的机器人控制表现，无需针对机器人更新模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型虽擅长开放式及语言或图像条件下的操作，但在高精度和长时程任务上仍较弱；研究旨在弥补这些能力差距，同时避免训练专用VLA模型或更新模型参数。

Method: 框架区分可选的示范上下文与记录模型自身动作及结果的交互记忆，两者采用统一的“观察—动作—回执—观察”结构。为跨任务阶段保留经验，方法结合抽样示范块与有界锚定记忆：固定锚点保留较早交互，最新交互则支持即时纠错；另可选用Jev门控动作复用以减少模型调用。

Result: 在30项RoboDojo任务中，除Open任务采用零样本外，其余任务使用一个示范。相较官方零样本基线，各类别进度分数提升20至27分；RoboICL在Memory和Open类别领先基线，在Precision上与最强基线表现相当，在Long-Horizon上保持竞争力。30项任务总体得分为50.64，高于最强基线的33.68；在另一10项任务子集上得分60.60，与混合方法相差2.00分以内。三项真实机器人任务的平均进度从零样本的14.45提升至一个示范时的63.33、三个示范时的78.89。两项开发任务中，动作复用使模型调用减少33%至48%。

Conclusion: 通过有效管理示范与运行时交互经验，RoboICL显著增强了通用视觉语言模型的机器人控制能力，尤其改善了记忆与开放类任务，并在真实机器人测试中显示出示范数量带来的性能提升。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models offer a promising way to zero-shot robot control: \gptastra{} excels at open-ended and language- or image-conditioned manipulation but remains substantially weaker on high-precision and long-horizon tasks. We introduce \emph{RoboICL}, an in-context robot-control framework that narrows these gaps without robot-specific parameter updates or a learned VLA. RoboICL separates \emph{demonstration context}, which provides recorded examples when available, from \emph{interaction memory}, which accumulates the model's own actions and observed outcomes. Both use a shared observation--action--receipt--observation grammar. To preserve experience across task stages, RoboICL combines sampled demonstration blocks with bounded anchored memory. Fixed anchors keep earlier rollout interactions available for in-context learning, while the latest interaction supports immediate error correction. Across 30 RoboDojo tasks, using zero shot for Open and one demonstration elsewhere, RoboICL improves on official zero-shot \gptastra{} by 20--27 progress-score points in every category. It leads the leaderboard baselines on Memory and Open, achieves comparable performance to the strongest Precision baseline, and remains competitive on Long-Horizon. Its 30-task Overall score is 50.64, versus 33.68 for the strongest baseline. On a separate ten-task subset, RoboICL scores 60.60, within 2.00 points of the $\pi_{0.5}$ + \gptastra{} hybrid approach. On three real-robot tasks, mean progress rises from 14.45 at zero shot to 63.33 at one shot and 78.89 at three shots. On two development tasks, optional Jev-gated action reuse reduces \gptastra{} calls by 33--48\%. Code is available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [83] [UMR: Universal Manipulation Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34256)
*Song Liu,Linyi Li,Yanshun Zhao,Rxuan Li,Xinrui Xu,Yi Ju,Yahui Deng,Senge Zhang,Guoyu Liu,Yixuan Li,Wuyang Zhang,Yao Li,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMR提出统一几何动作表示，使单一策略能够将人类示范零样本迁移至不同机器人和部署条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作策略依赖特定机器人的动作空间，难以规模化利用跨具身示范，也难以迁移到新机器人和空间变化场景。

Method: UMR将操作分解为世界坐标系中的具身无关物体运动（World Flow）与相对当前位姿的末端执行器运动（Ego Trajectory），并通过SE(3)共轭建立几何耦合。基于此构建0.5B参数的WEPVLA，采用双流Point Action Adapter和统一Point Action Expert；另以DES通过分阶段点云编辑扩充物体配置，同时保留示范中的接触几何。

Result: 仿真中，WEPVLA在LIBERO上平均成功率为97.5%，在RLBench十项任务上为85.7%。真实实验中，每项任务仅使用约10分钟人类示范且不使用机器人示范，在六种评测设置下平均成功率达91.7%，高于HumanEgo的60.8%。

Conclusion: 统一几何动作空间结合数据高效增强，有望支持从人类示范到异构机器人的零样本技能迁移，并提升跨部署条件的泛化能力。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied manipulation hinges on a unified action representation that generalizes across embodiments and scales readily. Yet existing policies rely on embodiment-specific action spaces, making cross-embodiment demonstrations difficult to leverage at scale and limiting transfer to new embodiments and spatial variations. To this end, we introduce Universal Manipulation Representation (UMR), a unified action representation that enables zero-shot skill transfer from human demonstrations to heterogeneous robots. UMR decomposes manipulation into two functionally distinct yet geometrically linked components: embodiment-agnostic World Flow, which describes task-relevant object motion in the world frame, and Ego Trajectory, which represents end-effector motion relative to the current pose. We instantiate UMR as World--Ego Point VLA (WEPVLA), a compact 0.5B-parameter policy that learns in the unified geometric action space through a dual-stream Point Action Adapter and a unified Point Action Expert, with an $SE(3)$ conjugation coupling the two components. To improve data efficiency, we complement UMR with a Data-Efficient Strategy (DES) that diversifies object configurations through stage-aware point-cloud editing while preserving demonstrated contact geometry. In simulation, WEPVLA achieves average success rates of 97.5\% on LIBERO and 85.7\% on the 10-task RLBench benchmark. In real-world experiments, a single policy trained on human demonstrations augmented by DES transfers zero-shot to diverse deployment conditions. With about 10 minutes of collected human demonstrations per task and no robot demonstrations, it achieves 91.7\% average success across six evaluation settings, compared with 60.8\% for HumanEgo. Code and additional materials are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [84] [WAM-OPD: Sharpening World Action Models via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34250)
*Panjun Liu,Xiaohan Lei,Shiqi Zhang,Yikun Wang,Yongxin Zhang,Mingyi Hu,Shida Sun,Jiateng Shou,Wengang Zhou,Jiajun Deng,Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAM-OPD 利用按策略蒸馏提升预训练机器人模型的目标任务能力，并通过无需新增环境交互的轨迹回放降低蒸馏成本，同时保留原有技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统按策略蒸馏需要随学生策略变化反复采集环境轨迹；在闭环机器人操作中，这一过程成本高，真实机器人上尤其难以实施。

Method: 提出 WAM-OPD，并设计前缀加权轨迹回放（PWTR）。PWTR 从固定轨迹池中回放初始学生策略与任务教师策略采集的轨迹；学生在存储的历史条件下生成新的去噪路径，由教师提供监督。针对固定轨迹与当前策略历史分布之间的偏移，PWTR依据每个决策之前轨迹前缀累积的路径分数构造代理重要性权重，对蒸馏损失进行重加权。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人实验表明，蒸馏期间无需额外环境交互即可进行任务适配。两种设置下，目标任务表现均有所提升，且未参与适配任务上的性能接近初始水平。

Conclusion: WAM-OPD 结合 PWTR，为预训练世界动作模型提供了一种减少环境采集需求、兼顾目标任务提升与原有能力保留的蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Pretrained world action models (WAMs) provide generalist capabilities across diverse robotic manipulation tasks, yet improving target-task performance to an expert level without degrading pretrained skills remains challenging. We explore on-policy distillation (OPD) for WAMs and introduce WAM-OPD. WAM-OPD inherits the advantage of OPD methods that transfer task-specific teacher knowledge under the student's own induced distribution, rather than directly fitting the student to a narrow task-specific data distribution. However, in closed-loop manipulation, the observation histories change as the student policy evolves, requiring fresh environment rollouts to remain on-policy. Applying OPD to WAMs entails repeated data collection, which is costly even in simulation and often impractical on real robots. To avoid repeated environment rollouts during distillation, we introduce prefix-weighted trajectory replay (PWTR). PWTR uses a fixed trajectory pool composed primarily of initial-student rollouts, supplemented with task-specific teacher rollouts to broaden trajectory coverage. For each trajectory replayed from this pool, PWTR conditions the current policy on successive stored histories to generate fresh denoising paths, along which the task-specific teacher provides supervision. Although these denoising paths are refreshed as the policy evolves, the replayed environment trajectories remain fixed. PWTR therefore reweights per-decision distillation losses using proxy importance weights derived from path scores accumulated over the trajectory prefix preceding each decision to mitigate the resulting shift in the history distribution. Simulated and real-world experiments demonstrate task adaptation without additional environment interaction during distillation. In both settings, WAM-OPD improves target-task performance while retaining near-initial performance on tasks excluded from adaptation.

</details>


### [85] [GAE: General Action Expert for Real-Time Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34233)
*Yuefan Wang,Huaicheng Zhou,Xiao He,Zhijie He,Mingchuan Yang,Huayi Zhang,Li Chai,Jinxin Liu,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAE 是一种面向人形机器人的通用全身遥操作框架，旨在实现多样、灵活且低延迟的人体动作跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作系统难以同时覆盖丰富动态的全身行为，并保持及时的人机动作同步；直接使用人体动作还会受到噪声及人体与机器人形态差异的影响。

Method: 从视频、动画和动作捕捉等异构来源构建并增强大规模人体动作数据集。采用两阶段训练：先训练特权生成策略在仿真中跟踪人体动作并生成可行的机器人轨迹，再训练可部署执行策略，通过课程式域随机化学习跟踪这些轨迹。此外，使用延迟条件化的动作预判机制补偿端到端时延。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 和 Westlake O1 机器人上的仿真与真实实验表明，GAE 能够平滑复现多样、敏捷且富有表现力的人体行为，并提升实时遥操作中的同步能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模动作数据、分阶段策略训练和时延补偿，构建了兼顾动作覆盖范围、机器人可执行性与响应性的通用人形全身遥操作方案。

Abstract: Humanoid avatars extend human physical presence beyond the body, enabling people to participate in social, service, and labor activities through remotely operated robots. This requires teleoperation systems capable of realizing diverse and dynamic whole-body behaviors while maintaining responsive human-robot synchronization. We present General Action Expert(GAE), a unified learning framework for general-purpose, low-latency humanoid whole-body teleoperation. To cover diverse human behaviors, GAE builds a large-scale human motion dataset from heterogeneous sources, including videos, animations, and motion capture, followed by standardization and augmentation. GAE then addresses the noise and embodiment mismatch in human motions with a two-stage training paradigm: a privileged generator policy first tracks human motion references in simulation and rolls out feasible humanoid trajectories; a deployable executor policy then learns to track these generated trajectories under curriculum domain randomization. For responsive human-robot synchronization, GAE introduces a latency-conditioned anticipation mechanism that adaptively compensates for end-to-end delay during real-time teleoperation. Simulation and real-world experiments on Unitree G1 and Westlake O1 robots demonstrate that GAE enables humanoids to smoothly mirror diverse, agile, and expressive human behaviors. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [86] [Proprioceptive Force Estimation for Quadruped Locomotion and Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34222)
*Run Wang,Xu Yang,Alapati Tuerxun,Yilin Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究利用本体感觉历史估计持续外力，并将同一三维力估计用于负载下行走与牵引引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人行走时需抵抗负载造成的外力，而牵引力也可作为期望运动指令。研究旨在验证：能否从本体感觉历史中估计以牛顿为单位的共享外力，并同时支持这两类任务。

Method: 联合训练外力估计器与行走策略，以监督信号预测外力和速度，并学习潜在上下文。估计外力用于调节行走；在牵引引导中，则通过解析映射转换为平面速度与偏航角速度指令。

Result: 持续受力仿真中，各力分量均方根误差的时间均值为1.44至2.83牛顿。相较于域随机化基线，速度跟踪误差和机身姿态误差指标分别降低21.6%和46.5%；单独的持续受力测试中，平均存活率从68.29%升至94.60%。Unitree Go1实验展示了静止状态下垂直及水平力估计、携带8.5千克负载行走，以及通过同一力估计接口进行牵引引导。

Conclusion: 共享外力估计可为负载适应和牵引引导提供统一接口，并在仿真中改善跟踪与稳定表现；实体机器人实验进一步展示了其在多种场景中的可行性，包括负载重量超过训练外力上限的情况。

Abstract: Payload forces must be accommodated during locomotion, while leash forces can specify desired motion. We investigate whether a shared three-dimensional force estimate in newtons, inferred from proprioceptive history under sustained loading, can support both tasks. An estimator and locomotion policy are jointly trained with supervised force and velocity outputs and learned latent context. The estimated force conditions locomotion and additionally generates planar-velocity and yaw-rate commands for leash guidance through an analytical map. In sustained-force simulation sweeps, temporal means of componentwise force root mean square error range from 1.44 to 2.83\,N. Compared with a domain-randomized baseline, the framework reduces velocity-tracking and base-orientation error scores by 21.6\% and 46.5\%, respectively, and increases mean survival from 68.29\% to 94.60\% in separate sustained-force tests. Unitree Go1 experiments demonstrate stationary vertical and horizontal force estimation, locomotion with an 8.5\,kg payload whose weight exceeds the 70\,N training force limit, and leash guidance using the same force-estimation interface.

</details>


### [87] [mmHRI: Towards Privacy-Preserving Human-Robot Interaction with Millimeter-Wave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34220)
*Junqiao Fan,Yuxuan Hu,Bofan Lyu,Yanshuo Lu,Pengfei Liu,Jiarui Zhang,Fangqiang Ding,Lihua Xie,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: mmHRI 利用毫米波雷达识别人类动作与姿态，并据此控制机器人完成隐私保护下的物品递送与取回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机交互系统常依赖持续拍摄人的 RGB 摄像头，在不宜直接观察人的场景中引发隐私顾虑；毫米波雷达无需生成可识别影像，但其数据稀疏且时序不稳定，影响动作理解与机器人操作。

Method: 提出 mmHRI 多模态机器人操作框架：双流架构联合处理未滤波雷达张量与雷达点云，估计人类动作和三维姿态；记忆型状态空间模型（MSSM）利用历史雷达特征缓解估计结果的突变。随后将估计的人类状态转为结构化文本指令，输入视觉—语言—动作（VLA）策略，实现闭环操作及对人的响应。

Result: 在隐私保护帘幕场景下，动作识别准确率达到 85.09%，优于既有雷达方案。机器人测试表明，系统可在视觉遮挡下完成递送与取回，并能适应未见过的用户、杂乱配置和不同环境。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，毫米波雷达结合多模态时序建模和 VLA 控制，可支持无需直接视觉观察的隐私保护型人机交互与机器人操作。

Abstract: Assistive robots increasingly operate in many human-centered environments and perform various human-robot interaction (HRI) tasks, such as object delivery. However, most existing HRI systems rely on RGB cameras that continuously observe humans to respond to non-verbal commands, such as hand gestures. This raises privacy concerns in privacy- critical environments, such as hospital wards or restaurants, where direct camera observation of humans is restricted. To develop privacy-preserving HRI, we leverage millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar, which can sense human motion through privacy barriers without identifiable imagery. We propose mmHRI, the first multi-modal robot manipulation framework that achieves mmWave radar-guided privacy-preserving HRI. mmHRI introduces two key designs to mitigate the sparsity and temporal inconsistency of radar data in cluttered robot manipulation environments. First, we propose a dual-stream architecture that jointly learns from unfiltered raw radar tensors and radar point clouds to estimate both human actions and 3D poses. To mitigate signal inconsistency, mmHRI further incorporates a memory-based state-space model (MSSM) that retains historical radar features to reduce abrupt changes in pose/action. These estimated human states are then converted into structured textual robot instructions, which control a vision-language-action (VLA) policy for closed-loop robot manipulation and human-aware reactions. Our evaluation covers human action recognition and closed-loop delivery and retrieval. In the privacy-preserving curtain setting, mmHRI achieves 85.09% action-recognition accuracy, outperforming existing radar-based alternatives. Robot trials further demonstrate successful delivery and retrieval under visual occlusion, with stable task performance across unseen subjects, clutter configurations, and environments.

</details>


### [88] [RLE-Bench: A Qualifying Exam for Coding Agents as Robot Learning Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34210)
*Haitong Ma,Chenxiao Gao,Rushi Qiang,Na Li,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 RLE-Bench，用于评估编码智能体在机器人开发中的多维工程能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准多聚焦单个策略或控制器的表现，难以衡量智能体构建、集成、诊断和改进多种产物的能力，也较少覆盖资源约束与多模态反馈。

Method: 基准涵盖交互控制、策略学习、感知与估计、机械设计四类工作流。研究采用任务专属指标评估智能体提交的产物，并汇总为 RLE Index，同时报告各工作流的能力画像；另通过案例研究分析智能体行为。

Result: RLE-Bench 提供了跨能力维度比较编码智能体的评估框架，并展示了代表性任务中的智能体能力与局限。摘要未提供具体实验数值或排名。

Conclusion: 该基准将机器人评估从单一产物性能扩展到完整开发工作流，可支持系统比较编码智能体，并为未来面向机器人任务的智能体训练提供参考。

Abstract: Coding agents are beginning to move beyond purely digital tasks to tackle physical-world challenges, particularly in robotics. Existing robotics benchmarks, however, primarily focus on the performance of individual artifacts, such as policies or controllers, offering limited coverage of coding agents' broader engineering capabilities. Real-world robotics extends beyond control: agents must build, integrate, diagnose, and improve heterogeneous artifacts under resource constraints and reason from multimodal feedback. To evaluate these broader capabilities, we introduce RLE-Bench, a benchmark of robot-learning tasks spanning four representative robotics development workflows: interactive control, policy learning, perception and estimation, and mechanical design. We use diverse task-specific metrics to evaluate the artifacts submitted by the coding agents, from the success rate the agents achieved to the policy agents trained, the harness agent built, and the mechanical structures the agent designed. We aggregate these metrics into an overall RLE Index and report workflow-specific capability profiles, enabling systematic comparison of coding agents' capabilities across multiple capability dimensions. Beyond performance ranks, we also conduct in-depth case studies examining agent behavior on representative tasks, highlighting both current capabilities and limitations, and pointing to the opportunities robotics tasks have to offer for future agent training.

</details>


### [89] [WB-WAM: Heterogeneous Body-Hand Pre-training for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34199)
*Chuan Qin,Shaoting Zhu,Siyuan Luo,Siqiao Huang,Hongyu Zhao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 WB-WAM，通过全身动作监督增强生成式视频预训练，提升人形机器人的移动操作能力与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人预训练数据对全身协调运动覆盖不足，且真实机器人示范数据有限，难以支持人形机器人同时完成身体移动与灵巧手操作。

Method: 构建统一的身体、根部及灵巧手动作空间，利用 1880.2 小时的部分标注视频与动作数据进行视频—动作联合学习；再通过 PICO 中期训练和辅助正向运动学监督适配机器人任务。配套的 WB-Datasets 纳入重定向的第一人称人类示范及机器人轨迹，以人类动作补充机器人数据。

Result: 仿真评估中，WB-WAM 在 HumanoidArena 达到 81.9% 的任务表现；真实机器人实验在五项任务上的平均成功率为 84.0%。任务对齐的 PICO 中期训练提升了下游表现，并减少了对真实机器人示范的需求。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，融合异构全身动作数据进行预训练，并将人类动作迁移至机器人，是提升人形机器人移动操作能力及数据效率的可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation demands coordinated body and hand behavior, while conventional robot pre-training data provide limited coverage of such whole-body motion. We present WB-WAM, a World Action Model that incorporates explicit whole-body action supervision into generative video pre-training. A shared physical action space integrates body, root, and dexterous hand annotations from heterogeneous sources, enabling joint video and action learning from 1880.2 hours of partially annotated video and motion data. The resulting priors are refined through PICO mid-training and adapted to robot tasks with auxiliary forward kinematics supervision. We construct WB-Datasets to support these stages with retargeted egocentric human demonstrations and robot trajectories, allowing task-aligned human motion to supplement limited robot data. Evaluations in simulation demonstrate strong whole-body task performance with 81.9% in HumanoidArena, while real-world experiments further validate WB-WAM with 84.0% mean success across five tasks. Moreover, task-aligned PICO mid-training improves downstream task performance while reducing the need for real-robot demonstrations. These results support heterogeneous whole-body pre-training and human motion transfer as a practical route to data-efficient humanoid loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [90] [Unified Visual-Tactile-Action Modeling from Human Demonstrations for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34182)
*Wenqiao Li,Qianyou Zhao,Jiawen Hao,Xuezhou Zhu,Tengyu Liu,Kaifeng Zhang,Chuan Wen,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究利用大规模人类触觉示范提升机器人灵巧操作，在五项真实机器人任务中平均成功率达到70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉遥操作反馈有限，难以规模化采集示范；人类示范来源更丰富。研究基于“手可不同、交互物理规律相通”的思路，探索将人类触觉经验迁移至机器人。

Method: 构建同步采集图像、触觉信号与手部动作的触觉动作捕捉系统，并建立包含五项接触密集任务的UVTA数据集，每项任务含1,000条人类示范和150条机器人示范。提出统一视觉—触觉—动作模型，将两种具身形式映射至对齐的触觉与动作表示，并联合预测未来动作和触觉轨迹；部署时仅执行机器人动作。

Result: 五项真实机器人任务的平均成功率为70%，高于最强视觉—触觉基线的29%和架构消融版本的42%。增加人类示范数量可持续提升性能；在每项任务1,000条示范时仍未出现性能饱和。

Conclusion: 人类触觉示范能够有效监督接触感知表示学习，并提升机器人灵巧操作。结果表明，规模化人类触觉数据具有迁移价值，更多示范仍可能带来进一步收益。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires tactile this http URL , robot tactile demonstrations are difficult to scale,because dexterous-hand teleoperation provides limited tactile feedback to the operator. In contrast, human demonstrations offer a substantially more scalable source of diverse tactile interactions. Motivated by a simple premise: hands can change, but the underlying physics of interaction does not. We leverage human tactile data to improve dexterous manipulation policies. Specifically, we first build a tactile motion-capture system that synchronously records images, tactile signals, and hand motions. Using this system, we construct the UVTA dataset spanning five contact-rich tasks, with 1,000 human demonstrations covering diverse interaction patterns and 150 robot demonstrations per task. To transfer the underlying physics of human interaction to robot control, we propose a Unified Visual-Tactile-Action Model that maps both embodiments into aligned tactile and action representations and jointly predicts future action and tactile trajectories. The joint objective enables human demonstrations to supervise contact-aware representation learning, while only robot actions are executed during deployment. In real-robot evaluations across five tasks, our method achieves an average success rate of 70%, outperforming the strongest visual-tactile baseline, which achieves 29%, and an architecture ablation, which achieves 42%. Performance improves consistently with additional human demonstrations and exhibits no saturation at 1,000 demonstrations per task, validating the effectiveness of scalable human tactile data for dexterous manipulation. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [91] [FailPatch: Failure Residual Patching for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34175)
*Peng Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Xuchong Zhang,Baoting Li,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuxiang Chen,Tiancai Wang,Hongbin Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FailPatch是一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的失败驱动残差修补方法，在冻结原策略的基础上，仅训练0.52%的参数，即可显著提升长时序任务的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA策略主要利用成功示范进行监督，能够学习正确动作，却难以覆盖容易失败的状态。部署轨迹可以暴露这些状态，但通常不包含对应的纠正动作，因此难以直接用于常规监督学习。

Method: FailPatch将动作监督与执行可靠性监督解耦：成功示范用于确定应执行的动作，部署轨迹用于识别不可靠状态。方法利用动作隐藏表示中可靠状态与失败相关状态的线性可分性，在冻结的VLA策略动作隐藏空间中引入带空门控的残差专家库，并通过“保持—重定向—信任”统一目标，在可靠状态保持原策略，在失败状态选择残差专家，并在有限干预下将失败区域表示重定向至成功相关区域。

Result: 在四个长时序RoboTwin任务中，干净测试条件下成功率提升11.0个百分点；在从干净环境到随机环境的泛化设置下提升9.5个百分点；在三个真实世界任务中，相较基线提升16.7个百分点。可训练参数仅占0.52%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，部署失败轨迹即使缺少纠正动作，仍可通过可靠性识别和隐藏表示修补有效利用。FailPatch以极少的可训练参数增强了冻结VLA策略的鲁棒性、泛化能力和真实环境执行性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically adapted using successful demonstrations, which provide direct action supervision but rarely cover failure-prone states. Deployment failures expose these states, yet lack the corrective actions needed for conventional supervised learning. We propose FailPatch, a failure-driven residual patching framework that decouples action supervision from execution-reliability supervision. Successful demonstrations ground how the policy should act, while deployment trajectories indicate when its behavior becomes unreliable. We further observe that action hidden representations exhibit clear linear separability between reliable and failure-associated states while directly conditioning action generation. Building on these insights, FailPatch introduces a Null-gated Residual Expert Bank into the action hidden space of a frozen VLA policy. A unified Preserve--Redirect--Trust objective retains the original policy in reliable states, selects residual experts in failure-associated states and redirects representations from failure regions toward success-associated regions under bounded intervention. With only 0.52% trainable parameters, FailPatch improves success rates by 11.0 percentage points on four long-horizon RoboTwin tasks under clean evaluation, 9.5 percentage points under clean-to-random generalization, and 16.7 percentage points over the baseline across three real-world tasks. Project and code: this https URL .

</details>


### [92] [RAVEL: Asynchronous Rolling Inference for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34170)
*Yuhan Chen,Ke Yu,Pengfei Liu,Shuxun Wang,Yi Yang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEL 通过异步推理降低视觉-语言-动作模型的控制延迟，同时保持任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式 VLA 依赖计算开销较大的视觉-语言模型编码和多步动作生成，导致推理延迟高，难以应对动态环境中的快速控制需求。

Method: RAVEL 将视觉-语言编码与滚动动作生成解耦，使动作专家持续使用最新可用的上下文；通过滚动缓冲区保留部分去噪的未来动作，使近期动作经过单步去噪即可执行；并引入轻量级快速观测通路，直接依据当前观测为动作专家提供条件信息。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界操作任务中，RAVEL 显著降低了响应延迟，同时保持底层 VLA 的任务能力。

Conclusion: 异步编码、滚动动作生成与快速观测通路相结合，可实现低延迟、高频且响应灵敏的闭环机器人控制。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models are highly effective for generalist robot manipulation, yet their reliance on computationally expensive VLM encoding and multi-step iterative action generation imposes a significant latency bottleneck. The resulting inference latency makes it difficult for robots to respond quickly, especially in dynamic environments. We address this limitation with RAVEL (Rolling Asynchronous VLA Enabling Low-Latency Control), an asynchronous inference framework that addresses the computational bottlenecks of both the VLM backbone and the action expert. To reduce the delay from multi-step action denoising, RAVEL allows near-term actions to be executed after a single denoising step by carrying partially denoised future actions forward in a rolling buffer. To avoid blocking on slow VLM encoding, RAVEL decouples VLM encoding from rolling action generation, allowing the action expert to operate continuously using the latest available VLM context, while a lightweight Fast Observation Pathway (FOP) directly conditions the action expert on current observations. Across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, RAVEL consistently achieves substantially lower response latency while maintaining the task capability of the underlying VLA, enabling high-frequency and responsive closed-loop control.

</details>


### [93] [Reliability-Aware Sparse Route Memory for Round-Trip Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34163)
*Bojun Long,Lingfan Bao,Tianhu Peng,Jingcheng Sun,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究连续往返视觉语言导航，提出基于可靠性评估的稀疏路线记忆系统；在模拟机器人任务中，在线返程成功率由语言-only基线的22.0%提升至55.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航通常只评估单程任务，但实际机器人可能需要从目标点返程。返程表现受方向可观测性不足、偏航恢复困难和终止判断不稳定等问题影响。

Method: 系统将出程轨迹记录为有序几何锚点，并在返程时反向查询；通过结构化提示、动作级仲裁和终止验证利用路线信息，同时评估几何证据的可靠性。实验使用NaVILA和模拟Unitree Go2，在50个成对反向任务中进行评估。

Result: 语言-only返程成功率为22.0%，在线系统达到55.1%；若提供精确路线信息，同一套交互接口可达到86.0%。在线系统的主要剩余差距来自不够可靠、因而不足以支持介入的几何证据。

Conclusion: 有效返程导航既依赖准确的路线信息，也依赖对该信息的一致执行；在线可靠性则构成另一项独立限制。研究据此区分了信息质量、行为一致性与在线可靠性这三类长时程导航瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) is typically evaluated as a one-way task, although deployed robots may need to return after reaching a goal. We study continuous round-trip VLN and diagnose failures in directional observability, deviation recovery, and termination stability. We propose a reliability-aware sparse route memory that records the executed Outbound trajectory as ordered geometric anchors and queries them in reverse through a structured hint, action-level arbitration, and terminal verification. On 50 reverse-paired episodes using NaVILA and a simulated Unitree Go2, language-only Return succeeds in 22.0% of episodes, while our online system reaches 55.1%. With exact route information, the same interfaces achieve 86.0%, showing that effective Return requires both accurate information and consistent action on that information. The remaining online gap arises mainly from geometric evidence that is too unreliable to authorise intervention. These results distinguish information quality, behavioural consistency, and online reliability as separate limits in long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Retrieval Relevance: Scene-Grounded Risk Entailment for Vision-Language Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34145)
*Jiaxin Liu,Ruilin Yu,Liang Peng,Jingkai Wang,Chengxiang Zhao,Zhenxin Zhu,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Hong Wang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将驾驶风险知识图谱与 SWRL 推理置于视觉语言模型决策之前，以筛选适用于当前场景的风险知识，并提升安全加权规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索到的风险规则可能与场景相关，却未必适用于该场景；若直接交给视觉语言模型，实体识别、跨时刻绑定和关系验证等依据不够明确，风险结论的支撑也难以追溯。

Method: 构建驾驶风险知识图谱（DRKG），根据结构化感知结果实例化场景事实，并通过 SWRL 规则在前置条件共同满足时推导事件及有向风险关系。再将识别出的事件、绑定的风险关系和已触发规则的语义描述作为紧凑证据，输入视觉语言模型和扩散规划器。

Result: 在 nuReasoning 上的匹配对比中，相较于基于相关性检索的基线，方法使 nuReasoning 规划得分（NPS）提高 1.30 分、非责任方碰撞得分（NC）提高 2.76 分。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，相比仅依赖语义相关的风险知识，提供经规则推理确认、适用于当前场景的风险证据，有助于改善安全加权驾驶规划。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) gives vision--language driving systems access to external safety knowledge, yet a retrieved risk rule may be relevant without applying to the current scene. A vision--language model (VLM) receiving such knowledge must ground objects, bind entities across time, and verify relations before deciding how to act, leaving the support for risk conclusions implicit. We address this relevance--applicability gap with a Driving-Risk Knowledge Graph (DRKG) and Semantic Web Rule Language (SWRL) reasoning stage before VLM decision-making. Structured perception instantiates scene facts, from which SWRL rules derive events and directed risk relations when their antecedents are jointly satisfied. Recognized events, bound risk relations, and semantic descriptions of activated rules form compact evidence that conditions the VLM and diffusion planner. In matched comparisons on nuReasoning, our method improved the nuReasoning planning score (NPS) by 1.30 points and the non-at-fault collision score (NC) by 2.76 points over the relevance retrieval-based baseline. These gains indicate that scene-applicable risk evidence improves safety-weighted planning relative to semantically retrieved risk knowledge.

</details>


### [95] [AD-E2E-JEPA: A Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture For End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34085)
*Haoran Zhu,Wancong Zhang,Yann LeCun,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 AD-E2E-JEPA，通过可学习投影器压缩世界模型的规划表示，在保留规划能力的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 JEPA 的自动驾驶世界模型存在准确性与效率之间的权衡：一些模型规划表现较好但计算开销大，另一些则较快却不足以支持有效规划。

Method: 作者在目标条件零样本规划设定下评估 LeWM、DINO-WM 和 JEPA-WM，以未来真实观测作为目标，并避免训练驾驶策略的影响。提出 AD-E2E-JEPA，在投影后的图像块嵌入上使用经 SIGReg 正则化的可学习投影器，将规划图块数减少 16 倍、嵌入维度减少 4 倍。

Result: AD-E2E-JEPA 实现约 100 倍推理加速；对 256 条候选轨迹进行 8 帧展开耗时 0.8 秒。在不训练驾驶策略的情况下，针对 20 米外目标，使用 256/8,192 条轨迹词汇表时，平均位移误差分别为 4.0/2.8 米。在 NAVSIMv2 零样本规划中，含乘性安全指标的 EPDMS 为 67.3/72.9，不含该指标的 EPDMS† 为 84.1/86.5。预训练投影器还使下游模仿学习 EPDMS 从 80.2 提升至 85.4。

Conclusion: 结果表明，压缩潜在规划表示可在显著提升推理效率的同时维持规划性能，并可能改善下游模仿学习表现。该评估刻意不训练驾驶策略，因此主要衡量世界模型本身的规划能力。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires \textit{world models} that can understand the physical world, reason and plan, and operate safely. In this paper, we first systematically evaluate existing action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) world models, including LeWM, DINO-WM, and JEPA-WM for end-to-end autonomous driving (E2EAD). To isolate world-model quality from policy learning, we employ a goal-conditioned zero-shot planning setting that evaluates these models using ground-truth future observations as goals, without training any driving policy. We find that existing JEPA-based world models are either accurate for driving but computationally expensive, or computationally efficient but insufficient for planning. To address this trade-off, we propose \textbf{AD-E2E-JEPA}, which introduces a SIGReg-regularized learnable projector applied to projected patch embeddings. The projector reduces the number of planning patches by $16\times$ and the embedding dimension by $4\times$, achieving a $100\times$ inference speedup while retaining planning performance, with a 0.8-second runtime for an 8-frame rollout over 256 candidate trajectories. \textit{Without} training any driving policy, the world model itself reaches the goals located 20 meters away on average within the displacement of respectively 4.0/2.8 meters, using world-model rollouts over trajectory vocabularies of respectively 256/8,192 candidates. On the NAVSIMv2 benchmark, it achieves 67.3/72.9 EPDMS with multiplicative safety metrics and 84.1/86.5 EPDMS$^{\dagger}$ without them in goal-conditioned zero-shot planning. Experiments further show that the self-supervised pretrained projector improves downstream imitation learning performance from 80.2 to 85.4 EPDMS. The source code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [96] [Quantile Head for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34061)
*Xuan Wang,Yinan Wu,Haoran Duan,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Quantile Head，在单次前向中预测动作的中位数及有序边际分位数，以兼顾高效控制与灵活采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 动作头各有不足：点回归仅给出动作分布的点估计，标准流匹配采样则需要多次迭代，计算开销较高。

Method: 作者将回归与流匹配统一到共享目标，并推导分位数目标，据此设计 Quantile Head。该模块预测中位数和正间隔，形成有序动作分位数；这些分位数可支持多种无需重新训练的采样策略，并联合监督默认中位数策略。局部分析表明，在邻近分位数校准、间隔固定且修正速度匹配等条件下，联合监督下的直接中位数更新方差低于仅监督中位数的情形。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro 和两项真实机器人任务中，联合监督的中位数策略取得了所比较方法中的最高平均成功率；在匹配的 LIBERO 基线上，其平均单回合耗时也最短。

Conclusion: Quantile Head 将高效的中位数动作预测与可复用的分位数采样能力结合，为 VLA 控制提供了一种兼顾任务表现、推理效率和采样灵活性的动作头设计。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models integrate pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) with action heads for robot control. Common action heads have distinct limitations: point regression provides only a point estimate of the action distribution, while standard flow-matching samplers require costly iterative sampling. To address these limitations, we unify regression and flow matching under a shared objective and extend it to derive a quantile objective. This quantile objective guides the design of our Quantile Head, which predicts a median and positive gaps to form ordered marginal action quantiles in one forward pass. These quantiles support multiple sampling strategies without retraining and are jointly supervised to train the default median policy. Our local analysis of this joint supervision shows that, with calibrated nearby quantiles, fixed gaps, and matched correction speed, direct median updates have lower variance than under median-only supervision. Experiments show that this jointly supervised median policy achieves the highest average success rates among the compared methods on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, and two real-robot tasks, together with the shortest mean episode time among matched LIBERO baselines; code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [97] [Estimate, Don't Imitate: Reusing Differentiable State-Based Policies for Visuomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34018)
*Denis Shcherba,Adrian Abel,Eckart Cobo-Briesewitz,Wojciech Samek,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种视觉状态估计方法，将视觉观测转换为可供冻结专家策略使用的状态；在五项目标条件操控任务中优于像素到动作模仿，并在真实 Panda 机械臂上实现 76% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教师—学生蒸馏要求学生同时学习状态推断与专家的动作映射。直接复用基于状态的专家、仅学习感知接口更为直接，但单纯降低状态估计误差未必能降低控制误差。

Method: 训练视觉状态估计器，同时采用直接状态监督和经冻结、可微专家策略反向传播的动作一致性损失。通过分阶段调度目标，先获得物理意义明确的状态估计，再逐步强调对专家动作影响更大的误差。

Result: 在五项目标条件操控任务中，保留并复用专家策略的方案始终优于使用相同专家演示数据进行的像素到动作模仿。该方法迁移至真实 Panda 机械臂后，无需重新训练底层专家，成功率达到 76%。

Conclusion: 以控制效果为导向训练视觉状态估计器，可有效连接部分视觉观测与已有状态策略，避免重新学习专家动作映射，并支持从仿真迁移至真实机器人。

Abstract: Simulation-trained manipulation policies can exploit privileged state information to learn effective contact-rich behaviours, but deployment requires acting from partial observations such as noisy camera images. A common solution is teacher-student distillation, in which a visuomotor policy is trained to reproduce the actions of the privileged expert. This requires the student to jointly infer the task-relevant state and relearn the expert's action mapping that is already available. An alternative is to reuse the state-based expert and learn only a perceptual interface that reconstructs its missing state inputs. However, minimising the state estimate error alone does not necessarily minimise the downstream control error induced by these estimates. To bridge this gap, we train a visual state estimator using both direct state supervision and an action-consistency loss backpropagated through the frozen, differentiable expert. A scheduled objective first establishes a physically meaningful state estimate and progressively emphasises errors that affect the expert's actions. Across five goal-conditioned manipulation tasks, retaining the expert consistently outperforms direct pixel-to-action imitation from the same expert demonstration corpus. We further demonstrate sim-to-real transfer on a physical Panda robot, achieving 76% success without retraining the underlying expert.

</details>


### [98] [ZeroBot: Learning from Scratch in Minutes with Generative Real2Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34010)
*Ivan Kapelyukh,Xiaohan Zhang,Stephen James,Laura Herlant,Edward Johns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroBot利用单视角图像生成物体三维模型，并结合仿真强化学习，使机器人在无需人工示范、预训练策略或已知物体模型的条件下快速学习操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人学习常依赖人工示范、已有策略或精确物体模型，获取这些资源耗时且限制了任务适用范围。研究旨在实现条件更少、训练更快的自主操作学习。

Method: ZeroBot通过图像到三维生成模型，从物体单视图重建完整网格，并将其用于仿真中的大规模并行强化学习。其设计一种利用生成几何信息和学习到的价值函数的动作空间，以采样机器人与物体接触状态，加速策略训练。

Result: 在抓取、推动、可动结构交互和多阶段操作等真实任务中，ZeroBot取得87%的成功率，平均训练时间为119秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将图像到三维模型生成与真实到仿真的学习框架结合，有助于实现快速、无需示范的机器人操作学习；该方法的效果依赖生成几何及仿真训练对真实任务的支持。

Abstract: We present ZeroBot, a real2sim framework for learning a robot manipulation task from scratch in minutes under challenging conditions: zero human demonstrations, zero policy pre-training, and zero known object models. Given only a single view of an object and a goal pose for that object, ZeroBot uses image-to-3D generative models to obtain a complete object mesh, which is used in simulation for large-scale parallel reinforcement learning. To accelerate training, we introduce an action space which leverages the generated geometry and learned value function to sample states involving robot-object contact. When evaluated on real-world tasks including grasping, pushing, articulated object interaction, and multi-stage manipulation, ZeroBot achieves an 87% success rate with an average training time of 119 seconds. These results show the value of using image-to-3D models in a real2sim framework for rapid, autonomous robot learning.

</details>


### [99] [TacGooseBumps (TacGB): Retrofitting Normal-Only Tactile Sensors with Shear Encoding for Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34006)
*Wenjie Li,Binyu Yang,Yuxin Chen,Ambrose Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacGooseBumps（TacGB）通过被动穹顶薄膜将切向载荷转化为压力图案变化，使机器人策略无需新增电子元件或定量剪切力测量，也能利用剪切相关触觉信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的任务中，不同物理状态可能具有相似的视觉外观，但需要不同动作。视觉和仅测法向压力的触觉传感器可能无法区分由切向接触决定的关键状态，因此研究策略是否必须获取校准后的剪切力，还是只需稳定、可区分的观测信号。

Method: 提出TacGB被动穹顶薄膜。切向载荷使穹顶倾斜，并重新分配其足迹范围内的压力，从而在现有传感器的压力图中编码切向交互。端到端策略直接使用这些压力图，不进行力重建，也不需要穹顶与单个触觉像素精确对齐。方法在四项模仿学习任务和两种数据采集流程中进行评估。

Result: TacGB提升了目标达成率、效率和接触质量：插入任务的成功率最高提升36个百分点，成功插入耗时更短；易碎物体放置更加轻柔，绘图轨迹更加连续、笔直。信号、阶段、失败模式和轨迹分析表明，收益主要出现在视觉与法向压力难以充分辨识任务相关切向交互的接触情形。

Conclusion: 剪切信息不必以精确的力值形式测量，也能有益于机器人学习。通过机械方式将切向载荷编码进压力图，TacGB可在不更换底层触觉传感器、也不改变策略输入格式的情况下改善操作表现。

Abstract: Contact-rich policies often fail because distinct physical states look alike yet require different actions. Cameras may not reveal whether a connector is aligned or fully seated, while many normal-only tactile sensors can miss the tangential interactions perpendicular to the grasping direction that distinguish these states. We ask whether a learning policy needs calibrated shear measurements, or only a repeatable observation that separates shear-dependent contact states. We introduce TacGooseBumps (TacGB), a passive domed film that mechanically encodes tangential loading as pattern changes in an existing sensor's pressure map. Tangential loading tilts each dome and redistributes pressure across its footprint; an end-to-end policy consumes the resulting maps without added electronics, force reconstruction, or taxel-level dome alignment. Across four imitation-learning tasks and two data-collection pipelines, TacGB improves goal attainment, efficiency, and contact quality: insertion success increases by up to 36 percentage points, and successful insertions are completed faster, while fragile-object placement becomes gentler and drawing becomes more continuous and straight. Signal, stage-wise, failure-mode, and trajectory analyses link these gains to contact regimes in which task-relevant tangential interactions are poorly resolved by vision and normal pressure alone. Together, these results show that shear need not be measured metrically to benefit robot learning; it can instead be mechanically encoded without changing the underlying tactile sensor or the policy's pressure-map input format.

</details>


### [100] [Test-Time Spatial Reasoning for Robot Manipulation Using Generative Real-to-Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33982)
*Ivan Kapelyukh,Yafei Hu,Ran Gong,Brandon May,Tushar Kusnur,Laura Herlant,Karl Schmeckpeper,Edward Johns,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Simify是一种无需训练的测试时空间推理框架，通过从单张RGB-D图像重建可仿真的场景资产，并利用大规模并行物理仿真与进化搜索，快速优化机器人执行复杂物体重排任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人完成多物体交互和长时程任务需要可靠的空间推理。现有基于基础模型的方法在复杂几何关系和动作规划方面存在局限，因此需要一种能够显式利用物理规律并高效搜索空间的推理方法。

Method: Simify首先结合3D生成模型和视觉语言模型，从单张RGB-D场景图像重建具有完整几何信息的仿真资产。随后根据任务奖励函数（如构建最高塔）启动数千个并行仿真轨迹，并通过进化搜索优化物体排列，最终将方案用于真实机器人执行。该流程无需额外训练，主要在测试时完成规划。

Result: 在真实机器人硬件上的定量实验表明，Simify能够端到端完成涉及未见物体的复杂重排任务，通常可在数秒内收敛。其性能优于已有基于基础模型的空间推理方法，并显示出大规模并行仿真能够显著提升推理和规划效果。实验还表明，完整且准确的物体几何建模对成功实现仿真到真实迁移至关重要。

Conclusion: Simify证明了将显式物理仿真、并行轨迹搜索和视觉重建结合起来，可以有效提升机器人空间推理与复杂操作能力。该方法的关键优势在于无需训练、推理速度快且能够适应未见物体；其主要限制和后续重点是提高单视角几何重建的准确性与仿真和现实之间的一致性。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is fundamental to general robot intelligence, as it enables robots to complete long-horizon tasks involving multi-object interaction. We introduce Simify, a training-free, test-time framework that performs explicit spatial reasoning via massively parallel physics simulation. From a single RGB-D image of a scene, Simify reconstructs simulation-ready assets leveraging 3D generative models and vision-language models. Then given a task specified by a reward function (e.g., build the tallest tower), Simify launches thousands of parallel rollouts in simulation and performs an evolutionary search to optimize object arrangements, typically converging within seconds. We conduct quantitative experiments on real-robot hardware to demonstrate the ability of our framework to execute complex object rearrangement tasks end-to-end with previously unseen objects. Results show that our framework outperforms prior work on foundation models for spatial reasoning by effectively exploiting large-scale parallel simulation during inference, and also highlight the importance of complete and accurate geometry for successful sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [101] [FINGR: Learning Dexterous Hand Control for Real-World Rubik's Cube Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33973)
*Yutong Liang,Quanquan Peng,Matthew Kim,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FINGR通过结合指尖相对几何表示与未来交互预测，提升灵巧手连续转动魔方层的能力；真实硬件上的转动成功率达到99.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单手操作魔方要求手指在支撑魔方与推动转层之间持续协调，并反复调整接触。现有策略难以稳定处理这种持续、密集的接触控制。

Method: 采用共享点云编码器，按指尖表示魔方几何关系，且不依赖小块索引。通过多时间尺度的未来预测任务，监督接触力变化、转层进度及手指关节位移；将学得的表示输入流策略，直接生成手指动作。

Result: 真实灵巧手在300次转层尝试中成功率为99.0%，高于基础流策略的79.7%。结合抓取与桌面辅助重新抓取后，系统成功解出全部10个打乱的2×2×2魔方，平均耗时约137秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，融合指尖相对几何与未来交互监督，有助于提升灵巧手在连续转层任务中的控制可靠性；整合抓取和重新抓取后，该策略也能完成完整解魔方流程。

Abstract: Manipulating a Rubik's Cube with a single dexterous hand is a challenging test of sustained, contact-rich control: the hand must execute successive layer turns while keeping the cube secure. Each turn requires some fingers to support the cube while others push a moving layer, release contact, and reset for the next move. To learn this coordination, we introduce FINGR (Future-supervised Interaction Network with Geometric Representations), a policy that combines finger-relative geometry with future interaction prediction. A shared point encoder expresses the cube relative to each fingertip and aggregates its points without depending on cubie indexing. Learned future tokens share the observation encoder and receive supervision for contact-force changes, layer-turn progress, and finger joint displacement at multiple time scales. The resulting representation conditions a flow policy that directly generates finger actions. On a real dexterous hand, our policy achieves 99.0% success over 300 turn attempts, compared with 79.7% for the base flow policy. Integrated with grasping and table-assisted regrasping, the policy solves all ten scrambled $2\times2\times2$ cubes in a mean complete-system time of approximately 137 seconds. The project website is available at this https URL

</details>


### [102] [ReSync: Re-Aligning the Two Clocks of Asynchronous World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33944)
*Xi Lin,Feihong Zhang,Yulong Shi,Yanghong Mei,Zuxing Lu,Xiaofan Zhu,Zihao Liang,Zhirui Gao,Zhaowen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReSync：利用模型采样日程识别动作与未来视频推理不同步造成的承诺—证据缺口，并在适当窗口内先推进世界状态、暂缓动作状态，以提升控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界—动作模型通常以不同步数生成动作和视频：动作较早完成，作为其依据的未来状态仍未充分解析。论文认为，这种时间错位会使候选动作效用更难判断，也会降低额外采样候选动作的收益。

Method: 提出“两时钟”异步推理视角及承诺—证据缺口指标，该指标可由模型自身的采样日程直接计算，无需搜索或 rollout。根据日程确定有效计算窗口：暂缓动作状态，仅推进世界状态，再恢复原有去噪过程。该方法不修改参数、不比较候选动作。

Result: 在冻结的 RoboCasa 成对评测面板上，ReSync 将成功率提高 4.48 个百分点；计算量相同但仅等待、不推进世界状态的对照方法没有带来提升。该规则也无需重新调参即可迁移至第二个基准和第二种骨干模型。

Conclusion: 异步推理中的计算分配并非越多越好：有效区间有上下界，且可在 rollout 前从采样日程确定。ReSync 利用该区间协调动作与世界状态推理，在不训练或改动模型的情况下改善任务表现。

Abstract: Jointly generating future video and actions has become a standard recipe for world-action models, and the strongest systems denoise the two streams on separate schedules: actions are decoded in few steps so control stays fast, while the video stream runs longer to keep the predicted future sharp. The design is deliberate, but it leaves the two streams on different clocks, and an action can become executable while the future that should justify it is still largely unresolved. We formalize this as a two-clock view of asynchronous inference and introduce the commitment-evidence gap, a quantity read directly from a model's own sampling schedule rather than measured by search. The gap is predictive: as it widens, candidate utility becomes harder to identify and extra candidate sampling buys less, while advancing the world stream buys more, and the two cross. Spending more world computation is therefore not simply better. The useful interval is closed at both ends, and both ends can be read off the schedule before any rollout. ReSync places the computation inside it: hold the action state, advance only the world within the supported window, then resume native denoising. No parameters change and no candidates are compared. On a frozen paired RoboCasa panel this improves success by 4.48 points, while an equal-compute control that waits without advancing the world does not move, and the same rule transfers to a second benchmark and a second backbone without retuning.

</details>


### [103] [EpiTransfer: Sparse, Training-Free Long-Range Depth Estimation from Temporal Monocular Aerial Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33939)
*Diksha Aggarwal,Rutvik Dagadkhair,Sanjana Srivastava,Bradley Denby,Kevin Kochersberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的几何深度估计方法，仅利用两幅单目图像和相机位姿，通过极线传递合成具有可调基线的虚拟双目，从而提升跨视角和跨高度场景中的深度估计可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式深度估计方法虽然在分布内精度较高，但对新视角、新高度和未见环境的泛化能力有限；直接基于两视图三角测量又容易受到几何退化影响，因此需要一种不依赖训练数据且更稳健的深度估计方案。

Method: 利用相机运动和位姿估计，将时间对应关系转换为虚拟双目立体匹配问题，并通过极线传递构造可自由选择基线的虚拟立体图像，随后采用立体三角测量恢复深度。该方法不需要模型训练或场景特定微调。

Result: 在户外无人机飞行数据和室内OptiTrack环境中，以LiDAR为真值进行验证，最大测试距离约为90米。室内AbsRel为0.092，δ<1.25为0.940；其精度接近直接三角测量的AbsRel 0.073，但在困难场景中保持有效深度的范围更大。相比未针对该领域训练或微调的ZoeDepth和Depth Anything V2，本文方法显著更优，后两者AbsRel分别为0.225和0.570。

Conclusion: 极线传递结合虚拟双目构成了一种有效、免训练且具有较强场景泛化能力的深度估计方法。它在保持较高精度的同时缓解了直接两视图三角测量的几何退化问题，适用于无人机导航、避障和三维场景重建等任务。

Abstract: Reliable 3D spatial understanding is essential for autonomous navigation, obstacle avoidance, and scene reconstruction. While state-of-the-art learned depth estimation techniques achieve high accuracy in-distribution, they often generalize poorly to novel viewpoints and altitudes. This paper presents a geometrically derived, training-free depth estimation method using epipolar transfer with only two monocular images and camera pose estimates. By leveraging camera motion to synthesize a virtual stereo pair with a freely chosen baseline, our approach transforms temporal correspondence into a stereo triangulation task while mitigating geometric degeneracies inherent to direct two-view triangulation. Validated across outdoor drone flights (to a maximum range of approximately 90\,m) and indoor OptiTrack environments against LiDAR ground truth, the method achieves an indoor AbsRel of 0.092 and $\delta < 1.25$ of 0.940, comparable to direct triangulation (AbsRel 0.073) while retaining valid depth over a larger fraction of challenging scenes, and substantially outperforms off-the-shelf learning-based baselines such as ZoeDepth (AbsRel 0.225) and Depth Anything V2 (AbsRel 0.570), which are not trained or fine-tuned for this domain, with no training data required.

</details>


### [104] [Residual Learning-Based Control of Vehicle Platoons with $\ell_2$ Stability Guarantees via Recurrent Equilibrium Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33892)
*Brian Delgado,Anh-Tu Nguyen,Hamid Taghavifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向异构车辆队列的残差学习控制框架，以提升存在参数不确定性和外部扰动时的跟驰性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统名义控制器受模型不确定性和外部扰动影响，可能产生较大的间距与速度误差；直接叠加学习模块又可能削弱闭环稳定性，因此需要兼顾性能改进与可验证稳定性的设计。

Method: 先通过线性矩阵不等式设计名义控制器，并结合扰动观测器进行补偿；再利用离线存储轨迹和名义模型预测误差训练循环平衡网络（REN），将其作为残差控制模块。对REN施加预设的ℓ₂增益约束，并借助小增益条件分析局部闭环稳定性与扰动串稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，与仅使用名义控制器相比，该框架降低了车辆队列的间距误差和速度误差。

Conclusion: 该方法将受约束的残差学习与传统鲁棒控制相结合，在改善队列跟驰精度的同时提供了稳定性分析依据；其结论主要由文中实验和所述条件支持。

Abstract: This paper proposes a residual learning-based control framework for heterogeneous vehicle platoons subject to parametric uncertainty and external disturbances. A nominal controller designed via Linear Matrix Inequalities (LMIs), along with disturbance-observer compensation, is enhanced by a Recurrent Equilibrium Network (REN) trained offline using stored trajectories and nominal-model prediction errors. The REN is constrained to satisfy a prescribed $\ell_2$-gain bound, enabling sufficient small-gain conditions for local closed-loop stability and disturbance string stability. Experiments demonstrate reduced spacing and velocity errors relative to the nominal controller.

</details>


### [105] [DexTaG: Tactile-as-Guidance in Reinforcement Learning for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33882)
*Han Yang,Yian Wang,Yunlong Song,Zhenjia Xu,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexTaG利用手套采集的触觉信号指导灵巧手动作重定向，使机器人更自然、稳定地复现人类的接触丰富型工具操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手与机器人手的运动学差异使手套记录的动作难以直接执行。现有强化学习或轨迹优化方法难以保留人类接触模式，容易产生不自然操作和不稳定抓取；且通常针对每条参考轨迹单独训练或优化，效率较低。

Method: 提出触觉引导的强化学习框架DexTaG，以手套触觉信号引导策略搜索，使机器人接触模式贴近人类示范，减少对精确接触几何监督的依赖。针对同一物体的多条训练轨迹联合训练一个可泛化的重定向器，并将其蒸馏为以目标物体轨迹为条件、无需触觉输入的学生控制器，用于真实部署。

Result: 在马克笔和锤子操作任务中，DexTaG学到了自然且接触丰富的动作，而采用基于距离的接触启发式方法的基线未能学得这些行为。该方法可泛化至同一物体和任务的未见轨迹，并在OakInk2上优于单轨迹基线。

Conclusion: 通过将示范中的触觉接触信息融入强化学习，并跨轨迹联合训练，DexTaG兼顾了操作质量与训练效率；蒸馏后的无触觉控制器也为真实机器人部署提供了途径。

Abstract: Glove-based motion capture is emerging as a scalable approach to collecting dexterous-hand demonstration data. However, due to the kinematic gap between the human and robot hand, the recorded human motions cannot be executed directly on the robot, especially for contact-rich tool-use tasks involving in-hand reorientation. Prior work bridges this gap in simulation through reinforcement learning (RL) or trajectory optimization, but the human contact pattern is hard to preserve under such formulations, often producing unnatural manipulation and unstable functional grasps. These methods also train a separate policy or solve a separate optimization for each reference trajectory, which is inefficient. To solve these problems, we propose DexTaG, a tactile-guided RL framework for dexterous manipulation. During training, tactile signals captured by the glove guide policy search toward the measured human contact pattern, reducing reliance on precise reference geometry for contact supervision. To improve efficiency, we train a single generalizable retargeter jointly on all training trajectories of the same object. The retargeter is further distilled into a tactile-free student controller conditioned on the target object trajectory for real-world deployment. On marker-pen and hammer manipulation tasks, DexTaG learns natural, contact-rich behaviors that baselines with distance-based contact heuristics fail to learn, generalizes to held-out trajectories of the same object and task, and outperforms single-trajectory baselines on OakInk2.

</details>


### [106] [Robot-GST: geometry-aware spatial-temporal robot policy representation and evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33872)
*Sichao Liu,Zekun Wang,Lixuan Tang,Yiming Li,Xiaohan Wang,Hanzhi Zhang,Daqiang Guo,Peng Zhou,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robot-GST 通过构建高保真的 Gaussian-SAM 仿真环境，在机器人执行动作前评估候选操作，从而提升长时程真实世界操作的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型驱动的操作策略通常缺乏对任务结果的显式预测和对动作是否能达到目标状态的评估，导致长时程执行中的错误不断累积。

Method: 利用 RGB-D 观测、3D Gaussian Splatting 和 SAM3D 重建机器人环境；结合视觉观测、语言指令与视觉语言模型进行时空任务规划；通过几何采样估计最终状态，并采用基于状态的轨迹规划连接高层计划与实际执行；执行前在重建环境中模拟并筛选候选动作序列。

Result: 在涉及刚性、柔性和可变形物体的代表性任务中进行验证，包括方块放置、玩具收纳和鸭子重排。摘要报告，几何感知的时空推理与状态感知执行提升了不同物体类别下的操作可靠性，但未提供具体量化结果。

Conclusion: 将几何感知重建、高质量渲染与仿真评估结合，可为机器人操作行为提供可扩展的执行前验证方案，有望改善真实环境中的部署可靠性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies are advancing rapidly with increasing reliance on vision-language models for end-to-end decision making. However, reliable deployment remains challenging because many policies lack explicit mechanisms for predicting task outcomes and evaluating whether generated actions will achieve desired final states, causing execution errors to accumulate during long-horizon manipulation. We present Robot-GST, a geometry-aware spatio-temporal behaviour representation and evaluation framework that constructs a Gaussian-SAM robotic environment for real-to-sim policy verification and improves the reliability of real-world manipulation deployment. Our approach constructs a high-fidelity robotic environment from RGB-D observations using 3D Gaussian Splatting and SAM3D, enabling ``simulation and evaluation before acting''. It integrates visual observations and language instructions with spatio-temporal reasoning for long-horizon task planning using large vision-language models. To bridge high-level planning and real-world execution, we introduce Gaussian-aware final-state estimation through geometric sampling and state-based trajectory planning. Before execution, candidate action sequences are simulated and evaluated in the Gaussian-SAM environment to filter infeasible behaviours. We validate our approach on representative manipulation tasks involving rigid, soft, and deformable objects, including cube placing, toy packing, and duck rearrangement, demonstrating that geometry-aware spatio-temporal reasoning and state-aware execution improve manipulation reliability across different object categories. Our results suggest that combining geometry-aware reconstruction with high-quality rendering and simulation provides a scalable approach for evaluating robotic manipulation behaviours. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [107] [DeltaSeek: Toward Active Perception in Evolving Construction Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33836)
*Sanjay Acharjee,Md Nazmus Sakib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DeltaSeek 初步框架，分析机器人感知配置对可获取观测的限制，并比较底盘相机与腕载相机在动态建成环境中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境持续变化会削弱静态建图与配准效果；主动感知需要明确机器人能从何处、以何种配置获取有效观测，并区分观测不可实现与获取代价较高。

Method: 在 IFC 派生的房间级基准中，使用搭载 UR5e 机械臂的 Husky A300，比较底盘安装与腕部安装的 RGB-D 相机。将可行观测集合形式化，以几何可见性评分观测效果、以可行驶距离衡量代价；对八种受控变化、四类可观测性条件下的 240 个底盘位姿和五种机械臂姿态进行穷举评估。

Result: 有两种变化无法从任何底盘相机视点观测到，但腕载相机能够识别。对于两种配置均可观测的变化，腕载相机的底盘行驶距离中位数为 6.0 米，底盘相机为 15.2 米。

Conclusion: 传感器安装方式决定观测能力，也影响获取观测所需的移动成本。区分感知限制与移动代价，有助于判断目标观测究竟不可实现，还是仅需付出更多行驶距离。

Abstract: Construction environments evolve continuously, causing large geometric changes that degrade static mapping and registration performance. This necessitates active perception, where robots deliberately select sensing configurations to resolve the environment's current state. We present DeltaSeek, an initial framework toward active perception in evolving built environments. While our broader objective is a system that reasons about where, how, and when to observe, this paper addresses a critical prerequisite: how a robot's sensing embodiment constrains the observations it can acquire. We formalize an embodiment's permissible observation set and evaluate with a Husky A300 equipped with a UR5e on an IFC-derived benchmark under chassis-mounted and wrist-mounted RGB-D configurations, scoring observations by geometric visibility and effort by drivable distance. In a room-scale scene with eight controlled changes spanning four observability conditions, exhaustive evaluation over 240 permissible base poses and five arm postures shows that two changes admit no chassis viewpoint whatsoever, while the wrist camera resolves both. For changes observed by both embodiments, the median base travel is $6.0$~m for the wrist camera and $15.2$~m for the chassis camera. These results distinguish sensing limitations from acquisition costs, clarifying whether an observation is impossible or simply requires more travel.

</details>


### [108] [Achieve What You Imagined: Learning to Align Actions with Visual Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33832)
*Yuheng Qiao,Ziran Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Daqiang Guo,Yichen Luo,Zhibo Pang,Peng Zhou,Sichao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该方法利用世界动作模型与冻结的动作条件世界模型之间的预测一致性反馈，优化机器人动作策略；在四项真实 UR5 操作任务中，平均成功率由 43.4% 提升至 75.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型可能先生成看似合理的任务完成画面，却无法生成可靠实现该结果的动作序列。直接将视觉预测视为可执行计划，因而存在预测与动作后果不一致的问题。

Method: 将世界动作模型生成的视觉结果视为目标条件视觉提案，而非直接执行计划。使用冻结的动作条件世界模型预测动作后果，并根据两类未来预测的一致性及其与终端目标的对齐程度构造反馈，再通过 Flow Policy Optimization 优化世界动作模型的动作头。该方法无需在线机器人交互或额外训练任务专用奖励模型。

Result: 在四项真实 UR5 操作任务中，所提方法的平均成功率为 75.1%，高于基线的 43.4% 和 π₀.₅ 的 61.4%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，在所评估的操作任务中，跨模型预测差异可转化为有效反馈，用于改进机器人策略；结论的适用范围目前限于所报告的任务与实验设置。

Abstract: World-action models can jointly predict future visual observations and robot actions. However, discrepancies may exist between their visual predictions and the consequences implied by generated actions. We observe that WAMs can often generate visually plausible task-completion outcomes before producing action sequences that reliably achieve them. Consequently, we treat the WAM-generated visual prediction as a goal-conditioned visual proposal rather than a directly executable plan. We use a frozen action-conditioned world model to predict action-conditioned consequences and construct feedback based on consistency between the two future predictions and alignment with the terminal goal. Leveraging this feedback, we employ Flow Policy Optimization (FPO) to optimize the action head of the WAM. This framework avoids online robot interaction and additional training of task-specific reward models. Across four real-world UR5 manipulation tasks, our method increases the mean success rate from 43.4% to 75.1%, compared with 61.4% for $\pi_{0.5}$. These results show that cross-model prediction discrepancy can provide useful feedback for improving robot policies under the evaluated manipulation tasks. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [109] [CodeActionBench: Evaluating Agentic Code-as-Policy for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33807)
*Yiheng Lyu,Xueying Jiang,Wenhao Li,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CodeActionBench评估通用多模态模型能否通过可执行代码，将视觉理解与推理转化为实体机器人操作能力。


<details>
