  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型的视觉推理能力能否可靠地转化为真实操作行为尚不明确；研究需要一种受控基准，衡量模型在缺少任务专用训练、示范和外部操作模块时的表现。

Method: 构建包含25项操作任务的基准，采用“代码即策略”方式评估智能体。智能体需自行选择视觉证据、估计任务相关的三维信息、生成操作目标，并根据机器人反馈迭代执行与修正策略。通过统一机器人API、固定任务实例、资源预算及隐藏的实体结果验证器，对9种配置进行共675次尝试。

Result: 各配置成功率介于2.7%至73.3%。表现最强的GPT-6 Astra与Codex CLI组合，在每项任务最多尝试三次的条件下，至少完成了25项任务中的22项。轨迹分析发现，空间对齐、物体保持和完成状态判断是主要困难；部分任务虽完成了动作，最终仍未达成目标。

Conclusion: CodeActionBench为受控评估通用模型如何将能力转化为实体操作行为提供了测试平台。结果显示，领先配置已具备一定操作能力，但可靠性仍有明显提升空间。

Abstract: How well can general-purpose multimodal models turn visual understanding and reasoning into embodied manipulation via executable code? We introduce CodeActionBench, a benchmark of 25 manipulation tasks that evaluates this capability through agentic Code-as-Policy. Without task-specific fine-tuning, demonstrations, external specialist perception or grasp modules, privileged scene state, or predefined task policies, agents should select visual evidence, form task-relevant 3D estimates, construct manipulation targets, and iteratively execute and revise their policies. A shared robot API provides RGB observations, calibrated geometric operations, robot feedback, and bounded motion, leaving task-dependent decisions to the evaluated agent. Fixed task instances, resource budgets, and a hidden physical-outcome verifier support controlled comparisons across models and harness configurations. Extensive evaluations across nine configurations and 675 attempts achieve success rates ranging from 2.7% to 73.3%. The strongest configuration, GPT-6 Astra with Codex CLI, solves 22 of 25 tasks at least once in three attempts, demonstrating the best performance while still leaving substantial room for improvement. Trajectory analyses reveal difficulties in spatial alignment, object retention, and completion judgment, including task failures despite successfully completed motions. CodeActionBench provides a controlled testbed for measuring how general-purpose models translate their capabilities into manipulation behavior and for examining typical failure scenarios in that process.

</details>


### [110] [Principal Steering Subspaces for Online Adaptation of Frozen Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33765)
*Jialeng Ni,Nathan Zhao,Kunpeng Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出主导转向子空间（PSS），利用生成器解码器的响应方向，在冻结生成策略的情况下进行低维在线强化学习；实验显示其通常比全潜变量控制收敛更快、最终表现更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线更新大型扩散或流匹配机器人策略成本高。虽然控制预训练生成器的初始噪声可避免更新模型，但高维噪声对解码动作的影响可能高度不均匀，因此需要更有效的低维控制空间。

Method: 通过有限差分查询解码器响应，构造固定的低维主导控制基。软演员评论家（SAC）控制响应最强的方向，其正交补空间则在每次查询时独立从高斯先验重采样。该方法也被用于冻结的闭源 30 亿参数视觉-语言-动作策略，并结合同步采集、重置时优化和考虑延迟的异步部署。

Result: 在 RoboMimic 的三个任务及扩散、流匹配策略上，响应谱显示效果集中于部分方向。五组任务与生成器配对实验中，PSS 通常比全潜变量控制收敛更快、后期训练更稳定，且总体最终表现更佳。Diffusion-Square 消融表明，相同维度下，主导响应方向优于随机方向和响应最弱方向。探索性螺丝刀放置测试中，冻结 VLA 策略 10 次试验成功 2 次，采用 SAC+PSS 后成功 6 次。

Conclusion: 解码器响应几何可为冻结的生成式机器人策略提供实用的在线适应基础，降低控制维度并改善训练表现；但 VLA 结果来自小规模探索性评估，仍需更充分验证。

Abstract: Generative robot policies provide expressive behavior priors, but updating a large diffusion or flow-matching model through online interaction is costly. Latent-space reinforcement learning avoids updating the pretrained generator by controlling its initial sampling noise, yet high-dimensional noise can have strongly anisotropic effects on decoded actions. We introduce Principal Steering Subspaces (PSS), a forward-query interface that constructs a fixed low-dimensional control basis from finite-difference decoder responses. Soft Actor-Critic controls the leading response directions, while the orthogonal complement is independently resampled from the Gaussian prior at each query. On three RoboMimic tasks with diffusion and flow-matching policies, response spectra reveal substantial concentration. Across five matched task-generator pairs, the training curves indicate that PSS generally converges faster and exhibits more stable late-training behavior than full-latent control, while achieving stronger final performance overall. Controlled Diffusion-Square ablations further show that leading-response directions outperform random and least-responsive subspaces of equal dimension. We further integrate PSS with a frozen, closed-source 3B-parameter vision-language-action (VLA) policy in a humanoid learning system with synchronous transition collection, reset-time optimization, and latency-aware asynchronous deployment. In an exploratory screwdriver-placement evaluation, success is observed in 2/10 trials for the frozen VLA policy and 6/10 after SAC+PSS adaptation. These results support decoder-response geometry as a practical basis for online adaptation of frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [111] [AnyStep-WAM: Budget-Aligned Distillation and Adaptive Inference for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33748)
*Rui Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Donglin Yang,Yibo Li,Canyang Chen,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyStep World Action Model 根据动作难度动态分配去噪预算，在维持任务成功率的同时显著减少平均去噪步数，并提升单步生成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型通常采用固定去噪步数，但操作动作对生成误差的敏感度不同：关键动作需要更高精度，其他动作则可用更少计算快速生成。

Method: 提出预算对齐的教师轨迹蒸馏方法，通过显式冻结教师模型的状态转移和共享低秩适配器，训练可按区间条件生成的流映射，支持单步预测至多步细化。另设计轻量级风险—收益调度器，从一次预览中估计基于教师曲率的难度及不同预算下的师生保真度，并选择满足风险自适应要求的最小预算。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上评估 Motus、FastWAM 和 LingBotVA，平均去噪步数分别减少 60.2%、49.8% 和 85.28%，同时保持基线任务成功率。单步预算下，三者成功率分别提升 7.07%、12.08% 和 8.94%；六项真实世界操作任务的实验也验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架将可调预算生成与场景依赖的计算分配结合起来，能够在维持任务表现的同时降低推理成本，并增强模型在极低去噪预算下的性能。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple predictive visual modeling with action generation, typically relying on iterative denoising with a fixed denoising steps. However, manipulation tasks contain actions chunks with varying sensitivity to generation errors: critical actions require precision, while less sensitive actions allow faster generation with fewer denoising steps. Here we introduce AnyStep World Action Model, a general framework for tunable-budget prediction and scene-dependent computation allocation. Our budget-aligned teacher-trajectory distillation trains interval-conditioned flow maps using explicit frozen-teacher transitions and shared low-rank adapters, supporting action generation from one-step prediction to multi-step refinement. Building on this capability, a lightweight risk-benefit scheduler predicts teacher-curvature-based difficulty and budget-specific student-teacher fidelity from a single one-step preview, selecting the smallest budget predicted to satisfy risk-adaptive fidelity requirements. We evaluate our framework on three widely used WAMs Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA using RoboTwin 2.0. Our method reduces average denoising steps by 60.2%, 49.8%, and 85.28%, respectively, while maintaining baseline task success rates. In particular, our AnyStep training substantially improves model performance under a one-step denoising budget, increasing task success rates by 7.07%, 12.08%, and 8.94% on Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA, respectively. Experiments on six real-world manipulation tasks further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [112] [MomWorld: Momentum-Aware Latent World Model for Long-Horizon Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33737)
*Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Lei Yang,Haozhuang Chi,Yuchen Liu,Jiangtao Su,Lin Liu,Ziyang Liu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MomWorld 是一种动量感知潜在世界模型，旨在提升自动驾驶车辆长时域规划的一致性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以将历史观测中的运动趋势可靠地延续至未来；基于单一潜在状态进行长时域推演，还可能削弱动态信息、保留过时运动模式，并影响近期规划。

Method: MomWorld 从历史与当前观测中提取场景运动趋势，并联合预测未来场景状态与动量状态。其设计包含可学习的动量持久性机制、场景条件动量更新和场景自适应重置门，以适应运动变化并抑制过时动量。此外，MoFlow 使用动量条件流匹配，在少量积分步骤中修正基础轨迹，并通过时域感知残差融合兼顾近期稳定性与远期调整能力。

Result: 在 NAVSIM、nuScenes 和 Bench2Drive 上的实验显示，MomWorld 提升了长时域规划一致性；在 6 秒规划时域内，相较 MomAD，平均碰撞率降低 12.2%。

Conclusion: 将动量信息显式融入潜在世界模型，并结合时域自适应轨迹修正，可改善自动驾驶长时域规划表现，同时减少对近期决策稳定性的干扰。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning enables autonomous vehicles to anticipate scene evolution and potential risks, supporting safe and stable decisions in complex interactions. However, existing methods struggle to propagate motion trends from observed history into the future. Long rollouts based on a single latent state may further attenuate useful dynamics, retain stale motion patterns, and disrupt reliable near-term plans. We introduce MomWorld, a momentum-aware latent world model for long-horizon planning. MomWorld extracts scene motion trends from historical-to-current observations and propagates latent momentum into future horizons, jointly predicting future configuration and momentum states. A learnable momentum persistence mechanism preserves stable trends, scene-conditioned momentum updates adapt future dynamics, and a scene-adaptive reset gate suppresses stale momentum under abrupt changes. We further propose MoFlow, a momentum-conditioned flow-matching module that refines a base trajectory to align with the predicted future scene evolution in only a few integration steps, with a horizon-aware residual fusion that preserves near-term planning stability while permitting stronger long-range corrections. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM, nuScenes and Bench2Drive demonstrate that MomWorld improves long-horizon planning consistency and reduces the average collision rate by 12.2% relative to MomAD over a 6-second planning horizon.

</details>


### [113] [Observability-Informed Optimal Sensor Placement for Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33681)
*Samuel Smocot,James Richard Forbes,Audrey Sedal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将基于可观测性 Gramian 的优化方法用于软体机器人传感器布置，并通过软体连续臂实验验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器布置方法往往难以适用于不同软体机器人形态，或未充分考虑系统动态特性，因此需要一种兼顾动态信息且具有可解释性的系统化方法。

Method: 采用凸优化，最大化基于可观测性 Gramian 的指标，以确定传感器配置；在软体连续臂上使用位置与应变测量进行实验评估，并通过卡尔曼滤波估计状态。

Result: 在所有正弦输入试验中，最优布置下的状态重建误差均低于基线，误差改善达到毫米量级。

Conclusion: 实验表明，线性控制理论工具能够指导软体机器人的传感器优化布置；该方法具有可解释性，并可能推广至其他机器人形态。

Abstract: This paper presents the application and experimental evaluation of a systematic method for optimal sensor placement in soft robots. Existing methods either lack generalizability across different soft robot morphologies or do not account for system dynamics. The applied method uses convex optimization to find the optimal sensor configuration that maximizes an observability Gramian-based metric. The framework is experimentally evaluated using position and strain measurements on a soft continuum arm. Kalman filter state estimates using optimal sensor placements yield lower reconstruction error than a baseline across all sinusoidal input trials, with improvements on the order of millimeters. This case study shows that linear control theory tools can guide optimal sensor placement in soft robots, suggesting an interpretable approach to sensor placement that may extend to other morphologies.

</details>


### [114] [Demonstration-Free Success-Probability Reward Learning for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33653)
*Duo Wu,Haifeng Wang,Rongwei Lu,Jinghe Wang,Tianyi Xiong,Zhimin Wang,Chao Yu,Shuai Ma,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需专家示范的奖励学习方法 eVTA₀，以及随策略变化持续更新奖励的闭环框架 RLER，以稀疏任务结果和策略经验学习稠密反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励限制了强化学习的效果。现有依赖专家示范的奖励模型在策略优化时容易与混合质量的轨迹分布不匹配，因而难以可靠评估次优或失败行为。

Method: 研究从理论上表明，终局任务结果可隐式提供中间时刻的成功概率反馈，并可通过自举递归学习。eVTA₀利用混合质量策略轨迹进行时序差分式自举，无需专家示范或中间标注；RLER则使用策略不断收集的新轨迹持续适配奖励模型。

Result: 在相同强化学习训练预算下，eVTA₀在所有LIBERO任务套件上取得最佳平均策略表现，较初始策略提升5.4%–13.8%的成功率，并提供了比先进奖励模型更有信息量的奖励。真实世界操作实验中，RLER使总体成功率提升20%–26%，分布外条件下提升35%–36%。

Conclusion: 结果支持无需示范地从稀疏结果学习稠密奖励，并随策略演化更新奖励，有助于提升机器人策略的训练效果及分布外表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables generalist robot policies to improve through trial-and-error interaction, yet its effectiveness is fundamentally constrained by sparse task rewards. Existing general-purpose reward models typically alleviate this issue by learning task progress from expert demonstrations, but introduce a distribution mismatch with the mixed-quality rollouts encountered during policy optimization, making their estimates unreliable on suboptimal and failed behaviors from which the policy must learn. In this work, we introduce a demonstration-free reward learning paradigm where dense reward feedback can be learned directly from sparse task outcomes and policy experience. We theoretically show that terminal task outcomes implicitly define dense success-probability feedback at intermediate timesteps, which can be recursively learned through bootstrapping. Based on this insight, we introduce eVTA$_0$, which learns success probabilities from mixed-quality policy rollouts through temporal-difference-style bootstrapping, without expert demonstrations or intermediate annotations. We further introduce RL with Evolving Rewards (RLER), a closed-loop framework that adapts eVTA$_0$ using newly collected rollouts as the policy evolves. Experiments show that eVTA$_0$ provides more informative rewards than state-of-the-art reward models and achieves the best average policy performance across all LIBERO task suites under the same RL training budget, improving success rates by 5.4%-13.8% over the initial policy. In real-world manipulation, RLER further improves overall success rates by 20%-26%, with 35%-36% gains under out-of-distribution conditions. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of demonstration-free reward learning and adapting rewards as the policy evolves. Project webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [115] [InfraVLA: Extending Vision-Language-Action Navigation with Infrastructure Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33647)
*Lukas Vierling,Benjamin Ramtoula,Luke Robinson,Ronald Clark,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出InfraVLA，将室内固定基础设施摄像头（CCTV）的外部视角接入预训练视觉-语言-动作导航模型，使机器人利用自身视野之外的信息进行导航决策，并在模拟与真实机器人任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航策略，包括近期VLA模型，通常只使用机器人自身摄像头，无法利用建筑物中已部署的固定摄像头。对于目标寻找和障碍绕行等任务，关键决策信息可能仅出现在基础设施视角中，因此需要一种能够有效融合外部视觉信息的导航方法。

Method: InfraVLA使用CCTV编码器将固定摄像头画面转换为输入序列中的视觉标记，并对预训练导航VLA进行端到端适配。由于CCTV信息只在少数关键决策点发挥作用，单纯微调难以促使策略主动使用这些信息，作者采用两阶段训练：首先利用上采样的反事实数据进行示范训练，使模型学习外部视角的作用；随后使用恢复数据训练模型处理导航失败或需要重新规划的情况。

Result: 在两个模拟仓库任务中，InfraVLA在分布内测试中均达到100%成功率，而不使用CCTV的基线分别为34.0%和73.6%。在分布外测试中，两个任务的成功率分别为88.2%和88.9%。在真实四足机器人上，仅使用少于10分钟的示范数据微调后，InfraVLA达到83.3%的成功率，显著高于仅使用机载摄像头基线的29.2%。

Conclusion: 固定基础设施摄像头能够为机器人提供互补的全局环境信息，显著提升VLA导航策略在目标定位、路线重规划和障碍绕行任务中的表现。两阶段训练是促使模型在稀疏关键决策点有效利用CCTV信息的关键。结果表明，该方法具有较好的分布外泛化能力和现实部署潜力。

Abstract: Many indoor environments in which robots operate, such as warehouses, offices, and hospitals, already have cameras installed. They observe parts of the building that the robot cannot see from where it stands, yet navigation policies, including recent vision-language-action (VLA) models, do not use them. We propose InfraVLA, an end-to-end method that adapts a pretrained navigation VLA to such static infrastructure views: a closed-circuit television (CCTV) encoder turns each external view into tokens of the input sequence. Because the views matter only at rare decision points, fine-tuning alone did not make the policy use them in our experiments; we therefore train in two stages, on demonstrations with upsampled counterfactual data and then on recovery data. We evaluate on two simulated warehouse tasks, finding an object named in the instruction and rerouting around blocked aisles, where the deciding information is often visible only to the infrastructure cameras. Tested in distribution, InfraVLA reached a success rate of 100% on both, against 34.0% and 73.6% for a baseline without CCTV input. On out-of-distribution test sets it reached 88.2% and 88.9%. On a real quadruped fine-tuned with under 10 minutes of demonstrations, the policy reached 83.3% against 29.2% for the on-board-only baseline.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond One-Step Accuracy: State-Affine Latent Transition for Reliable Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33595)
*Boyuan Zhang,Yingjun Du,Xiantong Zhen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SALT通过状态仿射潜在动力学和递归多步监督，改善视觉规划中的闭环表现，尽管其单步预测误差高于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型常以单步预测误差训练，但规划时会递归使用模型预测；单步精度无法充分反映误差在多步 rollout 中的传播。

Method: 将多步误差分解为各步引入的误差及其后续传播。证明状态仿射动力学是雅可比矩阵与状态无关的可微转移，因而消除非线性传播残差，使误差传播算子仅取决于动作序列。基于此提出 SALT：由动作调制状态变换与加性更新，并以递归多步 rollout 监督训练，将预测潜在状态反馈至下一步转移。

Result: 在四个视觉规划环境中，SALT 的单步预测误差为匹配的 LeWM 基线的 1.48–2.19 倍，但所有环境的闭环成功率均有提升，平均提高 10.0 个百分点。在 OGBench-Cube 中，执行后模型预测代价急剧上升的失败回合比例由 23.3% 降至 2.0%。

Conclusion: 针对规划中的递归误差传播来设计动力学结构和训练目标，可在单步精度较低时仍提升闭环规划效果与稳定性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding world models enable visual planning by learning action-conditioned dynamics in latent space. Yet they are commonly trained for one-step prediction on encoded states, while planning recursively applies the learned transition to its own predictions. One-step accuracy therefore does not capture how prediction errors propagate under recursive rollout. We decompose multi-step rollout error into the errors introduced at individual steps and their propagation through subsequent transitions. We show that state-affine dynamics are precisely the differentiable transitions with state-independent Jacobians, eliminating the nonlinear propagation residual and making the error propagation operators depend only on the action sequence. Guided by this result, we introduce SALT (State-Affine Latent Transition), an action-conditioned state-affine dynamics model in which the action modulates both the state transformation and the additive update. We train SALT through recursive multi-step rollout supervision, feeding each predicted latent state back into the transition so that training matches how the model is used during planning. Across four visual planning environments, SALT exhibits $1.48$--$2.19\times$ higher one-step prediction error than the matched LeWM baseline, yet improves closed-loop success in every environment by $10.0$ percentage points on average. On OGBench-Cube, the fraction of episodes that fail with a sharp rise in model-predicted cost after execution decreases from $23.3%$ to $2.0%$.

</details>


### [117] [SLIP-VLA: Single-Step Latent Imagination for Policy Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33575)
*Tianfu Li,Haoxuan Xu,Wenbo Chen,Haitian Li,Changchuan Yang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Yuan Liu,Lujia Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLIP-VLA 通过单步潜在想象增强视觉—语言—动作模型，使其以较低延迟预测未来并生成动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通常依据当前观测直接预测动作，缺少对未来场景变化的显式建模。密集未来预测虽有助于动作生成，却往往需要耗时的迭代去噪；单步方法则可能逊于多步方法。

Method: SLIP-VLA 使用一次去噪更新生成时间上密集的未来潜在表征，并将中间潜在变量与未来几何及语义特征对齐，以增强感知充分性。它还结合动作条件潜在世界模型与逆动力学模型，将潜在状态转移和机器人动作显式关联，以提升控制充分性。

Result: 该方法在多种仿真基准和真实世界操作任务中取得了最先进的性能；单步潜在想象耗时仅 12 毫秒。

Conclusion: SLIP-VLA 表明，单步未来潜在建模结合感知对齐和动作约束，有望兼顾未来感知、控制表现与推理效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are increasingly effective for robotic manipulation, yet most predict actions directly from current observations without explicitly modeling future scene evolution. Recent methods introduce future prediction to improve action generation, but dense future modeling often requires expensive iterative denoising, while one-step alternatives can underperform their multi-step counterparts. To reconcile efficient future modeling with strong action performance, we present SLIP-VLA, a policy learning framework that equips VLA models with a Single-Step Latent Imagination for future-aware action prediction. SLIP-VLA obtains temporally dense future latent representations with a single denoising update, and we improve the perceptual sufficiency of these representations by aligning intermediate latents with future geometric and semantic features. We further improve their control sufficiency through action-conditioned latent world modeling and inverse dynamics modeling, explicitly coupling latent transitions with robot actions. SLIP-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks, while its single-step latent imagination takes only 12 ms.

</details>


### [118] [FoLD: Force-Informed Learning for Dexterous Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33551)
*Haowei Shen,Tingai Li,Yumeng Liu,Wenyuan Guang,Xuanze Yang,Qing Fang,Kai Xu,Ligang Liu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoLD 利用显式力引导，将人类操作演示迁移到灵巧机器人，以提升关节物体操作效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人手与机器人手在形态和接触动力学上存在差异，直接重定向人类动作往往无法产生预期的物体运动。

Method: 根据人类演示和机器人当前交互状态计算补偿力场，形成促进目标物体运动的力先验，并将其用于引导残差策略，使重定向后的手部动作适应任务的接触需求。

Result: 在关节物体操作公开基准上，FoLD 在不同任务和机器人形态下持续优于当前先进基线；真实机器人实验也验证了人类操作技能向机器人执行的迁移效果。

Conclusion: 显式力引导可弥补人机形态与接触差异，为灵巧机器人迁移人类操作技能提供有效方案。

Abstract: Transferring human demonstrations to dexterous robots remains challenging because differences in hand morphology and contact dynamics often cause retargeted motions to fail at producing the intended object behavior. We present \textbf{FoLD}, a framework for learning dexterous manipulation of articulated objects through explicit force guidance. FoLD compute compensatory force fields from human demonstrations together with the robot's current interaction state, yielding a force prior that promotes the demonstrated object motion. This force prior informs a residual policy that adapts retargeted hand motions to the contact requirements of the task. We evaluate FoLD on a public benchmark for articulated object manipulation, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across tasks and embodiments. We further validate FoLD on real dexterous robot platforms, demonstrating successful transfer of human manipulation skills to robot execution. Here is the link of our project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [119] [Steer2Grasp: Inference-Time Embodiment-Aware Steering for Diverse Physically Feasible Grasp Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33546)
*Vignesh Vembar,Ayush Kaura,A Padmaprabhan,Siddharth Sinha,Kailash Nagarajan,Keshab Patra,Md Faizal Karim,K Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Steer2Grasp 是一种无需训练、与机器人本体无关的推理时抓取引导框架，通过粒子重加权与重采样提升可行抓取生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取扩散模型虽能提供丰富先验，但以物体为中心的生成方式可能违反机器人运动学与环境碰撞约束。已有本体感知方法多在生成结果附近进行局部修正，难以摆脱根本不可行的抓取模式。

Method: 在冻结的笛卡尔抓取扩散模型上，利用部署任务相关奖励进行 Feynman–Kac 启发的粒子重加权和重采样，将样本群体质量从不可行模式转移至高奖励模式，无需修改预训练模型或使用可微约束。该方法以可达性和避碰奖励统一处理单臂与双臂抓取，并通过无梯度的夹爪级局部优化进行细化。

Result: 摘要称，该方法在多种物体、机器人本体和受限环境中，显著提升了可行抓取的生成效果，同时保持生成结果接近原有抓取先验。摘要未提供具体数值或基准对比。

Conclusion: 粒子级重加权与重采样使推理时抓取引导能够实现群体层面的模式转换，为跨机器人本体、适应部署约束的抓取生成提供了一种无需训练的方案。

Abstract: Current grasp diffusion models provide rich priors for generation, yet their object-centric approach can violate the kinematic and collision constraints imposed by the embodiment and the environment. Existing embodiment-aware methods primarily perform local corrections around generated grasps through gradient guidance or optimization, making it difficult to recover from fundamentally infeasible modes. We present Steer2Grasp, a training-free, embodiment-agnostic framework for inference-time grasp steering that adapts a frozen Cartesian grasp diffusion model using deployment-specific rewards. Through Feynman-Kac (FK) inspired particle reweighting and resampling, the method reallocates population mass from infeasible to high-reward grasp modes, enabling population-level mode transitions without modifying the pretrained diffusion model or requiring differentiable constraints. The framework enables a unified treatment for single and dual arm grasping through reachability and collision aware rewards, followed by gradient free gripper level local refinement. Across diverse objects, robot embodiments, and constrained environments, our method substantially improves feasible grasp generation while maintaining proximity to the underlying grasp prior.

</details>


### [120] [A Disk-Shaped Magnetoelastic Torque Sensor for Robotic Joints Using Permanent Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33542)
*Bruno Brajon,Enrico Gasparin,Nicole Yazigy,Lisa Salamin,Florian Copt,Guzmán Borque Gallego,Elias Klauser,Gaël Close*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种薄型圆盘式磁弹性扭矩传感器，面向机器人关节集成，满量程为50 Nm，测量准确度为1.34% FS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人精密控制与交互需要直接测量扭矩；仅依据电机电流估算扭矩往往不够准确，因此需要紧凑且抗干扰的关节扭矩传感器。

Method: 设计轴向尺寸约1 cm的圆盘式磁弹性传感器，采用四磁力计测量受应力影响的窄磁化区域产生的磁场变化，并抑制寄生交叉力的影响；定制磁屏蔽结构以提升扭矩灵敏度并减弱外部杂散磁场。论文还介绍了机械设计、磁化流程及实验验证。

Result: 传感器实现了相对于50 Nm满量程1.34% FS的测量准确度；磁屏蔽使外部杂散磁场降低约7倍。

Conclusion: 该传感器兼具紧凑轴向尺寸、扭矩测量能力与一定的抗磁干扰性能，实验结果表明其具有集成于机器人关节的应用潜力。

Abstract: Direct torque sensing is a growing need in the robotics community to enable precise control and interactions where torque estimation from motor current is not sufficient. This paper presents a novel disk-shaped magnetoelastic torque sensor with a compact axial envelope of about 1 cm, suitable for integration in robotic joints. A four-magnetometer architecture is used to measure the field modulated by the stress affecting a narrow magnetized region while rejecting the effects of parasitic cross forces. A custom-designed magnetic shield enhances the torque sensitivity while reducing the external stray fields by a factor 7x. The device measures the torque with an accuracy of 1.34 %FS relative to the 50 Nm full scale (FS). The paper details the development of the sensor through the mechanical design, the magnetization procedure, and the experimental validation. The results demonstrate the potential of the proposed sensor for robotic applications.

</details>


### [121] [AI-Driven Collaborative Assembly Line Inspection: System Integration and Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33522)
*Asya Ünal,Amr Okasha,Ege Çırakman,Perin Ünal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文介绍了在厨房电器工厂部署的 AI 辅助协作检测单元，并报告其提升检测效率、减少人工目视检查负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配线人工目视检查容易因长时间作业产生疲劳，降低缺陷检出率，因此需要兼顾质量与人机协作效率的自动化方案。

Method: 在 AI-PRISM 项目中集成两台协作机器人：UR 10e 执行机器视觉缺陷检测，Comau Racer-5 执行功能测试；系统基于 ROS 2 Humble 和 Ubuntu 22.04，采集相机图像、麦克风声学信息及电气安全测量数据，并通过 Grafana 实时可视化。论文按四级人机交互框架梳理部署，并处理近距离安全、反光环境下的 AI 稳健性、ROS 2 命名空间冲突及系统依赖等问题。

Result: 单件质量检查时间由 82 秒降至 61 秒，约缩短 25%；最终检验资源效率由 0.75 提升至 0.88；操作员目视检查时间减少 82%；NASA-TLX 心理需求显著降低（p = 0.005）。

Conclusion: 该现场部署表明，多机器人协作与多模态检测可提升质量检查效率并减轻操作员负担；实际落地仍需重点解决安全、视觉鲁棒性及软件集成问题。

Abstract: Manual visual inspection on assembly lines is a persistent manufacturing bottleneck: operator fatigue over extended shifts lowers defect-detection rates. This paper presents the design, integration, and field deployment of an AI-assisted collaborative inspection cell at the Silverline kitchen-appliance factory, developed within the AI-PRISM project. The cell couples a Universal Robots UR 10e cobot carrying a machine-vision defect-detection pipeline with a Comau Racer-5 cobot for functional tests, coordinated through ROS 2 Humble on an Ubuntu 22.04 LTS server. Multi-modal data (Basler camera imagery, TIA microphone acoustics, and SPS electrical-safety measurements) are logged locally and visualised in real time with Grafana. We report the practical deployment challenges (close-proximity safety, AI robustness under glare and reflections, ROS 2 namespace collisions across two cobots, and operating-system and dependency issues) together with the engineering solutions adopted, and structure the integration through a four-level Human-Robot Interaction analysis. The deployed cell cuts per-unit quality-check time from 82 s to 61 s (about 25%), raises final-control resource efficiency from 0.75 to 0.88, reduces operator visual-inspection viewing time by 82%, and significantly lowers operator mental demand (p = 0.005, NASA-TLX).

</details>


### [122] [AMBIT: Anticipatory Multimodal Body Recruitment for Bimanual Tracking on a Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33484)
*Hanlong Li,Sihan Tan,Takeshi Ashizawa,Benjamin Yen,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AMBIT通过生成并筛选多样化的骨盆与腰部运动策略，提升了双臂轨迹跟踪成功率，并能适应训练时未见约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5自由度机械臂无法独立跟踪一般的双臂末端轨迹，需要协调躯干运动；但可行的骨盆和腰部动作并不唯一，直接回归容易将多种策略平均成无效动作。

Method: 使用条件变分自编码器根据目标轨迹提出候选策略，再由非学习式模块筛选、排序和验证，并通过带迟滞的滚动时域循环确定执行策略。该策略作为参考输入现有的全身微分逆运动学二次规划跟踪器，跟踪器仍负责修正残余误差。

Result: 在Unitree R1的160个有效留出任务中，完整MuJoCo动力学与力矩控制下，AMBIT成功率为85%，高于基线跟踪器的74%；提前调用躯干运动的比例为48%，高于35%。五种零样本约束变化下，AMBIT均优于热启动跟踪器，并达到计算成本高17倍的测试时重优化基线。迁移至Unitree G1后，成功率为0.85，对比基线0.53。实体R1上五种策略的末端轨迹中位误差为11毫米。

Conclusion: 结果支持以生成式模型保留策略多样性、再通过显式筛选处理约束的方案，可改善全身协调与零样本适应性。实体实验仅证明运动学可实现性；由于机器人受到外部支撑，不能据此认定其具备动态平衡能力。

Abstract: A humanoid with 5-DoF arms cannot track generic bimanual end-effector trajectories with its arms alone; pelvis and waist motion must be recruited, but which motion, and when, is not uniquely determined. On a Unitree R1 in fixed double support, the set of dynamically valid recruitment strategies (pelvis pose and waist trajectories) for a task is a diverse continuous manifold, and a deterministic regressor trained on it mode-averages into strategies valid only 35% of the time, against 52% for a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and 82% for the best of 16 CVAE samples. We introduce AMBIT: the CVAE proposes strategies from a preview of the commanded trajectory, a non-learned selector filters, ranks and verifies them, and a receding-horizon loop commits to one with hysteresis. The committed strategy is the reference of the same whole-body differential-IK QP a reactive tracker runs, which keeps authority over residual error. On 160 held-out episodes that admit a valid strategy, in full MuJoCo dynamics under a torque controller, AMBIT reaches 85% success at a 3 cm/15 deg tolerance against 74% for the tracker (disjoint confidence intervals) and recruits the body before the arms saturate in 48% of episodes against 35%. Because diversity is preserved, constraints unknown at training time are enforced by selection alone: under five zero-shot shifts AMBIT beats the warm-started tracker on every shift and matches a test-time re-optimisation baseline 17x more expensive. On a Unitree G1, with hyperparameters unchanged, the protocol reproduces the structure of the valid set and widens the gap over the tracker to 0.85 against 0.53. Five selected strategies execute on the externally supported physical R1, distinct in pelvis excursion and tracking the planned end-effector motion to a median of 11 mm by encoder forward kinematics, which establishes kinematic realisability, not balance.

</details>


### [123] [VIDEAS: Distilling Explicit Action Semantics from Demonstration Videos for World Models via Prior-Guided Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33464)
*Jianan Wang,Haoquan Zhai,Siyang Zhang,Bin Li,Juan Chen,Jingtao Qi,Zhuo Zhang,Enze Wang,Haoxiang Jin,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 VIDEAS，将操作视频中的连续物理过程转化为明确的动作语义，并据此训练语言型世界模型 VIDEAS-WM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽能预测环境状态，但要真正掌握物理因果规律，并显式推理动作前提与状态变化，仍具挑战。

Method: VIDEAS 将视觉演示拆解为离散动作轨迹，借助视觉语言模型提取动作前提和效果等结构化知识；再通过基于文本环境的先验引导轨迹模拟验证物理一致性，并加入负向轨迹以提升知识完整性和数据多样性。研究还基于 AgiBot-World 数据集构建了由 3.4 万条高质量样本训练的 8B/9B 参数 VIDEAS-WM 模型系列。

Result: 实验表明，VIDEAS-WM 在高层具身动作语义推理方面达到当前领先水平，并能在未见场景中展现物理理解能力与稳健泛化表现。

Conclusion: 将视频中的物理动态蒸馏为经过验证的动作语义，可为语言型世界模型提供有效训练数据，提升其对动作条件、后果及物理规律的推理能力。

Abstract: World models learn internal representations of environment dynamics to predict future states, enabling agents to optimize action plans without physical interactions. However, developing world models that genuinely internalize underlying causal physical laws to explicitly reason about action preconditions and subsequent state transitions remains an open challenge. In this paper, we propose VIDEAS, a data distillation framework that transforms continuous physical dynamics from operational videos into explicit action semantics for foundation models. Specifically, it deconstructs visual demonstrations into discrete action trajectories and utilizes advanced vision-language models (VLMs) to extract structured knowledge encapsulating action preconditions and effects. To ensure physical consistency, we introduce a prior-guided trajectory simulation mechanism grounded within a text-based environment to rigorously validate the extracted knowledge. Notably, we incorporate negative trajectories to enrich knowledge completeness and enhance data diversity to mitigate cognitive bias. Furthermore, we present VIDEAS-WM, an 8B/9B-parameter suite of language-based world models trained on 34K high-quality samples derived from AgiBot-World dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VIDEAS-WM establishes state-of-the-art performance in high-level embodied action semantic reasoning, exhibiting profound physical understanding and robust generalization across unseen scenarios.

</details>


### [124] [Large Language Models for Model-Based Robot Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33423)
*Andrew Wilhelm,Angelina Zhao,Nils Napp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出以大语言模型构建显式工程模型，再结合信息补充与多目标优化进行机器人设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接采用大语言模型生成的设计可能隐含未经检验的假设，且无法保证可行性或最优性；不同建模选择也可能显著改变设计结论。

Method: 让大语言模型建立包含物理关系、兼容性约束和优化目标的显式模型，并允许在正式优化前检查、修订及补充工程、制造商或系统专属信息；随后对四旋翼和循线机器人部件选择问题进行多目标优化，并比较直接生成结果、专家模型及模型逐步细化阶段。

Result: 30次直接大语言模型设计试验中，没有任何设计能在最终模型下验证为可行。与独立专家模型及不同模型细化阶段的比较表明，建模假设的变化会显著影响预测的可行设计集与帕累托最优设计集。

Conclusion: 显式工程建模使大语言模型采用的设计假设可供检查和修订，再由形式化优化提供相对于最终模型及指定设计空间的可行性与帕累托最优性保证，从而形成整合大语言模型知识、系统信息与设计优化的接口。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can contribute useful engineering knowledge to robot design, but directly generated designs may rely on implicit assumptions and provide no guarantees of feasibility or optimality. These assumptions are critical because different reasonable modeling choices can materially change which designs are predicted to be feasible or optimal. We therefore present a framework that uses LLMs to construct explicit engineering models containing physical relationships, compatibility constraints, and objectives, allowing these modeling choices to be inspected and revised before formal optimization. The model can then be updated with additional engineering, manufacturer, or system-specific information before formal multi-objective optimization provides feasibility and Pareto-optimality guarantees with respect to the finalized model and specified design space. We evaluate the framework on quadcopter and line-following robot component-selection problems. Across 30 direct LLM design trials, none could be verified as feasible under the corresponding finalized model. Comparisons with an independently developed expert model and successive stages of model refinement further showed that changes in modeling assumptions substantially altered the predicted feasible and Pareto-optimal design sets. Together, these results show that using LLMs to construct explicit engineering models makes the underlying design choices available for inspection and revision before those assumptions determine the optimized designs. Explicit modeling therefore provides an interface for combining LLM-generated engineering knowledge, system-specific information, and formal design optimization.

</details>


### [125] [Recursive Harness Distillation across Agents for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33378)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 递归式 Harness 蒸馏将机器人操作中的干预经验整理为可复用指南，并通过执行反馈持续改进，在不更新模型参数的情况下提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型虽具备广泛的操作能力，但在执行失败时可能难以诊断问题并调整行为；需要一种能积累并复用有效干预经验的方法。

Method: 强智能体将交互经验蒸馏为供轻量智能体使用的操作手册；随后依据轻量智能体的执行反馈递归修订手册，使不同智能体和新任务实例能够复用累积的干预知识，无需更新模型参数。

Result: 真实世界操作任务的成功率从37.3%提高至64.0%。在 SimplerEnv Bridge 上，轻量智能体使用手册后的成功率为66.7%，高于仅使用 GR00T 的基线41.7%，也优于未使用手册的强智能体；强智能体使用同一手册后成功率达到79.2%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，Harness 蒸馏可将机器人干预经验持续积累、通过执行反馈优化，并跨智能体复用，从而提升操作表现。

Abstract: A central goal in robotics is to enable manipulation across changing tasks and environments. Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad manipulation capabilities but can struggle when execution requires diagnosing failures and adapting behavior. Strong agents can discover effective interventions through interaction with these policies. We propose Recursive Harness Distillation to accumulate this experience as reusable guidance across agents. A strong agent distills its experience into a playbook for a light agent, then recursively refines the playbook using the light agent's execution feedback. The resulting playbook enables agents to reuse accumulated intervention knowledge in new task instances without updating model parameters. In real-world manipulation, the harness improves success from 37.3% to 64.0%. On SimplerEnv Bridge, the light agent with the playbook achieves 66.7% success, compared with 41.7% for the GR00T-only baseline, and outperforms the strong agent without a playbook. The same playbook also benefits the strong agent, which reaches 79.2% success. These results demonstrate the feasibility of harness distillation for robotics: intervention experience can be accumulated, refined through execution, and reused across agents to improve manipulation.

</details>


### [126] [Traceable Human-to-Humanoid Sign Language Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33354)
*Ao Liu,Shengeng Tang,Lechao Cheng,Yanbin Hao,Bingkun Bao,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HumanoidCSL-20K：包含20,648条句级中国手语序列及其四种对齐表示的数据集与基准，用于追踪视频动作到人形机器人执行轨迹的转换与误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语数据采集成本高，遥操作难以扩展，因此需要复用大规模视频语料。但将视频中的人体动作转换为机器人轨迹时，既要保留手语的语言动作线索，也要处理人体动作修复、动作重定向、机器人几何约束和控制等环节；仅凭最终轨迹难以判断误差来源。

Method: 数据集为每条序列提供源动作、修复后的人体动作、直接机器人参考轨迹和几何修复后的机器人参考轨迹，并通过有观测依据的局部人体动作修复、全机器人几何修复及跨表示溯源记录转换过程。基准采用成对评估，分别衡量人体动作连续性与内容保留、机器人参考轨迹可行性及实体执行表现；另设计手语专用的运动学参考评估协议，在完整计划动作中评分手形、位置、手掌朝向和双手关系。

Result: 全数据集结果显示，修复后异常手臂／手部动作步数减少，双手之间及手与身体之间的穿透也减少。控制实验区分了参考轨迹的可学习性与课程训练效应，分项评分则揭示了仍未解决的执行误差。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可追踪、多阶段的手语机器人动作数据与评测框架，有助于定位从视频到实体执行过程中的误差，并系统评估轨迹修复、可行性和机器人执行质量。

Abstract: Sign data collection is costly, and teleoperation scales poorly, motivating reuse of large video corpora. Humanoid signing requires converting video-derived human motion into robot trajectories while preserving linguistic motion cues. Errors from fitting, human-motion repair, retargeting, robot geometry repair, and control are hard to separate from the final trajectory alone. We introduce HumanoidCSL-20K, a dataset and benchmark of 20,648 sentence-level Chinese Sign Language sequences, each with four aligned versions: the source, the repaired human motion, the direct robot reference, and the geometry-repaired robot reference. Observation-supported local human-motion repair, full-robot geometry repair, and cross-representation provenance make each transformation traceable. Paired evaluations measure human-motion continuity and content preservation, robot-reference feasibility, and physical execution. A sign-specific kinematic-reference protocol scores handshape, location, palm orientation, and inter-hand relation over the full planned motion. Full-corpus results show fewer abnormal arm / hand steps and less inter-hand and hand-body penetration after repair. Control experiments separate reference learnability from curriculum effects, while component scores expose remaining execution errors.

</details>


### [127] [SocialHumanoid: Towards Expressive Humanoid Behavior via One-Step Co-Speech Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33311)
*Chengqun Yang,Tengjie Zhu,Liang Xu,Fulong Liu,Guanzhu Ren,Yitong Xing,Xuefeng Lu,Fei Shi,Siyuan Fan,Weijie Dong,Yao Mu,Xiaokang Yang,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SocialHumanoid，一种面向实体人形机器人的一步式共语全身动作生成系统，并配套构建AffectMoCap情感动作数据集，实现具有情感表达、语音同步和低延迟特性的连续机器人行为生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共语动作生成方法主要面向数字人，通常缺乏对情感控制、实体机器人运动约束以及低延迟连续生成的联合支持，难以直接用于真实人形机器人的面对面交互。

Method: SocialHumanoid以响应语音和指定情感条件为输入，通过单次前向推理生成全身动作窗口，并利用运动历史条件连接相邻窗口以保持连续性。生成的人体动作在线转换为符合机器人形态与约束的参考轨迹，再由全身控制器实现实体执行。作者还提出AffectMoCap数据集，包含约4小时、由两名专业演员采集的同步语音、身体动作、精细手部动作和情感标注，用于提供情感身体表达监督。

Result: 在BEAT2数据集上，SocialHumanoid取得对比生成方法中最佳的FGD，并保持具有竞争力的语音—动作同步性能；在相同实验协议下，其推理速度约为GestureLSM的6倍。感知评估显示，使用AffectMoCap训练能够提升生成动作的情感识别效果；真实机器人实验验证了情感条件行为的连续生成和长时稳定执行。

Conclusion: 该工作将情感控制、低延迟动作生成、机器人形态适配与全身控制整合到统一流程中，为实体人形机器人的自然社交交互提供了有效方案。实验结果表明，专门的情感动作数据和一步式生成机制能够同时改善情感表现、推理效率及实际部署能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly expected to serve as embodied social agents that communicate naturally with humans through face-to-face interaction. During such communication, humanoid robots require body behaviors that are synchronized with speech, affectively expressive, and suitable for real-time execution. However, existing co-speech methods are primarily developed for digital humans and lack joint support for affective control and low-latency continuous generation on physical embodiments. To bridge this gap, we present SocialHumanoid, a system for expressive humanoid behavior via one-step co-speech motion generation. Given response speech and a specified affective condition, SocialHumanoid generates each full-body motion window in a single forward pass and connects successive windows through motion-history conditioning. The generated human motion is further converted online into embodiment-compatible robot references and tracked by a whole-body controller for physical execution. To provide explicit supervision for affective body expression, we further introduce AffectMoCap, a 4-hour dataset captured from two professional actors, containing synchronized speech, body motion, fine-grained hand motion, and emotion annotations. On BEAT2, SocialHumanoid achieves the best FGD among the compared generation methods, competitive speech-motion synchrony, and approximately $6\times$ faster inference than GestureLSM under the same protocol. Perceptual evaluations further show that training with AffectMoCap improves affect recognition from generated body motion, while real-robot experiments demonstrate continuous affect-conditioned behavior and stable long-horizon execution. Our project page is this https URL .

</details>


### [128] [CompliantWBC: Whole-Body Compliance for Heavy Humanoids via Force Latent Estimation and Residual Impedance Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33310)
*Tan-Dzung Do,Cuc T.Trinh,Tuan Dat Phuong,Chien Le,Thanh Ly,Vien Anh Ngo,An Thai Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CompliantWBC 为重型人形机器人提出全身柔顺控制方法，支持全身任意受力位置，并在仿真和实机任务中进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的力感知控制方法多聚焦于末端或上半身，难以处理重型平台上涉及下肢的任意位置扰动。

Method: 方法包含三部分：以多位置全身阻抗参考控制器引导强化学习，训练基础策略；在冻结的基础策略上训练有界残差策略，调整各连杆阻抗平衡点，并通过梯度屏障联合训练力编码器以修正粗略力估计；使用 Phong 加权的力源采样器和轴向解耦的骨盆锚点，构建包含下肢的柔顺训练课程。

Result: 仿真中，该方法相较柔顺与刚性基线取得最佳柔顺保真度，与解析解的偏差为 2.58 厘米。研究还在重型人形机器人上展示了静态与动态受力响应、擦板、负载深蹲及协作搬运负载等任务。

Conclusion: 该方法将全身多位置阻抗控制、力估计修正和课程训练结合，为重型人形机器人在负载及人机共处场景中的全身柔顺控制提供了一种方案。

Abstract: Whole-body compliant control is essential for deploying heavy humanoids under high payload in human-centric environments. Most prior force-aware learning-based pipelines focus on end-effector resistance, per-link upper-body springs, or end-effector stiffness modulation, leaving arbitrary-site perturbations on heavy platforms with lower-body engagement largely unaddressed. We close this gap with CompliantWBC comprising: (1) A base policy trained with RL to maximize compliance-fidelity reward, guided by a multi-site whole-body impedance reference controller, extending classical Cartesian impedance to any controlled link; (2) A bounded residual policy that edits the per-link impedance equilibrium over a frozen base, correcting the coarse but structured wrench estimate supplied by a force encoder co-trained behind a gradient barrier; (3) A Phong-weighted force-origin sampler with an axis-decoupled pelvis anchor induces lower-body-inclusive compliance curriculum training via two interpretable parameters. We evaluate CompliantWBC in simulation against both compliant and stiff baselines, achieving best compliant fidelity of 2.58cm deviation from analytical solutions, and demonstrate it on a real heavy humanoid across static/dynamic force reaction, board wiping, squat under payload, and cooperative payload transport. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [129] [AquaWAM: A Dynamics-aware World Action Model for Underwater Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33299)
*Cunhao Zhu,Yifeng Wang,Dongliang Xu,Yunzhong Hou,Yue Yao,Chi Harold Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaWAM是一种面向水下具身智能的世界动作模型，显式建模动作驱动与被动运动动力学，在USIM基准上取得72.6%的任务成功率，并以更低计算成本实现更快决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人受到惯性、浮力、流体阻力、持续漂移和环境流等被动动力学影响，动作结束后仍会继续运动。现有主要预测动作条件视觉观测的世界动作模型难以准确描述这些现象，且计算开销较高。

Method: AquaWAM不直接预测高维未来图像，而是建模紧凑的导航状态及其动力学。模型融合DVL、IMU、压力传感器和关节编码器，用摄像头提供目标语义与目标位姿信息，并显式刻画推进器死区、指令结束后的惯性滑行以及环境流等动作条件和被动动力学。

Result: 在USIM的20项水下任务中，AquaWAM达到72.6%的成功率，优于现有方法；在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上，其动作决策速度达到U0的2.7倍。缺少DVL速度测量时，仍取得61.6%的成功率，而U0为39.4%，表明模型具有较好的传感器缺失鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，针对水下场景显式建模被动动力学，并采用紧凑状态表示替代视觉预测，能够同时提升任务性能、推理效率和传感器不完整条件下的可靠性。AquaWAM为水下具身智能中的高效世界动作建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are becoming increasingly important and useful for embodied intelligence, as they enable robots to anticipate the consequences of candidate actions before interacting with the physical environment. However, underwater robots are usually subject to passive dynamics, such as inertia, buoyancy, hydrodynamic drag, and persistent drift, which can continue to affect the vehicle even after an action is completed. Existing WAMs, which primarily predict action-conditioned visual observations, are not explicitly designed to capture such passive motion dynamics. In this paper, we present AquaWAM, the first World Action Model designed for underwater embodied agents. Instead of predicting future images, AquaWAM models both action-conditioned and passive physical dynamics, including the thruster dead band, the inertial glide that outlasts each command, and ambient currents. Specifically, it senses through the DVL, IMU, pressure sensor and joint encoders, while cameras supply only semantics for understanding goals and target pose. By modeling compact navigation states rather than high-dimensional visual observations, AquaWAM substantially reduces the model size and computational cost compared with conventional WAMs. Experimentally, AquaWAM achieves a 72.6% task success rate across 20 underwater tasks on the USIM benchmark, outperforming existing methods while making action decisions 2.7x faster than U0 on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Our model also remains effective when some onboard sensor measurements are unavailable. For example, without DVL velocity measurements, our method still achieves a 61.6% success rate, compared with 39.4% for U0.

</details>


### [130] [Q-WAM: 4-Bit Quantization of World Action Models with Action-Subspace Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33269)
*Arash Akbari,Arman Akbari,Jingwu Luo,Yuhao Lei,Yi Gao,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Zhenman Fang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q-WAM 是面向世界动作模型的 4-bit 权重与激活量化方法，在显著降低内存占用的同时，较好保留机器人动作生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型联合生成视频与机器人动作，但计算和内存开销较高，部署困难。现有后训练量化方法难以充分维持动作生成精度。

Method: 提出动作可观测 Gramian（AOG），衡量层内舍入误差经多步去噪对最终动作的影响；提出动作子空间保护（ASP），将少数动作敏感的通道组合保留在小型 16-bit 低秩分支中，其余部分量化为 4-bit。再依据各专家的 AOG 动作敏感度，仅对关键专家应用 ASP，以控制额外开销。

Result: 在三个世界动作模型的仿真与真实部署评测中，Q-WAM 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上取得 89.6%–93.0% 的平均成功率，与 16-bit 模型相差不超过 1.1 个百分点；量化块内存减少 3.1–3.4 倍。相较 SVDQuant，成功率提升 2.5–8.7 个百分点；在 Unitree G1 和双臂 UR3 实机上提升 12.8–17.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: Q-WAM 通过识别并保护对最终动作最敏感的子空间与专家，实现了高效量化与动作精度之间的较好平衡，显示出在机器人部署中的实用潜力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate video and robot actions through iterative diffusion and perform strongly in robotic manipulation. However, their prohibitive compute and memory costs pose substantial deployment challenges. Post-training quantization (PTQ) can reduce these costs, but existing PTQ methods such as smoothing and rotation are insufficient to maintain the precision of action generation. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-WAM, a new 4-bit weight-activation quantization for WAMs that preserves the actions the model generates. Specifically, we introduce the \textit{Action Observability Gramian (AOG)}, which measures how much rounding errors in each weighted combination of a layer's input channels change the final action through all denoising steps. We also develop Action-Subspace Protection (ASP), which keeps the few most action-sensitive channel combinations in a tiny 16-bit low-rank branch and quantizes the complementary weights and activations to 4 bits, both as dense matrix multiplications that run efficiently on GPUs. Finally, to preserve action quality with minimal overhead, we identify the experts that matter most for the generated action by aggregating the AOG-derived action mass across the layers of each expert and apply ASP only to those experts. We evaluate Q-WAM on three WAMs, both in simulation and in real-world deployment. On the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, it reaches 89.6--93.0\% average success rate, within 1.1 percentage points of the 16-bit models, while reducing the memory of the quantized blocks by 3.1--3.4$\times$. Our method outperforms the strongest baseline, SVDQuant, by 2.5--8.7 percentage points. On a Unitree G1 humanoid and a bimanual UR3 robot, it improves success over SVDQuant by 12.8-17.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [131] [PORTER: Edge-Cloud Residency for Persistent 3D Scene Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33258)
*Yue Chang,Yifan Tian,Jiajing Peng,Dazhi Huang,Rufeng Chen,Zhaofan Zhang,Li Chen,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PORTER通过将轻量锚点保留在机器人端、将大型对象载荷在边缘与云端之间迁移，在有限本地内存下维持可复用的3D场景图信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按任务构建或激活稀疏表示虽能减少当前任务的开销，但多次任务切换后，历史载荷会持续占用本地内存；完全丢弃又可能导致未来重复构建。仅依据相关性决定保留内容，也无法识别多个载荷之间的信息冗余。

Method: PORTER将信息持久化与本地驻留分离，并把任务拆解为功能需求。其核心指标“不可替代支持擦除”（ISE）衡量卸载载荷造成的需求覆盖损失：降低可替代支持的影响，并在剩余覆盖较弱时加重损失。系统在内存预算下反复卸载单位字节边际ISE最小的载荷。

Result: 在JITOMA-Bench上，PORTER针对四种3D场景图构建器进行了评估。渐进压缩实验显示，载荷字节卸载比例达到91%时，汇总相对mR@3仍保持100%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，借助需求覆盖与信息可替代性来指导载荷驻留，可显著压缩机器人端内存，同时保留任务性能。摘要未提供评估设置、对比基线或其他指标的具体结果。

Abstract: Recent task-driven and just-in-time 3D Scene Graph (3DSG) methods reduce per-task representations by constructing or activating only task-relevant information. Yet sparse per-task working sets do not bound onboard memory usage over a robot's lifetime: as tasks change, payloads accumulated for earlier tasks may become irrelevant to the current task but can be useful again in future tasks. Over repeated task switches and expanding environments, retaining such reusable payloads causes local memory to grow, whereas discarding them entirely can lead to costly repeated construction of the same payloads later. We introduce PORTER, which decouples persistence from residency: lightweight anchors remain in the limited memory of the edge robot while heavy object payloads migrate between the edge and the cloud. Relevance alone is insufficient for deciding residency because multiple relevant payloads may provide redundant information. We therefore decompose each task into functional requirements and introduce Irreplaceable Support Erasure (ISE), which measures the loss in requirement coverage caused by offloading. ISE discounts replaceable support and penalizes losses more strongly when the remaining coverage of a requirement is weak. PORTER constructs a budget-aware local working set by repeatedly offloading the payload with the smallest marginal ISE per byte. Experiments on JITOMA-Bench evaluate PORTER across four 3DSG builders. Under progressive compression, pooled relative mR@3 remains at 100% through 91% payload-byte offloading.

</details>


### [132] [ActionGround: Training-Free Runtime Refinement of Frozen VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33256)
*Namai Chandra,Madhur Thareja,Shriram Damodaran,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActionGround 是一种无需训练、可直接包裹冻结式视觉—语言—动作策略的运行时层，通过阶段感知规则修正和动力学约束改善机器人操作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通常未显式表示操作任务的阶段结构，也未充分纳入执行过程中的刚体动力学，因此可能出现动作不稳定、效率较低等问题。

Method: ActionGround 使用有限状态机识别接近、抓取、搬运和放置阶段，并应用阶段特定的规则修正；同时在每个控制步加入惯性加权的 Euler–Lagrange 项，将动力学残差作为一致性诊断记录，而非控制门控。该方法无需访问或修改模型权重，单步额外开销低于 1 毫秒。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Spatial 的十项任务及四种 VLA 主干模型上，固定参数的 ActionGround 将成功率最高提升 6 个百分点、稳定性最高提升 19.3 个百分点，轨迹效率最高提升 15%。Robosuite 噪声测试显示，其轨迹急动度鲁棒性约提升 10 倍；配套仿真中，基线成功率由 35% 提升至 95%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，轻量级、免训练的神经符号运行时层可提升 VLA 操作的稳定性、效率和抗噪性。不过，研究范围仅限刚体物体抓取与放置；实体机械臂实验仅为定性部署展示，尚未报告逐试验定量成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, but they do not explicitly represent the phase structure of manipulation tasks or the rigid-body dynamics governing execution. We present ActionGround, a neuro-symbolic, training-free runtime layer that wraps a frozen VLA policy without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, adding less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. A symbolic phase-aware finite-state machine identifies the manipulation phase (approach, grasp, transport, or place) and applies a phase-specific rule-based correction. In parallel, an always-on, inertia-weighted Euler-Lagrange term incorporates the robot's equations of motion into each control step, while its dynamics residual is logged as a consistency diagnostic rather than used as a gate. We evaluate ActionGround across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial pick-and-place tasks using a 7-DoF Franka Panda. With fixed parameters across tasks and backbones, ActionGround improves success rate by up to 6 percentage points and stability by up to 19.3 percentage points, while improving trajectory efficiency by up to 15%. In a separate Robosuite noise sweep, ActionGround provides approximately a 10x improvement in trajectory-jerk robustness under injected action noise. In a matched-seed Robosuite simulation companion to a real Agilex Piper trial, simulated baseline success increases from 35% to 95%. The physical-hardware experiment is presented as a qualitative deployment demonstration; quantitative per-trial success on the real arm is left for future work. Our evaluation is limited to rigid-object pick-and-place manipulation.

</details>


### [133] [SurgFlow: 3D Object-Centric Contact Flow for Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33237)
*Changwei Chen,Xiao Liang,Yinuo Yang,Nicole Shen,Peihan Zhang,Sara Wickenhiser,Zekai Liang,Soofiyan Atar,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgFlow 从无动作标注的立体手术视频中学习物体中心的三维接触流，用于指导机器人操作，并在多种手术任务及跨平台迁移中取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对的视频与动作示范数据稀缺，而仅从视频学习物体运动也无法确定工具应在何处、何时接触物体；接触信息对手术操作尤为关键。

Method: 针对物体上的每个点预测未来三维轨迹和接触分数。通过三维跟踪及工具—物体邻近关系提取训练目标，使用流匹配生成器进行学习；推理时依据接触预测触发抓取与释放，并根据预测流优化末端执行器运动。

Result: 在 dVRK 上，SurgFlow 在组织牵引、双手显露、针拾取和交接等任务的 39 次阶段评估中成功 37 次，优于使用相同数据训练的基线（无论是否含动作标签）。迁移至人形机器人平台的腹腔镜机器人后，在相似和新颖视角下的平均成功率分别为 85% 和 70%。

Conclusion: 将三维物体运动与接触时机、位置联合建模，使无动作标注的手术视频能够支持机器人操作学习；实验结果表明，该方法具备较强任务表现及一定的跨平台、跨视角迁移能力。

Abstract: Paired video-action demonstrations enable autonomous surgical behavior, but such data is scarce: robots perform roughly 1% of surgeries, while video-only data is abundant. Learning 3D object flow offers an embodiment-agnostic way to utilize video data, but flow alone specifies how an object should move, not where and when the tool should engage it, a distinction that is critical in surgery. We introduce SurgFlow, a framework that learns 3D Object-Centric Contact Flow from stereo surgical video without action labels. For each object point, it predicts a future 3D trajectory and contact scores. We extract targets via 3D tracking and tool-object proximity, train a flow matching generator to predict them, and use predicted contact to trigger grasp and release while optimizing end effector motion from flow. On the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK), SurgFlow succeeds in 37 of 39 stage evaluations across tissue retraction, bimanual reveal, needle pickup, and handover, outperforming baselines trained on equal data with or without action labels. Zero-shot transfer to a humanoid-based laparoscopic robot achieves 85% and 70% average success under similar and novel camera viewpoints, respectively.

</details>


### [134] [Learning with Object-centric Representations of Tactile Interactive Perception for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33235)
*Xinyi Yang,Zilin Si,Zhuowei Xu,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以物体为中心、具备上下文感知能力的机器人操作框架，通过触觉探索学习与任务无关的物体表征，并利用其指导物体选择和属性相关的操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉难以直接判断材质、容器内容物和柔软度等隐含物体属性，而触觉信号具有瞬时性和稀疏性，导致有效触觉事件提取及其融入机器人操作仍具挑战。

Method: 框架首先通过Token Learner从长时程触觉探索中自动筛选代表性触觉片段；随后将触觉信息与描述性文本嵌入进行对比对齐，构建能够编码多种物理属性的潜在表征；最后将这些表征作为语义上下文，输入物体中心的操作策略，以支持目标选择和基于物体属性的策略适应。

Result: 在物体属性估计任务中，模型对已见物体和未见物体分别达到93%和84%的准确率。在多物体重排、倾倒和开箱三个涉及视觉歧义物体的任务中，相较于不使用物体上下文的基线，已见物体的总体任务成功率由41%提升至92%，未见物体由19%提升至67%。

Conclusion: 触觉探索结合文本语义对齐能够学习具有泛化能力的多属性物体表征，并显著改善机器人在视觉不充分场景下的目标选择与属性依赖型操作。该方法验证了稀疏触觉信息作为操作语义上下文的有效性，但其性能仍依赖触觉探索质量及跨物体、跨任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Implicit object properties that are difficult to directly infer from vision, such as material, container contents, or softness, can be revealed through tactile sensing and exploratory interactions. However, because tactile signals are transient and sparse, extracting informative tactile events and effectively incorporating them into robotic manipulation remains a challenge. In this work, we present an object-centric context-aware manipulation framework that learns task-agnostic object representations through tactile exploration. A token learner autonomously selects representative tactile segments from long-horizon exploration, while contrastive alignment with descriptive text embeddings enables a latent space that captures multiple physical object properties. These learned representations are then used as semantic context to guide object-centric manipulation policies and adapt strategies based on object properties. Experiments show that the learned representations achieve 93% and 84% property estimation accuracy on seen and unseen objects. Evaluated on three tasks involving visually ambiguous objects, i.e. multi-object rearrangement, pouring, and box opening, the proposed framework improves both target selection and property-dependent manipulation adaptation, raising task success, aggregated over all evaluation trials, from 41% to 92% on seen objects and from 19% to 67% on unseen objects over baseline policies without object-context conditioning. Videos and additional results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [135] [TAO-DA: Towards Autonomous Operation--A Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Model for Coordinated Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33197)
*Yongsheng Zhao,Han Gao,Baoping Cheng,Jingyao Tang,Dian Zhou,Deng Liang,Ji Ge,Xuanzhang Wen,Lei Zhao,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双臂专家（DAE）架构，通过解耦双臂策略并协调任务进度，提升视觉—语言—动作模型执行双臂任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉—语言—动作模型缺少明确机制来区分左右臂的状态与意图，容易产生跨臂干扰，降低任务成功率；协作任务也需要双臂间的进度同步。

Method: 在共享视觉—语言模型骨干上构建对称的双臂专家架构，为两臂配置相互解耦的专家塔。采用两阶段意图路由：先依据显式语言指令选择专家，再依据隐式视觉语义进行路由。另以轻量级进度预测模块，通过交叉注意力融合动作分块前的时序特征与本体感觉、视觉观测的语义表征，估计逐帧任务完成进度。

Result: 实验表明，该模型能够有效进行双臂意图路由并减少跨臂干扰。结果还初步显示，技能可在单臂与双臂任务之间双向泛化，并支持跨臂运动领域的技能迁移。

Conclusion: DAE通过臂间策略解耦、分阶段意图路由及任务进度同步，为协调双臂操作提供了一种有效方案；技能迁移与泛化的证据仍属初步。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework for grounding high-level semantic information into low-level robot actions, enabling scalable robotic manipulation across diverse tasks. However, existing VLA models lack explicit mechanisms to disentangle the states and intents of the two arms, leading to unintended cross-arm interference that degrades task execution success. To address this issue, we propose a symmetric Dual-Arm Expert (DAE) architecture built upon a shared Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone with decoupled, arm-specific expert towers. Expert selection is carried out through a two-stage dual-arm intent routing scheme, in which experts are routed either by explicit language instructions in the first stage or by implicit visual semantics in the second stage. Moreover, we introduce a lightweight task progress prediction module that leverages cross-attention between the pre-chunk temporal features and semantic representations of proprioceptive and visual observations to accurately estimate frame-wise task completion progress. This module facilitates task progress synchronization to support coordinated scheduling for collaborative multi-robot tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our model in dual-arm intent routing and the disentanglement of cross-arm interference, and further provide preliminary evidence of emergent skill generalization from single- to dual-arm tasks (as well as the reverse), together with cross-arm motion-domain skill transfer.

</details>


### [136] [DeltaWAM: Change-Centric Visual Foresight via Delta Tokens for an Efficient World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33177)
*Tianyun Jiang,Wenrui Bao,Bingxin Xu,Yu Tian,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeltaWAM以紧凑的视觉变化向量预测未来，并据此生成机器人动作，在LIBERO上取得92.8%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间世界动作模型需要反复重建完整未来场景，计算开销大且存在时空冗余。操作决策更需要关注连续帧之间的变化，而非重复的视觉背景。

Method: 基于大规模视频预训练的DeltaWorld，DeltaWAM将相邻稠密DINO特征图之间的变化编码为单个delta token，并自回归预测每个未来帧的变化。随后，流匹配动作专家结合预测的变化以及作为空间锚点的当前DINO特征，生成动作片段。

Result: 模型包含7.25亿参数，使用两块H100 GPU训练256小时。在LIBERO上平均成功率为92.8%，并能在LIBERO-Pro的程序化扰动下稳健泛化。每个动作片段的推理耗时为142.1毫秒，峰值内存为3.86 GB。

Conclusion: 以视觉变化而非完整场景作为预测单位，有望减少世界动作模型的冗余计算，同时保留动作规划所需的空间信息；实验结果表明该设计兼具任务表现、扰动泛化能力与较低推理开销。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) offer visual foresight for robotic manipulation, but pixel-space models repeatedly reconstruct entire future scenes, incurring high computational cost and spatio-temporal redundancy. In physical manipulation, consecutive frames often share most of their visual context; the changes between them are what an action policy needs to anticipate. We introduce DeltaWAM, a change-centric WAM that makes a compact delta token the unit of future prediction. Each token is a single vector encoding changes between consecutive dense DINO feature maps. DeltaWAM builds on DeltaWorld, a latent world model pretrained on large-scale videos, to autoregressively predict one delta token per future frame. A flow-matching action expert then conditions on the predicted transitions and current DINO features, which serve as spatial anchors, to generate action chunks. Trained for 256 GPU hours on two H100 GPUs, DeltaWAM has 0.725B parameters and achieves 92.8% average success on LIBERO. It also shows robust generalization under procedural perturbations on LIBERO-Pro. Inference takes 142.1 ms per action chunk with 3.86 GB peak memory.

</details>


### [137] [Multi-Terrain Mastery: A Comprehensive Controller for Bipedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33174)
*Oluwami Dosunmu-Ogunbi,Aayushi Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于多种地形的双足机器人控制器，并在20自由度Cassie机器人上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态控制器通常针对特定地面设计，难以应对真实环境中多变且不可预测的地形。单一控制器若能适应多种地形，可提升双足机器人的实用性与鲁棒性。

Method: 基于此前利用支撑踝关节电机提升斜坡和崎岖地面稳定性的工作，扩展控制器以适应陡峭、湿滑且不平整的草坡，以及沙地、砾石、岩石等柔顺地形和楼梯等受限地形。提出新的冲击映射，并详细说明可在机器人上实时部署的控制结构。

Result: 控制器在20自由度Cassie双足机器人上进行了验证。摘要称新冲击映射对于维持性能、增强对未见地形的鲁棒性至关重要，但未提供定量结果。

Conclusion: 该工作探索了以单一控制器应对多类复杂地形的方案，拓展了Cassie机器人的地形适应能力；其实际性能仍需结合摘要未报告的实验指标进一步评估。

Abstract: Advancing bipedal robots to navigate diverse terrains remains a significant challenge in robotics. Traditional locomotion controllers excel on specific surfaces but struggle across varied environments, limiting their practical applications. Given the unpredictable nature of real-world environments, a single controller capable of handling multiple terrains is ideal, eliminating the need for multiple specialized controllers. We propose a multi-terrain controller to enhance the versatility and robustness of bipedal locomotion. Building on previous work with a stance ankle motor for stability on inclined and rough surfaces, this paper extends capabilities to steep wet uneven grassy slopes, and compliant terrains such as sand, gravel, rocks, and constrained terrains like staircases. To address the unique demands of these terrains, we introduce a new impact map that is essential for maintaining performance and robustness against unseen terrains. We also discuss in detail the control structure for real-time deployment on the robot. We validate our controller on the 20 degree-of-freedom Cassie bipedal robot.

</details>


### [138] [Dynamic Manipulation with World-Action Models via Counterfactual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33172)
*Sunwoo Park,Wonbin Lee,Seonghyun Jin,Youngmin Kim,Jangho Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 动态预测规划（DPP）通过反事实规划，使仅在静态示范上训练的世界-动作模型能够应对移动目标，无需额外动态数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这类模型操纵移动目标时可能出现“目标响应坍缩”：执行越深入，策略越倾向延续既有动作，越难响应目标位置变化。问题在于模型已学会的技能与当前情境下能生成的行为之间存在差距。

Method: DPP利用模型的预测 rollout 估计预计发生交互的时间，并结合观测到的目标运动预测目标届时的位置。随后，它构造一个反事实观测，将预测位置置于模型熟悉的机器人情境中，以调用已有操纵技能；生成的计划再与机器人实际状态衔接执行。

Result: DPP可在单张消费级 GPU 上实时运行，无需使用动态数据进行额外训练。仿真与真实机器人实验显示，该方法在多种目标运动条件下均有稳定提升；仿真表现优于所有受测基线，包括使用动态数据额外训练的方法。

Conclusion: 将计划生成所依据的情境与实际执行状态解耦，有助于静态示范训练的世界-动作模型将既有操纵技能迁移到动态目标任务。

Abstract: World-Action models (WAMs) trained on static demonstrations often fail to manipulate moving targets even when they possess the required manipulation skills. We attribute this failure to target-response collapse: as execution advances, the policy becomes increasingly biased toward the learned continuation of its ongoing behavior and less responsive to target relocation. To bridge the gap between what the model has learned and what it can generate from the current context, we formulate dynamic manipulation as counterfactual planning by decoupling the context used for plan generation from the physical state used for execution. Our framework, Dynamic Predictive Planning (DPP), first uses the WAM's predictive rollout to estimate when an interaction is expected to occur, and combines this timing estimate with observed target motion to predict the target's future interaction position. DPP then constructs a counterfactual observation that places this predicted target position in a familiar robot context, allowing the model to invoke an existing manipulation skill rather than generate a recovery behavior from an unfamiliar robot-target configuration. The resulting plan is connected to the robot's actual state during execution. DPP enables real-time dynamic manipulation on a single consumer GPU without additional training on dynamic data. Experiments in simulation and on a real robot demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse target motions, with simulation performance surpassing all evaluated baselines, including methods additionally trained on dynamic data. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Tasks: A Vision for Reproducing an Animal-like Behavioral Substrate Using Modern Robot Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33165)
*Samiyuru Menik,Hemadri Jayalath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以“动物行为基底”作为研究视角，关注具身智能体如何在不断变化的物理、环境与社会需求下保持持续适应、行为协调与互动连贯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习更关注能力提升，对动物持续展现的基础能力关注不足：在多变情境与相互竞争的需求中保持响应、适应和行为连贯。

Method: 提出概念性研究框架，将动物行为视为持续协调的整体，而非彼此孤立的技能；并建议以机器人动物伴侣作为研究场景，考察长期互动中的社会行为、记忆与持续学习。

Result: 本文提供了一个用于界定和研究持久行为能力的视角，并指出机器人动物伴侣有助于探索以人为中心环境中的长期互动与适应；摘要未报告实证实验或量化结果。

Conclusion: 持久、协调的行为能力是具身智能体值得深入研究的目标，可补充其更高层次的能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have produced increasingly capable embodied agents. Yet comparatively less attention has been given to a more basic form of competence that animals exhibit continuously: the ability to remain situated, responsive, and behaviorally coherent as physical, environmental, and social demands change over time. We propose the ethological behavioral substrate as a conceptual lens for studying this form of competence in artificial agents. Rather than treating these behaviors that animals exhibit as a set of isolated skills, we argue that their continual coordination under competing demands constitutes an important and underexplored target for modern robot learning. We further propose robotic animal companions as a useful research setting for studying sustained interaction and adaptation in human-centered environments. Such systems provide an opportunity to investigate how social behavior, memory, and continual learning develop over long periods of interaction. This perspective motivates further investigation of how such persistent behavioral competence may complement higher-level capabilities in embodied agents.

</details>


### [140] [TimelyDAgger: Timing-Aware Expert Querying for VLA Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33157)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Peiyan Li,Enhao Zhang,Yueran Tao,Hao Wang,Chenghao Yue,Lei Lv,Wentao Zhao,Jiahao Chen,Xin Liu,Kangyao Huang,Yu Luo,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TimelyDAgger，通过更合理地安排专家接管时机，提升机器人策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人门控 DAgger 主要关注何时需要帮助，但接管时机也会影响示范内容及其训练价值；因此，仅检测失败风险不足以优化策略学习。

Method: 结合 Bridge-PCA 对视觉—语言—动作（VLA）模型内部特征进行监测，并依据专家行为采用反馈引导的阈值自适应来调整接管时机。另构建连接失败检测、接管时机与策略改进的评估框架，并提出无需重新训练即可评估监督质量的目标对齐监督比例（TASR）。

Result: 实验表明，接管时机会影响策略学习。在专家动作预算相同的条件下，TimelyDAgger 的失败检测表现具有竞争力，并在多数评估设置中取得更高的训练后成功率。

Conclusion: 专家接管的时机不仅关系到是否及时避免失败，也决定了示范对学习的价值。TimelyDAgger 为协调检测性能与监督质量提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: DAgger improves robot policies by aggregating expert supervision from states visited during policy execution. Robot-gated DAgger automates expert queries, allowing the robot to decide when to request expert takeover. While existing gates emphasize detecting the need for assistance, takeover timing also shapes the content of these demonstrations and their value for policy learning. We propose TimelyDAgger, combining Bridge-PCA monitoring of internal vision-language-action (VLA) features with Feedback-guided Threshold Adaptation based on expert behavior to improve takeover timing. We introduce an evaluation framework linking failure detection, takeover timing, and policy improvement, including Target-Aligned Supervision Ratio (TASR) for assessing supervision quality without retraining. Experiments show that takeover timing affects policy learning, with TimelyDAgger achieving competitive failure detection and higher post-training success in most evaluated settings under matched expert-action budgets.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond State-as-Action: Exploiting Command-State Discrepancy for Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33145)
*Peiyan Li,Yueran Tao,Enhao Zhang,Zhixuan Zhao,Chenghao Yue,Hao Wang,Lei Lv,Wentao Zhao,Jiahao Chen,Xin Liu,Kangyao Huang,Yu Luo,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究受交互约束时，命令与机器人实际状态之间的差异是否能为模仿学习提供有用监督，并提出命令—状态差异加权方法 CSDW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据测得的机器人运动构造动作目标，可能忽略命令所表达、但受交互约束而未能实现的控制需求。研究旨在识别这类信息何时重要，以及如何利用它。

Method: 在三项真实机器人任务中分析不同任务与阶段的监督差距，并通过选择性保留命令检验命令信息的局部作用。随后提出 CSDW：考虑机器人响应时间，将后续进展、持续未满足的需求及需求变化结合起来，生成连续的命令监督权重；无需任务阶段标注，也不改变策略架构或推理流程。

Result: 任务与阶段分析显示，受约束交互下的监督差距更大；选择性保留命令的结果表明，命令信息在局部具有实用价值。CSDW 在受约束任务上优于均匀命令监督，而在约束较少的任务上，各方法表现相近。

Conclusion: 命令—状态差异在交互受限时可揭示运动数据未充分表达的控制需求。利用 CSDW 对命令监督进行加权，可在无需额外标注或改动策略结构的情况下改善受约束任务中的模仿学习效果。

Abstract: Constructing action targets from measured robot motion is an established approach in imitation learning. Under interaction constraints, however, command-state discrepancy may reflect control demands that motion alone does not capture. We investigate when this information matters and how to exploit it. Across three real-robot tasks, task and phase analyses reveal larger supervision gaps under constrained interaction, while selective command retention provides evidence of locally useful command information. Building on these findings, we propose Command-State Discrepancy Weighting (CSDW), which accounts for robot response times and combines subsequent progress, persistent unmet demand, and demand changes into continuous weights for command supervision. The method requires no task-phase annotations or changes to policy architecture or inference. CSDW improves over uniform command supervision on constrained tasks, while methods perform similarly in the less constrained task.

</details>


### [142] [Multi-Modal Non-Prehensile Estimation of Physical Parameters via Press-and-Pull Tipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33138)
*Steven M. Hyland,Jing Xiao,Cagdas D. Onal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合滑动与按压-拉动倾倒的非抓取式方法，可估计未知物体的质量、质心高度和表面摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一非抓取操作难以辨识所有物理参数：平面推滑会使质量与摩擦耦合，传统倾倒则无法估计摩擦，且低摩擦或曲面底物体可能滑动或旋转而无法倾倒。

Method: 先通过滑动交互获取相关信息，再采用按压-拉动倾倒操作，提高物体与桌面的滑动阈值并稳定支点。融合腕部力/力矩传感器、RGB-D感知和机器人本体感知数据，无需预先建立物体几何模型或学习交互动力学。

Result: 在ABB IRB120上对四种几何形状、质量、质心和摩擦特性各异的物体进行实验，估计相对误差较低；该方法还可处理传统向前倾倒无法成功操作的曲面底物体。

Conclusion: 互补的非抓取交互能够在无需抓取、物体先验模型或已学习交互动力学的条件下，恢复一组紧凑的物理参数。

Abstract: Recovering physical properties of unknown objects through non-prehensile interaction is challenging because no single manipulation primitive reveals all relevant parameters. Planar pushing couples mass and friction, while conventional tipping cannot recover friction and may fail entirely when low-friction or curved-base objects slide or rotate instead of tipping. We introduce a multi-modal estimation framework that combines a sliding interaction with a press-and-pull tipping primitive to recover object mass, center of mass height, and surface friction. The press-and-pull interaction increases the object-table sliding threshold and stabilizes the pivot, enabling controlled tipping without any prior geometric object model. Wrist force/torque sensing, RGB-D perception, and robot proprioception are fused to estimate the physical parameters from the two complementary interaction modes. Experiments on an ABB IRB120 across four objects with varied geometry, mass, center of mass, and friction achieve low relative error, while successfully operating on curved-base objects that fail under conventional forward tipping. The results demonstrate that complementary non-prehensile interactions can recover a compact set of physical parameters without grasping, a prior object model, or learned interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [143] [VPTwin: Real-Sim-Real Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33104)
*Zhenghao Xiao,Minting Pan,Nantian He,Dongzhan Zhou,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VPTwin利用与真实演示同步的仿真数字孪生，提升长时程视频预测的物理可信度，并改善机器人操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的视频预测在长时程推演中容易累积误差并生成不符合物理规律的结果，降低下游动作规划的可靠性。

Method: 针对目标操作任务，先由视觉语言模型根据真实演示重建可执行数字孪生；再在随机物理参数配置和候选动作轨迹下，通过Isaac Sim生成多种动态推演。框架将这些仿真结果作为上下文参考，结合仿真动力学与真实视频中的接触交互信息进行预测，并以预测结果验证视觉语言模型提出的动作，形成规划与执行闭环。

Result: 评估显示，该方法显著减少视频预测中的物理幻觉，并明显提升操作规划表现。

Conclusion: 通过融合仿真先验与真实观测，VPTwin增强了预测的物理合理性，为更可靠的机器人操作规划提供了支持。

Abstract: While action-conditioned video prediction provides an intuitive world model for robotics, purely data-driven predictors often suffer from compounding errors and physically implausible hallucinations in long-horizon rollouts, severely undermining downstream action planning. We propose VPTwin, a Real-Sim-Real video prediction framework that anchors real-world future prediction using real-synchronized simulation twins. For a target manipulation task, a VLM reconstructs an executable digital twin from a real demonstration episode. To accommodate the ill-posed estimation of unobserved physical properties, Isaac Sim simulates multiple forward dynamic rollouts across randomized physical configurations under candidate action trajectories. Using these rollouts as in-context references, VPTwin harmonizes both domains, using simulation dynamics to enforce physical plausibility while capturing unmodeled contact interactions from real video. Furthermore, we establish a predictive planning loop using VPTwin to visually verify VLM-proposed actions and guide reliable real-world execution. Evaluations show substantial reductions in physical hallucinations during video prediction and marked improvements in manipulation planning performance.

</details>


### [144] [Evolving Dexterous Robots from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33101)
*Zihan Guo,Shuzhe Zhang,Muhan Li,Peiyang Li,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究通过进化搜索与强化学习，从零设计并验证能够灵巧操作多种物体的自由形态机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作器设计常借鉴动物结构或预设身体部件，但这些方案未必适合人工机器；如何系统地发现更优、更多样的操纵结构仍不清楚。

Method: 利用对比学习构建可搜索的设计空间遗传嵌入，以自回归发育模型生成设计，通过进化策略筛选方案，并用强化学习训练各设计。最终将优选方案自动转化为可制造蓝图，打印、组装并进行零样本真实环境测试。

Result: 进化得到的机器人能够拾取、持握、旋转并使用多种物体。研究报告称，其在性能、多样性和复杂度方面达到进化机器人领域的先进水平，且部分设计可在无需预设身体结构的情况下形成新颖操纵方案。

Conclusion: 将生成式设计、进化搜索、强化学习与实体验证结合，可探索超越传统仿生范式的机器人操纵器，并为自动化机器人设计提供新途径。

Abstract: Little is known about how to manually design agents capable of dexterous manipulation. Some design principles have been inferred from close examination of how animals manipulate objects, but these structures and behaviors have so far resisted biomimicry and may not be optimal for artificial machines. Here we evolve freeform robots to pick up, hold, rotate, and use diverse objects. Unlike other approaches to optimizing robot hands, we do not presuppose the presence, articulation, or geometry of any part of the body. Although familiar prehensile forms such as tails, beaks, paws and claws may emerge spontaneously under certain conditions--and while such conditions could be of interest to evolutionary biologists--de novo manipulator design can also reveal whole new solutions, overlooked or unknown structures which may be better suited for the task at hand. We use contrastive learning to create a highly searchable genetic embedding of design space, an autoregressive developmental model to decode designs, evolutionary strategies to find good designs, and reinforcement learning to train each evolved design. Winning designs were automatically converted into a manufacturable blueprint, printed, assembled and tested in the real world in a zero-shot manner. The results represent the state-of-the-art in evolutionary robotics in terms of performance, diversity and complexity.

</details>


### [145] [Humanoids for Robot-Assisted Surgery: Bimanual Base Placement and Tool-Mount Optimization via Capability Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33096)
*Peihan Zhang,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Nikita Thareja,Ryan Broderick,Shanglei Liu,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出一种联合优化人形机器人基座位置与工具安装方向的方法，以评估其执行腹腔镜手术的可达性；效果因手术类型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人距离满足机器人辅助手术的运动学需求还有多远尚不明确，尤其缺少对机器人摆位和工作空间覆盖的量化分析。

Method: 基于能力地图，联合优化机器人基座位置和工具安装方向，在考虑工具尖端运动学及远程运动中心（RCM）约束的同时，最大化双臂可达性。研究评估了三种尺寸和运动冗余度各异的人形平台，以及胆囊切除术、腹股沟疝修补术和袖状胃切除术三类代表性手术。

Result: 联合优化方案持续优于仅优化基座位置的方案及启发式基线。对于工作空间较小、重叠较少的胆囊切除术和腹股沟疝修补术，可达性接近90%；对于涉及较大且相互重叠的多端口手臂工作空间的袖状胃切除术，覆盖率明显较低。

Conclusion: 人形机器人近期有望应用于部分腹腔镜手术，但其工作空间覆盖能力受手术任务特征制约；扩大应用范围仍需解决相关运动学与工作空间限制。

Abstract: Rapid advances in humanoid robotics have motivated growing interest in the application of humanoids for healthcare and clinical tasks. However, it remains unclear how close contemporary humanoids are to meeting the kinematic demands of robot-assisted laparoscopic surgery. In this work, we address the question of optimal robot positioning through a quantitative analysis of workspace and robot setup configurations. We present a capability-map-based robot setup framework that optimizes humanoid base placement and tool mounting orientation to maximize bimanual humanoid reachability while accounting for tool-tip kinematics and remote-center-of-motion (RCM) constraints. We evaluate three humanoid platforms spanning different body dimensions and kinematic redundancy on workspace reachability for three representative general surgery procedures: cholecystectomy, inguinal hernia repair, and sleeve gastrectomy.
