The proposed joint optimization of base placement and tool mounting consistently outperforms base-only optimization and heuristic baselines. For cholecystectomy and inguinal hernia repair, which are characterized by relatively small and minimally overlapping workspaces, humanoid reachability approached 90%. For the larger, overlapping multi-port arm workspace of sleeve gastrectomy, humanoids yield substantially lower coverage. These results quantify the near-term promise of humanoids for selected laparoscopic procedures and clarify key limitations that must be addressed for broader deployment.

</details>


### [146] [REALM: A Coarse-to-Fine Generative Framework for Embodied Reactive Listening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33095)
*Peizhen Li,Longbing Cao,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REALM 是一种用于音频驱动倾听者面部动作生成的粗到细框架，通过结合说话者音频、倾听者动作历史与随机表情细化，生成更具响应性和自然变化的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时处理说话者线索与倾听者动作之间的时间滞后及动作连续性，也难以预测眨眼、短暂表情等局部随机变化。

Method: Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion 模块利用以延迟为中心的注意力先验和自适应门控，融合倾听者动作历史与说话者音频。粗解码器先预测基础动作轨迹，再通过音频条件化的随机残差细化表情子空间，同时保留粗略姿态参数。

Result: 在 ViCo 和 L2L 数据集上，REALM 在多项动作质量指标上优于所评估的基线。补充分析考察了延迟敏感性、门控行为和眨眼动态；Ameca 人形机器人部署及感知用户研究进一步验证了其实体应用效果。

Conclusion: REALM 将历史感知的时间对齐与随机表情细化结合，提升了音频驱动倾听者动作生成的质量，并展示了在实体机器人中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Generating responsive listener facial motion is an important task for embodied conversational AI. Two modeling challenges are central: accounting for the timing of speaker cues while maintaining continuity with the listener's ongoing motion, and capturing locally variable facial events alongside the overall motion trajectory. Listener responses may follow preceding cues with a temporal lag, while brief expressions and blinks introduce variation that is difficult to predict deterministically. These challenges motivate a framework that combines history-aware temporal alignment with stochastic expression refinement. We propose REALM (Reactive Embodied Audio-driven Listening Model), a coarse-to-fine framework for audio-driven reactive listening. A Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion module combines listener motion history with speaker audio through a delay-centered attention prior and adaptive gating. A coarse decoder predicts a base motion trajectory, which is augmented by audio-conditioned stochastic residuals in the expression subspace while retaining the coarse pose parameters. Evaluations on ViCo and L2L show improvements over the evaluated baselines across multiple motion-quality metrics. Additional analyses examine delay sensitivity, gate behavior, and blink dynamics. Finally, deployment on an Ameca humanoid robot and a perceptual user study demonstrate the applicability of the generated behavior to physical embodiment. Code: this https URL Demo: this https URL

</details>


### [147] [SwingRL: Adaptive Observation Reinforcement Learning with World-Model Prediction for Cable-Suspended Hoisting Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33053)
*Guangming Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Yucheng Xin,Wanli Ma,Jiucai Liu,Yunxiang Ma,Joe Ingham,Haibing Wu,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwingRL结合时延感知世界模型、抗摆控制先验与循环残差强化学习，实现缆索悬吊载荷在观测延迟、丢失及动力学不确定条件下的精确插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬吊载荷具有欠驱动特性，易受扰动影响；视觉观测延迟或丢失会导致控制所依据的状态过时，增加精确插入任务的难度。

Method: 世界模型利用已执行的控制命令，将最新收到的载荷观测推演至当前控制时刻；经典先验提供跟踪与抑摆控制；循环残差策略在先验基础上学习有界修正，以应对参数变化、外部扰动和状态估计误差。

Result: 在涵盖参数变化、风扰、感知退化和强阵风的递增难度测试中，最困难条件下严格与宽泛成功率分别为69.5%和77.3%，均比所有基线至少高60个百分点。消融实验显示，时序对齐的状态估计有助于在观测丢失增加时维持插入成功率；未经真实机器人微调，系统在实体平台上成功率达90%。

Conclusion: 将时延补偿、经典抗摆控制与残差强化学习结合，可提升缆索悬吊精密操作在感知不可靠和动力学不确定环境中的鲁棒性，并展现出实体平台应用潜力。

Abstract: Cable-suspended hoisting is widely used to move heavy or bulky payloads that cannot be handled conveniently by rigid pick-and-place systems, for example in crane-assisted construction. Robotic hoisting using flexible cables is challenging because payload motion is underactuated, external disturbances vary, and delayed or lost visual observations can make the perceived payload state stale at control execution. These effects are particularly critical during precise insertion of a suspended payload's sockets onto rebar pins, which is a very common task in construction environments. We present SwingRL, a residual reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that combines an age-aware world model, a classical anti-swing prior, and a recurrent residual policy to address two coupled problems: stale feedback and uncertain dynamics. The world model propagates the newest received payload observation to the current control step using the executed commands, providing a time-aligned state estimate under delayed and lossy sensing. The prior supplies nominal tracking and swing damping. The residual policy learns bounded corrections to the prior rather than the complete control law, compensating for system-parameter variation, external disturbances, and remaining state-estimation errors. We evaluate SwingRL against classical and learning-based baselines across a cumulative difficulty ladder covering system-parameter variation, wind disturbance, degraded sensing, and strong gusts. Under the most difficult setting, SwingRL achieves 69.5% strict and 77.3% broad success, exceeding all baselines by at least 60 percentage points, respectively. World-model ablations support the role of time-aligned state estimation in maintaining insertion success as observation loss increases. Finally, without real-robot fine-tuning, SwingRL achieves 90% success on the physical rig.

</details>


### [148] [Is Online Interaction Necessary for Recovery? A Minimalist Approach to Robust Planning via Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33049)
*Bumgeun Park,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PARS，一种仅利用现有示范数据、通过扰动本体感知状态并对动作序列后段进行锚定监督来提升行为克隆策略闭环恢复能力的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在闭环执行中容易受到协变量偏移影响：微小的预测或执行误差可能使机器人进入示范数据未覆盖的状态。传统缓解方法通常需要额外纠正示范、环境交互或专家策略，成本较高。

Method: PARS对机器人的本体感知状态施加扰动，并仅将预测动作序列的后半段锚定到原始示范轨迹，允许前半段生成纠正动作，使策略在有限规划范围内恢复到示范行为。与保持整个预测时域原始监督目标的常规输入噪声增强不同，PARS提供了面向轨迹的恢复监督。该方法可通过对行为克隆目标进行小幅修改，适配流模型、Transformer和扩散模型等动作序列策略。

Result: 在涵盖51个RLBench操作任务的实验中，PARS分别应用于流式、Transformer和扩散式策略，均提升了策略在闭环执行期间应对协变量偏移的鲁棒性。该方法不需要额外环境交互、纠正示范或专家查询。

Conclusion: PARS是一种简单、通用且数据高效的恢复训练方法，能够利用已有示范显式学习从扰动状态恢复的轨迹行为，并有效增强多类动作序列策略的闭环执行稳定性。

Abstract: Behavior cloning (BC) is vulnerable to covariate shift during closed-loop execution, where small prediction or execution errors can drive the robot toward states poorly covered by the demonstration data. We focus on action-sequence planning, where a policy predicts a finite-horizon sequence of actions as a reference trajectory for robot execution. Existing approaches to covariate shift often rely on collecting additional corrective demonstrations, requiring further environment interaction and access to an expert or reference policy. We propose Perturbation-Augmented Recovery Supervision (PARS), a simple training approach for improving recovery using only existing demonstrations. PARS perturbs the robot's proprioceptive state and anchors only the later portion of the predicted trajectory to the original demonstration, leaving the earlier portion free to generate corrective motion and recover toward the demonstrated behavior within the planning horizon. Unlike conventional input-noise augmentation, which preserves the original supervision target over the entire prediction horizon, PARS explicitly provides trajectory-level supervision for recovery from perturbed states. PARS requires neither additional environment interaction nor expert queries and can be instantiated across different action-sequence policy classes with only minor modifications to their BC objectives. Experiments on 51 RLBench manipulation tasks with flow-based, transformer-based, and diffusion-based policies demonstrate that PARS improves robustness to covariate shift during closed-loop execution.

</details>


### [149] [CAPEX: Efficiently Distilling Foundation Model Behavior into Deployable Robot Policies through Experience-Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33007)
*Shivam Aarya,Zhang Xi-Jia,Chengyue Huang,Junhyun Kim,Huishu Xue,Hrishit Leen,Roman Yakunin,Animesh Garg,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPEX 利用先前执行经验调整基础模型的观察、推理与重规划频率，以更高效地自动收集机器人示范数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工遥操作示范难以直观扩展且过程异步；基础模型虽能生成有效操作轨迹，但执行时频繁调用模型会带来较高延迟与成本。

Method: 提出经验条件化示范收集框架 CAPEX，根据此前尝试的执行经验自适应控制模型调用频率。研究在 RoboCasa 任务及 Franka、双臂 YAM 机器人平台上评估，并比较使用同等规模的人类遥操作数据与模型生成数据训练的 Diffusion Policy 和 ACT。

Result: CAPEX 将成功示范数量提升至 4.3 倍，并使每条成功示范的成本降低 80%。基于 CAPEX 数据训练的策略表现接近使用匹配人类示范训练的策略；从头训练且训练时间更长时，性能差距大幅缩小。

Conclusion: 基础模型可作为可扩展、可复用的机器人经验来源；通过自适应减少模型调用，CAPEX 提高了自动示范收集的效率，并产出具有较强下游学习价值的数据。

Abstract: Robot learning has largely relied on human-teleoperated demonstrations to acquire effective learnable behaviors. However, human-operated data collection processes can be unintuitive, difficult to scale, and inherently asynchronous. We explore an alternative: distilling physical behavior from general-purpose multimodal foundation models into deployable robot policies by using the foundation model itself as an autonomous demonstrator. While sufficiently capable models can generate successful zero-shot manipulation trajectories, repeatedly invoking them during physical execution is slow and expensive, limiting their utility as scalable data generators. As a solution, we introduce CAPEX, an experience-conditioned demonstration collection framework that uses execution experience from previous attempts to adapt how frequently the foundation model must observe, reason, and replan. We evaluate across RoboCasa tasks and on physical Franka and bimanual YAM-arm platforms, measuring task success, model calls, token usage, collection time, and cost. We further train Diffusion Policy and ACT on matched sets of human-teleoperated and foundation-model-generated demonstrations to evaluate the downstream learning value of autonomously collected data. We find that CAPEX increases the number of successful demonstrations by 4.3x while reducing the cost per successful demonstration by 80%. Policies trained on CAPEX-generated data approach the performance of those trained on matched human demonstrations; with longer training, this gap largely closes for policies trained from scratch. These results suggest that foundation models can serve as scalable sources of reusable robot experience. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [150] [Learning Geometry-Aware Virtual Fixtures From Sparse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32958)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Marcella Piacentino,Raffaella Mancino,Paolino De Risi,Bernhard Weber,Margarida Campos,Thomas Hulin,Sylvain Calinon,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Julian Klodmann,Fanny Ficuciello,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于黎曼线性二次跟踪（LQT）的虚拟夹具生成方法，使操作者仅凭少量途经点即可创建带位置与方向引导的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统示教学习通常需要大量示范数据，在许多遥操作场景中难以获取。研究旨在降低轨迹示教成本，同时允许操作者在夹具影响范围之外执行未建模操作。

Method: 利用LQT运动先验，根据少量途经点生成引导轨迹；将经典LQT扩展至黎曼流形，以处理方向引导并引入几何先验。通过对LQT解进行概率解释，估计轨迹各点的协方差，并据此调节夹具刚度、定义夹具有效区域。

Result: 作者在玩具示例中评估了黎曼LQT方法，并在达芬奇研究平台（dVRK）的高精度切割任务中评估了完整框架。摘要未报告定量结果。

Conclusion: 该方法将少量途经点、几何方向约束与不确定性估计结合起来，实现途经点附近较强、远离途经点时较柔和的引导，并支持操作者离开夹具有效区域开展其他操作。

Abstract: In many teleoperation applications, collecting a large number of demonstrations as required for traditional probabilistic learning from demonstration (LfD) approaches may not be feasible. To still give operators the ability to intuitively create trajectories as Virtual Fixtures (VFs), we propose to leverage a motion prior in the learning process. Particularly, by using Linear Quadratic Tracking (LQT), users are able to define guiding trajectories from the demonstration of just a few via points. To account for orientation guidance, we further reformulate classical LQT on Riemannian manifolds, introducing an additional geometric prior. Through a probabilistic interpretation of the LQT solution, we derive a covariance estimate at each trajectory point which we use to modulate the stiffness of the resulting fixture, resulting in strong guidance around the via points and softer guidance when far away. The covariance information is also used to define a validity region of the fixture, allowing the operator to leave its influence area and conduct unmodeled tasks. We evaluate the proposed Riemannian LQT formulation in a set of toy examples and the full framework on a cutting task requiring high precision in a minimally invasive surgery setting on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK).

</details>


### [151] [Communication-Aware Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Decentralized Multi-Human Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32935)
*Ziqin Yuan,Ruiqi Wang,Baijian Yang,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CommHG通过联合学习任务分配与通信策略，提升通信受限的多人人机团队协同效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人人机团队需要协调稀缺且动态可用的人类支持与分布式机器人执行；通信受限会延迟信息交换和人工干预，增加任务分配难度。

Method: 提出通信感知的异构图学习框架CommHG，以信息可用性和时效性构建局部交互图，并通过合作式多智能体强化学习联合优化任务分配及通信决策，使机器人学习何时、向谁查询或共享信息。另构建包含异构人机、动态任务和受限通信链路的基准。

Result: 在最多16台机器人、6名操作员和112项任务的异构团队实验中，CommHG随协同规模增加仍能提升及时加权任务完成表现，并在大型场景中优于最强基线。

Conclusion: 联合考虑任务分配、信息时效与通信资源，有助于提升大规模通信受限多人人机团队的协同任务表现。

Abstract: Multi-human multi-robot (MH-MR) teams combine robotic autonomy with human expertise, but effective task allocation requires coordinating scarce, dynamically available human support with distributed robot execution. Limited robot-robot and human-robot communication further complicates this coupling by delaying information exchange and supervisory intervention. We introduce CommHG, a communication-aware heterogeneous graph learning framework for decentralized MH-MR task allocation. CommHG represents coupled human-robot-task interactions through local graphs conditioned on information availability and age. Learned communication actions enable robots to decide when and which operator to query, and when to share information with peers. Allocation and communication are jointly optimized through cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, allowing the team to acquire useful information while managing limited communication and supervisory resources. We also introduce a benchmark integrating heterogeneous humans and robots, dynamic tasks and operational states, and constrained robot-robot and bidirectional human-robot links. Experiments across heterogeneous teams with up to 16 robots, 6 humans, and 112 tasks show that CommHG improves timely weighted mission completion as coordination scale increases, outperforming the strongest baseline in the Large scenario.

</details>


### [152] [RoboFoundry: System-as-Policy Evolution for Self-Learning Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32862)
*Jingsong Liang,Shuhao Liao,Shizhe Zhang,Diyuan Hou,Yuxin Cai,Xinjian Deng,Chengyang He,Wenhui Huang,Runjia Tan,Zhidong Wang,Lan Yu,Xuesong Tian,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jie Luo,Yao Mu,Wenjun Wu,Wanhua Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFoundry提出具身智能体的“自进化系统即策略”框架，将执行经验转化为经过验证、可持续复用的系统更新，并在多个基准和真实机器人部署中取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多分别优化记忆、上下文、技能或动作接口，缺少对智能体支持系统的整体优化；仅有交互经验也不足以促成自我改进，除非经验能转化为持久且经过验证的系统变更。

Method: 框架诊断决策与记忆管理中的能力缺口，并将执行轨迹转化为任务级系统更新，再将反复有效的改进推广至通用系统。进化覆盖两类组件：管理当前上下文和持久文件记忆的上下文系统，以及由原子技能、可复用组合和基于失败的恢复机制构成的分层技能系统。共享语义接口将与具身平台无关的决策和平台相关的执行分离，以支持跨机器人迁移。

Result: 在 EmbodiedBench 上达到当前最佳表现，使 GPT-5.5 的成绩提升 27.8%；Qwen3.7-Plus 达到 70.3%，接近 GPT-5.5 的 72.7%。在 RoboMemArena 上领先所有基线至少 39.0%，包括使用外部基础模型辅助的方法；在 LIBERO-PRO 的所有扰动类型上，较 Cap-Agent0 提升 243.8%–679.7%。真实部署还展示了跨机器人、跨任务的零样本迁移与在线进化。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将具身智能体的上下文、记忆与技能作为统一系统持续进化，有助于提升任务表现、长程记忆能力及跨平台适应性，为构建自主具身智能体提供了一种框架。

Abstract: A foundation model should not act in isolation as an embodied agent. Yet, existing methods often optimize individual components of the agent stack, such as memory, context, skills, or action interfaces, rather than treating the supporting system itself as a unified policy. Moreover, interaction alone does not yield self-improvement unless execution experience is converted into persistent, validated system changes. We therefore propose RoboFoundry, the first embodied agentic framework that formulates this process as Self-Evolving System-as-Policy. RoboFoundry diagnoses capability gaps in decision-making and memory management, converts execution traces into validated task-specific system updates, and promotes recurring improvements to the general system. Evolution operates over two complementary surfaces: a context system that manages active internal context and persistent file-system memory, and a hierarchical skill system that organizes atomic skills, reusable compositions, and failure-conditioned recovery. A shared semantic interface separates embodiment-invariant decisions from embodiment-specific execution, allowing evolved system capabilities to transfer across heterogeneous robots. On EmbodiedBench, RoboFoundry achieves state-of-the-art performance, notably improving GPT-5.5 by 27.8%. It also brings Qwen3.7-Plus to near parity with GPT-5.5 (70.3% vs. 72.7%), showing consistent gains from system-as-policy evolution across foundation models. For long-horizon memory, RoboFoundry outperforms all baselines on RoboMemArena by at least 39.0%, even against methods assisted by external foundation models. On LIBERO-PRO, it further outperforms Cap-Agent0 by 243.8%-679.7% across all perturbation types. In real-world deployments, RoboFoundry demonstrates zero-shot transfer and online evolution across robots and tasks, highlighting its potential for fully autonomous embodied agents.

</details>


### [153] [FINE: Future-Informed Navigation Encoding for Data-Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32855)
*Khang H. Nguyen,Hoang Pham Quang Nguyen,Ha Phuong Nguyen,Khanh Dinh Binh,Xuan Ha Nguyen,Vien Ngo,Duy Ho Nguyen Minh,Huan Nguyen,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FINE利用导航示范中的未来观测信息辅助视觉语言导航，在完整及有限示范数据下提升导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉语言导航训练主要学习从当前观测到动作，未充分利用轨迹中未来观测所包含的指令相关地标信息；而收集更多具身示范成本较高。

Method: FINE为导航模型加入两类辅助表示：显式地标令牌按指令中的地标顺序，预测未来地标的二维语义图像特征和视点相关的三维几何特征；隐式未来令牌则借助视频世界模型生成的同场景反事实未来，学习辨别实际到达的地标状态。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE的val-unseen评测中，使用完整训练数据时，FINE使InternVLA-N1的成功率分别提高2.6和4.5个百分点。示范数据仅为70%时，成功率提高6.8个百分点，弥补了约三分之一由减少示范造成的性能损失。

Conclusion: 从已有轨迹中提取未来地标的语义与几何监督，可提升视觉语言导航性能，且在示范数据有限时收益更显著。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language navigation (VLN) policies to new environments is expensive because every additional route and instruction requires an embodied demonstration. Yet standard observation-to-action training uses only a small fraction of the information already contained in each trajectory. In particular, future observations reveal the instruction-relevant landmarks that the agent will encounter, including what they look like and how they are arranged in 3D. We introduce FINE, a Future-Informed Navigation Encoding framework that extracts this latent supervision from existing demonstrations. FINE equips a VLN backbone with two complementary auxiliary representations. First, explicit landmark tokens follow the ordered landmarks specified by the instruction and are trained to predict future landmark regions in both semantic 2D patch-feature space and viewpoint-dependent 3D geometric feature space. Second, an implicit future token learns to distinguish the landmark state that is actually reached from plausible same-scene counterfactual futures generated by a video world model. On R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen, FINE improves InternVLA-N1 by 2.6 and 4.5 success-rate points, respectively, at full training data. More importantly, as demonstrations become limited, the benefit grows: at a 70% demonstration budget, FINE improves success rate by 6.8 points, recovering roughly one-third of the performance lost by reducing the training demonstrations. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [154] [Scanning While Imagining: A Scene-Graph World Model for Robotic Ultrasound Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32837)
*Xuesong Li,Shuai Chen,Feng Li,Zhongliang Jiang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SonoGraph-WM：基于场景图预测解剖结构变化，并用于机器人超声探头的前瞻式导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多机器人超声导航方法未显式预测探头移动引起的解剖视图变化；训练还常依赖带跟踪信息和解剖标注的超声序列。

Method: 将可见解剖结构、几何形态及空间关系表示为场景图。Transformer 根据历史场景图与探头位姿联合预测未来场景图和位姿；滚动时域规划器递归模拟候选轨迹，选择到达目标场景图的最短预测路径，并在执行短时动作后依据新观测重新规划。训练数据由 CT 标签图沿受表面约束的探头轨迹生成。

Result: 在 4 个留出 CT 病例上，预测 20 步后空间关系 F1 仍超过 93%。使用标注生成的场景图进行闭环导航时，胆囊和胰腺目标成功率分别为 77.50% 和 75.00%；机器人—仿体实验中，基于标签图生成的场景图使目标视图到达率达到 73.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，CT 监督的解剖世界模型可支持机器人超声探头规划。可靠导航需要频繁获取新观测并据此更新规划。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) acquisition depends on the operator's ability to interpret anatomy and anticipate how the view will change with probe motion. Many robotic US navigation methods select actions without explicitly predicting these anatomical changes. We propose SonoGraph-WM, an action- and goal-conditioned world model for anticipatory probe navigation. The model represents anatomy as scene graphs (SGs), capturing visible structures, their geometry, and spatial relationships without synthesizing US images. Given a history of SGs and probe poses, a unified Transformer jointly predicts future SGs and poses. A receding-horizon planner recursively imagines candidate trajectories, selects the shortest predicted path reaching a goal graph, and follows it over a short execution horizon before replanning from new observations. To reduce reliance on tracked and anatomically annotated US sequences, we generate aligned SG--pose training data from computed tomography (CT) label maps along surface-constrained probe trajectories. On four held-out CT cases, spatial relation F1 remains above 93% over 20 prediction steps, and closed-loop navigation achieves 77.50% and 75.00% success for the gallbladder and pancreas, respectively, using annotation-derived SGs. In robot--phantom navigation experiments with label-map-derived SGs, the planner reached the target view in 73.7% of trials. These findings support CT-supervised anatomical world modeling for probe planning and highlight the importance of frequent observation updates for reliable navigation. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [155] [Towards Kinematic Actionable Infeasibility Detection in Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32806)
*Aayush Rath,Lakshya Jindal,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向高维机器人运动规划的几何方法，可在有限时间内认证路径不可行，并指出导致不可行的具体几何原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有完备方法通常局限于低维空间；采样式规划虽能扩展至较高维度，却无法提供有限时间的不可行性证明，因此高维空间中的不可行性认证仍具挑战。

Method: 利用有符号距离场，在构型空间中追踪由障碍物边界诱导的分离流形，以分析空间拓扑并检测不可行性。针对计算开销，设计了利用 GPU 并行扩展前沿的算法，用于高维空间中的单纯形重建。

Result: 在 4 自由度和 5 自由度机器人场景中进行了验证：4 自由度案例可在数秒内完成不可行性认证，5 自由度案例则可在四分钟内完成。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了基于几何与拓扑分析的高维不可行性认证潜力，并能提供具体的几何解释；扩展至更高维度仍需进一步提升可扩展性。

Abstract: Motion planning in robotics requires not only computing collision-free paths but also certifying infeasibility when no such path exists. Complete methods are limited to low-dimensional spaces, while sampling-based planners scale efficiently but cannot provide finite-time infeasibility certificates, leaving this problem largely unresolved in high-dimensional spaces. In this letter, we present a geometry-driven framework for certifying infeasibility through an explicit resolution-dependent analysis of configuration space topology. Leveraging signed distance field representations, the proposed method traces separating manifolds induced by obstacle boundaries directly in configuration space, enabling both detection of infeasibility and identification of the specific geometric cause. To address computational challenges, we develop a parallel frontier-expansion algorithm that exploits GPU acceleration for efficient simplicial reconstruction in high-dimensional spaces. We validate the approach on 4-DOF and 5-DOF robot scenarios, certifying infeasibility within seconds for 4-DOF cases and under four minutes for 5-DOF cases. We further discuss avenues for improving scalability to higher-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [156] [CollisionGAT: Controller-Agnostic One-Step Collision Screening for Multi-Agent Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32783)
*Alan Debbas,Edwin Meriaux,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CollisionGAT通过图注意力网络评估多机器人拟议动作的碰撞风险，可与不同控制器或规划器配合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人移动前需检查拟议动作是否会与其他机器人或障碍物发生碰撞，因此需要一种可供不同控制系统调用的风险评估方法。

Method: CollisionGAT以移动智能体的当前状态和拟议状态，以及局部相关的静态障碍物为输入，为每个移动智能体输出一个碰撞风险分数。研究将其接入连续路径跟踪控制器和GATeD规划器；后者使用带类型的否决信息更新规划图。训练标签由精确几何检查生成，且每个实际执行的动作均由独立几何检查审计。

Result: 摘要未报告定量实验结果；仅说明了模型的输入、输出、集成方式，以及训练标签和执行动作的几何检查机制。

Conclusion: CollisionGAT旨在提供可复用的碰撞风险评估接口，使控制器能够据此接受、修正、重新规划或推迟拟议动作。

Abstract: Before a team of robots moves, each proposed step must be checked for collisions with other robots and with obstacles. We present CollisionGAT, a graph-attention network that reads the current and proposed states of moving agents together with locally relevant stationary obstacles and returns one collision-risk score per moving agent. Any controller can use these scores to accept, repair, replan, or postpone a proposed step. We mount CollisionGAT on a continuous path-following controller and on GATeD, an obstacle-blind D* Lite planner that uses typed vetoes to update its planning graphs. Exact geometric checks supply the training labels and independently audit every executed step.

</details>


### [157] [Copper-Policy: Focus on the Representation for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32779)
*Zexin Feng,Yixu Feng,Lingyu Xiao,Shang Su,Kexin Zheng,Chang Xu,Mengkai Shi,Shuo Feng,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Copper-Policy通过紧凑的未来表征预测与动作解码联合训练，在降低训练成本的同时提升机器人控制表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型通常预测像素或潜在空间中的详细未来，计算开销较大。已有证据表明，即使测试时不生成未来，联合训练仍能带来收益，因此关键问题是：模型需要学习何种世界表征，才能改善控制？

Method: Copper-Policy不依赖预定义的预测目标空间，而是与策略共同学习紧凑的世界表征。它采用时间联合嵌入预测，根据任务意图预测未来观测的嵌入，不重建像素；预测任务与动作解码共同塑造表征，同时保留当前帧的空间细节供策略执行。

Result: 表征分析显示，模型能更好地区分任务驱动的变化与扰动，并提供对控制有补充作用的信息。紧凑预测目标减少了每个样本的训练词元数；一个20亿参数模型在8张RTX 5090 GPU上训练耗时9.67小时，在匹配的A100 GPU上比Fast-WAM快6倍。模型在RoboTwin上优于所有未使用具身预训练的对比方法，在LIBERO-Plus上达到80.85%，并在三项真实机器人任务中表现与π0.5相当、平均得分更高。

Conclusion: 与策略联合学习紧凑的未来表征，可在显著提高训练效率的同时保持或提升控制性能，说明高成本的详细未来生成并非获得世界模型训练收益的必要条件。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) acquire behavioral priors by modeling future scene evolution, but predicting detailed futures in pixel or latent space incurs substantial cost. Recent evidence that co-training gains persist without test-time generation raises a question: what must a WAM learn to improve control? We introduce Copper-Policy, which learns a compact World representation with the policy rather than relying on a predefined target space. Through temporal joint-embedding prediction, it predicts future observation embeddings conditioned on task intention without reconstructing pixels. This prediction and action decoding shape the representation jointly, while the policy retains access to current-frame spatial detail for execution. Representation analyses show that the learned features better separate task-driven change from perturbations and provide complementary information for control. Compact prediction targets reduce training tokens per sample, enabling a 2B-parameter model trained in 9.67 hours on 8$\times$ RTX 5090 GPUs and 6$\times$ faster than Fast-WAM on matched A100 GPUs. Copper-Policy outperforms every compared method without embodied pretraining on RoboTwin and several embodied-pretrained VLAs on LIBERO-Plus (80.85%). On three challenging real-robot tasks, it performs comparably to $\pi_{0.5}$ and attains a higher average score. Together, these results show that Copper-Policy combines strong control performance with efficient training.

</details>


### [158] [CLAP: Closed-Loop Alignment with Pressure for Precise Suction Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32767)
*Yixian Zou,Chongyang Xu,Yuling Xin,Ziliang Feng,Fanman Meng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAP 将真空压力读数融入视觉—语言—动作策略，使机器人能够判断吸附状态并及时中断动作、重新观测，在真实堆叠任务中取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 堆叠和码垛要求精确放置，前一层的误差会传递至后续层。视觉难以在关键时刻判断吸盘是否附着，而既有相关策略未报告使用实测真空信号进行状态条件化或中途动作调整。

Method: 在真空管路接入压力模块，将解码后的压力读数作为策略本体感知输入并与视觉特征融合；该信号还用于结束开环执行窗口，触发重新观测和推理。通过真实机器人采集目标状态拆解数据，并在线下反转关节状态序列生成训练数据；另以占训练帧 8.3% 的定向示范覆盖吸附状态转换及中断抓取后的构型。

Result: 在真实 Unitree Z1 上，单一多任务检查点在两种颜色设置下的平均成功率均为 96.67%，较最强基线分别高 16.67 和 10.00 个百分点。单色四块任务的平均误差为 15.92 毫米；四种消融设置的成功率低 5.00 至 13.33 个百分点。

Conclusion: 实测压力反馈与基于新观测的动作重规划有助于提升吸附状态判断和精确堆叠表现。研究计划公开代码和训练权重。

Abstract: Stacking and palletising demand precise placement: error left in one layer is inherited by the next, and a flat pad offers no feature to funnel a wrong pose into the right one. Top-down suction suits such dense arrangements, and suction has already been brought into vision-language-action (VLA) policies. What that work does not report, however, is a policy conditioned on a measured vacuum signal, or one that uses it to abandon an action already under way. Vision does not settle the question here, because at the moment it matters the cup and the face it holds occlude each other. We present CLAP, which makes the attachment state observable through a pressure module tapped into the vacuum line. The decoded reading replaces the suction command in the policy's proprioception, is fused with the visual features, and terminates the open-loop execution window so that the policy re-infers from a fresh observation. For data, we record goal-state disassembly on the physical robot and reverse the joint-state sequence offline, without a simulation replay. Targeted phase demonstrations, 8.3% of the training frames, cover the suction transitions and the configurations an interrupted grasp leaves behind. On a real Unitree Z1, one multi-task checkpoint reaches 96.67% average success in both colour settings, 16.67 and 10.00 points above the strongest baseline, its monochromatic four-block successes averaging 15.92 mm of error. Four ablation settings fall 5.00 to 13.33 points short. We will release code and trained weights.

</details>


### [159] [Human Motion Prediction for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32346)
*Placido Falqueto,Elena Basei,Edoardo Lamon,Giovanni Perantoni,Matteo Saveriano,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较三种人体运动预测方法在协作场景中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测人的后续动作有助于机器人提前规划运动，并提升动态环境中的安全性与效率。

Method: 比较基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的聚类方法、TransFusion 和 Graph-Mixer。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验设置、评价指标或比较结果，无法判断哪种方法表现最佳。

Conclusion: 人体运动预测可为人机协作中的机器人运动规划提供支持；三种方法的相对优劣仍需依据论文的实验结果确定。

Abstract: This paper compares three human motion prediction methods - GMM-based clustering, TransFusion, and Graph-Mixer - evaluating their effectiveness in human-robot collaboration. These predictions are valuable for improving robot motion planning by anticipating human movements and enhancing safety and efficiency in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [160] [WSM-Aware HRI: An IoT-Enhanced Framework for Early Detection and Norm-Guided Repair of Failures with LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32336)
*Hanlin Zhang,Yuquan Wang,Tianwei Zhang,Zhenglong Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 WSM-Aware HRI 框架，将人机交互故障建模为人类指令隐含假设与机器人世界状态模型之间的世界状态不匹配（WSM），并在意图形成阶段主动检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多交互失败并非孤立的技术故障，而是源于人和机器人对当前世界状态的理解不一致。既有方法多侧重故障发生后的恢复，难以提前识别潜在问题。

Method: 框架结合多模态感知与数字增强构建机器人世界模型，并使用大语言模型显式提取指令中的隐含假设、归类不匹配类型，以及确定核验所需证据。检测结果可支持考虑安全、规范遵循和多用户协调的干预，并提供透明解释。

Result: 研究在涵盖视觉与潜在状态不匹配的 10 个日常案例中评估识别能力，报告系统能够准确生成预期结果。消融实验表明，可靠识别依赖适当的状态表征与面向核验的细化过程。

Conclusion: 将交互故障显式建模为世界状态不匹配，有助于在执行前发现潜在失败，并为整合外部证据与社会约束提供系统化机制。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) failures remain a major barrier to deploying robots in real-world environments. Prior work often treats failures as isolated technical faults or focuses on post-hoc recovery behaviors. In practice, many breakdowns arise because humans and robots operate under inconsistent assumptions about the current world state. We propose WSM-Aware HRI, an IoT-enhanced modular framework that unifies diverse HRI breakdowns as World-State Mismatches (WSMs) between a human's instruction-implied assumptions and a robot's grounded world model built from multimodal perception and digital augmentation. A Large Language Model (LLM) is used to make implicit assumptions explicit, map them to a small set of mismatch types, and specify the evidence needed for verification against the robot's world state. WSM-Aware HRI shifts failure handling from execution-time recovery to proactive mismatch detection during intention formation, enabling interventions guided by safety, norm compliance, and multi-user coordination with transparent explanations. We evaluate mismatch identification in ten everyday cases spanning both visual and latent-state mismatches. The system can accurately produce the expected output results, and ablations show that reliable identification depends on appropriate grounding representations and verification-oriented refinement. These results indicate that treating interaction breakdowns as explicit world-state mismatches enables earlier detection of impending failures and offers a principled mechanism for integrating external evidence and social constraints into human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [161] [MemTransfer: Benchmarking Memory Beyond Matched Experience in Embodied Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32313)
*Haiming Tang,Xianjie Dai,Gujie Shao,Zuyi Guo,Jingguang Li,Kailang Ma,Yihong Tang,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemTransfer 显示，记忆表示的效果取决于测试时的变化类型；记住经验并不意味着能在新情境中有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体虽可利用过往经验，但起点、路线或历史信息变化时，能否正确调用和应用记忆仍不明确。

Method: 提出 MemTransfer 基准，在共享的冻结视觉语言模型策略下比较六种记忆表示：一种工作记忆基线和五种经验记忆。基准包含模拟仓库中的 100 个导航案例、10 类任务及专家示范，并分别改变起始位置、路线可用性和历史数量与相关性。

Result: 每项任务仅有一条示范、且从示范起点出发时，Full-context 和 Episodic 成功率分别为 95.3% 和 100.0%；换到新起点后，两者均下降 48—49 个百分点。四条相关示范下，路线受阻时 Summary 保留的原路线成功率比例为 39.3%，低于 Working memory 的 44.8% 和两种轨迹记忆的 56%—58%。在新起点下，相关示范从一条增至四条，使 Episodic 成功率提高 14.3 个百分点，其他受测表示提高不超过 1.3 个百分点。将一半相关历史替换为其他任务经验，会降低两种轨迹记忆的成功率。

Conclusion: 对一种情境变化具有鲁棒性，不代表对其他变化也鲁棒。评估记忆系统时，应同时考察存储的信息及其在决策时的调用和应用能力。

Abstract: Memory lets an embodied agent reuse past experience, yet retaining useful information does not ensure that the agent can apply it when conditions change. We present MemTransfer, a benchmark comparing six memory representations, a working-memory baseline and five representations of past experience, under a shared frozen vision-language-model policy. It comprises 100 navigation cases across ten task types in a simulated warehouse, with expert demonstrations supplying the history. Three comparisons vary the starting pose, route availability, and amount and task relevance of history. With one demonstration per task, Full-context and Episodic memory reach 95.3% and 100.0% success at the original demonstration start, but lose 48-49 percentage points at a new test start. Summary changes little between these two test starts, yet with four demonstrations per task it retains a smaller fraction of its unchanged-route success after blocking (39.3%) than Working memory (44.8%) or the two trajectory memories (56-58%). At the new test start, increasing from one to four relevant demonstrations raises Episodic success by 14.3 percentage points, while the other evaluated representations gain no more than 1.3 percentage points. Replacing half of the relevant histories with other-task experience lowers success for both trajectory memories. These results show that robustness to one kind of mismatch does not imply robustness to another, motivating evaluation of both stored information and its use at decision time.

</details>


### [162] [Affordance-Conditioned Decision Making: Bridging the Semantic-Spatial Gap in Zero-Shot Cross-Floor Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32292)
*Xuekang Yang,Lu Chen,Shuang Luo,Jialing Zhu,Qi Zhang,Yue Gao,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE通过可通行姿态目标与失败感知偏好优化，提升视觉语言导航器在跨楼层场景中的执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语义规划器即使能正确找到楼梯、门口或狭窄通道，也未必能可靠通过；空间受限的转场需要可执行的目标姿态，并要求智能体纠正累积的动作误差。

Method: 提出监督式局部执行模块PACE，保持零样本语义规划器冻结，将转场语义映射为以智能体为中心的长时程可通行姿态，并据此生成短时程动作。随后利用实地运行轨迹构造偏好样本，对比正常或恢复行为与会放大偏差的行为，进行失败感知的偏好优化。

Result: PACE集成到六种开源零样本视觉语言导航器后，在R2R-CE跨楼层子集上的平均成功率由16.35%提升至27.65%，在RxR-CE上由4.76%提升至12.06%。真实环境实验也显示其可应用于未见环境。

Conclusion: 将语义意图落实为可通行姿态，并针对执行偏差进行闭环优化，有助于弥合高层规划与可靠具身行动之间的差距。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation increasingly relies on general-purpose semantic planners, yet translating correct high-level intent into reliable physical execution remains difficult in spatially constrained transitions. Reaching a staircase, doorway, or narrow passage does not ensure traversal; the agent must identify an executable affordance pose and recover from accumulated action errors. We propose PACE (Preference-refined Affordance-Conditioned Execution), a supervised local execution module that augments frozen zero-shot semantic planners for reliable cross-floor navigation. PACE grounds transition-related semantics into a long-horizon, agent-centric traversable affordance pose and conditions short-horizon action generation on this spatial target, thereby aligning semantic goals with physical execution. We further post-train PACE through failure-aware preference refinement using rollout-derived pairs that contrast normal or recovery behaviors with deviation-amplifying behaviors, thereby improving closed-loop correction. We integrate PACE into six open-source zero-shot VLN navigators and demonstrate consistent improvements on the cross-floor subsets of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, increasing the average success rate from 16.35% to 27.65% and from 4.76% to 12.06%, respectively. Real-world experiments further demonstrate PACE's applicability in unseen environments, highlighting the potential of traversable affordances to bridge semantic intent and reliable embodied behavior.

</details>


### [163] [DS-VLA: A Dendritic-inspired Vision-Language-Action Model for Robust Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32253)
*Yaxing Lyu,Jingyi Li,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DS-VLA 将树突启发的脉冲动态融入视觉-语言-动作控制，在动作受扰时展现出较强的闭环鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型在常规评测中的成功，未必能转化为执行动作遭受短暂扰动时的可靠闭环表现；需要机制来抑制不可靠的新信息，同时保留任务相关历史信息。

Method: DS-VLA 为动作神经元设置多个稀疏连接的树突分支，并采用异质且可学习的衰减因子，以支持模块化特征处理和时间信息整合。另引入逐神经元抑制门，在躯体动力学处理前自适应调节新多模态证据进入树突状态的程度。模型在四个 LIBERO 套件上接受常规测试及统一的闭环动作扰动测试，并通过消融和动态分析考察其机制。

Result: DS-VLA 的平均常规成功率为 91.6%，扰动下为 87.35%，保留了常规表现的 95.4%。同一扰动设置下，OpenVLA-OFT、FAST、π₀ 和 GR00T 的成功率分别为 39.45%、23.90%、28.55% 和 30.75%。消融分析单独考察了逐神经元共享抑制的作用；神经动态与扰动后轨迹分析显示，选择性抑制证据及有效行为恢复与鲁棒表现相关。

Conclusion: 结果表明，树突启发的计算机制可作为提升具身智能鲁棒性的有效架构先验，为超越单纯扩大视觉-语言骨干网络或生成式动作解码器提供了方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in language-conditioned manipulation, yet success under nominal evaluation does not necessarily translate into robust closed-loop behavior when executed actions are transiently corrupted. We introduce DS-VLA, a dendritic-inspired action architecture that incorporates dendritic spiking dynamics into VLA control to address this limitation. Specifically, to enable modularized feature processing and temporal information integration, DS-VLA equips action neurons with multiple sparsely connected dendritic branches, each featuring heterogeneous, learned decay factors. Furthermore, to suppress unreliable state updates while preserving task-relevant historical information, we introduce a neuron-wise inhibitory gate that adaptively regulates the admission of new multimodal evidence into dendritic states prior to somatic dynamics. We evaluate DS-VLA on all four LIBERO suites under both nominal rollouts and a unified closed-loop action-perturbation protocol. DS-VLA achieves a 91.6\% average nominal success rate and an 87.35\% average perturbed success rate, retaining 95.4\% of its nominal performance. Under the same reported perturbation setting, OpenVLA-OFT, FAST, $\pi_0$, and GR00T achieve 39.45\%, 23.90\%, 28.55\%, and 30.75\%, respectively. A controlled ablation isolates the contribution of neuron-wise shared inhibition, while analyses of neural dynamics and post-perturbation trajectories associate robust performance with selective evidence suppression and effective behavioral recovery. Together, these results demonstrate that integrating brain-inspired computational mechanisms offers a promising architectural prior for robust embodied intelligence beyond merely scaling vision-language backbones or generative action decoders.

</details>


### [164] [Federated Subspace Guided Vision-Language-Action Policy Distillation for Non-IID Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32239)
*Biprodip Pal,Kaushik Roy,Yanming Zhu,Brendan Tidd,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FedDRMan 通过子空间引导的知识蒸馏、客户端兼容性聚类和谱重平衡，提升非独立同分布异构机器人操作任务中的联邦学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同机器人面临的任务与环境分布不一致，容易导致表示漂移和相互冲突的策略更新，使直接聚合损害模型性能；同时，弱但关键的任务相关更新方向可能在聚合中被削弱。

Method: 每轮通信时，服务器模型作为冻结教师指导本地行为克隆；通过低秩多模态子空间蒸馏和动作分布蒸馏，保留有用的全局表示结构与策略行为。服务器按更新兼容性对客户端分组，为各簇维护独立模型，并对兼容客户端的聚合结果进行谱重平衡。

Result: 在 LIBERO 的多种非独立同分布设置、异构程度和客户端参与变化实验中，FedDRMan 均优于所评估的强联邦基线；峰值平均成功率为 80.7%，比最强基线高 11.6 个百分点。消融实验与聚合分析也支持各组件的作用。

Conclusion: 兼容性分组与子空间蒸馏、谱重平衡相结合，可改善异构机器人间的知识迁移，缓解直接参数聚合带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Federated learning offers a natural way for multiple robots to jointly improve manipulation policies without requiring centralized access to training demonstrations. However, non-IID task and environment distributions can induce representation drift and mutually incompatible robot-policy updates, making naive parameter aggregation destructive. We present FedDRMan, a federated subspace-guided distillation framework for heterogeneous robot manipulation. At each communication round, the server model provides a frozen teacher for local behavior cloning, while low-rank multimodal subspace and action-distribution distillation preserve globally useful representation geometry and policy behavior. To address heterogeneous aggregation, FedDRMan groups clients by update compatibility and maintains a persistent model for each cluster. The server then spectrally rebalances each compatible aggregate to mitigate attenuation of weaker task-relevant robot-policy update directions. Extensive experiments on LIBERO across diverse non-IID settings, heterogeneity levels, client participation variation, together with ablations and aggregation analyses, show that FedDRMan substantially improves knowledge transfer and consistently outperforms strong federated baselines achieving a peak mean success rate of 80.7%, 11.6 percentage points above the strongest evaluated federated baseline.

</details>


### [165] [RoboFFT: Finetuning generative robot policy via online reinforcement learning with forward process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32236)
*Yu Li,Shenghe Hu,Yuhan Wang,Yaoxiang Pu,Haotong Zhang,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFFT 利用前向加噪与加权 score/flow matching 损失，为生成式机器人策略构造代理策略比率，并通过 PPO 风格更新进行强化学习微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略容易受示范质量不佳和分布偏移影响；但生成式策略的似然难以计算，使其难以直接应用强化学习进行微调。

Method: 对采样动作执行前向加噪，利用加权 score/flow matching 损失估计代理策略比率，并据此进行 PPO 风格的策略更新。

Result: 在仿真基准的长时程规划与稀疏奖励任务中，RoboFFT 提升了多种生成式机器人策略的表现，并改善训练稳定性与效率；在真实机器人任务中也展示了有效性。

Conclusion: RoboFFT 提供了一种无需直接计算生成式策略似然的强化学习微调框架，可用于仿真及真实机器人策略优化。

Abstract: Generative models, such as diffusion and flow-based models, have shown strong promise for robot policy learning by capturing complex and multimodal action distributions from demonstrations. However, policies trained solely with imitation learning often suffer from imperfect demonstrations and distributional shifts, while further improvement typically requires additional expert data. Reinforcement learning offers a natural solution through environment interaction, but effectively finetuning generative robot policies remains challenging due to the intractability of likelihood estimation. In this work, we propose RoboFFT, a forward-process reinforcement learning framework for finetuning generative robot policies, which applies forward noising to sampled actions and uses the weighted score / flow matching loss to construct a surrogate policy ratio for PPO-style updates. We evaluate RoboFFT with popular generative robot policies on representative simulation benchmarks, including long-horizon planning and sparse reward settings. Extensive experiments and analysis demonstrate that RoboFFT consistently improves performance while achieving better stability and training efficiency. We further integrate RoboFFT into a real world RL framework and demonstrate its effectiveness in real world tasks. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [166] [FutureRay: Control-Aligned Future Range for Agile Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32158)
*Tianhao Zang,Shanze Wang,Ziqian Wang,Liyou Luo,Zihan Liu,Xingjian Xie,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureRay预测未来各方向的可通行距离与遭遇风险，帮助四足机器人更安全地避开动态障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖当前距离信息难以应对机器人运动期间出现的动态障碍；导航还需考虑机体占用空间以及反应和制动所需时间。

Method: FutureRay根据深度测得的距离历史和可观测机器人运动，预测不同方向、未来时刻的距离及遭遇风险。训练侧重近期净空，并惩罚高估可用空间的误差。局部规划器将预测与当前观测结合，检查候选方向上的机体净空，并依据反应—制动模型选择速度；速度指令交由固定的运动策略执行。

Result: 在60个静态与动态仿真场景的配对评估中，FutureRay的任务完成率为93.3%，高于当前距离保持基线的75.0%和笛卡尔卡尔曼外推基线的80.0%，且碰撞次数更少。实体四足机器人上的定性试验显示，机器人能在障碍物接近路线时开始避让，随后继续朝目标行进。

Conclusion: 联合预测未来距离与遭遇风险能够提升障碍物规避表现，无需重新训练底层运动策略。

Abstract: Moving obstacles can block a previously clear route while a quadruped robot executes a motion command. We investigate whether predicting changing clearance improves navigation when motion selection accounts for the robot footprint and the time needed to react and brake. We present FutureRay, which predicts ranges across viewing directions and future times, together with encounter risk, from depth-derived range history and observable robot motion. Training emphasizes near-term clearance and penalizes errors that overstate available space. A local planner queries the same forecast for candidate headings and combines it with current observations to check clearance around the robot footprint. Model-based reaction--braking limits guide speed selection, and the resulting velocity commands are passed to a fixed locomotion policy. In paired evaluations on 60 static and dynamic simulation scenes, FutureRay achieves 93.3% completion, compared with 75.0% for current-range persistence and 80.0% for Cartesian Kalman rollout, with perception, planning, and locomotion held fixed. FutureRay also records fewer collisions than both baselines. Qualitative trials on a physical quadruped show avoidance initiated while an obstacle is approaching the route, followed by renewed goal progress. These results show that joint range and encounter-risk prediction can improve obstacle avoidance without retraining the locomotion policy.

</details>


### [167] [AquaBEV-Nav: Learned BEV Occupancy for Underwater Navigation and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32156)
*Trung Tien Dong,Zhenqi Wu,Sahasra Kondapalli,Jiayi Wu,Yi Sheng,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaBEV-Nav 通过单帧图像直接预测鸟瞰占据图，避免依赖不可靠的水下单目深度估计，实现安全水下探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉式水下探索通常先估计单目深度，再将几何信息投影并累积为二维占据图。水下散射和波长相关的光衰减会削弱视觉线索，使深度估计不可靠，进而影响环境理解与安全运动。

Method: 在 CORAL 分层探索框架中，AquaBEV-Nav 以 AquaBEV 替换深度感知前端。AquaBEV 将单帧 RGB 特征映射到学习得到的极坐标表示，沿距离维度进行因果推理，并重建局部笛卡尔占据图，无需显式预测中间深度。占据图被累积到 CORAL 的持久空间记忆中，为视觉语言模型高层规划提供空间上下文，并为考虑动力学的局部轨迹生成提供碰撞约束。

Result: 在 10 个模拟珊瑚礁环境及统一评测协议下对 6 种占据图骨干网络进行评估，AquaBEV-Nav 达到 37.48 的结构 IoU 和 53.2 的目标 IoU；闭环覆盖率为 88.95%，且未发生碰撞。

Conclusion: 直接从图像预测鸟瞰占据图可绕开水下单目深度估计这一薄弱环节，并与分层规划及轨迹生成结合，实现具有较高覆盖率且无碰撞的模拟水下探索。

Abstract: Safe underwater exploration requires a robot to understand where surrounding structures are located and which regions are available for motion. Existing vision-based underwater exploration systems commonly obtain this information indirectly by estimating monocular depth, unprojecting the geometry into 3D space, and accumulating it into a 2D bird's-eye-view occupancy map. This reliance on intermediate depth estimation is particularly problematic underwater, where scattering and wavelength-dependent attenuation degrade visual cues and limit the reliability of monocular depth estimates. We introduce AquaBEV-Nav, an underwater exploration framework that bypasses explicit monocular depth estimation through direct bird's-eye-view occupancy prediction. Built upon the CORAL hierarchical exploration framework, AquaBEV-Nav replaces its depth-based perception front end with AquaBEV. Given a single RGB frame, AquaBEV maps visual features into a learned polar representation, performs causal reasoning along the range dimension, and reconstructs local Cartesian occupancy without relying on intermediate depth prediction. The resulting occupancy map is accumulated into CORAL's persistent spatial memory, providing spatial context for VLM-based high-level planning and collision constraints for dynamics-aware local trajectory generation. Across ten simulated reef environments and six occupancy backbones evaluated under a single protocol, AquaBEV-Nav reaches 37.48 structure IoU and 53.2 target IoU, 88.95% closed-loop coverage with zero collisions.

</details>


### [168] [RecastVLA: From Past Interaction to Future Control with Adaptive Policy States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32155)
*Wenbo Li,Jun Yang,Yiteng Chen,Wei Zhang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RecastVLA将动作生成过程转化为可持续更新的策略状态，使机器人能够利用历史交互信息进行序列操作，并在多个仿真与真实机器人任务中提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列操作中，当前视觉场景可能无法完整反映既往交互结果，因此策略需要显式保留历史信息。现有方法通常依赖外部历史表示，本文探索能否直接通过动作生成过程形成并维护持久化状态。

Method: 在流匹配视觉-语言-动作策略中引入基于动作侧测试时训练的自适应策略状态。该状态由共享快速权重表示，并在一次动作生成期间保持不变；不同深度的接口读取同一状态，跨网络深度的特征与多次流评估共同计算下一次策略调用的状态更新。训练时通过对后续动作损失反向传播，联合学习初始化参数、接口和状态更新规则；部署时则使用策略自身的动作生成特征进行无专家标签更新。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin、RoboDojo和12项真实机器人任务上，RecastVLA相较于不使用测试时训练的匹配基线取得更高平均成功率；在RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean设置下提升10.68个百分点。受控实验表明，保留策略状态能够提高成功率，共享状态设计比独立训练的逐层局部测试时训练方法高2.58个百分点。

Conclusion: 动作生成不仅可以输出控制动作，还能够构建用于后续决策的持久化内部状态。共享快速权重与跨深度更新机制有效增强了策略对历史交互的记忆和适应能力，并在仿真及真实机器人场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Sequential manipulation requires a robot to track what has already happened, even when the current scene no longer reveals it. Policies with explicit history representations make past interactions available as context for current decisions. We ask how action generation itself can form a persistent state for subsequent control. Building on action-side test-time training, RecastVLA maintains an adaptive policy state within a flow-matching vision-language-action policy. The state is represented by shared fast weights and remains fixed throughout action generation. Depth-specific interfaces read the same state, while features across depths and flow evaluations jointly define one update for the next policy call. Subsequent action losses train the initialization, interfaces, and update rule by differentiating through earlier state transitions. At deployment, updates use the policy's own action-generation features without expert action labels. Across LIBERO, RoboTwin, RoboDojo, and twelve real-robot tasks, RecastVLA improves mean success over a matched policy trained without test-time training, including 10.68 percentage points on RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean. In controlled RoboTwin comparisons, retaining state improves success, and the shared design exceeds independently trained layer-local TTT by 2.58 points.

</details>


### [169] [GAUGE: Planner-Conditioned Active Calibration of Opaque Quadruped Velocity Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32154)
*Tianhao Zang,Zihan Liu,Shanze Wang,Liyou Luo,Xingjian Xie,Chengtai Li,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAUGE 是一种面向导航任务的主动校准框架，通过选择信息价值高的试验，学习四足机器人速度指令与实际运动之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用四足机器人的底层运动控制器通常不可访问，导致速度指令与实际运动存在系统性偏差；而导航规划器只使用指令范围中的特定部分，因此校准应优先关注与规划任务相关的区域。

Method: GAUGE 维护贝叶斯指令—运动模型，并依据规划器诱导的指令分布，选择预期能最大程度降低后验认知不确定性的授权试验。所得后验用于基于验证的停止判定、有界逆向补偿，以及检测到接口变化后的任务相关再校准。

Result: 在三种受控响应族中，GAUGE 用少于被动校准、D-optimal 校准和任务无关方法的试验次数，达到面向任务的精度与不确定性联合标准。在六个留出的 Isaac Sim 导航地图上，其表现满足相对于密集校准预先声明的非劣效界限。

Conclusion: 将校准目标与规划器实际使用的指令分布相结合，可减少达到任务要求所需的试验次数，并支持对接口变化进行针对性再校准。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a Goal-Aware Uncertainty-Guided Exploration (GAUGE) framework for planner-conditioned active calibration of opaque quadruped velocity interfaces. Commercial quadrupeds commonly expose planar-velocity commands, but the underlying locomotion controller remains inaccessible and can produce systematic discrepancies between commanded and realized motion. A navigation planner typically uses a structured subset of the command envelope. GAUGE maintains a Bayesian command-to-motion model and selects authorized trials according to their expected reduction of posterior epistemic uncertainty under the planner-induced command distribution. The resulting posterior supports validation-based stopping, bounded inverse compensation, and task-relevant recalibration after detected interface shifts. In three controlled response families, GAUGE reaches the joint criterion for task-facing accuracy and uncertainty with fewer trials than passive, D-optimal, and task-agnostic alternatives. Across six held-out Isaac Sim navigation maps, it meets the declared noninferiority margins against dense calibration. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [170] [Residual Denoising Enables Sample-Efficient Multi-Agent Coordination on Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32129)
*Dayi Dong,Maulik Bhatt,Aayushi Shrivastava,Lasse Peters,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALTER 将预训练单机器人扩散策略适配为可按需协作的多机器人策略，同时尽量保留独立操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练机器人策略通常面向单机器人场景；多机器人适配需要协调能力，又不能显著损害原有单机器人技能，而协作示范数据往往有限。

Method: 训练一个协调头，预测残差去噪器，在需要时将单机器人行为转化为协同行为。利用少量协作示范，并加入由基础策略生成的自蒸馏数据，以保持单机器人能力。执行时各机器人仅依据自身视觉观测决策，不进行显式通信。

Result: 仿真结果显示，ALTER 相比基线取得更高的协作成功率，并保留更多原有技能；硬件实验呈现出相似趋势，在协作表现与单机器人技能保持之间实现了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: ALTER 能以少量协作数据将单机器人策略适配为可按需协作的去中心化多机器人策略，并兼顾协作性能与单机器人技能保留。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies offer strong manipulation skills but are typically limited to single-agent settings, where a robot acts in isolation. In this work, we study how to adapt pretrained single-agent diffusion policies to multi-agent settings using minimal collaborative data, co-optimizing for two key objectives: high coordination performance and single-agent skill retention. To this end, we introduce ALTER, an adaptation method for coordination on demand: the adapted policy coordinates with other robots when deployed in a team while remaining capable of acting independently when operating alone. Execution is decentralized: each robot acts only on its own visual observations, without explicit inter-agent communication. Our method trains a coordination head that predicts a residual denoiser to transform single-agent behavior into coordinated multi-agent behavior when necessary while also preserving single-agent capabilities. To preserve single-agent capabilities, we augment a small number of collaborative demonstrations with self-distilled data generated by the base policy during training of the residual denoiser. In simulation,ALTER achieves higher coordination success over our baselines while retaining much higher source-skill retention. In our hardware experiments, we find similar trends where ALTER better co-optimizes for coordination success and single-agent skill retention than the baselines

</details>


### [171] [Active 3D weaves for load-bearing and damage-resilient locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32099)
*Guowei Wayne Tu,Evgueni T. Filipov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出将主动材料集成到三维编织壳体中的通用框架，制成兼具承载、驱动与抗损能力的机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编织结构柔韧且适合人机交互，但柔性限制了承载能力；机器人系统因此需要同时实现高轴向刚度、低弯曲刚度和整体韧性。

Method: 以三维编织结构的“编织角”作为模块化单元，集成主动材料；通过特征值计算分析结构的承载与驱动机制，并据此设计五种可运动机器人。

Result: 机器人能够实现运动，并可携带相当于自身重量70倍的载荷；经受极端压缩后，仍能保持可重复的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为设计、制造和仿真兼具承载、主动变形、运动能力与系统级韧性的三维编织机器人提供了路径。

Abstract: The craft of weaving, where different materials are interlaced, has tremendous potential for creating active and functional systems for use in soft robots, prosthetics, wearables, exoskeletons, and more. These textile-like systems are flexible and safe for human-machine interaction; however, this inherent flexibility limits their ability to carry loads, which is essential for many robotic functions. In this work, we introduce a general framework for integrating active materials into three-dimensional (3D) woven shells to create robotic structures that combine high axial stiffness for load bearing, low bending stiffness for efficient actuation, and system-level resilience for damage tolerance. These woven robots can be modularly assembled from 'woven corners', a fundamental unit of 3D woven structures. We use eigenvalue calculations to identify load bearing and actuation mechanisms of the 3D woven structures, and use that information to make five different robots capable of locomotion. We demonstrate that these 3D woven robots can locomote carrying loads 70 times their self-weight, and can maintain repeatable performance even after being subjected to extreme compression. This work is a pathway toward the design, manufacturing, and simulation of future 3D woven robotic systems where load bearing, high stiffness, active functional deformation, locomotion, and system-level resilience are all needed.

</details>


### [172] [Fiber-Normalized Manipulability and Determinant Proxies: Intrinsic Redundancy Optimization Across and Within Task Fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32079)
*Antonio Franchi,Mirko Mizzoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文证明，在固定任务纤维内，基于行列式的可操作性指标与不同坐标表示及度量补全后的指标等价；并提出纤维归一化指标，用于坐标与任务空间度量无关的跨纤维比较及轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可操作性指标依赖任务坐标和任务空间度量，直接跨不同任务纤维比较时可能产生歧义，因此需要厘清其固定任务优化性质，并构造适用于跨任务比较的内在指标。

Method: 在每个正则任务纤维上，分析行列式代理、任意任务坐标下的表示及度量补全指标之间的关系；进一步将内部状态处的能力除以同一纤维上的最优能力，定义无量纲的纤维归一化可操作性，并以其损失构造轨迹优化目标。

Result: 固定纤维上的相关指标仅相差正比例常数，因而具有相同的排序、约束极值、梯度方向、临界点和局部最优性分类。纤维归一化指标对内部状态与任务流形的坐标变换不变，且不依赖任务空间度量。平面机械臂与冗余气动分配示例验证了固定纤维等价性、跨纤维差异及归一化轨迹优化的不变性。

Conclusion: 行列式可操作性是固定任务冗余优化的精确目标；跨任务纤维比较则应采用纤维归一化指标，以获得具有内在性且适用于物理可行轨迹优化的目标。

Abstract: This work establishes that determinant-based manipulability is an exact objective for fixed-task redundancy optimization, despite its dependence on task coordinates and the choice of task-space metric used for volume measurement. On every regular task fiber, the determinant proxy, its representation in any task chart, and every metric-completed manipulability differ only by positive constants. They consequently induce the same complete ordering, constrained extrema, gradient directions, critical points, and local optimality classifications. For comparisons and trajectory optimization across task fibers, this work introduces fiber-normalized manipulability: the capability attained at an internal state divided by the best capability available on the same fiber. The resulting dimensionless scalar is invariant under coordinate changes on the internal-state and task manifolds and independent of the task-space metric. Its associated loss provides an intrinsic objective for physically admissible cross-fiber trajectories, including problems with prescribed or free task evolution and temporal coupling. Planar-manipulator and redundant aerodynamic-allocation examples demonstrate fixed-fiber equivalence, task-dependent cross-fiber differences, and invariant fiber-normalized trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [173] [Find Something You Can't Do: Agentic Real-World Reinforcement Learning for Self-Improving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32069)
*Yuan Fang,Zechu Li,Haolei Tong,Puze Liu,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIND 是面向真实机器人操作的自主强化学习框架，利用场景变化和近期成功率选择练习任务，并在无需在线人工奖励标注的情况下提升策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉—语言—动作模型通常作为冻结策略部署，难以从失败中持续改进；而真实环境强化学习又受制于人工重置环境和提供任务成功监督。

Method: FIND 在持续存在的工作空间中闭环执行场景理解、任务选择、自我评估与策略改进。视觉—语言智能体从预定义任务库中识别当前场景可执行的任务，并优先练习近期成功率较低的任务；通过执行前后的成对观测评估结果。框架以冻结的 π₀.₅ 视觉—语言—动作模型为基础，结合残差式离策略强化学习。

Result: 在八项真实世界操作任务中，独立人工评估的成功率从 55% 提升至 71.9%。一次代表性运行在 6 小时交互内完成 456 个自主回合，仅需 30 次场景恢复干预，在线学习期间无需人工提供奖励标签。研究还通过消融和系统评估考察了关键设计、智能体评估准确性及人工干预需求。

Conclusion: FIND 展示了在持续工作空间中依据实际场景和策略弱点自主安排练习、评估结果并改进机器人的可行性，可减少在线学习对人工奖励标注和频繁环境重置的依赖。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models provide strong priors for robotic manipulation but are typically deployed as frozen policies, unable to improve from their own failures. Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) offers a path to continued improvement, yet manual environment resets and task-success supervision hinder autonomous learning. We introduce \textbf{FIND}, an agentic real-world RL framework that closes the loop between scene understanding, weakness-aware practice, self-evaluation, and policy improvement in a persistent workspace. FIND reframes autonomous practice as a scene-conditioned, performance-aware task-selection problem: instead of restoring a predefined scene after each rollout, it uses the resulting scene to determine what to practice next. A vision--language agent identifies feasible tasks from a predefined library, prioritizes those with lower recent success rates, and evaluates outcomes using paired pre- and post-execution observations. We instantiate FIND with a frozen $\pi_{0.5}$ VLA and residual off-policy RL. Across eight real-world manipulation tasks, the independent human-assessed success rate improves from $55\%$ to $71.9\%$. A representative run completes 456 autonomous episodes within 6 hours of interaction, requiring 30 scene-recovery interventions and no human-provided reward labels during online learning. Ablations and systematic evaluations further examine key design choices, agent evaluation accuracy, and human intervention requirements. Our website is made publicly available at: this http URL .

</details>


### [174] [Grasp2Twist: Learning Bimanual Dexterous Jar Opening by Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32064)
*Mo Xu,Yunfu Deng,Jianuo Wang,Josiah Hanna,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Grasp2Twist 利用强化学习实现双手灵巧抓取并持续旋转容器盖，能够零样本迁移至真实系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开盖任务要求策略协调抓取与旋盖多个阶段，并在受限的手部运动范围内持续旋转瓶盖；同时，仿真策略还需可靠迁移到真实环境。

Method: 提出统一策略学习方法，以连续包围度引导抓取形成，以二值包围指标指导从抓取到旋盖的转换；两者依据物体中心与手掌、指尖构成的凸包之间的几何关系计算。针对持续旋转所需的手指接触重配置，采用三阶段课程学习促进探索，并增强仿真到真实环境的迁移鲁棒性。

Result: 学习得到的策略能够通过手指步态调整手物接触并持续旋盖，在六种家用容器上实现零样本真实迁移，任务成功率为 88%。消融实验验证了几何包围指标对抓取形成的作用，以及课程学习对接触重配置探索的贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法将几何引导的抓取与课程式接触重配置相结合，有效支持多阶段灵巧开盖任务，并展现出较好的真实环境迁移能力。

Abstract: This paper presents Grasp2Twist, a bimanual dexterous manipulation system that learns to grasp and twist open jar lids using reinforcement learning. Learning this task raises three challenges: learning a unified policy for a multi-stage task, sustaining lid twisting, and sim-to-real transfer. To address the first challenge, we introduce a continuous enclosure measure to guide grasp formation and a binary enclosure indicator to guide the grasp-to-twist transition for unified policy learning. We derive both from the geometric relationship between the object center and the convex hull formed by the hand's palm and fingertips. Kinematic constraints limit how far the hand can rotate the lid with fixed contacts, so sustained twisting requires finger contact reconfiguration. We use a three-stage curriculum to facilitate exploration of these contact changes and also improve robustness for sim-to-real transfer. With our approach, the learned policy demonstrates finger gaiting, reconfiguring hand-object contacts to sustain lid rotation. It transfers zero-shot to the physical system and achieves an 88% task success rate across six household containers, including peanut-butter, vitamin, and instant-coffee jars. Ablations further validate the roles of the geometric enclosure in grasp formation and the curriculum in contact-reconfiguration exploration.

</details>


### [175] [Inertia-Corrected Newton Method For Generalized Nash Equilibria in Dynamic Games with Optimality Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31929)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于受约束动态博弈的快速牛顿求解器，可高效检验局部广义纳什均衡的二阶充分条件，并改善求解收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿法可快速求解动态博弈的 KKT 必要条件，但较小的 KKT 残差并不能保证所得解满足局部广义纳什均衡的二阶充分条件，因此需要高效的最优性检验方法。

Method: 将个体 KKT 矩阵的惯性与约束零空间上投影的代价函数约化 Hessian 的正定性联系起来，据此检验二阶充分条件；同时提出惯性校正更新步，通过扰动具有较弱个体间耦合的严格鞍点，促进算法收敛至局部广义纳什均衡。

Result: 数值基准测试表明，该求解器在实际多智能体规划问题中具有良好的运行时间与收敛表现；微型自动驾驶赛车平台上的物理实验进一步验证了其实时运行能力。

Conclusion: 该方法将快速牛顿求解、局部最优性验证和鞍点规避结合起来，为多智能体模型预测控制中的动态博弈求解提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Newton methods efficiently find Generalized Nash Equilibria (GNE) in dynamic games by solving for the KKT necessary conditions. These methods are fast and can support multi-agent Model Predictive Control (MPC) for highly dynamic robots. However, a small KKT residual alone does not certify that the returned solution satisfies the second-order sufficient conditions for a local GNE. In this paper, we propose an efficient numerical method to verify the second-order sufficient conditions (SOSC) for a local GNE. We connect the inertia of the agent KKT matrix with the positive definiteness of the reduced Hessian of the cost function, projected onto the null space of the constraints. Furthermore, we introduce an inertia-corrected update step that improves convergence to local GNEs by destabilizing strict saddle points with weak cross-agent coupling. Our main contribution is a fast Newton solver for Constrained Dynamic Games that provides efficient optimality checking. Through numerical benchmarks, we demonstrate the proposed solver's runtime and convergence performance in practical multi-agent planning problems. We also validate the solver's real-time capabilities in physical experiments using a platform of miniature autonomous race cars.

</details>


### [176] [HapticWorld: an Interactive World Simulator with Real-time Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31924)
*Shaoting Peng,Litian Liang,Yixuan Wang,Ming Yang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Mark Cutkosky,James Jingxi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HapticWorld通过学习型世界模型实时预测并反馈关节力矩，使操作者能够在虚拟环境中获得触觉信息，从而提升接触丰富型机器人操作的数据采集效率与策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富型操作依赖力觉信息，但仅凭视觉难以准确推断接触力。真实机器人采集成本高，物理仿真中的接触力与真实测量存在系统偏差，而视觉型学习世界模型无法提供力/力矩标注和触觉反馈，因此难以规模化获取高质量力觉数据。

Method: 构建HapticWorld交互式世界模拟器，在生成视觉观测的同时预测关节力矩，并将力矩实时反馈给操作者，形成连接人类与学习型世界模型的闭环。利用该系统采集带力觉信息的示范数据，并在多个接触丰富型任务上训练和评估策略，同时比较真实世界数据和视觉-only基线。

Result: 在三个接触丰富型任务中，力矩反馈使数据采集吞吐量平均提升1.6倍。基于HapticWorld示范训练的策略在真实世界60次试验中成功54次，接近真实世界数据训练所得的56/60，并显著优于视觉-only基线的19/60。HapticWorld内部评估的成功率与真实世界结果高度一致。

Conclusion: HapticWorld能够以更高效率规模化采集带力觉信息的机器人操作示范，并训练出具有较强真实世界泛化能力的策略；同时，其内部评估结果与真实表现相符，可作为独立的力/力矩条件策略评估平台。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation depends on force sensing that is hard to infer from visual signals alone, both for collecting demonstrations and for training policies. Force-annotated data, however, remains hard to obtain at scale: real-robot collection ties every demonstration to physical hardware, physics simulators report contact forces that deviate systematically from real measurements, and learned world simulators, though scalable and realistic, are vision-only, so operators feel nothing during data collection and the data carries no force/torque (F/T) labels. We present HapticWorld, an interactive world simulator that predicts joint torque together with observations and renders it back to the operator in real time, closing the haptic loop between a human and a learned world model. Across three contact-rich tasks, torque feedback raises data collection throughput by 1.6 times on average. Policies trained on HapticWorld-generated demonstrations succeed in 54/60 real-world trials, approaching the 56/60 upper bound of real-world data, and far exceeding the 19/60 success rate of the vision-only baseline. Moreover, the success rates measured inside HapticWorld closely match real-world evaluation, demonstrating that HapticWorld can serve as a stand-alone F/T-conditioned policy evaluation platform.

</details>


### [177] [End-to-end QP-based policies: A unified perspective on robust control and robot learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31905)
*Fausto Vega,Priyanka Supraja Balaji,Chase Dunaway,Joe Koszut,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端、基于二次规划（QP）的策略框架，兼顾系统化构造、较少领域专用设计以及可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制策略的构造往往依赖领域专用设计，或难以兼顾自动调优、未知动力学下的控制与模型透明度。本文旨在提供统一的策略表示，覆盖多种控制场景。

Method: 构建端到端QP策略表示，支持在扰动和模型变化分布上进行基于模型的策略自动调优，也支持在无需显式定义状态、输入或系统动力学的情况下进行黑箱策略构造。论文还分析该表示与鲁棒控制、多层感知机及机器人学习等方法之间的联系。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验展示了该框架在鲁棒控制、策略自动调优以及未知系统动力学下控制等任务中的应用。

Conclusion: 端到端QP策略可作为多种策略表示与控制范式的共同抽象，为兼具适用范围、透明度和可解释性的控制策略构造提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end QP-based policy framework that enables systematic policy construction with minimal domain-specific design, while preserving the transparency and interpretability of model-based control. The proposed policy representation supports both domain-randomized model-based auto-tuning, where policy parameters are optimized over distributions of disturbances and model variations, and black-box policy construction, where the problem is formulated in terms of a (possibly) unknown model without requiring explicit notions of states, inputs, or the underlying system dynamics. We establish connections to existing policy representations and control paradigms, including robust control, multilayer perceptrons, and robot learning, and interpret our end-to-end QP policies as a common abstraction of these approaches. We validate the resulting framework in both simulation and hardware, demonstrating a broad range of applications spanning robustness, automatic policy tuning, and control under unknown system dynamics.

</details>


### [178] [GT-VLA: Target-Conditioned Trace Guidance for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31904)
*Ninghan Zhong,Jing-Chen Peng,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GT-VLA 将通用视觉语言模型提供的语义指导转换为二维视觉轨迹，并据此条件化机器人动作生成，以提升 VLA 的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 容易过拟合训练场景，难以适应未见任务、配置、长时序操作及新语言指令。通用 VLM 泛化能力较强，却不能直接控制机器人。

Method: 框架由外部通用 VLM 为当前技能识别语义指导，将其转化为二维视觉轨迹，并将轨迹渲染后的观测输入动作策略。该设计分离语义目标获取、轨迹生成与低层动作执行，并采用包含技能专属轨迹模块和动作模块的混合专家架构。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准和实体机器人平台上的评估显示，GT-VLA 相比近期 VLA 基线具有更强的泛化表现。

Conclusion: 通过将通用 VLM 的语义指导转化为可供策略使用的视觉轨迹，GT-VLA 将高层指导传递至低层机器人动作，为提升机器人操作泛化能力提供了一种可控框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong performance on robotic manipulation, but they often struggle to generalize to unseen tasks, configurations, and long-horizon settings. A key challenge is that VLAs overfit to training scenes and fail to follow novel language instructions. Off-the-shelf vision-language models (VLMs) often provide stronger generalization, but cannot directly control robot actions. To combine the common sense of VLMs with VLA control, we propose Guided Trace VLA (GT-VLA), a steerable framework that accepts guidance from an external generalist VLM through trace-conditioned action generation. GT-VLA uses a generalist model to identify semantic guidance for the current skill, converts this guidance into a 2D visual trace, and conditions its action policy on the resulting trace-rendered observation. This design separates semantic target acquisition, trace generation, and low-level action execution, allowing high-level guidance to propagate to robot actions. GT-VLA uses a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with skill-specific trace and action modules for robust execution. We evaluate GT-VLA on LIBERO and a physical robot platform, showing improved generalization over recent VLA baselines in both settings. The code and additional supplemental materials are available on our project website at this https URL .

</details>


### [179] [Game-Theoretic Control with Constrained Potential Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31901)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速、通用的受约束动态博弈内点求解器，并通过高效二阶修正提升其收敛至局部广义纳什均衡（GNE）的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNE求解器虽已能实时处理小规模动态博弈，但求解速度仍是瓶颈，难以扩展到较多智能体；此外，基于一阶条件的牛顿求解器可能收敛到非纳什鞍点。

Method: 设计用于受约束动态博弈的内点求解器，并加入计算效率较高的二阶修正，以改善局部GNE解的收敛表现。

Result: 通过数值基准测试和缩比赛车实物实验评估了该方法。摘要未提供具体性能数据或与基线方法的量化比较。

Conclusion: 该方法旨在兼顾动态博弈求解速度与解的可靠性，为多智能体规划提供更实用的求解方案；其实际优势仍需结合论文中的详细实验结果判断。

Abstract: Constrained general-sum dynamic games are a popular formulation for highly interactive multi-agent planning problems. In recent years, Generalized Nash Equilibrium (GNE) solvers have achieved real-time performance for small dynamic games. However, solution speed still remains a bottleneck, and controlling even a small number of agents (e.g., more than four) in a dynamic task remains elusive. In addition, Newton solvers that focus on the first-order conditions are vulnerable to non-Nash saddle points, limiting the usefulness of the provided solution. In this work, we propose a fast and versatile interior point solver for constrained dynamic games, along with a computationally efficient second-order correction that increases the probability of converging to a local GNE solution in constrained dynamic games. The performance of the proposed method is evaluated on numerical benchmarks and a physical experiment involving scaled race cars.

</details>


### [180] [Differentiable Dynamics for Autonomous Micro-Mobility Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31885)
*Grace Cai,Joey Lee,Nithin Parepally,Laura Zheng,Ming C. Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Autonomous micro-mobility vehicles (MMVs) such as wheelchairs, scooters, and bicycles have the potential to improve mobility access and support safe low-speed transportation in pedestrian-shared spaces. Achieving MMV autonomy will require realistic, predictable MMV motion. However, many existing autonomous vehicle stacks rely on simplified kinematic models that fail to capture key MMV characteristics such as tire slip, friction, and wheel layouts, limiting realism and gradient-based optimization. In this paper, we explore differentiable formulations of dynamics models for autonomous micro-mobility systems. We first construct DiffKBM, a differentiable version of the kinematic bicycle model (KBM). Then, we introduce DiffGM3, a differentiable formulation of the General Micro-Mobility Model (GM3), a unified tire-based dynamics formulation for micro-mobility vehicles that supports a wide range of MMV configurations. DiffKBM and DiffGM3 enable end-to-end differentiable optimization through MMV dynamics, making them suitable for integration into differentiable autonomy stacks. We evaluate these dynamics models in both open-loop and closed-loop settings: (1) open-loop trajectory matching, where DiffKBM and DiffGM3 are integrated as a dynamics layer within DiffStack and optimized to reproduce real-world MMV trajectories, and (2) closed-loop autonomous navigation, where DiffKBM and DiffGM3 are paired with a differentiable MPC controller in CrowdNav pedestrian scenarios. In the open-loop setting, DiffGM3 outperforms DiffKBM in reproducing trajectories with improvements in ADE and NLL across bicycle, scooter, and motorcycle modes, and reductions in planning loss for bicycle and motorcycle trajectories. We also find that, in closed-loop settings, DiffGM3 improves on DiffKBM's CrowdNav performance by producing 55\% fewer collisions and a 75\% lower discomfort frequency for the bicycle mode.

</details>


### [181] [Efficient Bezier Velocity Optimization for Free-Floating Space Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31880)
*Duo Zhang,Zhizhuo Zhang,Xiaoli Bai,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAVOR 是面向无驱动航天器机械臂的无碰撞运动规划器，通过优化关节速度曲线，实现高成功率的点到点运动、目标跟踪与定时拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器基座会受机械臂运动耦合影响，规划不仅要满足任务目标，还需处理碰撞与运动约束；传统基线在成功率和计算效率方面表现有限。

Method: 将关节速度表示为满足速度、加速度及连续性线性约束的贝塞尔曲线，并通过递归解析灵敏度计算任务与避障梯度。决策维度不随轨迹采样分辨率变化；结合并行评估、缓存和增量式碰撞检测降低计算开销。

Result: 在七自由度机械臂和五种仿真航天器模型上，点到点规划成功率为99.8%，平均计算时间为1.779秒；基线分别为62.9%和50.270秒。跟踪任务完成30/36例，单步QP完成15例；拦截任务完成36/36例，样条基线完成22例。消融实验显示，有限差分使平均规划时间增加4.7倍。

Conclusion: 实验表明，FAVOR 能够高效处理航天器机械臂的耦合运动规划，并在点到点、跟踪和拦截任务中优于所比较的基线。

Abstract: We present FAVOR (Free-floating Arm Velocity Optimization with Recursive Sensitivities), a planner for collision-free reaching, tracking, and prescribed-time pre-grasp interception on an unactuated spacecraft. It optimizes Bezier joint-velocity curves with linear velocity, acceleration, and continuity constraints. Decision dimension is independent of rollout resolution. Analytical recursive sensitivities provide task and clearance gradients through the coupled base-arm motion. Parallel evaluation, caching, and incremental collision discovery reduce computation. With a seven-DoF arm and five simulated spacecraft models, FAVOR achieves 99.8% point-to-point success with 1.779 s mean computation, versus 62.9% and 50.270 s for an IK-initialized position-spline baseline. Means include failures and timeouts. FAVOR completes 30 of 36 tracking cases, versus 15 for single-step QP, and all 36 interception instances, versus 22 for the spline baseline. A controlled ablation shows that finite differences increase mean planning time 4.7-fold.

</details>


### [182] [PHIRL: Aligning Learned Rewards with Task Progress for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31855)
*Hang Yu,James Staley,Cheng Xi Tsou,Xiujin Liu,Wenchang Gao,Jindan Huang,Shijie Fang,Zhegong Shangguan,Angelo Cangelosi,Reuben Aronson,Elaine Short*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHIRL结合人类示范与描述任务累计进展的反馈，学习更可靠且数据高效的奖励函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类示范能提供策略层面的指导，但局部精度有限；人类反馈能指出局部问题，却通常较稀疏，缺乏直接的策略指导。

Method: PHIRL迭代地从示范中通过逆强化学习推断奖励函数，再计算带有进展标注的示范所对应的奖励，并从四个维度使奖励与进展标注对齐。研究还探索使用微调后的视觉语言模型生成进展反馈。

Result: 在真实和模拟机器人任务中，PHIRL显著优于基线，在仅有20%的示范带标注时，仍取得更高的环境回报和任务成功率。对奖励作弊情形的分析也表明，其学习到的奖励函数较能抵御利用。

Conclusion: 将任务进展反馈与人类示范结合，可提升奖励学习的数据效率、任务表现及对奖励作弊的稳健性。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide dense policy-level information but sometimes lack local precision. Human feedback presents accurate local critiques, but offers sparse evaluations rather than direct policy guidance. We propose Progress-Heuristicized Inverse Reinforcement Learning (PHIRL), a data-efficient framework that learns robust reward functions by jointly leveraging demonstrations and feedback. Specifically, we use progress, a feedback modality that describes cumulative task completion. PHIRL iteratively infers a reward function from demonstrations via inverse reinforcement learning, calculates the learned rewards over the progress-annotated demonstrations, and aligns the rewards with progress annotations over four dimensions. We evaluate PHIRL on real and simulated robot tasks, with additional exploration using a fine-tuned vision-language model to provide progress feedback. Results demonstrate that PHIRL significantly outperforms the baselines, achieving substantially higher environmental return rewards and task success with only twenty percent of demonstrations annotated.
