Analysis of reward-hacking scenarios demonstrates that PHIRL learned reward functions are reliable against exploitation.

</details>


### [183] [Humanoid Badminton: Learning Dynamic Racket Skills from Limited Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31840)
*Jingzhi Cui,Zhexiong Wang,Bangjie Xu,Pengyu Zhao,Youyuan Li,Zhi Su,Peng Ren,Mengdi Xu,Chao Yu,Yi Wu,Luyang Wang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三阶段分层强化学习框架，使人形机器人能够根据羽毛球状态在线选择并执行多种击球动作，并在真实环境中与人类持续对打。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 羽毛球要求机器人迅速判断击球时机，在短暂接触窗口内精确控制球拍姿态与速度，并协调全身动作。现有运动参考数据有限：直接跟踪难以适应多样来球，纯任务驱动优化则可能产生不自然动作。

Method: 首先，通过任务随机化的动作增强，将稀疏标注的击球事件扩展为面向不同目标的可执行动作变化，构建连续潜在技能空间；其次，由高层规划器根据观测到的羽毛球状态输出连续潜在技能码，在线组合技能；最后，使用上下文条件对抗正则化器，鼓励更自然的技能使用，同时保持回球表现。

Result: 真实人形机器人实现了与人类的持续多技能对打，涵盖正手、反手和高动态跳跃回球。

Conclusion: 该框架将有限的人类运动先验转化为可适应来球的多样化技能，并在真实机器人羽毛球对打中展示了自然性与任务表现兼顾的潜力。作者称其为首个实现包含高动态跳跃回球在内的人机多技能对打的真实人形机器人系统。

Abstract: High-speed racket sports provide a demanding testbed for humanoid robots, requiring time-critical decisions, precise striking, and dynamic whole-body coordination. In badminton, fast-changing shuttle trajectories require timely contact decisions, while successful returns demand precise racket pose and velocity within a brief contact window and across a broad three-dimensional striking workspace. Human motion data provide valuable priors for such athletic skills, but usable badminton references are limited and imperfect. Direct tracking provides insufficient executable variation for diverse shuttle conditions, while purely task-driven optimization may produce unnatural motion. To address these challenges, we present a three-stage hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for dynamic humanoid badminton. First, task-randomized motion augmentation expands sparse annotated hitting events into executable target-conditioned stroke variations, forming a continuous latent skill space. Second, a high-level planner outputs continuous latent skill codes to compose these skills online according to the observed shuttle state. Third, a context-conditioned adversarial regularizer encourages more natural planner-level skill usage while preserving return performance. When deployed on a real humanoid robot, our system achieves sustained multi-skill rallies with human players, including forehand, backhand, and highly dynamic jump returns. This is the first real-world humanoid racket-sport system to demonstrate multi-skill human--robot rallies including highly dynamic jump returns.

</details>


### [184] [Path Planning with Motion Primitives in Dynamic Environments: SIPP on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31803)
*Marat Agranovskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于状态格的安全时段路径规划方法，在动态环境中生成平滑且可执行的机器人轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高连通网格规划虽能高效寻找无碰撞路径，但依赖停车转向，产生对受运动学约束机器人不够平滑、难以执行的分段线性轨迹。

Method: 将安全时段路径规划（SIPP）扩展到状态格，使用预计算的运动学平滑运动原语；将移动障碍物的时空扫掠体栅格化，并将网格单元视为空间原子单位。通过多种拓扑环境，与高连通网格规划器进行比较。

Result: 状态格方法因搜索空间扩大而增加计算开销，但生成的轨迹在角度变化、累计航向变化和弯曲能量等运动学指标上更优；其可达性与高连通网格方法相当。

Conclusion: 该方法以额外计算成本换取平滑、连续且符合运动学约束的轨迹，适用于动态环境下的实际机器人部署。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in dynamic environments is a critical challenge, particularly when spaces are shared with other mobile agents whose future trajectories are known. While traditional grid-based planners efficiently find collision-free paths, their reliance on stop-and-turn mechanics over $2^k$-connected grids produces piecewise-linear trajectories that are kinodynamically highly sub-optimal for differentially constrained robots. In this paper, we present an adaptation of Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP) that operates on state lattices, utilizing precomputed, kinodynamically smooth motion primitives. To efficiently handle dynamic environments, we rasterize the spatiotemporal swept volumes of moving obstacles directly onto the grid, treating grid cells as atomic units of space, whose resolution is typically dictated by inherent localization noise. We perform a comprehensive comparative analysis between our lattice-based approach and $2^k$-connected grid planners across diverse topological environments. Our evaluation considers a broad spectrum of performance metrics, including planning time, path angularity, cumulative heading change (angle-over-length), and bending energy. The results demonstrate that while the expanded state space of lattice-based search increases computational overhead, it yields trajectories with significantly superior kinodynamic properties. Specifically, our method achieves a reachability comparable to highly connected grids while ensuring smooth, continuous, and physically executable paths ready for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [185] [Timed Rule-Based Supervision of an End-to-End Autonomous Parking Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31773)
*Kejia Gao,Liguo Zhou,Lei Yu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定 CARLA 停车场中，规则式参数化安全屏蔽器（PSS）将停车成功率从 85.16% 提高到 97.66%，但结果仅适用于该仿真设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端停车策略反复出现越界、制动延迟及停滞或振荡等失败，需要在不改变其视觉 Transformer 架构的情况下进行运行时干预。

Method: 在策略控制输出上叠加人工设定的 PSS，根据速度、位置和持续时间阈值处理观察到的失败模式，并在固定 CARLA 停车场中进行闭环评估。

Result: 在 384 次测试中，基线策略成功 327 次（85.16%），加入 PSS 后成功 375 次（97.66%）。成功案例的平均位置误差为 0.21 米，平均朝向误差为 0.33 度。

Conclusion: PSS 在所测仿真环境中提升了停车成功率，但测试共用同一地图、车辆和传感器配置，且屏蔽器使用仿真世界坐标。因此，结果不能证明其可泛化至其他停车场或真实车辆，也不构成形式化安全保证。

Abstract: We study whether a manually specified runtime supervisor can correct recurring failures of an existing end-to-end parking policy in a fixed CARLA parking lot. The vision-based Transformer architecture is inherited from Yang et al.; our contribution is a timed, rule-based Parametric Safety Shield (PSS) applied to its control outputs. The PSS uses hand-calibrated speed, position, and duration thresholds to intervene in observed failure modes, including boundary exits, delayed braking, and stalled or oscillatory control. In the reported closed-loop evaluation, 16 held-out target slots and six initial poses are each evaluated in four rounds (384 attempts per configuration). Target success increases from 327/384 (85.16%) for the retrained policy to 375/384 (97.66%) with the PSS; mean position and orientation errors among successful attempts are 0.21m and 0.33 degrees. These results show an improvement within this simulator setup. The repeated attempts share one map, vehicle, and sensor configuration, and the PSS uses simulator world coordinates; thus the results do not establish generalization to other lots or real vehicles, or a formal safety guarantee.

</details>


### [186] [Reduced Cartesian Kinetostatics for Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots: Residual-Stabilized Full-Shape Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31771)
*Ke Wu,Fangju Yang,Xiaohui Zhang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jingang Yi,Jian S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于平面、轴向可压缩腱驱连续体机器人的降阶笛卡尔方法，可沿指定驱动路径快速预测完整骨架形状与分布应变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多连续体机器人规划与控制任务需要完整的笛卡尔骨架几何；逐点求解几何可变应变模型计算开销较大，难以高效跟踪驱动变化。

Method: 以两个全局位置场表示骨架，通过精确变分和 Taylor–Galerkin 降阶，将预设空间属性与分布载荷压缩为离线矩向量，形成解析残差与雅可比矩阵。结合解析微分和残差修正，在初始平衡对齐后，每次速率计算仅需一次固定维数线性求解。

Result: 四种仿真工况下，预测的笛卡尔形状与分布应变均接近逐点几何可变应变平衡解；残差修正能抑制传播漂移，额外耗时约为未修正 Euler 更新的 0.98%。平均每次更新耗时 0.508 ms，约比逐点求解快 11 倍。位移驱动实验中，骨架位置归一化平均误差最大为 1.02%，末端位置误差最大为 0.28%。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持较高形状预测精度的同时显著降低计算成本，适用于沿指定驱动路径高效预测连续体机器人的完整骨架形状。

Abstract: Many planning and control tasks for tendon-driven continuum robots (TDCRs) require the complete Cartesian backbone geometry. We present a reduced Cartesian framework for planar, axially compressible TDCRs that propagates equilibrium configurations along prescribed tendon-force and tendon-displacement trajectories. The backbone is represented by two global position fields. Following exact variation, a Taylor-Galerkin reduction condenses prescribed spatial properties and distributed loads into offline moment vectors, yielding analytic reduced residuals and Jacobians without online spatial quadrature or numerical differentiation. Analytical differentiation and residual correction yield first-order rate systems requiring one fixed-dimensional linear solve per rate evaluation after initial equilibrium alignment on a regular branch. Across four simulated cases covering variable tendon routing, nonuniform geometry, axial compression, and their combined effects, the propagated Cartesian shapes and distributed strains closely match pointwise geometrically variable-strain (GVS) equilibrium solutions. Residual correction suppresses propagation drift across the tested step sizes while adding only about 0.98% to the mean update time of uncorrected Euler. The proposed method requires 0.508 ms per update on average, approximately 11 times faster than pointwise GVS solves. Displacement-driven experiments yield a maximum normalized mean backbone position error of 1.02% and a maximum end-effector position error of 0.28%. These results support efficient and accurate Cartesian full-shape prediction along prescribed actuation paths.

</details>


### [187] [Robot Manipulation with GPT-6-Astra: Body Knowledge, Experience Reuse, Emergent Skills, and Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31770)
*Sida He,Lingxi Xie,Yunning Cao,Pengfei Chen,Kaiwen Duan,Jiannan Ge,Xinyue Huo,Jiacheng Shao,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，提供机器人机体信息、同步示范经验及可复用视觉反馈技能，可显著缩短 GPT-6-Astra 控制 XLeRobot 完成电梯按钮任务的时间，并支持一定程度的跨起点与仿真到实机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态智能体虽能编写机器人控制程序，但反复探索和模型介导的动作选择会拖慢执行。研究旨在评估外部机体知识、成功经验与可执行技能能否提升操作效率及迁移能力。

Method: 在模拟环境和实体 XLeRobot 上执行电梯按钮任务，比较不同机体信息、示范经验和反馈程序条件下的完成时间；测试固定起点、偏移起点及仿真到实机迁移。实机按钮接触由操作员确认。

Result: 模拟中，完整机体几何与相机信息使平均完成时间较基线缩短57.4%；带同步动作和状态记录的图像使其缩短68.6%。在偏移10–100厘米的起点，原起点经验使时间缩短58%–63%。研究者改编的视觉反馈程序在27次模拟试验中使局部任务时间缩短29%–31%。12次实机试验中，共用起点下仿真模型资产与仿真经验分别使时间缩短53.0%和49.9%；实机经验也迁移至两个新起点。

Conclusion: 结构化机体描述、同步示范记录和可复用反馈技能有助于提升智能体操控效率；智能体仍可依据当前图像调整动作。结果支持在所测任务与起点范围内的经验泛化及仿真到实机复用。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal agents can write robot-control programs, but repeated exploration and model-mediated action selection can make execution slow. We study how external body knowledge, successful experience, and executable skills improve an XLeRobot controlled by GPT-6-Astra in a simulated and a physical elevator-button task. In 30 fixed-start simulation trials, complete robot geometry and camera information reduce mean completion time by 57.4% relative to a baseline with only the common control interface and no prior experience; images with synchronized action and state records reduce it by 68.6% without additional body assets. In nine paired comparisons (18 trials) at starts displaced by 10-100 cm, experience recorded at the original start reduces mean time by 58-63% relative to no experience, demonstrating generalization to the tested new starting positions. During experience experiments, GPT-6-Astra spontaneously generates a short visual-feedback program. Researcher-refactored versions reduce mean local-task time by 29-31% in 27 simulation trials. Finally, 12 real-robot trials using operator-confirmed button contact demonstrate sim2real reuse: at a shared nominal start, simulation XML assets and simulation experience reduce mean time by 53.0% and 49.9%, respectively; real experience also transfers to two new starts. These results suggest a practical way to build general-purpose manipulation experiments around GPT-6-Astra: supply machine-readable body descriptions and synchronized demonstrations, and turn useful agent-generated feedback routines into reusable skills, while the agent adapts actions from current images. We release all task prompts, trial-level experimental data, and acquired skill implementations at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Reliability Engineering for AI Systems: Challenges, Methods, and Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35316)
*Rong Pan,Yili Hong,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将传统可靠性工程方法扩展到人工智能系统，提出涵盖组件、运行闭环、智能体行为及网络与治理的诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均基准准确率只能反映能力，无法证明系统在特定运行条件和时间范围内能否持续、可靠地完成任务；自主系统还涉及检索、记忆、工具使用、权限、人类监督及系统间交互。

Method: 结合失效定义、运行边界、失效模式与影响分析（FMEA）、加速测试、现场监测和可靠性增长等方法；以四级框架分类失效，并通过测试、评估、验证与确认（TEVV）、序贯监测及FRACAS持续更新证据。SMART提供统计测量与测试规划指导，NIST AI风险管理框架提供组织治理指导。

Result: 论文以卷积神经网络对抗测试、感知错误传播和自动驾驶车辆脱离接管事件三个案例，展示了该可靠性工程方案的应用。

Conclusion: 成熟的可靠性工程为评估AI系统提供了可用基础，但面对持续演进的系统，仍需开发新的测量方法与安全防护措施。

Abstract: AI reliability concerns whether an AI system performs its intended function dependably over a stated period and under stated operating conditions, with stated evidence. As these systems become more autonomous, that function includes more than a correct output. Retrieval, memory, tool use, permissions, human oversight, and interactions among systems must operate consistently and safely, and, for generative systems, so must the reasoning process that produces the output. Average benchmark accuracy measures capability; it does not quantify this broader reliability claim. This paper adapts established reliability engineering methods, from failure definitions and operational envelopes to FMEA, accelerated testing, field monitoring, and reliability growth, to AI systems. A four-level diagnostic framework classifies failures as component, operational-loop, agentic-conduct, or network and governance failures. Test, evaluation, verification, and validation (TEVV), sequential monitoring, and FRACAS create and refresh evidence. SMART provides statistical guidance for measurement, analysis, assessment, and test planning; the NIST AI Risk Management Framework provides organizational guidance for governance, evaluation, monitoring, and mitigation. Three cases illustrate the program: adversarial testing of a convolutional neural network, perception-error propagation, and autonomous-vehicle disengagements. Established reliability engineering provides a usable foundation; new measurements and safety guardrails are still needed as these systems are self-evolving.

</details>


### [189] [Nociception as a Control Primitive: Afferent Channels and Nociceptive Memory for Agents Deployed in One Body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34840)
*Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究一种“第一世代”单体学习设定：个体在出生前固定策略，生命周期内不能更新，因此无法通过试错直接学习身体耐久性。研究表明，带有负荷门控伤害感知通道和记忆的个体，能够根据已感知的损耗调整工作分配，从而延长寿命并提高职业总产出；其优势主要来自个体经验，而非跨个体训练形成的先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应控制或强化学习通常假设智能体可以通过反复试验更新策略，但在身体会因使用而磨损的场景中，获取耐久性信息本身会消耗身体。本文旨在刻画没有生命周期内策略更新、也没有跨生命周期学习的条件下，感知与记忆如何产生实际价值，并区分个体经验收益与群体先验收益。

Method: 建立固定权重策略的epoch-one模型，引入负荷门控的伤害感知通道与记忆模块。理论上分析感知成本如何改变工作分配、记忆缺失时为何约束不生效，以及感知通道在哪些情况下最有价值。实证上进行个体级对照实验，将带感知和记忆的个体与同一身体上不具备这些机制的个体比较；使用2000个模拟地板铺设工人的膝部模型，并以已发表的损耗率校准磨损参数。同时比较人口训练策略，并利用消融分解个体经验与既有先验的贡献，最后在护理机器人和野外车队场景中验证预测的适用性。

Result: 理论结果显示，感知到的损耗会使个体转向尚未经历过且报酬更高的工作，而不是简单选择最温和的工作；没有记忆的个体不会使感知成本约束真正生效。感知、记忆和替代工作使模拟工人的工作寿命由55.2岁延长至59.6岁，职业产出由33.7提高至36.1；69.3%的个体受益，且没有个体受损。仅保留当日感知但不保留长期记忆可获得4.4年增益中的一部分，其余增益来自记忆。人口训练策略仅带来0.65年的寿命提升，同时使产出下降0.54，说明个体机制与群体先验的收益来源不同。感知价值取决于威胁是否难以预测、规避成本是否低且忽略成本是否高。在适用区域，护理机器人认证寿命提高约6倍，野外车队报废率由0.55降至0.15；在不适用区域，漫游车相较于盲目谨慎策略几乎没有额外收益。

Conclusion: 本文证明，在无法跨生命周期学习、且身体磨损会反过来限制探索的环境中，负荷门控感知与记忆能够提供显著的个体适应优势。其核心并非普遍增加谨慎，而是利用已感知损耗有选择地替代高风险工作。研究还提出了区分个体经验价值和群体先验价值的统一框架，并通过多个应用场景表明，该机制的收益具有明确的制度条件和可预测的适用边界。

Abstract: An agent deployed in a single body cannot learn how fast that body wears, because every trial that would reveal its wear resistance wears the body it would protect. We study this \emph{epoch-one} setting, in which the parameters of a fixed-weight policy are set before the body is drawn and never updated in life. The agent carries a load-gated nociceptive channel and a memory that retains what was felt. We prove that felt cost moves the allocation to the best-\emph{paid} work not yet felt rather than the gentlest, that an agent without retention never sees the felt-cost constraint bind, and that the channel pays only where the threat is individually unpredictable, cheap to avoid and expensive to ignore. We measure per body, setting the agent with channel and memory against the same individual without them, where neither carries a schedule learned across lives. On $2{,}000$ simulated floor-layer knees, with wear anchored to published loss rates, feeling, retaining and substituting extends the working life from age $55.2$ to $59.6$ and raises career output from $33.7$ to $36.1$. $69.3\%$ of bodies gain and \textbf{none lose}. A body that feels but retains nothing past the day gains one of the $+4.4$ years, and retention carries the rest. A population-trained agent gains $+0.65$ years from the same channel at $-0.54$ output. The difference is what a species prior already supplies, and a single body has none. The two are related by an identity, the ablation mean reporting $(1-\chi)$ of the per-body value with $\chi$ the share a blind schedule already captures, so we report both. Where the regime map predicts value, a care robot sextuples its certified service life and a field-anchored fleet writes off $0.15$ of its machines instead of $0.55$. Where it predicts none, a rover gains little over blind caution, so the map holds in both directions.

</details>


### [190] [UniOPSD: Unifying Outcome and Hindsight Feedback for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34810)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Xiaofeng Han,Zelong Zheng,Haoyu Wu,Tianyu Fu,Chenxu Zhao,Minghui Wu,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniOPSD通过按决策点自适应融合环境结果反馈与成功同伴的 hindsight 反馈，改善语言模型智能体的信用分配，并在多个任务上取得较高成功率与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的结果奖励稀疏且延迟，难以为长交互序列中的决策提供细粒度信用信号。尽管结果反馈与 hindsight 反馈平均而言具有正向一致性，局部仍存在明显分歧，因此需要动态分配二者的影响。

Method: 在共享交互锚点上，将环境回报与成功同伴 hindsight 转换为可比较的信用估计。依据历史一致性确定全局混合水平，并根据当前信号可用性和相对精度，在各决策点调整来源权重；保留回合级结果贡献，再以有界 token 调制细化融合后的步骤信用，用于策略优化。

Result: 使用 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 时，ALFWorld 成功率分别为 82.8% 和 83.6%，WebShop 成功率分别为 75.0% 和 82.0%，Search-QA 综合准确率分别为 45.3% 和 49.8%。在 3B WebShop 上，UniOPSD 比 SDAR 高 7.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，按局部信号状态与精度自适应仲裁不同反馈，能够有效补充稀疏结果奖励，并提升多个交互任务上的智能体表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become an effective approach to training language model agents, but sparse and delayed outcome rewards provide limited guidance for credit assignment across long interaction sequences. Recent work on on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers complementary supervision by evaluating a policy's sampled responses under privileged training-time context. However, our diagnostics show that positive average agreement between outcome and hindsight feedback coexists with substantial local disagreement, raising the question of how to allocate influence between them at each decision. We introduce UniOPSD (Unified On-Policy Self-Distillation), which unifies these feedback sources through adaptive local credit arbitration. UniOPSD constructs comparable credit estimates from environmental returns and successful-peer hindsight at shared interaction anchors. Historical agreement determines the global mixing level, while current signal availability and relative precision adjust each source's influence at individual decisions. The episode-level outcome contribution is retained, and bounded token modulation refines the fused step credit for policy optimization. With Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, UniOPSD achieves ALFWorld success rates of $82.8\%$ and $83.6\%$, WebShop success rates of $75.0\%$ and $82.0\%$, and Search-QA aggregate accuracies of $45.3\%$ and $49.8\%$, respectively. On 3B WebShop, UniOPSD improves over SDAR by $7.0$ percentage points. Our code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [191] [RSI-Router: Evolving Subtask-Level LLM Routing and Skills for Cost-Efficient Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34712)
*Hao Li,Hangfan Zhang,Zhiyao Cui,Chunjiang Mu,Yiqun Zhang,Bo Zhang,Danyang Jia,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSI-router通过递归自我改进，为长程智能体任务分配不同规模的模型与专属技能，在降低推理成本的同时提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务通常需要较高的推理能力，单纯使用大型模型成本较高；小型模型虽难以独立完成完整任务，却可能胜任部分子任务。论文旨在改善任务表现与推理成本之间的权衡。

Method: RSI-router迭代执行四个阶段：从训练轨迹中挖掘子任务及识别规则；提出并评估不同的模型路由方案；比较路由轨迹与仅使用大型模型的轨迹，诊断失败并提炼可复用技能；结合历史经验与新方案更新帕累托候选集，同时保留被支配方案供后续改进。

Result: 在五个智能体基准上，使用 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 与 Qwen3.5-9B 的 RSI-router 相比仅使用 DeepSeek 的基线，推理成本约降低 48.3%。在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 和 WebShop 上，成本降低 74.7%—82.2%，且表现同步提升；在 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上，相对表现提升 16.7%，成本降低 18.0%。其性能—成本帕累托前沿优于九种路由方法。

Conclusion: 递归利用任务经验来共同优化子任务划分、模型分配与专属技能，可以有效提升长程智能体的成本效益，并取得更具竞争力的性能—成本折衷。

Abstract: Practical deployment of large language model (LLM) agents requires strong task performance at affordable inference cost. For long-horizon agentic tasks, this performance-cost trade-off can be improved through within-task large-small model collaboration, as smaller models can handle some stages even when they cannot solve the full task. In this paper, we introduce RSI-router, a routing framework that constructs subtask-level model assignments and model-specific skills through recursive self-improvement over accumulated experience. Each iteration consists of four stages: Subtask Mining derives subtask definitions and identification rules from training trajectories; Routing Strategy Evolution proposes and evaluates diverse model assignments; Model-Specific Skill Evolution compares routed and large-model-only trajectories to diagnose failures and develop reusable execution skills; and Pareto-Optimal Router Selection updates the Pareto population using historical and newly generated routers while retaining dominated routers as experience for subsequent evolution. Routing between DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash and Qwen3.5-9B, RSI-router consistently surpasses the DeepSeek-only baseline at roughly half the inference cost (48.3%) across five agentic benchmarks. In particular, on ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and WebShop, it cuts inference cost by 74.7-82.2% while simultaneously improving performance; on Terminal-Bench 2.0, it achieves a 16.7% relative performance gain at 18.0% lower cost. Moreover, RSI-router establishes a stronger performance--cost Pareto frontier than 9 routing methods.

</details>


### [192] [VCN-Bench: A Video-Contextualized Navigation Benchmark for Spatial Reasoning over Prior Visual Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34687)
*Siqi Zhang,Meng Wei,Chenyang Wan,Shaohao Zhu,Shufan Shen,Xihui Liu,Zhihua Wei,Tai Wang,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 VCN-Bench，用于评估多模态大语言模型能否利用先前视频中的空间信息，完成目标推断与闭环导航；并提出基线方法 MV-DualVLN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准多停留在离线预测，导航基准则常将空间推理与指令跟随、探索混合评估，因而难以判断模型能否将既有视觉经验用于连续交互，以及失败发生在哪个环节。

Method: 基于 Matterport3D 构建 VCN-Bench，包含五类指令、10 万个训练回合和 1,250 个评测回合。智能体根据覆盖起点和目的地的先前视频，推断指令指定的目标并导航。基准以导航为主要指标，并通过目标识别诊断任务区分目标解析错误与后续导航失败。另提出规划导向基线 MV-DualVLN，联合利用先前视频和当前回合观察。

Result: 实验显示，模型整体导航表现有限；从目标识别到实际导航存在明显性能落差，即使目标识别正确，导航仍经常失败。

Conclusion: 将视频中的空间理解迁移到闭环导航仍具挑战。VCN-Bench 通过拆分目标识别与导航评估，揭示了目标推断和执行规划两阶段的能力缺口，可用于更细致地诊断相关模型。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is fundamental to embodied agents, yet it remains unclear whether spatial understanding can be carried forward to guide sequential interactions. Existing spatial-reasoning benchmarks typically terminate at offline predictions, while navigation benchmarks evaluate spatial reasoning as part of instruction following and exploration. We introduce VCN-Bench, a \textbf{V}ideo-\textbf{C}ontextualized \textbf{N}avigation benchmark for probing closed-loop spatial reasoning over prior visual experience in MLLMs. Given a prior video covering both the initial location and destination, the agent is tasked with reasoning out the instruction-specified target and navigating toward it with the inferred spatial context. Built on Matterport3D, VCN-Bench contains five instruction types, 100k training episodes, and 1,250 evaluation episodes. Navigation serves as the primary evaluation, while diagnostic goal identification helps distinguish destination-resolution errors from subsequent navigation failures. We further propose MV-DualVLN, a planning-oriented baseline that jointly leverages prior video and in-episode observations. Experiments reveal limited navigation performance, a substantial destination-resolution-to-navigation gap, and frequent navigation failures even after correct destination identification.

</details>


### [193] [Calibrated Uncertainty for Informative Path Planning in Aquatic Environmental Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34577)
*Samuel Yanes Luis,Alejandro Casado Pérez,Alejandro Mendoza Barrionuevo,Dame Seck Diop,Sergio Toral Marín,Saniel Gutiérrez Reina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，针对油污扩散重建，深度集成模型比高斯过程更准确；在其可靠的不确定性估计下，多步规划优于贪心策略，而蒙特卡洛树搜索兼具效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息路径规划依赖预测不确定性选择采样位置。高斯过程的平稳各向同性核难以刻画油污等非均质现象，可能导致不确定性校准失准并损害规划效果，因此研究替换模型及其与规划算法的交互影响。

Method: 使用基于物理模拟的油污扩散数据训练深度集成模型，并比较五种规划策略：ε-贪心、价值贪心、不确定性贪心、蒙特卡洛树搜索和滚动时域定向规划；在留出的随机扩散场景上与高斯过程基线进行评估。

Result: 深度集成模型相较高斯过程将归一化重建误差降低83%。模型不确定性校准良好时，不同规划算法的表现差距显著，多步前瞻规划较贪心策略最多降低32%的重建误差，交并比（IoU）超过0.85。蒙特卡洛树搜索的重建质量与定向规划相当，计算成本约低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 提升不确定性估计质量不仅能改善重建，也会凸显规划策略选择的重要性。研究推荐蒙特卡洛树搜索，因为它在保持高重建质量的同时显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Informative Path Planning for scalar field reconstruction uses predictive uncertainty to direct sensing vehicles toward maximally informative locations. Gaussian Processes provide this signal but their stationary isotropic kernels are misspecified for non-homogeneous phenomena such as oil spills, producing miscalibrated estimates that degrade planning. We investigate whether replacing the Gaussian Process with a well-calibrated Deep Ensemble improves path planning outcomes, and whether uncertainty quality interacts with the choice of planning algorithm. Five strategies ($\epsilon$-Greedy, Value Greedy, Uncertainty Greedy, Monte Carlo Tree Search, and Receding Horizon Orienteering) share a common Deep Ensemble backbone trained on physics-based oil spill simulations. On held-out stochastic spill scenarios, the Deep Ensemble reduces normalised reconstruction error by $83\%$ relative to the Gaussian Process baseline. Crucially, well-calibrated uncertainty amplifies the importance of the planning strategy: the performance gap between algorithms is negligible under miscalibrated models but becomes substantial under the ensemble, where multi-step lookahead planners outperform greedy selection by up to $32\%$ in reconstruction error and achieve IoU above $0.85$. Monte Carlo Tree Search is the recommended planner, matching Orienteering in reconstruction quality at an order-of-magnitude lower computational cost.

</details>


### [194] [Diffusion Subgoal Planning for Long-Horizon Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34575)
*Hengrui Zhang,Yuhu Cheng,C. L. Philip Chen,Xuesong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSP 使用扩散生成模型进行离线目标条件强化学习中的高层子目标规划，减少对不稳定价值估计的依赖，并在多种导航与操作任务上优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程离线目标条件强化学习面临稀疏奖励与折扣效应，导致价值函数难以提供稳定的规划指导。层级方法虽通过子目标分解缓解问题，但高层决策仍容易受到噪声敏感的价值估计影响。

Method: 提出扩散子目标规划（DSP），将高层规划建模为对子目标的引导式生成推断，同时学习条件流与无条件流，并在推理时使用无分类器引导注入目标导向偏置。该方法以生成模型产生可达且朝向目标的子目标，保留层级执行机制，并移除高层规划中的显式价值引导。

Result: 在离线目标条件强化学习基准测试中，DSP 在多种导航和操作任务上优于先前方法；在需要多步子目标规划的迷宫任务中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 生成式子目标规划为离线目标条件强化学习提供了一种减少价值估计依赖的高层规划方案，尤其适用于复杂、长时程任务。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) learns goal-directed policies from reward-free data, but in long-horizon tasks, goal-conditioned value functions often provide unstable guidance due to sparse rewards and discounting. Hierarchical methods partially mitigate this issue via subgoal decomposition; however, high-level decision-making still relies on noise-sensitive value estimates, leading to unstable behavior in complex environments. We address this limitation by proposing \textbf{D}iffusion \textbf{S}ubgoal \textbf{P}lanning (\textbf{DSP}), a diffusion-based framework for high-level subgoal generation. DSP casts high-level planning as guided generative inference over goal-conditioned subgoals and learns both conditional and unconditional flows, enabling classifier-free guidance to introduce a goal-directed bias at inference time. By removing explicit value-based guidance from high-level planning, DSP generates reachable and goal-directed subgoals through a generative model while retaining hierarchical execution. Experiments on offline GCRL benchmarks demonstrate that DSP outperforms prior methods on a range of navigation and manipulation tasks, with particularly strong performance in maze environments that require multi-step subgoal planning.

</details>


### [195] [SGG-ReflAct: Sub-Goal Guided ReflAct with Structured Planning for Reliable Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34548)
*Jaeho Jung,Sung Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SGG-ReflAct 将显式子目标融入反思流程，提升了 LLM 智能体在长时程任务中的可靠性；效果取决于计划质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理任务变长，智能体内部信念不一致会导致中间步骤出错并偏离目标。现有 ReflAct 主要围绕最终目标反思，未显式处理各步子目标。

Method: 提出 SGG-ReflAct：由单路径 LLM 规划器生成子目标，并将其整合进反思过程；进一步提出 BeamSGG-ReflAct，以基于束搜索的 LLM 规划器探索结构化计划。在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 和 Jericho 上使用多个 LLM 进行评估。

Result: 在几乎所有设置中，SGG-ReflAct 均优于 ReflAct。使用 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 时，ALFWorld 和 ScienceWorld 的成功率最高分别提升 14.9 和 8.0 个百分点。分析显示，该方法减少了幻觉动作，在具有程序化顺序要求的任务上收益最大。BeamSGG-ReflAct 的表现受计划质量影响；仅进行计划搜索不足以取得同等收益，明确列出所需操作则能恢复收益。

Conclusion: 显式子目标引导的反思是一种易于集成且有效的推理增强方法，可改善复杂长时程任务中的智能体表现；高质量、操作明确的计划是取得稳定收益的关键。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning backbones have empowered large language model (LLM)agentstotackle complex, multi-step tasks. However, as reasoning horizons grow, inconsistent internal beliefs induce intermediate errors that cause agents to drift from their goals. This limitation also persists in REFLACT, which reflects only on the end-goal at each step without explicitly considering intermediate sub goals. To address this problem, we propose SGG-ReflAct (Sub-Goal Guided Re flAct), a reasoning backbone that integrates sub-goals generated through a single path LLM planner into the reflection process. We further extend this framework to BeamSGG-ReflAct, which replaces the single-path planner with a beam search based LLM planner for structured plan exploration. We run experiments on ALF World, ScienceWorld, and Jericho with multiple LLM models. SGG-ReflAct out performs REFLACT in nearly all settings, achieving best success rate gains of 14.9 percentage points on ALFWorld and 8.0 percentage points on ScienceWorld with Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. Our experimental analysis shows that SGG-ReflAct re duces hallucinated actions and achieves its largest gains on procedurally ordered tasks. Furthermore, experimental results with BeamSGG-ReflAct show that the backbone's effectiveness depends on plan quality: explicitly specifying the re quired operations recovers gains that plan searching alone cannot achieve. These results demonstrate that SGG-ReflAct offers a practical and highly effective rea soning backbone, enabling LLM agents to achieve reliable performance in com plex, long-horizon tasks through easy integration.

</details>


### [196] [SemRD-V2X: Closure-Guided Communication with Bounded Inference for Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34353)
*Hu Xu,Chun Li,Siyuan Qiu,Zeyan Li,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出闭合保真视角及 SemRD-V2X，在大幅压缩协同感知特征通信量的同时提升检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远端特征可能重复自车观测已能推断的上下文。现有方法多以经验方式优化掩码或编码，尚未明确接收端已有观测条件下哪些远端证据不可或缺。

Method: 在有限演绎抽象及明确条件下，分析闭合保真率失真函数，证明其可按不可约核心分解，且精确零失真率为 P_A H(π_A)。据此设计 SemRD-V2X：精确预算的 BEV 支持选择、逐点信道压缩，以及融合前的掩码共享权重重建。

Result: 在 V2XSet 和 DAIR-V2X 上进行实验。受控的五次运行比较中，相较于本地复现的 V2X-ViT-v1 基线，SemRD-V2X 将分析特征负载降低 26.6 倍，AP@0.5 和 AP@0.7 分别提高 4.13 和 8.57 个百分点，平均计算延迟额外增加 3.81%。

Conclusion: 闭合保真既可用于分析协同感知所需的最小远端证据，也可指导实际设计：传输紧凑证据，并由接收端以有界推理恢复可推导上下文。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) cooperative perception improves 3-D detection by sharing intermediate features, but dense remote features may repeat context that the ego agent can infer locally. Most communication-efficient designs optimize masks or codes empirically, leaving a more basic question open: which remote evidence is indispensable given the receiver's own observation? We introduce a closure-fidelity perspective on ego conditioned remote perception. Under a finite deductive abstraction and explicit conditions, its rate--distortion function decomposes over an irredundant core, and the exact zero-distortion rate becomes $P_A H(\pi_A)$. This analysis suggests a concrete design principle: transmit compact evidence and recover derivable context with bounded receiver-side inference. Guided by this principle, SemRD-V2X is an operational neural proxy that combines exact-budget BEV support selection, pointwise channel compression, and masked shared-weight reconstruction before standard fusion. Experiments on simulated V2XSet and real-world DAIR-V2X validate the resulting design. In a controlled five-run V2XSet comparison against a locally reproduced V2X-ViT-v1 baseline on one Tesla V100, SemRD-V2X reduces the analytical feature payload by $26.6\times$ while improving AP@0.5/AP@0.7 by 4.13/8.57 points, with 3.81\% additional mean compute latency. These results position closure fidelity as both an analytical lens and an actionable design principle for communication-efficient cooperative perception.

</details>


### [197] [ControlScope: Workflow Revision and Reliability in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34313)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ControlScope 比较智能体在工作流执行中保留现有代码、修改下一次工具调用参数或替换未完成工作流的效果。更广泛的修复权限并不稳定地提升成功率，实际收益取决于智能体选择及后续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作流出错或需要调整时，智能体应获得多大范围的修复权限尚不明确；权限范围、智能体实际采取的修复，以及修复后的执行结果需要分别考察。

Method: 在文件系统任务、ALFWorld 和 AppWorld 上比较 KEEP（继续原方案）、ARG（修改下一次调用的数据参数）和 FULL（替换未完成工作流）。评估一次及重复审查，并结合冻结轨迹回放、中途审查离线比较、五次调用保护和参数编辑捷径分析。

Result: 文件系统任务中，部分设置下 FULL 完成15–16项，KEEP完成13项；另一些快速审查设置中，FULL完成10–13项，低于KEEP的13项。ALFWorld各条件得分接近；AppWorld官方测试集的总体差异较小。回放显示，后续审查可能中断原本可行的替代方案；中途审查也可能促成修复。五次调用保护减少19.4%的模型输出，但在20次新运行中少成功1次。参数编辑捷径则表明，较宽泛的策略可能错过更经济且可行的修改。

Conclusion: 扩大修复权限本身不足以保证性能提升。评估工作流修复时，应关注智能体实际选择、修复的成本及后续审查对执行的影响；不同任务和审查设置下的收益并不一致。

Abstract: How much of a running workflow should a language model agent revise? ControlScope compares continuing generated code, editing the next tool call's data arguments, and replacing the unfinished workflow from the same public execution state. The nested permissions separate available repairs from the actions an agent selects. We evaluate one-time and repeated reviews across filesystem tasks, ALFWorld, and AppWorld. Across two source programs per task and three reasoning-reviewer draws on 20 filesystem tasks, FULL completes 15-16 tasks versus 13 for KEEP; across four fast draws it completes 10-13 versus 13. Fresh student-record confirmation reproduces a batch-read repair. ALFWorld fast panels yield KEEP/ARG/FULL scores of 85/86/87 on 87 tasks across 52 scenes and 134/134/127 on 134 tasks across four scenes; reasoning on the 87-task cohort also yields 85/86/87 with substantial review cost. An AppWorld V1 official-test panel of 585 task instances from 195 scenario templates shows small net differences. Frozen replays expose viable agent-written replacements interrupted by later revision in two failed file-organization runs. An offline source-trajectory midpoint comparison shows later reviews completing an insufficient repair. Five-call protection saves 19.4% of logged model output and loses one success across 20 fresh source runs. An argument-only shortcut shows that the broader sampled policy can overlook a cheaper successful edit available in both operation sets. These outcomes tie repair access to actual choices and subsequent execution.

</details>


### [198] [A Differentiable Optimization Framework for Registering Sequential Bounding Boxes with Point Cloud Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34103)
*Xuesong Li,Jinguang Tong,Jie Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练数据的联合优化方法，在 LiDAR 序列中同时精修 3D 边界框的几何精度与时间平滑性；其优势取决于目标可见程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐帧配准后再进行轨迹平滑，会使边界框偏离几何最优位置，且平滑过程不会重新优化配准结果，因而难以兼顾高 IoU 与低轨迹粗糙度。

Method: 将时间平滑约束直接纳入配准目标，对所有帧的姿态进行联合优化，并使用 L-BFGS 求解。通过改变目标可见性评估方法边界，同时分析方向对齐项在速度估计不可靠时的影响；另提出无需真值的规则选择时间尺度，以满足粗糙度预算。

Result: 目标观测充分时，联合优化在相同低粗糙度预算下优于两种后处理平滑器，并能在精度相当时将粗糙度相较逐帧配准降低数倍。视角逐渐变为单侧后，优势单调减弱；对于近乎侧视的目标，优势趋近于零。在包含距离相关点云密度与自车运动的射线模拟器中，紧粗糙度预算下，逐帧配准加后处理平滑的方案略占优势。

Conclusion: 将平滑约束融入联合配准可有效改善观测充分目标的精度—平滑性权衡，但效果受可见性制约。其失效边界与方向对齐项有关：速度估计噪声增大时，该项会导致航向估计不可靠。

Abstract: Refining a sequence of coarse 3D bounding boxes against a LiDAR point-cloud stream demands tracks that are geometrically accurate (high IoU) and temporally coherent (low roughness), preferably without training data. The usual recipe keeps the two concerns apart: register each frame independently, then smooth the trajectory afterwards with a Kalman~RTS or Savitzky--Golay filter. Smoothing displaces boxes from a geometric optimum and never re-optimises, so it trades accuracy for smoothness. We instead fold the temporal smoothness constraint into a training-free registration objective and solve for all poses jointly with L-BFGS. The payoff depends on how well the object is seen. On well-observed tracks it is large: within the low-roughness budget, the joint objective beats both post-hoc smoothers on paired multi-seed statistics and cuts roughness several-fold relative to frame-wise registration at matched accuracy. Treating visibility as an experimental variable exposes the limit. The advantage decays monotonically as views become one-sided, until it is indistinguishable from zero for near-edge-on objects and slightly negative under a ray-cast simulator with range-dependent density and ego motion, where the decoupled pipeline is in fact ahead at tight roughness budgets. We locate that boundary and trace it to one term: orientation alignment ties yaw to the estimated velocity and fails once that estimate is noisy. A ground-truth-free rule can choose the temporal scale and keep every track inside the roughness budget.

</details>


### [199] [Trajectory Unlearning on LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33639)
*Yingdan Shi,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“轨迹级遗忘”问题，旨在让大语言模型智能体停止执行特定的不期望动作轨迹，而不仅是删除其掌握的事实知识。作者提出GiRPO方法，在保持正常任务能力的同时有效遗忘目标轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘研究主要针对事实、隐私或其他知识内容，难以处理自主智能体在长时序任务中重复执行不期望行为的问题。轨迹遗忘关注智能体实际执行的动作，并且动作之间存在序列依赖，不能简单拆分为独立的提示—回答样本。

Method: 提出Group-injected Relative Policy Optimization（GiRPO）。该方法将待遗忘轨迹注入策略 rollout 分组，并为其设置惩罚奖励；同时隔离遗忘轨迹的归一化统计量，从而产生稳定且有界的遗忘信号，避免干扰正常任务轨迹的梯度更新。作者在ALFWorld家居任务和WebShop在线购物任务上构建轨迹遗忘基准，并设计三类指标评估遗忘效果与模型效用。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop实验中，GiRPO能够有效降低目标动作轨迹的复现，同时保持较高的任务成功率。在遗忘质量和任务效用两个方面，均优于现有知识遗忘基线方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹级遗忘是面向自主智能体的重要新问题，与知识遗忘在目标形式和序列结构上存在本质差异。GiRPO为该问题提供了稳定有效的优化方案，能够在减少不期望行为的同时保留智能体的正常任务能力。

Abstract: Existing large language model (LLM) unlearning has focused primarily on removing specific knowledge, such as harmful facts, private data, or copyrighted content. However, as LLMs are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, a fundamental yet overlooked problem emerges: beyond suppressing what an agent knows, an agent should not reproduce undesired behaviors through its action trajectories. In this work, we introduce trajectory-level unlearning, a new problem formulation that targets the removal of specific action trajectories in long-horizon agentic tasks, rather than factual knowledge. We identify two fundamental challenges that distinguish trajectory unlearning from knowledge unlearning: (1) our unlearning target is what the agent \emph{does}, not what it \emph{says}; and (2) trajectories are sequentially dependent action sequences that cannot be decomposed into isolated prompt-response pairs without losing inter-step structure. To address these challenges, we propose Group-injected Relative Policy Optimization (GiRPO), which injects forget trajectories into the policy rollout group with penalized rewards and isolates the normalization statistics, yielding a stable and bounded unlearning signal that does not corrupt gradient updates for normal task trajectories. We construct trajectory unlearning benchmarks from two application scenarios, household tasks (ALFWorld) and online shopping (WebShop), and design three complementary metrics for evaluating forgetting quality and model utility. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that GiRPO effectively unlearns target trajectories while preserving task success rates, outperforming existing knowledge-unlearning baselines on both forgetting quality and task utility.

</details>


### [200] [Nutri-ATLAS: Embodied Agent for Tabulated Lookup and Assistance for Smarter nutrition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32803)
*Uttej Kallakuri,Boxun Hu,Ankur A. Butala,Najim Dehak,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nutri-ATLAS 将营养知识图谱、混合检索、大语言模型与机器人实地感知结合，用于提供更贴合现实食物供给与个人约束的营养辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的营养系统多依赖被动文本交互和静态上下文，面对含糊的食物描述或缺失的营养证据时可靠性不足，也难以依据现实中的食物可得性调整建议。

Method: 系统融合 USDA FoodData Central 与 FoodKG，构建食品—营养知识图谱，并学习 64 维 GATv2 食品和食谱嵌入。共享的图文混合评分机制支持营养信息提取、缺失值补全、替代食品检索和餐食组合；由大语言模型引导的技能接口则支持机器人导航、更新饮食与食品可及性记忆，并通过现场观察为建议提供依据。

Result: 在 HealthyFoodSubs 上，混合检索取得 37.9% MAP、80.7% RR@5 和 90.1% RR@10；在 NutriBench v2 上，Dense+GAT 检索为九种量化 Qwen3.5-9B 配置提供营养估算依据；在 PFoodReQ 上，系统取得 78.8% MAP、83.0% MAR 和 77.5% F1。患者档案研究中，所有选定案例均满足过敏与健康目标约束。

Conclusion: 结果初步表明，将图谱推理、硬件适配的大语言模型与机器人实地证据采集相结合，有助于提升营养辅助的检索表现、个性化约束遵循和现实环境适用性；摘要未提供各项任务的基线对比或显著性检验，效果优势仍需结合完整实验评估。

Abstract: Generative and Agentic IoT systems offer a promising foundation for digital healthcare applications that combine sensing, personalized reasoning, and autonomous interaction in real-world environments. Nutrition assistance is a natural use case, but existing Large Language Model (LLM)-based systems are often limited to passive text interaction and static context, making them unreliable when food descriptions are ambiguous or nutritional evidence is missing. We propose Nutri-ATLAS, an Embodied Agent for Tabulated Lookup and Assistance for smarter nutrition in the real world. It integrates graph-grounded nutrition reasoning, hardware-aware LLM selection, and robot-based evidence acquisition. Nutri-ATLAS builds a unified Food-Nutrient knowledge graph from USDA FoodData Central and FoodKG and learns 64-dimensional GATv2 food and recipe embeddings. A shared hybrid graph-text scoring mechanism supports food nutrition extraction, nutritional gap filling, substitute retrieval, and recipe-level meal composition, while an LLM-guided skill interface navigates landmarks, updates dietary-context and food-accessibility memory, and grounds recommendations in observed food availability. We evaluate Nutri-ATLAS across nutrient estimation, substitution retrieval, recipe recommendation, patient-profile adherence, edge deployment, and real-world embodied execution. On HealthyFoodSubs, the hybrid retriever achieves 37.9% MAP, 80.7% RR@5, and 90.1% RR@10. On NutriBench v2, Dense+GAT retrieval grounds nutrient estimation across nine quantized Qwen3.5-9B configurations. On PFoodReQ, Nutri-ATLAS reaches 78.8% MAP, 83.0% MAR, and 77.5% F1. A patient-profile study shows adherence to allergy and healthy-target constraints for all selected cases.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Scripted Search: Sample-Efficient Reward Discovery via Agentic Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32394)
*Minghao Li,Rui Tan,Ruihang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARBO 利用具备持久工作区的 LLM 智能体，根据奖励函数评估历史动态制定搜索策略，在固定评估预算下提升多个控制任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低层强化学习的奖励函数设计困难；现有 LLM 搜索方法通常需对每个候选奖励函数完整训练策略，导致复杂任务中的评估成本高、样本效率低。

Method: 提出 Agentic Reward Black-box Optimization（ARBO）。评估历史包含评估器记录的候选得分、各奖励项训练曲线和错误回溯，以及智能体维护的诊断与后续计划。智能体通过工具查询这些信息，并据此生成下一批奖励函数候选，而非依赖固定提示一次性生成。

Result: 在四类控制领域的共同评估预算下，相较基线平均表现，ARBO 的操作成功率提升 29.9%，电网得分提升 192.8%。消融实验考察了各组成部分的贡献及其对基础模型选择的敏感性。

Conclusion: 将评估记录与智能体维护的判断结合，可使奖励函数搜索随训练反馈自适应调整，并在有限评估预算下改善控制任务表现。

Abstract: Designing dense reward functions for low-level reinforcement learning (RL) control remains difficult. Recent work uses large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate and refine reward functions using policy-training feedback within scripted search algorithms. However, evaluating each candidate requires a full RL training run, making sample efficiency a central challenge for reward search on complex control tasks. To address this limitation, we propose an Agentic Reward Black-box Optimization (ARBO) framework, in which an LLM agent builds the search strategy at run time from an evaluation history maintained as its persistent workspace. The evaluation history comprises two components: observations maintained by the evaluation oracle, including candidate scores, per-term training curves, and error tracebacks; and an agent-maintained belief that records diagnoses and intended next steps. The agent queries both with tools and generates the next batch of reward candidates, rather than generating them in a single pass from a fixed prompt. Across four control domains, ARBO achieves gains of 29.9% in manipulation success rate and 192.8% in power-grid score over baseline means under a shared evaluation budget. Ablations examine each component's contribution and sensitivity to backbone choice.

</details>


### [202] [RAO-Nav: Probing Omni-Language Models for Zero-shot Semantic Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32224)
*Qilang Ye,Meng Liu,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAO-Nav，使全能语言模型能够在无需训练数据的情况下执行零样本语义音视频导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专用模型即使经过大量任务训练，在通用多模态导航方面仍表现受限。研究旨在检验全能语言模型能否直接用于语义音视频导航，并发挥其隐含的音视频知识与推理能力。

Method: 构建 RAO-Nav 部署流程，使具身智能体能够感知声音与视觉信息、进行推理并采取行动；同时提出可在推理时集成至解码空间的潜在导航推理模块（LNR），以引导模型获取更相关的目标观测并改善导航决策。

Result: 在公开语义音视频导航基准上的实验显示，RAO-Nav 无需使用训练数据，即超越现有先进基线。论文还提出全局导航指令设置，用于进一步评估全能语言模型作为具身导航智能体的能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，全能语言模型结合测试时推理模块，可作为零样本语义音视频导航方案；其在新设定下的表现也为评估通用具身导航能力提供了补充。

Abstract: We explore whether Omni-Language Models (OLMs) can be directly applied to zero-shot Semantic Audio-Visual Navigation (SAVN). Recent work demonstrates that even state-of-the-art specialized models still struggle to achieve generalist multimodal navigation, despite extensive task-specific training. In this paper, we introduce RAO-Nav, short for Reasoning All-in-One OLM, a deployment pipeline for zero-shot SAVN. By leveraging the rich implicit audio-visual knowledge encoded in OLMs, the embodied agent is enabled to ``hear'', ``see'', ``reason'', and ``act'' in the environment. To further elicit the built-in thinking ability of OLMs, we propose a test-time Latent Navigation Reasoning (LNR) module that can be seamlessly integrated into the decoding space. LNR encourages the model to retrieve more target-relevant observations and make effective navigation decisions. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that our framework surpasses existing state-of-the-art baselines on public SAVN benchmarks without using any training data. Moreover, we introduce a new \emph{Global Navigation Instruction} setting to further evaluate the ability of OLMs to serve as embodied navigation agents. Code: this https URL \_Nav.

</details>


### [203] [DriveHierarchy: A Benchmark for Diagnosing VLM Driving Capabilities from Open-Loop Understanding to Closed-Loop Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31814)
*Chengkai Xu,Jiaqi Liu,Yicheng Guo,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DriveHierarchy 是面向视觉语言模型（VLM）自动驾驶的分层评测框架，结合大规模开放环问答基准与闭环模拟评测，用于分析和改进模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多侧重开放环理解或闭环驾驶，缺少对驾驶能力结构、能力间关系及模型诊断方式的系统刻画。

Method: 将自动驾驶能力划分为感知定位、上下文记忆、心智推理和闭环执行四个层级；整合多个开源数据集，构建包含76,798组问答、覆盖84,279帧的开放环基准，并基于真实道路网络搭建交互式闭环模拟平台，筛选100个驾驶场景进行具身评测。

Result: 对15个VLM的实验表明，该基准能够呈现结构化且非冗余的能力差异，揭示开放环理解与闭环驾驶之间的关联，并支持模型诊断及基准引导的优化。

Conclusion: DriveHierarchy为VLM自动驾驶提供了统一的分层评测框架，可用于系统评估能力、定位短板并指导改进。

Abstract: Evaluating VLM-based autonomous driving remains difficult because driving competence is composite, where a capable system must ground traffic participants and hazards, integrate context across views and time, reason about future evolution, and act appropriately under closed-loop interaction. Existing benchmarks usually assess either open-loop understanding or closed-loop driving but provide limited structure for explaining how these abilities are organized, how they relate, and how they may inform model diagnosis and improvement. We present \textsc{DriveHierarchy}, a hierarchical benchmark that organizes VLM-based autonomous driving into four ranks, spanning perceptual grounding, contextual memory, mental reasoning, and closed-loop execution. To instantiate this hierarchy, we integrate multiple open-source autonomous-driving datasets into a unified open-loop benchmark with 76,798 question-answer pairs over 84,279 frames and develop a closed-loop simulation platform with interactive scenario construction on a real-world road network, from which 100 driving scenarios are curated for embodied evaluation. Experiments on 15 VLMs show that \textsc{DriveHierarchy} captures structured but non-redundant capability variation, relates open-loop understanding to closed-loop driving, and provides a practical basis for diagnosis and benchmark-guided optimization. \textsc{DriveHierarchy} therefore serves as a unified framework for evaluating and improving VLM-based autonomous driving systems. An anonymized project has been released on this https URL

</details>


### [204] [Working with AI: A Design Framework for Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31793)
*Yuqian Lu,Regina Lee,Rui Zhou,Lixin Jiang,Andrew McDaid,Amy Lawrence*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向工业场景的人机协作设计框架，并通过协作装配系统中的协作机器人案例展示其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在工业中的应用日益增多，但有效采用不仅取决于技术能力，也取决于人们如何体验并与系统协作。因此，需要系统性指导，确保人机协作同时满足个人与组织需求。

Method: 构建涵盖人、人工智能系统、任务、组织及更广泛社会环境的实践框架，阐述有效协作的特征、影响条件、设计要求与组织决策；再以协作装配系统案例将设计要求转化为具体协作功能，并开展案例评估。

Result: 白皮书提供了人机协作设计框架及其工业应用示例，说明如何从设计要求推导协作功能并通过案例进行评估。摘要未报告具体评估数据或量化结果。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在为组织提供清晰、实用的设计指导，支持构建有效、以人为本且负责任的人机协作。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly GenAI, is becoming an increasingly important part of modern work. In industrial settings, AI can support decision-making, automate routine activities, assist humans, and improve productivity. However, successful AI adoption depends on more than what the technology can do. It also depends on how people experience and work with it. This raises an important question: how should human-AI collaboration be designed so that it works well for both people and organisations? This white paper addresses that question by presenting a practical framework for designing human-AI collaboration. The framework considers the human, the AI system, the task, the organisation, and the wider societal environment. It explains what effective collaboration looks like, what conditions influence it, what requirements should be met, and what design decisions organisations should consider. The report also includes a human-AI collaborative assembly system with cobot use case to demonstrate how the framework can be applied in practice. The use case shows how design requirements can be translated into specific collaboration features and evaluated through a case study. The aim of this white paper is to provide a clear and practical guide for designing human-AI collaboration that is effective, human-centred, and responsible.

</details>


### [205] [Prediction Limits and Koopman Closure of Geometry-Induced Soft State Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32652)
*Mohit Kumar,Somayeh Kargaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究由几何结构诱导的软状态抽象何时能够支持准确的有限维线性动力学。核心贡献是构造一个无需拟合预测矩阵、可由独立状态—后继状态样本计算的预测误差下置信界，并分析其与坐标表示误差、动力学闭包及 Koopman 特征函数之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软状态坐标可将复杂状态映射到有限维单纯形，但相同坐标未必保留足够的动力学信息，因此需要判断线性预测误差是否存在不可突破的下限，并区分表示误差与动力学不可预测性。

Method: 使用基于类别特定 Kernel Affine Hull Machine 重构分数构造单纯形值软坐标，以矩阵线性映射预测下一时刻坐标。在给定谱范数约束下，结合后继坐标的类内变化以及软坐标相对独热标签的偏差，推导可计算的最小均方根预测误差下置信界。证明在固定坐标和评估分布下，该证书几乎处处收敛到总体下界；同时利用重构分数间隔控制软分配到硬分配的误差。进一步分析确定性动力学与精确坐标闭包下，闭包矩阵及其转置约化矩阵的特征向量如何诱导 Koopman 与伴随 Koopman 特征函数，并通过四状态构造展示相同软坐标在不同动力学映射下可产生完全不同的闭包性质。

Result: 理论上，任何低于渐近下界的预测容差最终都会被证书判定为不可达到；仅凭软坐标相似性不能保证线性动力学可预测性。四状态例子证明，同一坐标表示可能在一种动力学下实现精确闭包，而在另一种动力学下必然产生正预测误差。Duffing、Van der Pol、CartPole、MountainCar 和 Acrobot 实验比较了直接软坐标预测与状态空间 DMD/EDMD，并报告预测、表示变化和谱性质诊断；但实验未对排除证书本身进行数值评估。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一个连接几何软抽象、线性动力学可预测性和 Koopman 分析的误差认证框架。其主要价值在于：无需训练预测模型即可证明某些误差目标不可达，并明确指出表示质量、类内后继变化、谱约束和动力学闭包共同决定线性抽象的有效性。实验支持方法的诊断价值，但由于未直接数值验证证书，证书的经验紧致性和实际判别能力仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We study when geometry-induced soft state abstractions admit accurate finite-dimensional linear dynamics. Each state is represented by simplex-valued coordinates obtained from class-specific Kernel Affine Hull Machine (KAHM) reconstruction scores, and a matrix is used to predict the next-state coordinates. Our main result is a computable lower confidence bound on the minimum root-mean-square prediction error over all matrices satisfying a prescribed spectral-norm limit. The bound combines within-class variation of successor coordinates with the deviation of soft coordinates from their one-hot reference labels, and can be evaluated from independent state-successor pairs without fitting a prediction matrix. For fixed coordinates and evaluation distribution, the certificate converges almost surely to a population lower bound as the sample size grows; any tolerance below this limit is eventually certified unattainable. Reconstruction-score margins further control the soft-to-hard assignment error. Under deterministic dynamics and exact coordinate closure, eigenvectors of the closure matrix and its reduced transpose induce Koopman and adjoint Koopman eigenfunctions, respectively. A four-state KAHM construction shows that identical soft coordinates can permit exact closure under one dynamics map yet force positive prediction error under another. Experiments on Duffing, Van der Pol, CartPole, MountainCar, and Acrobot compare direct soft-coordinate prediction with state-space DMD/EDMD baselines and report prediction, representation-variation, and spectral diagnostics. The benchmarks assess fitted models but do not numerically evaluate the exclusion certificate.

</details>


### [206] [JRDB-AVR: An Active Visual Reasoning Benchmark for Embodied Agents in Real-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35032)
*Zhixi Cai,Fucai Ke,Sukai Huang,Maria Garcia de la Banda,Peter J. Stuckey,Gholamreza Haffari,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 JRDB-AVR 基准及参考智能体，用于评估具身视觉推理中的主动证据获取与答案依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉推理基准多关注被动观察和最终答案，难以判断智能体是否真正观察到支持答案的对象、时间和视角。

Method: 基于真实 JRDB 机器人数据构建问题，要求智能体按时间戳和视角请求有限观测，并同时评估答案与视觉证据。另提出 JRDB-AVR-Agent，通过显式、观测依据的图结构世界模型进行推理。

Result: 实验显示，现有基线的答案准确率与证据准确率存在明显差距；视觉语言模型可能给出正确但缺乏观测依据的答案。

Conclusion: 具身视觉推理评估应纳入主动证据获取及证据可靠性，而不能仅依据最终答案判断系统表现。

Abstract: In complex embodied visual reasoning scenarios, an agent often has only a limited field of view, and the evidence needed to answer a question may be distributed across time, viewpoint, and interacting objects. A model may therefore give a plausible answer without ever observing the relevant object, time, or view that supports it. Current visual reasoning benchmarks largely evaluate passive observations and final answers, overlooking settings that require active reasoning and evidence acquisition. We introduce JRDB-AVR, a benchmark derived from existing real-world JRDB robotics data through a structured question-generation engine that turns this gap into an explicit evaluation: an embodied agentic system receives a visual reasoning question, requests bounded observations by timestamp and viewing angle, and is evaluated on both the final answer and the grounded visual evidence supporting it. The benchmark contains diverse questions over multiple real-world environments involving temporal search, viewpoint selection, and human-oriented compositional reasoning. We also introduce JRDB-AVR-Agent, a reference active reasoning agentic method that maintains an explicit observation-grounded graph-based world model and answers through solving. Experiments reveal a substantial gap between answer accuracy and evidence accuracy in current baselines, showing that current VLMs can produce unsupported correct answers and that active evidence-aware evaluation is necessary for embodied visual reasoning. Code and benchmark are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [Does Adversarial Training Improve Generalization in Multi-View VLAs? Revealing and Mitigating View Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33707)
*Futa Waseda,Shuhei Kurita,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究多视角视觉-语言-动作模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，发现对抗训练的收益具有选择性，并可能导致模型过度依赖某一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角 VLA 在部署环境变化时性能可能下降。对抗训练能否提升其对自然分布偏移的泛化能力，以及提升是否来自更可靠的视觉感知，仍不明确。

Method: 在由预训练视觉-语言模型直接适配的多视角 VLA 上，评估对抗训练在 LIBERO-Plus 七类偏移下的效果；通过控制视角干预分析视角依赖变化，并引入 View Swap 干预后重新评估对抗训练。

Result: 直接对抗训练显著改善相机视角和传感器噪声偏移，但对其他偏移的效果不一或为负。视角干预揭示了“视角坍塌”：模型可能转而过度依赖腕部视角，造成表面上的鲁棒性提升。结合 View Swap 后，对抗训练还改善了机器人初始状态偏移，但对其他偏移仍无一致收益。

Conclusion: 多视角鲁棒性需要区分单一视角内的稳健感知与跨视角的可靠融合。对抗训练并非通用的分布偏移解决方案，其效果取决于视角依赖如何变化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for closed-loop robot control, transferring their perceptual and semantic capabilities to action prediction. Despite strong in-distribution performance, however, VLAs often degrade under deployment shifts. Adversarial training (AT) offers a model-adaptive approach to robustness without explicitly anticipating individual shifts, but its effect on natural distribution-shift generalization in multi-view VLAs remains unclear. We study this question using a multi-view VLA directly adapted from a pretrained VLM and evaluate generalization across seven LIBERO-Plus shift axes. Direct AT substantially improves Camera Viewpoint and Sensor Noise, the two shifts affecting only the third-person view, yet produces mixed or negative effects on other shifts. Controlled view interventions reveal a surprising failure mode that we term view collapse: Direct AT can shift cross-view reliance so strongly that the policy becomes dominated by the wrist view. This exposes a \textit{robustness shortcut}: apparent robustness to a shifted view can arise from reduced use of that view rather than more robust perception of it. This motivates a distinction between robust perception, extracting reliable information under within-view shifts, and robust fusion, adapting reliance across views according to their reliability. To reduce fixed view reliance, we use a simple View Swap intervention and then re-evaluate AT. With View Swap, AT further improves Camera Viewpoint, Sensor Noise, and Robot Initial State, while its effects remain mixed on other shifts. Our results show that multi-view robustness requires separating improved perception from changes in cross-view reliance, and that AT provides selective rather than generic distribution-shift benefits.

</details>


### [208] [PlanGuard: A Guardrail for Multi-Step Plan Safety in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32801)
*Junchi Chen,Changtao Miao,Yuxiao Xiang,Zhenchao Jin,Haojie Yuan,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,He Liu,Bo Zhang,Jiansheng Cai,Zhe Li,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PlanGuard，在执行前评估多步骤具身计划的整体物理安全性；PlanGuard-2B 平均准确率为 87.15%，F1 为 87.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防护机制主要关注语义危害，或孤立评估单个子任务，难以识别子任务依赖关系及其与环境交互共同产生的整体物理风险。

Method: 构建多步骤计划安全数据集 MSP-Safe，并提出 STAC-OPD：沿紧凑模型的策略轨迹提供自适应强教师监督，结合词元级分布迁移与按概率路由的序列级补偿。

Result: 在所有测试子集上，PlanGuard-2B 的平均准确率达到 87.15%，平均 F1 达到 87.21%，表明紧凑模型能够有效检测完整计划的物理风险。

Conclusion: PlanGuard 将安全评估从单个子任务扩展到结合当前环境的完整多步骤计划；STAC-OPD 有助于缩小紧凑模型与大型模型之间的能力差距。

Abstract: Embodied task planners may produce multi-step plans whose subtask dependencies and interactions with the environment create physical risks during execution. Yet existing safeguards overlook such compositional risks, as general-purpose guardrails focus on semantic harm and embodied safety detectors assess subtasks in isolation. To address this gap, we introduce PlanGuard, the first pre-execution detector that evaluates the physical safety of a complete multi-step plan in its current environment. For training and evaluation, we construct a Multi-Step Plan Safety (MSP-Safe) dataset through paired task construction, plan generation using diverse planners, and safety annotation by three judges. Task-oriented SFT on MSP-Safe establishes fundamental plan-safety assessment capabilities, yet a substantial gap remains between compact models suitable for real-time deployment and stronger but costlier large models. Accordingly, we propose Strong-Teacher Adaptive Compensation for On-Policy Distillation (STAC-OPD), which provides compact models with adaptive strong-teacher supervision along their on-policy trajectories. It combines token-level distribution transfer from a fine-tuned strong teacher with probability-routed sequence-level compensation, retaining student-generated targets when the student favors the reference safety decision and using teacher-reconstructed targets otherwise. Across all test subsets, PlanGuard-2B achieves average 87.15% ACC and 87.21% F1, demonstrating effective whole-plan physical-risk detection at compact model scale. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [209] [Are Vision-Language-Action Models Robust to One-Step Observation Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32550)
*Shojiro Yamabe,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型对单步观测扰动的鲁棒性，并提出动态调整动作块执行长度的方法 CARE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有安全研究主要关注贯穿整个任务过程的持续扰动；仅在单个时间步短暂发生的严重观测损坏尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 评估每个任务回合中仅施加一次扰动的影响，并据此提出 CARE：根据当前预测动作块与先前动作块的一致性，动态选择执行长度。

Result: 实验显示，单步扰动会显著降低 VLA 模型的表现，影响程度与动作块执行长度有关。CARE 能以较低计算开销提升扰动下的鲁棒性，并通过仅在扰动时缩短执行长度来保持无扰动场景下的性能。

Conclusion: 短暂的单步观测扰动是 VLA 模型的重要鲁棒性挑战；按预测一致性自适应调整执行长度，可在提升抗扰能力的同时减少对正常表现的影响。

Abstract: Understanding the safety risks of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for their deployment in the physical world. Existing safety research has mainly considered persistent perturbations that are applied continuously to observations throughout an episode. However, momentary observation corruption, in which observations are severely perturbed only briefly within an episode, remains an underexplored safety threat. To address this gap, this work investigates robustness to one-step perturbations applied at a single time step per episode. Our experiments reveal that these perturbations substantially degrade VLA performance and that their impact depends on the action chunk execution length. Based on them, we propose CARE, which dynamically selects the execution length based on consistency with the previously predicted action chunk. CARE improves robustness with low computational overhead while preserving clean performance by selecting shorter execution lengths only under perturbations.

</details>


### [210] [Metro-WM: Long-Horizon Latent Planning with Realisable Sub-Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31868)
*Royson Lee,Fady Rezk,Titouan Parcollet,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Metro-WM，一种基于真实历史观测状态检索的分层世界模型规划方法，用于解决JEPAs模型预测控制在长时域规划中存在的子目标不可实现问题。该方法通过构建观测帧图并进行路径搜索，实现了更高的长时域成功率、更快的规划速度和更低的训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于JEPAs的模型预测控制能够进行零样本目标到达，但有效规划范围较短。现有分层方法通常由宏观规划器生成潜在子目标，而这些未受约束的潜在向量可能对应物理上无法实现的状态，从而导致后续微观规划失败。因此，研究旨在以可验证、可执行的真实状态替代无依据的潜在子目标，并提升长时域任务中的鲁棒性。

Method: Metro-WM从离线专家示范或随机动作轨迹中收集真实观测帧，将其作为图中的顶点，并依据状态转移关系构建图结构。规划器通过检索和连接来自不同轨迹的观测帧，生成通往目标的路线；微观规划器负责在相邻子目标之间执行。当执行出现偏差时，系统根据当前状态在完整图上重新搜索最优路径，从而实现在线纠错和跨轨迹路径拼接。

Result: 实验表明，现有先进宏观规划器会频繁产生物理上不可实现的子目标。Metro-WM相较于次优分层方法，长时域成功率最高提升37.33个百分点，速度最高提升10.9倍，离线计算量减少约13至56倍，并且需要调节的超参数更少。此外，Metro-WM生成的路径短于离线示范路径，优于使用查询样本自身示范轨迹的预言机方法，并能在极度稀疏的数据条件下保持较强性能。

Conclusion: 研究说明，直接生成未受约束的潜在子目标会限制分层规划的可靠性。通过从真实历史状态中检索子目标并在状态图上进行全局规划，Metro-WM有效提升了子目标的可执行性、长时域成功率和执行鲁棒性，同时降低了计算与调参成本。该结果支持将基于经验状态的检索式规划作为长时域世界模型控制的重要方向。

Abstract: Model-predictive control with Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provides a strong zero-shot goal-reaching planner, but it is only effective over short planning horizons. Hierarchical extensions attempt to bridge this gap by learning a macro planner to predict intermediate latent sub-goals to guide the micro planner. In this work, we demonstrate that unconstrained latent sub-goal prediction is fundamentally flawed. A rigorous evaluation reveals that a leading state-of-the-art macro planner routinely emits physically unrealisable sub-goals. To resolve this, we introduce Metro-WM, a hierarchical framework that issues sub-goals by retrieving genuine states from prior experience rather than generating ungrounded latent vectors. Specifically, Metro-WM constructs a graph whose vertices are observed frames from offline expert demonstrations or random-action trajectories, allowing frames from different episodes to be connected and stitched into routes to the goal. Planning over the full graph also makes the system highly robust to execution errors: if the micro planner drifts off course, Metro-WM instantly finds a new optimal path from the current state. Our experiments show that Metro-WM achieves superior long-horizon success rates of up to 37.33 percentage points over the next best hierarchical approach while being up to 10.9 times faster, requiring both 13-56 times less offline compute and fewer tuned hyperparameters. Additional analysis reveals that Metro-WM finds shorter paths than the offline demonstrations, outperforms an oracle relying on the query's own demonstration, and maintains robust performance under extremely sparse dataset conditions.

</details>


### [211] [ConflictVLA-Bench: Benchmarking Behavioral Responses of Vision-Language-Action Models to Premise Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31792)
*Liyu Hou,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ConflictVLA-Bench，用于评估视觉-语言-动作模型面对无效任务前提时的行为过程，并发现模型常在任务已无法成立时仍继续执行，呈现“失败持续”（Failed Persistence）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据任务最终成败，无法区分模型是否主动停止，还是继续追逐原目标后因执行错误而失败。因此，有必要同时考察任务结果与执行过程。

Method: 基于 LIBERO 构建包含 2,826 项提示条件冲突任务的基准，覆盖四类冲突、四种结构配置及两种提示条件；将冲突任务与前提一致的参考任务配对，对八种 VLA 模型的结果和动作过程进行评估。

Result: 无效前提使原目标完成率至少下降 17.3 个百分点，OpenVLA 的降幅达 56.2 个百分点。即便在配对的前提一致任务中成功、在冲突任务中失败，模型仍常接近原目标、保留早期轨迹结构，且动作幅度抑制有限。“失败持续”在所有受测模型中反复出现；显式检查前提也未能稳定带来有选择且协调的行为变化。

Conclusion: 任务失败本身不能证明模型已停止执行或拒绝任务，单看最终结果不足以评估 VLA。评估应纳入执行过程，以识别模型在前提冲突下的持续行为。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models perform strongly on manipulation tasks, their responses to invalid task premises remain underexplored. Existing evaluations of premise conflicts often focus on terminal task outcomes, yet task failure alone cannot distinguish behavioral disengagement from continued pursuit followed by an execution error. We call the latter pattern Failed Persistence. To study this phenomenon, we introduce ConflictVLA-Bench, which pairs conflict rollouts with premise-consistent reference rollouts and evaluates both outcomes and execution processes. Built on LIBERO, the benchmark contains 2,826 prompt-conditioned conflict tasks spanning four conflict families, four structural configurations, and two prompt conditions. Across all eight VLAs, invalid premises reduce original goal completion by at least 17.3 percentage points, with the reduction reaching 56.2 percentage points for OpenVLA. Crucially, even when models succeed on premise-consistent tasks and fail on their matched conflict tasks, they often continue to approach the original targets, retain early trajectory structure, and show limited action magnitude suppression. Failed Persistence therefore recurs across the evaluated models. Explicit premise checking does not consistently produce selective and coordinated behavioral changes. These findings show that terminal failure alone establishes neither behavioral disengagement nor refusal and that outcomes alone are insufficient for VLA evaluation. Experimental data and additional details are available on the project page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [X-Reset: Scaling Object-Centric Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Embodiment Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35715)
*Prithwish Dan,Chenyang Ma,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: X-Reset 将人手与物体的演示转化为经筛选的仿真重置状态，帮助强化学习训练仅依赖物体状态和目标的通用灵巧操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零开始训练通用策略时，机器人难以探索出接近、抓取和重定向多种物体的有效行为。现有方法往往依赖高质量机器人演示、逐任务奖励塑形或受限的行为模式。

Method: 对人手—物体状态进行运动学重定向，映射到带噪声的机器人状态；过滤仿真中不稳定的状态，并将其作为强化学习训练的重置分布。策略使用通用的物体中心奖励，演示仅通过重置分布参与训练，而非用于动作模仿或轨迹跟踪。

Result: 在20种物体和三种机器人构型上训练出通用策略：两种不同机械臂搭载的22自由度灵巧手，以及平行夹爪。该方法可随训练物体数量扩展、泛化到未见物体、利用不完美的手部姿态估计进行学习，并支持从仿真到现实的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: X-Reset 以人手—物体演示构造有效的训练重置状态，缓解了灵巧操作强化学习的探索难题，同时保留通用策略的跨物体泛化能力及仿真到现实迁移潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation can train dexterous manipulation policies without robot demonstrations, but training a single generalist policy with task-agnostic rewards faces a severe exploration problem: approaching, grasping, and reorienting diverse objects with many degrees of freedom is difficult to discover from scratch. Prior works make exploration tractable with high-quality robot demonstrations, per-task reward shaping, or by restricting policies to narrow modes of behavior. We propose X-Reset, a framework that instead resolves exploration with human hand-object demonstrations. Rather than imitating or tracking retargeted human motion, X-Reset kinematically retargets hand-object states to noisy robot states, filters out states that are unstable in simulation, and samples the remainder as resets during RL training with general-purpose object-centric rewards. The resulting policy depends only on object state and goal, with demonstrations entering training through the reset distribution. We show that X-Reset trains generalist policies on 20 objects across three embodiments---a 22-DoF hand on two different arms and a parallel-jaw gripper---and resolves the exploration challenges of RL from scratch. X-Reset scales with the number of training objects, generalizes to unseen objects, can learn from imperfect hand-pose estimates, and transfers behaviors zero-shot from sim-to-real.

</details>


### [213] [Behavioral Foundation Models for Quality Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35615)
*Nazim Bendib,Nicolas Perrin-Gilbert,Olivier Sigaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BFM-QD，在行为基础模型（BFM）的紧凑潜在空间中进行质量多样性（QD）搜索，以发现多样且高性能的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 QD 方法通常直接在高维策略参数空间搜索，效率和效果可能受限。研究旨在检验 BFM 学得的潜在行为空间能否成为更有效的搜索空间，并拓展 BFM 在零样本任务求解之外的用途。

Method: 将 QD 搜索从策略参数空间转移至 BFM 潜在空间，并提出一种闭式、无梯度的策略改进算子，用以近似策略梯度更新，无需训练评论家或进行反向传播。

Result: 在涵盖密集运动、稀疏导航和接触丰富操作的连续控制基准上，BFM-QD 整体优于参数空间基线；在稀疏且具有欺骗性的任务中，优势尤其明显，而所有测试的参数空间 QD 方法均接近零性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，潜在空间降维与多样化行为数据的离线预训练相结合，能够提升 QD 搜索效果。BFM 可作为通用的 QD 优化骨干，用于发现多样化行为库。

Abstract: Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) are an emerging paradigm in reinforcement learning, playing a role analogous to large language models in natural language processing: they have shown remarkable versatility, enabling zero-shot performance, fast imitation, and online adaptation, all by exploiting the structure of a latent space. In this work, we investigate whether the latent behavioral space induced by BFMs can serve as an effective search space to discover large repertoires of behaviorally diverse and high-performing policies through Quality-Diversity (QD) methods. While QD methods generally search directly in high-dimensional policy parameter space, in this paper, we present BFM-QD, a framework that performs QD search in the compact latent space of a BFM. We further show that the BFM-QD framework provides a closed-form, gradient-free policy improvement operator that approximates a policy gradient update, but requires no critic training and no backpropagation. Across continuous-control benchmarks spanning dense locomotion, sparse navigation, and contact-rich manipulation, BFM-QD consistently outperforms parameter-space baselines, with particularly stark gains in sparse and deceptive settings, where all tested parameter-space QD methods collapse to near-zero performance. These results show the effectiveness of the BFM-QD framework, benefiting from the synergy between dimensionality reduction of the search space and offline pretraining from diverse behavioral data. This positions BFMs as a general-purpose backbone for QD optimization, extending their utility beyond zero-shot task solving to the discovery of diverse behavioral repertoires.

</details>


### [214] [Control-Geometry Straightening for Sampling-Based Latent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35603)
*Ziang Fu,Ning Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出控制几何拉直（CGS），通过辅助损失使潜在空间中的运动结构更适合规划，从而以更少采样和优化步骤提升控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型即使能准确预测状态转移，也不一定能让规划目标易于优化；在候选采样和计算预算有限时，寻找优质动作序列仍然困难。

Method: CGS利用像素—动作对中的局部转移，匹配动作之间的成对余弦相似度与相应潜在状态差之间的相似度，以拉直控制几何。该损失可用于多种世界模型架构及端到端学习或预训练的表征。在线性动力学假设下，理论分析将其与时间拉直、规划全时域内更均衡的终端代价曲率联系起来，并推导MPPI有限预算保证、CEM局部收缩结果及梯度下降收敛界。

Result: 在四种控制环境和多种规划器上的实验显示，CGS能以更少候选样本和细化步骤改善规划表现。每次更新使用128个候选样本时，成功率较LeWorldModel提高最高20个百分点，较其时间拉直变体提高最高12.6个百分点。探针实验、与DINO-WM架构的比较及规划器消融进一步分析了潜在运动组织、状态依赖性和动力学上下文的作用。

Conclusion: 拉直潜在空间中的控制几何有助于降低规划优化难度，使规划器在有限预算下更容易找到优质动作序列。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures enable planning with latent world models, but accurate transition prediction alone does not ensure that the planning objective is easy to optimize. We introduce Control-Geometry Straightening (CGS), a single auxiliary loss that learns planner-friendly representations by directly straightening control geometry for sampling-efficient planning. CGS matches pairwise cosine similarities among actions to those among corresponding latent differences only using local transitions from pixel-action pairs. The loss can be applied across world-model architectures using end-to-end learned or pretrained representations. Under linear-dynamics, our theoretical analysis connects this objective to temporal straightening and more balanced terminal-cost curvature across the full planning horizon, yielding finite-budget guarantees for MPPI, local contraction results for CEM, and convergence bounds for gradient descent. Across four control environments and multiple planners, CGS improves planning with fewer sampled candidates and refinement steps, achieving success-rate gains up to 20 and 12.6 percentage points over LeWorldModel (LeWM) and its temporal-straightening variant (LeWM+TS), respectively, with sampling-based planners using 128 candidates per update. Probes, comparisons with DINO-WM architecture, and planner-side ablations clarify how latent motion organization, state dependence, and dynamical context shape planning behavior. Straightening control geometry thus makes good action sequences easier to find under limited planning budgets.

</details>


### [215] [Teacher-Student Gaps Are Not Enough: Outcome-Guided On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35319)
*Tong Zhang,Zhou Liu,Yihao Liu,Jiahua Bao,Xuchen Li,Honglin Lin,Tao Cheng,Zhihan Yu,Kai Tang,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结果引导的 on-policy 蒸馏方法 OG-OPD，根据教师指导对学生最终任务表现的实际帮助调整监督权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将较大的师生 token 分布差异视为优先干预信号，但局部差异不一定意味着教师指导有益：学生可能偏离教师仍能成功，也可能采纳教师偏好后反而无法完成任务。

Method: OG-OPD 对教师监督进行相对于学生轨迹的加权，并利用成对学生续接的最终任务结果校准权重，从而强化教师指导确实能改善当前学生表现的决策点。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 和 WebShop 上，OG-OPD 在多种设置中均优于基线；任务成功率较 vanilla OPD 提高 3.6–17.7 个百分点，较最强基线最高提高 7.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 教师监督的价值应由其对当前学生最终任务结果的影响决定，而非仅由局部师生分布差异决定。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with dense teacher supervision. Recent work on OPD for multi-turn autonomous agents often treats large teacher-student token-level distributional gaps as promising intervention points, linking larger gaps to a greater need for correction. Yet, our empirical analysis reveals a supervision-benefit mismatch: large gaps can be benign, while small gaps can be outcome-critical. Teacher-student gaps capture differences at the current turn, whereas the benefit of teacher guidance depends on how the current student interacts with the environment afterward. The student may still succeed despite choosing an action that differs from the teacher's, while a teacher-preferred action may lead to a state from which the student cannot complete the task. Local gaps alone are therefore not enough to determine whether teacher guidance benefits the current student. Effective supervision should instead emphasize guidance that the current student can translate into better final task outcomes. Accordingly, we propose Outcome-Guided On-Policy Distillation (OG-OPD), which applies trajectory-relative weighting to teacher supervision and calibrates these weights using final task outcomes from paired student continuations. This calibration selectively strengthens supervision on the student's original trajectories at turns where teacher guidance benefits the current student. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and WebShop, OG-OPD consistently outperforms baselines under diverse settings. It improves task success rates by 3.6-17.7 percentage points over vanilla OPD and by up to 7.0 percentage points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [216] [SpikeCredit: Temporal Credit Carrier for Reinforcement Learning with Sparse Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35268)
*Yingchao Yu,Pengfei Sun,Wenxuan Pan,Wei Chen,Yitian Hong,Kuangrong Hao,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpikeCredit利用脉冲神经网络的时间动态保存并传递信用相关信息，提升稀疏奖励强化学习表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励的延迟反馈难以指出哪些中间计算促成了成败，因此需要能随时间保留并显露信用信息的策略动态。

Method: 提出时间信用载体（TCC）框架，并构建SpikeCredit：先自适应选择TCC，再通过快速读取通路结合自运动反馈约束恢复转移级信用，并由慢速写入通路将信用对齐结果反馈至策略，以改善后续TCC动态的可读性。

Result: 在稀疏奖励MuJoCo任务中，相较稀疏奖励SNN基线，Last10回报在Ant、Hopper、Swimmer和Walker2d上分别提升1169%、953%、723%和1781%；在Swimmer上还比密集奖励基线高113%。机制分析显示，其与密集奖励的对齐程度显著强于稀疏奖励SNN基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持将脉冲神经动态视为稀疏奖励强化学习中保留信用信息的有效载体。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse rewards is challenging because delayed outcomes provide little guidance about which intermediate computations caused success or failure. We argue that reliable credit assignment requires policy dynamics that preserve and expose credit-relevant information over time, a role we formalize as Temporal Credit Carriers (TCCs) and that spiking neural networks (SNNs) naturally fulfill through graded membrane traces and event-driven spikes. Based on this hypothesis, we propose SpikeCredit, an SNN-based framework for RL with sparse rewards that first performs task-adaptive TCC selection and then closes the loop between a fast TCC-reading pathway, where self-motion feedback constraint uses local behavior-grounded cues to constrain transition-level credit recovery, and a slow TCC-writing pathway, where credit-targeted trace alignment feeds recovered credit back into the actor to make future TCC dynamics more credit-readable. Across sparse-reward MuJoCo tasks, SpikeCredit improves Last10 return over sparse SNN baselines by +1169% on Ant, +953% on Hopper, +723% on Swimmer, and +1781% on Walker2d, and exceeds the dense-reward baseline on Swimmer by +113%. Mechanistic analyses further show substantially stronger alignment with dense rewards than the sparse SNN baseline. These results position spiking dynamics as credit-preserving substrates for sparse-reward RL.

</details>


### [217] [FlexiWorld: Learning and Planning via Flexible Action Chunks Across Multiple Time Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35138)
*Shidu Ren,Qilin Gu,Zhenghao Ni,Junhan Sun,Jiaqi Wang,Damien Scieur,Yunze Liu*
