Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexiWorld结合混合跨度目标监督、可变长度动作块与ARCEM规划，提升了长时程控制效果和规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型常采用固定长度动作块，且目标条件动作生成缺失或仅受短跨度目标监督，限制了长时程规划能力。

Method: 训练时采样不同目标跨度，并将动作随机划分为可变长度块；联合训练世界模型、因果动作编码器和自回归动作生成器。Student Forcing通过生成的动作前缀训练以减轻暴露偏差。规划时采用ARCEM，结合动作残差搜索、块内自回归反馈与块边界潜在状态预测。

Result: 在四个基准及不同目标距离上，FlexiWorld与ARCEM的平均成功率为89.29%，高于最强基线的83.98%。PushT消融实验显示，混合跨度监督、可变长度动作块和Student Forcing均有助于直接控制。无需重新训练，较长规划块可使ARCEM平均加速约1.3倍，同时保持相近的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 结合灵活的目标跨度与动作块长度，并采用适配块内反馈的规划方法，有望改善长时程控制表现及规划效率。

Abstract: Latent world models predict future states for goal-directed planning using action chunks spanning multiple primitive steps. Existing methods typically use fixed-length chunks and either omit goal-conditioned action generation or limit their supervision to short goal spans. We introduce FlexiWorld, a JEPA-based world model that combines mixed-span goal supervision with variable-length action chunks to improve long-horizon control. During training, we sample varying goal spans and randomly partition the actions into variable-length chunks. We jointly train the world model with a causal action encoder that embeds variable-length chunks and an autoregressive actor that generates primitive actions sequentially. Student Forcing reduces exposure bias by training on generated action prefixes. For planning, Actor-Residual Cross-Entropy Method (ARCEM) combines action-residual search with within-chunk autoregressive feedback and chunk-boundary latent prediction. Across four benchmarks and goal distances, FlexiWorld with ARCEM achieves 89.29% mean success, compared with 83.98% for the strongest baseline. PushT ablations show improved direct control from mixed-span supervision, variable-length chunks, and Student Forcing. Without retraining, FlexiWorld supports different planning chunk lengths: longer chunks accelerate ARCEM by approximately $1.3\times$ on average while maintaining comparable average success.

</details>


### [218] [GraphHCA: Closed-Form Hindsight Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35084)
*Haodong Zhu,Yangyang Ren,Changbai Li,Sheng Xu,Linlin Yang,haiguang liu,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphHCA 是一种无需显式估计事后分布的模型无关信用分配方法，通过状态成功潜势的变化为强化学习轨迹提供逐步奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体任务中，稀疏的终局奖励使逐步信用分配十分重要。现有方法通常依据动作之后的采样结果分配信用，未显式建模动作与最终结果之间的回溯关系；既有事后信用分配方法则需要额外模型或前向计算来估计事后分布。

Method: 针对具有确定性转移的终局目标任务，GraphHCA 利用贝叶斯公式将事后与行为策略的概率比化简为相邻状态下行为策略成功概率之比。取对数后定义状态成功潜势，并以转移前后的潜势差作为逐步信用信号。该潜势通过汇总轨迹形成的转移图及折扣递归估计；递归在任意有向图上均有唯一不动点。最终将逐步信号与轨迹级优势结合；逐步信号权重为零时退化为 GRPO。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop（两种 LLM 规模）以及使用视觉语言智能体的 Sokoban 上，GraphHCA 均取得所比较基线中的最佳结果。ALFWorld 上，其总体成功率较 GRPO 最多提高 24.6 个百分点，较最强逐步信用分配基线最多提高 4.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: GraphHCA 通过转移图上的成功潜势递归，实现了无需学习事后模型或额外前向计算的逐步信用分配，并在多类智能体任务上展现出优于基线的表现。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) has advanced large language models (LLMs) and is increasingly extending to agentic tasks, where sparse terminal rewards make step-level credit assignment essential. Existing methods assign credit from what follows an action in sampled rollouts, but do not explicitly capture its retrospective relation to the realized outcome. Hindsight credit assignment (HCA) instead attributes credit through the ratio of hindsight to behavior-policy probabilities, but estimating the hindsight distribution requires an auxiliary model or an extra pass. To address this estimation bottleneck, we propose GraphHCA, a model-free realization of HCA that eliminates explicit hindsight-distribution estimation. For terminal-goal tasks with deterministic transitions, Bayes' rule reduces the hindsight ratio to a ratio of behavior-policy success probabilities at consecutive states. Taking logs yields a state-wise success potential, whose increment across a transition provides step-level credit. GraphHCA estimates this potential from pooled rollouts through a discounted recursion on the induced transition graph, which admits a unique fixed point on any directed graph. The resulting step-level signal is combined with the trajectory-level advantage, requiring neither a learned hindsight model nor an extra forward pass and recovering GRPO when the step-level weight is zero. Among all compared baselines, GraphHCA achieves state-of-the-art results on ALFWorld and WebShop at both LLM scales, and on Sokoban with a vision-language agent. For example, on ALFWorld it improves overall success rate by up to 24.6 points over GRPO and by up to 4.7 points over the strongest step-level baseline.

</details>


### [219] [Cross-Rollout Bellman Closure for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35082)
*Yangyang Ren,Haodong Zhu,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Peichao Lai,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRBC利用同一任务组内多条轨迹构成的经验过程，通过贝尔曼闭包传播跨轨迹证据，生成逐步信用信号；在多个基准上提升了性能与学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群组式强化学习通常比较同一任务的多条轨迹，但缺少学习型评论家。长时序轨迹会重复访问共享状态，因而可提供跨轨迹的步骤级信用证据。现有局部平均方法无法递归传播证据，而最短路径估计可能让少见路径过度影响状态价值。

Method: CRBC将每组轨迹合并为带有成功、失败吸收边界的有限经验过程，使用一次线性求解计算行为策略下的贝尔曼固定点，并按经验动作与转移频率聚合和传播证据。随后基于观测转移回传动作价值，以动作价值相对状态价值的增益作为步骤级信用，并将归一化信用与轨迹级群组优势结合用于策略优化。其有限深度版本在零深度时退化为访问局部的回报平均，随深度增加收敛至完整闭包。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Sokoban基准及多种模型规模上，CRBC均提升了最终表现和学习效率。以ALFWorld上的Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct为例，其表现比所评估的最强基线高5.59个百分点。

Conclusion: CRBC无需额外环境采样，即可利用群组内轨迹的经验频率进行跨轨迹信用传播，在局部回报平均与可能受稀有路径支配的全局估计之间提供递归式估计，并在所测基准中带来稳定收益。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning such as GRPO trains LLM agents by comparing rollouts sampled for each task, without a learned critic. In long-horizon settings, these rollouts revisit shared anchor states, offering cross-rollout evidence for step-level credit. Ideally, step-level credit should incorporate evidence beyond the realized suffixes observed at an anchor while aggregating alternative continuations according to their empirical frequencies. Visit-local averaging pools realized suffix returns at shared anchors and respects observed frequencies, but does not recursively propagate evidence across rollouts, whereas shortest-path estimators have global reach but allow a rarely observed route to dominate an anchor's value. We introduce Cross-Rollout Bellman Closure (CRBC), which merges each rollout group into a finite empirical process with absorbing success and failure boundaries and evaluates its behavior-policy Bellman fixed point with one linear solve. This fixed point uses the same empirical action and transition frequencies to propagate evidence through shared anchors and aggregate alternative continuations. Backing up the resulting state values through observed transitions yields action values, whose gain over the corresponding state value provides step-level credit. A corresponding finite-depth family recovers visit-local return averaging at zero depth and converges to the exact closure as depth increases. The normalized closure credit is combined with the trajectory-level group advantage for policy optimization, without additional environment rollouts. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and Sokoban benchmarks with multiple model scales, CRBC consistently improves final performance and learning efficiency. For example, CRBC outperforms the strongest evaluated baseline by 5.59 percentage points on ALFWorld with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct.

</details>


### [220] [DivOPD: Spread Wide, Look Close for Asynchronous On-Policy Distillation of Multi-turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34838)
*Hanyang Wang,Zeyuan Liu,Zhengyu Chen,Jingqing Ruan,Chaoxu Pang,Zhongda Su,Wulin Xie,Zhizhao Zeng,Ke Zeng,Tianxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DivOPD通过在固定训练预算下扩大参与训练的轨迹数量，并优先选择师生累计分歧较大的有效交互轮次，提升了异步多轮在策略蒸馏的样本利用率与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步多轮训练中的到达顺序批处理容易使少数较早到达或耗时较长的轨迹主导参数更新，而其他已生成轨迹在使用前变旧，导致经验浪费和训练效率下降。

Method: DivOPD仅在学习器侧改变训练样本选择，不改变逐轮损失函数和优化器。它在固定轮次预算内分配更多轨迹，并在每条轨迹中优先选择累计师生分歧较大的轮次，同时排除缺少可用教师反馈的轮次。对于无进展轨迹，可选地让教师短暂接管后再交还学生。

Result: 在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和WebShop的六种师生配置中，使用1.5B至7B学生模型时，平均峰值成功率由77.4提升至84.4，最后五次评估的平均成功率由71.5提升至78.6；相较原始OPD，达到各设置目标的几何平均训练token加速为1.84倍，学习器GPU时间加速为1.87倍。加入教师干预后，最后五次评估平均成功率进一步达到82.4，同时保持约1.7倍学习器GPU加速。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对异步轨迹的学习器侧动态筛选能够在不改变核心蒸馏损失和优化过程的情况下，减少过时经验和训练预算浪费，显著提升OPD的成功率与效率。教师干预可进一步改善无进展轨迹，但会引入额外机制。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains student agents through teacher supervision on their own interactions with an environment. However, in asynchronous multi-turn training, arrival-order batching can allow a few early or long rollouts to dominate learner updates while other valid rollouts become stale before being used, wasting already-generated experience. To address this problem, we introduce DivOPD, a simple learner-side batch-selection method that spreads a fixed turn budget across more rollouts and, within each rollout, prioritizes turns with larger cumulative teacher-student disagreement. Turns without usable teacher feedback are excluded. The per-turn loss and optimizer remain fixed; selection only changes which student-visited turns receive training weight. For no-progress rollouts, an optional extension briefly hands control to the teacher before returning it to the student. Across six teacher-student settings on the simulated ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and WebShop benchmarks, with 1.5B-7B students, DivOPD raises cross-setting mean peak success rate from 77.4 to 84.4 and mean success over the last five evaluations from 71.5 to 78.6. It reaches all reported setting-specific targets with geometric-mean speedups of 1.84x in training tokens and 1.87x in learner GPU time relative to vanilla OPD. Teacher intervention further raises this last-five mean to 82.4 while retaining about 1.7x learner-GPU speedup over vanilla OPD. Code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [221] [GenMem: Generative Symbolic Memory for Self-Evolving Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34633)
*Xinke Jiang,Tao Feng,Weixuan Xu,Zhixin Zhang,Zhibang Yang,Wentao Zhang,Runchuan Zhu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenMem 将长程记忆管理建模为生成式符号寻址，使智能体生成稳定的记忆地址，并在地址不变的情况下更新其内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法偏重判别式检索，难以应对经验的稀疏、层级化与冗余结构；任务反馈稀疏且延迟，也增加了记忆保留、检索和修订的学习难度。此外，记忆持续演化与检索策略所需稳定地址之间存在架构张力。

Method: GenMem 使用符号标识符（SID），以多级离散 token 元组表示记忆地址，用少于一百个离散符号因式分解百万级稀疏记忆空间。系统由 MemRetriever 和 MemEvolver 组成：前者生成 SID，后者在固定地址下改写记忆内容；训练采用 GRPO，并结合过程奖励与结果奖励进行双通道优化。

Result: 摘要称，实验在 ALFWorld、WebShop、多跳问答、医学推理和深度研究任务上，将 GenMem 与强记忆增强基线进行比较，并采用离线记忆演化设置；但未提供具体实验结果或性能数值。

Conclusion: GenMem 的核心思路是将可变记忆内容与稳定寻址接口分离，以生成式符号地址支持长期记忆的检索与演化。摘要提出了跨多类任务的评估，但现有文本不足以判断其实际性能优势。

Abstract: Long-term memory supports the self-evolution of LLM agents by retaining experience and skills across tasks and enabling their retrieval, reuse, and revision in subsequent long-horizon decision-making. Yet existing memory management approaches remain limited to discriminative retrieval and to address the sparse, hierarchical, and highly redundant structure of reusable experience: only a small, task-dependent subset of trajectories and memories warrants retention, retrieval, or revision. Learning these operations is further complicated by sparse, delayed, and indirect task-level feedback, with weak supervision across the memory lifecycle. Moreover, continual memory evolution introduces an architectural tension as addressing invariance: stored experience is perpetually revised, yet the addressing interface consumed by learned retrieval policies must remain stable. To address, we present GenMem, which reformulates memory management as generative symbolic addressing. Its core mechanism is the Symbolic Identifier (SID), a multi-level discrete token tuple drawn from a Cartesian-product address space that factorizes a million-scale sparse memory space using fewer than one hundred discrete symbols. Instead of generating ever-changing raw content, the memory agent learns to generate SIDs, while memory evolution rewrites the payload at a fixed address without shifting the address itself. Architecturally, GenMem couples a MemRetriever and a MemEvolver within a multi-agent harness, trained via GRPO with dense process and outcome rewards with two-channels optimization. Under offline memory evolution, experiments spanning ALFWorld, WebShop, multi-hop QA, medical reasoning, and deep research evaluate GenMem against strong memory-augmented baselines...

</details>


### [222] [Shaping Persistent Representations from Independent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34604)
*Ji Dai,Quan Fang,Junyu Gao,Rongfeng Guo,Haoyan Rong,YipingHuang,Yongxi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPRII利用不同交互之间的关系作为弱监督，促使世界模型学习可复用的持久上下文；在多种任务和模型中，相较基线提升了下游性能与持久属性读取表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准预测训练可能仅凭局部证据降低误差，而未将跨交互持续存在的信息组织为可复用上下文。研究旨在引导模型学习此类信息，同时保留其原有训练目标，且不依赖数值属性标签。

Method: SPRII包含两个可组合组件：Align促使相关交互的上下文保持一致；Cross利用一次交互的上下文预测另一次交互的未来。分析框架分别考察持久信息能否形成于上下文（Formation）、上下文是否影响固定预测器（Use），以及是否降低任务误差（Value）。实验覆盖13类场景及多种学习器。

Result: 受控实验显示，交互关系越可靠，表示组织通常越好；但加入共享属性约束也可能降低模型对某些共享属性的获取能力。固定模型权重时替换上下文会改变预测；历史信息的收益取决于预测时域和读出方式。相较对应基线，SPRII使下游任务表现平均提升超过10%，持久属性读取表现平均提升超过15%。

Conclusion: 跨交互关系可作为弱监督，促进持久上下文学习并带来整体性能收益。但上下文形成、实际使用和任务价值并非必然同步，因此评估时应分别检验这三个环节。

Abstract: World models learn environment dynamics from interaction experience. These dynamics depend on the current state and actions, as well as on properties that persist across interactions. Yet standard predictive training can reduce error using local evidence alone, without organizing persistent information into reusable context. We introduce SPRII, a training principle that uses relations between interactions as weak supervision for persistent context while retaining the learner's native objective. For example, different trajectories of the same system share persistent properties even when their states and actions differ. SPRII uses such relations to guide context learning without numerical property labels. Two composable components encourage contexts from related interactions to agree (Align) and use one interaction's context to predict another's future (Cross). Our analysis distinguishes three linked questions: what persistent information is accessible in the learned context (Formation), how that context influences a fixed predictor (Use), and whether it reduces task error (Value). Success at one stage does not guarantee success at the next. Controlled experiments show that more reliable relations improve representation organization, but adding a shared-property constraint can reduce access to a property that remains shared. Context substitutions change predictions at fixed model weights, while the benefit from history depends on prediction horizon and readout. Evaluations span thirteen settings, including controlled physical systems, public dynamics tasks, robotic and tactile data, and partner interaction, across multiple learner families. Relative to the corresponding baselines, SPRII yields average gains of over 10% in downstream task performance and over 15% in persistent-property readout. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [223] [The Low-Rank Structure of VLA Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34599)
*Minjae Oh,Yoonah Park,Jongwon Lim,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习如何改变视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，发现参数更新低秩且主要集中于动作专家中的 Timestep Modules；这些模块编码的更新信号与任务成功及跨任务迁移相关，并可用于进一步提升策略表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习正被广泛用于 VLA 模型的后训练，但其如何重塑策略、哪些参数承载性能提升，以及这些变化包含何种任务信息，仍缺乏系统理解。

Method: 在多个流式 VLA 模型及 LIBERO、ManiSkill、MetaWorld、CALVIN 基准上分析强化学习前后的参数变化；通过模块替换实验评估各组件对性能增益的贡献，并探测 Timestep Modules 的输出、时间步专化、更新方向与跨任务相似性；最后沿 shift 更新方向进行策略引导。

Result: 强化学习产生的参数更新显著低秩，并高度集中于 Timestep Modules；替换实验表明这些模块承载了不成比例的性能增益。模块会专化于 rollout 使用的离散去噪时间步，其中 shift 向量变化最明显，其更新方向预测任务成功的 ROC-AUC 最高达 99.6%。shift 更新之间的相似性还与跨任务迁移模式相关；沿这些方向引导可在不追加强化学习训练的情况下进一步提升策略表现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了强化学习后训练中一个关键但易被忽视的参数子模块，并表明其 shift 更新方向能够表征任务成功与任务关系，为更高效、可解释的 VLA 后训练提供了分析依据和策略改进途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models, yet how RL reshapes these policies remains poorly understood. We find that RL across widely used flow-based VLA models, including $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T~N1.5/N1.6, on LIBERO, ManiSkill, MetaWorld, and CALVIN induces substantially lower-rank parameter updates that are highly concentrated in the action expert's Timestep Modules, a small and previously overlooked component. Through systematic module-replacement experiments, we further show that these modules capture a disproportionate share of the performance gains from RL. We then characterize what is encoded in these Timestep Modules. First, we show that RL specializes them to the discrete denoising timesteps used during rollouts, and that this discrete-timestep training underlies the low-rank updates. Second, we find that among their outputs, the shift vector changes most distinctly under RL, and through probing, we show that shift update directions strongly predict task success (ROC-AUC up to $99.6\%$). Third, we find that the geometry of shift updates reflects task relationships, as their pairwise similarity correlates with cross-task transfer patterns. Building on these findings, we show that steering along shift update directions further improves RL-trained policies without additional RL training. Overall, we provide a systematic understanding of how RL reshapes VLA policies by studying how learned signals are encoded in parameter space, offering insights into more efficient and interpretable VLA post-training.

</details>


### [224] [Brain-Conditioned Action Policies for Neural Motor Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34561)
*Luyao Jin,Running Zhao,Huan Zhao,Vincent C. K. Cheung,Wei-Hsin Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainVLA 将神经活动与语言语义对齐，并以此引导预训练视觉—语言—动作模型生成运动指令，从而提升神经运动解码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经运动解码依赖任务专属的神经活动—动作配对数据，但这类数据稀缺，限制了模型泛化与训练效率。

Method: 构建包含配对神经活动、动作信号、语言指令和渲染视觉观测的数据集；通过 LoRA 微调 OpenVLA-OFT 以适配目标动作空间；训练神经编码器，使神经表示与语言表示对齐，并将其作为意图信号引导策略生成动作，视觉观测则补充任务状态信息。

Result: 在两个动作维度不同的神经运动数据集上进行因果滚动解码评估。BrainVLA 在跨会话解码 R² 和任务成功率方面优于所比较的基线，并展现出较高的数据效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，语言中介的神经—语义对齐可将大规模机器人先验引入脑机接口运动解码，为利用神经意图驱动视觉—语言—动作策略提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Motor brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) aim to decode motor intention, enabling people with paralysis to control external devices. Neural motor decoding typically learns task-specific mappings from neural activity to kinematics, yet remains constrained by scarce paired neural-action data. We propose BrainVLA, a framework that enables neural motor decoding by drawing on a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model through language-mediated alignment. BrainVLA mitigates reliance on scarce paired neural-action data by leveraging VLA policies. We first construct VLA-compatible datasets including paired neural activity, action signals, language instructions, and rendered visual observations. Then, we adapt the OpenVLA-OFT policy to the target action spaces through LoRA fine-tuning. To establish an effective interface through which neural activity can convey motor intention to adapted VLA policies and guide action generation, we train a neural encoder via neural-language alignment, using language representations as semantic targets to capture latent motor intent from neural activity. The resulting neural representations serve as an endogenous intention signal to guide VLA policies to generate executable actions, while visual observations provide complementary information about the evolving task state. BrainVLA is evaluated on two neural motor datasets with different action dimensionalities using causal rollout decoding. It outperforms the evaluated baselines in cross-session decoding $R^2$ and task success rate, while demonstrating high training data efficiency. These results establish a route for neural motor decoding to draw on large-scale robotic priors through brain-conditioned VLA policies.

</details>


### [225] [QAMM: Adjoint MeanFlow Matching for Few-Step Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34497)
*Yuehu Gong,Shutong Ding,Mokai Pan,Yimiao Zhou,Jiashu Hou,Ye Shi,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAMM 将基于价值函数的伴随信号转化为 MeanFlow 平均速度的训练监督，使流策略能够以极少网络调用生成动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略能够表示丰富的动作分布，但迭代采样会降低决策速度。现有伴随匹配虽可利用价值函数的动作梯度优化策略，却针对瞬时速度定义监督，难以直接支持 MeanFlow 的有限区间平均速度学习。

Method: 提出 QAMM，将价值函数导出的伴随信号构造为 MeanFlow 平均速度的训练目标；推导相应目标并明确梯度边界，再通过离线 actor-critic 进行训练。

Result: 在十个 HumanoidMaze 任务上，QAMM 实现了仅需两次网络调用的有效策略，性能可与强流策略基线竞争。

Conclusion: 将伴随式 Q 优化与平均速度学习结合，可在保留流策略表达能力的同时，实现少步动作生成。

Abstract: Flow policies can model rich action distributions, but their iterative sampling limits decision speed. Adjoint matching uses the critic's action gradient to improve a flow policy without backpropagating through its sampling trajectory, yet its supervision is defined for instantaneous velocities. We propose QAMM, a method that turns the critic-derived adjoint signal into supervision for MeanFlow's average velocity. The resulting policy learns finite-interval transport directly and generates actions with few network evaluations. We derive the adjoint MeanFlow target, specify its gradient boundaries, and train it with an offline actor-critic. On ten HumanoidMaze tasks, QAMM produces effective two-call policies and achieves competitive performance against strong flow-policy baselines. These results show that adjoint-based Q optimization can be combined with average-velocity learning to obtain expressive offline policies with few-step action generation.

</details>


### [226] [Alignment-Guided Flow Transformer for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34467)
*Shengchao Hu,Peng Wang,Qiyang Zhou,Guodong Zheng,Yuqi Huang,Li Shen,Ya Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AGFT通过显式促进视觉、语言与动作三模态对齐，并采用流匹配目标，提升VLA模型的任务适应能力与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型存在视觉、语言和动作之间的三模态错位，导致动作 grounding 不足、泛化能力受限且微调效率偏低；基于扩散的策略推理步骤也较多。

Method: 提出Alignment-Guided Flow Transformer（AGFT），以专门的对齐损失缩小三模态表征差距，并采用流匹配目标减少推理步数。通过消融与分析研究三模态对齐的作用，并从理论上建立对齐差距与流匹配优化紧致性之间的定量联系。

Result: 摘要报告称，在综合基准上，AGFT相较于先进基线取得更高的任务成功率和更低的推理延迟；消融与分析进一步用于评估三模态对齐对适应能力和鲁棒性的贡献。

Conclusion: 三模态对齐是提升VLA模型适应性与鲁棒性的关键因素；结合流匹配，有望在保持准确性的同时加快部署。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models point toward general-purpose robotic intelligence by unifying perception, instruction, and control. Despite impressive progress, existing VLA models often adapt poorly due to \emph{tri-modal misalignment} among vision, language, and action, which weakens action grounding and hurts generalization and fine-tuning efficiency. In this work, we present Alignment-Guided Flow Transformer (AGFT), a novel framework that explicitly enforces tri-modal alignment through a dedicated alignment loss, bridging the representational gap across modalities and enhancing task adaptation. While prior research has predominantly emphasized bi-modal vision--language alignment, we systematically formalize and study tri-modal alignment in VLA models, and provide both ablations and analysis to isolate its role in improving adaptation and robustness. To further accelerate deployment, we adopt a flow-matching objective, enabling substantially fewer inference steps than diffusion-based policies while maintaining accuracy. Theoretically, we establish a quantitative connection between the tri-modal alignment gap and the optimization tightness of flow matching; empirically, experiments on the extensive benchmark show that AGFT achieves superior success rates and lower inference latency compared to SOTA baselines, underscoring tri-modal alignment as a key ingredient for scaling robust VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [227] [Q-learning Penalized Transformer for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34426)
*Shengchao Hu,Peng Wang,Jifeng Hu,Qiyang Zhou,Anning Hu,Li Shen,Ya Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Q-learning Penalized Transformer（QPT），通过将条件序列建模与约束感知价值估计结合，解决安全离线强化学习中的安全、回报与行为正则化权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全离线强化学习需要在满足安全约束、最大化奖励和遵循离线数据分布之间取得平衡，这些目标彼此关联且可能相互冲突。

Method: QPT 训练 Transformer 根据轨迹上下文及目标回报和成本生成动作，并利用学习到的奖励与成本 Q 函数构造惩罚项，引导策略偏好高回报、低约束违反的行为。推理时复用同一组 Q 函数筛除不满足成本阈值的动作，并在可行动作中选择奖励最高者，从而保持训练与部署的一致性。

Result: 在 DSRL 基准的 38 项任务中，QPT 持续优于具有竞争力的安全离线强化学习基线；面对不同约束阈值时，也表现出稳健的零样本适应能力。论文还在特定的近确定性 CMDP 条件下分析了该方法对安全性和性能的影响。

Conclusion: QPT 将条件序列生成与价值函数引导的安全决策相结合，为安全离线强化学习提供了一种兼顾数据分布约束、任务回报和安全要求的训练—推理一致框架。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of safe offline reinforcement learning, which involves training a policy to satisfy safety constraints using an offline dataset. This problem is inherently challenging as it requires balancing three highly interconnected and competing objectives: satisfying safety constraints, maximizing rewards, and adhering to the behavior regularization imposed by the offline dataset. To tackle this trilogy challenge, we propose Q-learning Penalized Transformer policy (QPT), a \emph{training--inference consistent} framework that bridges conditional sequence modeling with constraint-aware value estimation. QPT trains a Transformer policy that generates actions conditioned on trajectory context and target return/cost, retaining strong behavior regularization. To inject explicit safety semantics during learning, we augment sequence-model training with a Q-shaped penalty using learned reward and cost Q-functions to favor high return under low constraint violation. At inference, the same Q-functions enforce the cost threshold and choose the highest-reward feasible action, closing the loop between training and deployment. We provide a principled analysis under stylized near-deterministic CMDPs, characterizing how Q-penalized conditional generation improve safety and performance. Empirically, QPT consistently outperforms strong safe offline RL baselines across 38 tasks on the DSRL benchmark, and exhibits robust zero-shot adaptation to different constraint thresholds.

</details>


### [228] [Learning Perturbation Robust Policies for LLM Agents with Stable Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34064)
*Pengxin Wang,Yuanzhe LI,Yuxin Ren,Huanrui Yang,Jingdi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPrPO，通过在强化学习训练中施加自适应、感知敏感度的扰动，提升长时程大语言模型智能体对策略扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经强化学习后训练的策略可能对隐状态噪声、剪枝和量化等扰动敏感，影响部署可靠性；因此需要在策略优化阶段增强其鲁棒性，同时避免破坏训练稳定性。

Method: 提出扰动鲁棒策略的概念，并分析扰动后的策略更新保持稳定单调改进的条件。在此基础上设计 SPrPO，在强化学习训练中施加自适应且依据策略敏感度调整的扰动。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上进行评估，并系统测试多种扰动类型及强度。结果表明，SPrPO 提升了策略的扰动鲁棒性，同时保持策略优化稳定。

Conclusion: 通过将敏感度感知的扰动纳入策略优化，可增强长时程大语言模型智能体对多类策略扰动的适应能力，且不明显牺牲训练稳定性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective post-training paradigm for long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents. However, we find that the resulting policies can be sensitive to various policy perturbations, such as hidden-state noise, pruning, and quantization. In this work, we study how to improve perturbation robustness during policy optimization. We first introduce the notion of a perturbation robust policy and analyze conditions under which perturbed policy updates preserve stable monotonic improvement. Based on this analysis, we introduce Stable Perturbation-Robust Policy Optimization (SPrPO), which applies adaptive and sensitivity-aware perturbations during RL training. We evaluate SPrPO on ALFWorld and WebShop and conduct systematic experiments across multiple perturbation types and scales, showing improved perturbation robustness while maintaining stable policy optimization.

</details>


### [229] [Do World Models Learn Global Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34058)
*Alexander Detkov,Matt Thomson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，模型虽能拟合局部状态转移，却难以从中学习并传播全局约束；以隐藏中间状态的组合式训练可显著提升约束泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型常出现理解与应用脱节、行为随情境变化的问题。论文将这一现象归结为未能把局部信息提升为全局理解，并希望以形式化任务研究模型如何学习约束及其推论。

Method: 构建基于幺半群世界的学习任务：状态由动作转移连接，训练转移与未见约束共同决定测试转移。考察逆、交换、组合和周期性约束，并比较注意力、循环和状态空间架构。对比直接预测下一状态的训练与组合式训练；后者采用相同路径，但不向模型提供输入中的中间状态。另以证明深度衡量推导测试转移所需的最少推理轮数，并改变组合路径长度。

Result: 直接下一状态训练能拟合数据，但难以传播非平凡约束。组合式训练在逆、交换和组合约束上达到96%的准确率，并改善具身环境世界模型的几何泛化及经Wikidata微调的语言模型的关系泛化。泛化能力随证明深度增加而显著下降；增大组合路径长度T可提升泛化。

Conclusion: 组合式训练有助于模型整合信息并传播约束后果，为研究语言模型与世界模型的全局理解提供了形式化框架。结果也表明，模型推断未见事实的能力受推理链深度影响。

Abstract: AI systems often feel brittle and fragmented. A large language model (LLM) may correctly explain a concept but fail to apply it, or follow safety instructions in one context but not another. This behavior suggests a general failure to lift local information to a global understanding. To gain fundamental insight, we frame "understanding" as learning constraints and propagating their consequences. We construct learning tasks on monoid worlds, sets of states connected by action transitions, where observed training transitions and an unseen constraint jointly determine held-out transitions. Measuring generalization tests whether models can learn global constraints from local transitions and propagate their consequences. We consider inverse, commutativity, composition, and periodicity constraints relevant to spatial and semantic structure. Across attention, recurrent, and state-space architectures, next-state training fits the data but fails to propagate non-trivial constraints. Compositional training, which uses identical paths but hides intermediate states from the input, achieves 96% accuracy on inverse, commutativity, and composition constraints across architectures, yields corresponding improvements in geometric generalization of world models trained on embodied environments and relational generalization in Wikidata-finetuned LLMs. How far do models propagate constraints when inferring an unseen fact may depend on first inferring others? We define proof depth d of a held-out transition, measuring the minimum number of inference rounds to infer the transition, and find that model generalization decreases sharply with proof depth. Increasing compositional path length T improves generalization. These results provide a formal way to investigate global understanding in language and world models and demonstrate that compositional training promotes information propagation and integration.

</details>


### [230] [Vision--Language Signals in Constrained RL: Safety Gains Without Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34041)
*Samuel Tetteh,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning seeks policies that maximise task performance while satisfying safety constraints. In driving benchmarks, however, collision costs typically appear only at the time of collision, providing no advance warning of an approaching hazard. Frozen vision--language models can provide dense semantic feedback, yet it remains unclear whether their scores anticipate collisions and which component drives an observed safety improvement. Episodic cost can also favour policies that make little task progress. To address these gaps, we propose VLM-Safe-RL, a framework that integrates frozen CLIP signals into PPO-Lagrangian through reward shaping and an augmented multiplier update. On MetaDrive Hard, which combines the densest traffic with the largest map, the catastrophe rate falls from 31.6\% to 19.4\%. FormulaOne-L2 analysis finds no evidence that the CLIP signals anticipate collisions and shows that the VLM term has a negligible effect on the Lagrange multiplier. These findings show a conditional reduction in observed catastrophe rate without evidence of collision anticipation.

</details>


### [231] [UOPD: Uncertainty-Aware Intervention for On-Policy Distillation of Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34036)
*Wenbo Zhang,Pengcheng Xu,Weizhi Du,Jing Zhang,Hengrui Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UOPD 根据教师对学生动作的不确定性，在关键回合介入并让学生模仿教师动作，从而提升多轮任务中的在策略蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮环境中，学生在关键决策步骤犯错可能使后续轨迹偏离良好结果。研究发现，教师对学生动作置信度较低的步骤适合进行纠正，且一次教师纠正即可改善后续行为与任务成功率。

Method: UOPD 按不确定性选择动作来源：低不确定性回合执行学生动作并采用标准在策略蒸馏损失；高不确定性回合采样并执行教师动作，再通过监督微调训练学生模仿。该模仿目标在期望意义下最小化前向 KL 散度，并通过自适应阈值控制预定的干预比例。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 Search 等智能体任务上的实验表明，UOPD 优于在策略蒸馏基线及其变体；相较标准在策略蒸馏，其 WebShop 得分最高提升 15.8%。

Conclusion: 基于不确定性的选择性教师干预有助于纠正关键决策，并提升多轮任务中的蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts using dense supervision from a teacher. In multi-turn environments, a mistake at a critical decision step can redirect the subsequent rollout toward poor outcomes. We use low teacher confidence on student actions to select high-uncertainty steps for correction. In a controlled ALFWorld study, a single teacher correction at a low-confidence step improves subsequent student behavior and task success, motivating selective intervention during distillation. We propose UOPD, an uncertainty-aware intervention method for on-policy distillation. At low-uncertainty turns, UOPD executes student actions and applies the standard OPD loss. At high-uncertainty turns, it samples and executes teacher actions and trains the student to imitate them through supervised fine-tuning, which minimizes forward Kullback-Leibler divergence in expectation. UOPD utilizes adaptive uncertainty thresholds to target a scheduled intervention rate. Empirically, we evaluate UOPD across a broad range of agentic tasks, including ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search, demonstrating its superior performance over OPD methods and their variants. UOPD improves WebShop score by up to $15.8\%$ relative to standard OPD.

</details>


### [232] [Behavioral Monitoring of JEPA World Models with Jacobian Centroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33940)
*Thomas Walker,Randall Balestriero,Richard Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用模型质心监测世界模型（WM）的行为特性，并在分布偏移下提前识别规划失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于世界模型的规划需要确认模型是否针对当前任务作出恰当响应；仅观察激活信号不足以揭示模型内部表征与行为之间的错位。

Method: 将模型子组件雅可比矩阵的行和定义为质心，通过雅可比向量积高效计算，用其刻画输入空间几何结构、分析内部表征并生成任务相关显著图；在连续控制任务中的 JEPA 世界模型上进行评估。

Result: 研究发现，编码器能够正确表征目标，但预测器可能对目标在行为上无响应；这种结构性分离可在采取行动前预测规划失败。重新采样目标有助于恢复分布外成功，且质心方法作为分布偏移检测器优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 质心为世界模型提供了高效的行为监测视角，可补充传统激活分析，并支持在分布偏移下及时诊断失效、调整目标与改善规划表现。

Abstract: Detecting failures in World Model (WM)-based planning requires monitoring whether the model is behaviorally aligned with the current task, which in turn requires studying its internal representations. Here, we show that centroids---sub-component Jacobian row-sums---effectively identify the behavioral properties of WMs, complementing traditional activation-based knowledge signals. The centroids of a model are easily computed through Jacobian vector products and characterize how the model organizes the geometry of its input space, yielding an efficient perspective on internal representations, including the generation of task-relevant saliency maps. Evaluated on continuous control tasks using JEPA WMs, this behavioral view reveals a structural dissociation, where the encoder correctly represents the goal while the predictor remains behaviorally unresponsive. This failure mode directly predicts planning failure before any action is taken, allowing for goal resampling to recapture out-of-distribution success. Moreover, centroid-based methods outperform baseline methods as distribution-shift detectors. Together, these tools yield a behavioral monitoring stack that is operational and consequential under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [233] [ALDER: Discovering the Laws of a World by Acting in It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33728)
*Teng Cao,Yu Deng,Quentin Delfosse,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALDER通过主动设计实验来发现、验证和修订显式方程式世界模型，并利用验证后的模型进行目标导向控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖固定轨迹或预设候选公式，难以区分同样符合现有证据的假设，也无法发现初始假设空间之外的规律。

Method: ALDER提出参数化方程，由数值优化器拟合系数，并通过独立验证器在留出数据上检验。面对多个有效假设时，系统依据成本与安全因素选择干预实验；实验反例更新证据记录，并指导模型结构修订，同时淘汰不相容的规律。

Result: 在自建基准、常微分方程发现任务及机器人实验中，ALDER能够发现初始公式集之外的规律、修复失败的模型提案、以较少交互区分候选模型，并提升分布外预测表现。它还可依据已验证模型求解逆问题，选择实现目标的控制动作。

Conclusion: 主动交互使显式方程式世界模型能够被检验和迭代修订；经过验证的模型还可直接支持目标导向控制。

Abstract: Reliable world models should not only predict future states but express how actions change the world in an explicit, transparent and testable form, such as equations. Yet methods that rely on a fixed set of trajectories cannot distinguish equally good competing hypotheses, while searches over a fixed set of predefined candidates cannot discover equations outside the initial hypothesis space. We introduce ALDER (Action-guided Law Discovery, Evaluation, and Revision), a method that actively proposes novel experiments to test and revise models. Specifically, ALDER proposes parametric equations; a numerical optimizer fits their coefficients; an independent verifier tests these candidates on held-out data. To distinguish between competing valid hypotheses, a cost- and safety-aware selector queries interventions, in the form of novel experiments. The resulting counterexamples update the evidence ledger and guide the next structural revision, while incompatible laws are discarded. Across an in-house benchmark, ODE equation discovery tasks, and robotic experiments, ALDER discovers laws beyond its initial formula set, repairs failed model proposals, distinguishes fixed candidate models with fewer interactions, and improves out-of-distribution prediction. Furthermore, given a current state and a target, ALDER selects control actions by solving the inverse problem defined by its validated world model. Together, these results show that explicit equation-based world models can be tested and revised through interaction, then naturally used to guide goal-directed control.

</details>


### [234] [Hamiltonian JEPA: Action-Conditioned World Models with an Inherited Control State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33497)
*Tamim Zoabi,Ameen Ali,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H-JEPA 将感知表征与控制状态分离，并以结构化动力学和端口逆一致性提升像素控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件 JEPAs 让同一嵌入同时服务于感知与控制，可能难以形成适合规划的状态空间；规划所需表征不仅要稳定、可预测，还应体现动作对系统状态的影响。

Method: 构建宽感知码，并用 Bures–Wasserstein 先验约束其几何性质；从中取固定正交切片作为控制状态。状态由相位条件耗散型端口哈密顿动力学演化，输入端口列正交。端口逆一致性（PIC）通过端口转置读取已执行动作；作者证明，该读数等于沿端口方向投影的滚动预测误差，因此无需额外动作解码器。

Result: 在四项像素控制基准上，H-JEPA 至多训练 10 个 epoch，即达到或超过无重建基线及动作解码型 Delta-JEPA；在 OGB-Cube 上取得 91.9%，高于对照的 79.3%。实验还通过消融考察结构化预测器、PIC、预测时域、状态秩和防坍塌先验。

Conclusion: 将控制状态嵌入感知表征的结构中，并让动作读出与动力学输入端口绑定，可有效提升像素控制表现。若将读出与端口解绑，相关数学恒等关系不再成立，性能增益也会减半。

Abstract: Planning from pixels needs more than a latent space that is stable and predictable. The state the planner scores must also be organized by how actions move the system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) avoid pixel reconstruction by predicting future representations, but existing action-conditioned JEPAs ask one embedding to serve both perception and control. We introduce H-JEPA, which separates the two. A wide perceptual code is regularized toward a well-scaled isotropic geometry with a Bures-Wasserstein prior, and a fixed orthonormal slice of that code is the control state, which inherits the code's covariance without any objective of its own. The state evolves under phase-conditioned dissipative port-Hamiltonian dynamics whose input port has orthonormal columns. Port-inverse consistency (PIC) reads the executed action back through the transpose of that port. We show that this readout is exactly the rollout error projected onto the port directions, so PIC is a parameter-free reweighting of prediction error and not an auxiliary action decoder. Untying the readout from the port breaks this identity and loses half of the gain. H-JEPA matches or exceeds reconstruction-free baselines, including the action-decoding Delta-JEPA, on four pixel-based control benchmarks after at most $10$ training epochs, and its largest gain is on OGB-Cube ($91.9$ against $79.3$ percent). Ablations on PushT and OGB-Cube separate the contributions of the structured predictor, PIC, the prediction horizon, the state rank, and the anti-collapse prior.

</details>


### [235] [Beyond Timestamps: Decision-Aligned On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33391)
*Mingju Chen,Can Lv,Jinrong Liu,Huan Zhang,Heng Chang,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 AlignOPSD，通过对齐监督与决策跨度，改善长时程智能体的强化学习信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习通常只有稀疏结果奖励；在线策略自蒸馏虽能提供稠密反馈，但教师指导可能与学生的实际决策在上下文和时间范围上错位，导致信用分配不准。

Method: AlignOPSD 分两步处理错位问题：首先，在功能匹配的同级轨迹上下文中，对同一条学生采样响应重新评分，以校准局部教师证据；随后依据对应关系变化识别可变时长的决策跨度，并将经校准的证据与最终结果结合，分配到跨度及其内部轮次。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-3B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上，对 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 Search-QA 进行评估。AlignOPSD 在八项骨干模型与汇总指标比较中均优于 GRPO 和 StepOPSD；相较 GRPO 提升 5.5%–8.7%，并在六项比较中排名第一。

Conclusion: 先使监督与功能性决策对齐，再进行跨时间跨度的信用分配，有助于提升稀疏奖励环境中的智能体表现。论文还分析了两个对齐阶段及任务间的超参数敏感性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often relies on sparse outcome rewards, providing coarse supervision for long-horizon agents. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) complements this signal with dense privileged feedback. However, we identify \emph{Decision--Timestamp Mismatch}: privileged guidance may be misaligned with the student's functional decision because the corresponding decision can occur at a different timestep, while the student's decision itself may span multiple timesteps rather than being tied to a single timestamp. Thus, timestamp-local supervision can misalign both the context and the temporal scope of credit. To address this mismatch, we introduce \textsc{AlignOPSD}, following the principle of aligning supervision before assigning credit. Decision-Aligned Supervision Rectification re-scores the same student-sampled response in functionally matched contexts across sibling rollouts to calibrate local teacher evidence. Semi-Markov Hierarchical Credit Assignment then derives variable-duration decision spans from correspondence changes and uses rectified evidence to allocate outcome-grounded credit across spans and their constituent turns. We evaluate \textsc{AlignOPSD} with Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-7B on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA against representative baselines. \textsc{AlignOPSD} outperforms both GRPO and StepOPSD across all eight backbone--aggregate-metric comparisons, improving on GRPO by 5.5--8.7 \% and ranking first in six. Additional analyzes examine the two alignment stages and hyperparameter sensitivity between tasks. Our code is avaliable at this https URL

</details>


### [236] [Beyond Conservatism: Recoverability-Conditioned Exploration for Model-Based Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33336)
*Xuanlin Chen,Ziyue Wang,Xunlan Zhou,Yuan-yih Shang,Qiang Wu,Shenghua Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECON通过将保守的任务执行策略与主动探索策略分离，并依据可恢复性引导探索，提升基于模型的模仿学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保守策略可减少世界模型误差导致的策略利用，但若也用于采集真实交互数据，专家分布附近的不确定区域仍可能采样不足。通用不确定性探索则可能浪费交互于与任务无关的新颖状态。

Method: 维护主策略和探索策略：主策略负责任务执行；探索策略依据认知不确定性进行优化，并利用主策略的多步想象估计可恢复性，优先采集那些模型未知、且主策略能够引导回专家行为的状态。

Result: 在运动、导航和操作任务上的实验显示，RECON持续提升交互效率、模仿表现和鲁棒性，并学得更适用于模仿任务的世界模型。

Conclusion: 将不确定性探索与可恢复性结合，可使真实环境交互集中于专家分布附近、但以往方法采样不足的区域，从而改善基于模型的模仿学习。

Abstract: Model-based imitation learning (MBIL) improves real-environment interaction efficiency by optimizing policies on imagined rollouts from a learned world model. However, the gap between model-induced and real-environment occupancies makes policy learning sensitive to model error. Conservative MBIL mitigates model exploitation during policy optimization, but when real-environment interactions are collected by the same conservative policy, uncertain regions around the expert distribution remain insufficiently sampled. Generic uncertainty-driven exploration, on the other hand, may allocate interaction to novel but task-irrelevant dynamics. We propose REcoverability-CONditioned Exploration for Model-Based Imitation Learning (RECON). RECON separates conservative policy learning from active data collection by maintaining a main policy for task execution and an explorer for real-environment interaction. The explorer is optimized based on epistemic uncertainty conditioned on recoverability estimated from multi-step main-policy imagination, focusing data collection on unknown states from which the main policy can still return toward expert behavior. Experiments on locomotion, navigation and manipulation show consistent gains in interaction efficiency, imitation performance, and robustness, indicating that RECON directs real-environment interaction toward recovery regions around the expert distribution that are underexplored by prior methods, and thereby learns a world model better suited for imitation.

</details>


### [237] [GTRL: Grounding Divide-and-Conquer Value Learning with Temporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33259)
*Abdul Monaf Chowdhury,MD Sameer Iqbal Chowdhury,Shifat E Arman,Md Mehedi Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Grounded Transitive RL（GTRL），一种用于离线目标条件强化学习的价值学习算法，旨在提升随机动力学下长时程任务的学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分治式价值更新在随机环境中容易偏向数据中最幸运的轨迹；同时，要求子目标位于同一条轨迹上，会使无法由单条轨迹连接的状态—目标对得不到价值更新。事后重标记还可能引入偏差。

Method: GTRL 将一步时序差分（TD）目标加入分治组合更新，而非取代组合更新：TD目标对后继状态求平均，不依赖子目标，可为所有状态—目标对提供更新；组合项则保留长时程推理能力。此外，算法依据目标从其他后继状态的可达性进行重加权，以校正事后重标记偏差。

Result: 在涵盖随机、确定性及轨迹拼接场景的 19 个 OGBench 任务上，GTRL取得最高的平均成功率。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 将一步TD监督与长时程分治组合结合，可缓解随机动力学下的幸运轨迹偏差和轨迹连接限制，并改善离线目标条件强化学习的任务表现。

Abstract: In offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), divide-and-conquer scales to long horizons by joining two shorter segments at a subgoal. However, under stochastic dynamics, the base case of this rule values the luckiest trajectories through the data. The subgoal must also lie on a shared trajectory, so a state-goal pair that no trajectory connects gets no value update at all. To address both, we present Grounded Transitive RL (GTRL), an offline GCRL value learning algorithm that grounds the divide-and-conquer update with a one-step TD target. Over a single step, TD is correct, as its target averages over the successors and needs no subgoal. GTRL adds this target to the composition rather than replacing it, so every pair receives an update, and the composition still carries the long horizon. GTRL also corrects the bias from hindsight relabeling by reweighting each goal against how reachable it was from other successors. We evaluate our algorithm on nineteen OGBench tasks spanning stochastic, deterministic, and stitching environments, where it achieves the highest average success rate. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [238] [Downstream-Aware Context Selection for Online In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33166)
*Ruihan A. Li,Shangtong Zhang,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种有界历史上下文管理框架，使语言模型智能体在维持任务表现的同时减少上下文令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文强化学习依赖交互历史适应新环境，但历史不断增长会带来较高的令牌成本；固定保留策略也难以适应不同任务与决策的需求。

Method: 以完整滚动历史为参照，预测移除各段历史交互对下游任务的影响，据此排序并筛选历史内容；通过统一选择准则，为每个决策动态确定保留的上下文量。

Result: 在 SUMO 闭环驾驶的三种测试设置中，相比使用完整上下文，令牌总量分别减少25.7%、25.8%和23.2%，驾驶表现保持相当。在 ScienceWorld 持续式上下文强化学习任务中，令牌总量较完整上下文减少52.1%，较 Recent 和 Similarity 基线分别减少30.2%和37.8%，同时保持任务表现。

Conclusion: 根据历史信息的任务相关性动态管理上下文，可显著降低令牌开销，且在所评估的驾驶与科学任务中不明显损害性能。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) enables large language model agents to adapt to new environments using their interaction history without updating model parameters. However, repeatedly conditioning on growing histories can lead to substantial token cost. We propose a bounded-history context-management framework that predicts the task-dependent downstream effect of removing historical interactions to guide history selection and determine a decision-dependent context budget. Formally, our framework uses the full rolling history as a reference. The predictor evaluates removal effects, defines a deletion ordering, and applies a shared selection criterion to determine how much history to retain at each decision. We evaluate the method in closed-loop SUMO driving under held-out in-distribution, unseen-domain, and unseen-route settings, and in ScienceWorld under a continual ICRL protocol. Relative to a baseline using the full context, our method reduces total token usage by 25.7%, 25.8%, and 23.2% across the three driving settings while maintaining comparable closed-loop driving performance. In ScienceWorld, it reduces total token usage by 52.1% compared to full context and uses 30.2% and 37.8% fewer tokens than the Recent and Similarity baselines, respectively, while maintaining performance.

</details>


### [239] [Policy Plasticity Matters in Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning: Refitting Offline Policies for Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33127)
*Yuheng Huang,Yunpeng Qing,Yixiao Chi,Yilun Kong,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 REFIT，通过将离线策略迁移到新初始化的学生网络，提升离线到在线强化学习中的策略适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据上的长期训练会逐渐降低网络可塑性；即使离线性能已基本饱和，较低可塑性仍与较弱的在线改进相关。

Method: 在线微调前，将离线策略蒸馏到新初始化的学生网络，并在蒸馏期间暂时冻结学生网络中随机选取的部分单元，以迁移已学行为并保留适应空间。

Result: 在 D4RL 和 OGBench 上，REFIT 与 Cal-QL、IQL 两种骨干结合时，整体表现均优于现有 O2O 插件方法；可塑性诊断和消融实验也支持其恢复网络可塑性的效果。

Conclusion: 离线到在线的策略适应不仅取决于价值校准，也受离线训练后网络可塑性的影响。REFIT 提供了一种轻量级模型级方法，改善在线微调的初始化状态。

Abstract: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning (O2O RL) has emerged as a practical paradigm that pre-trains the policy using static offline datasets and subsequently adapts the policy through online interactions. Existing O2O methods primarily address the transition through value calibration, while generally treating the offline-trained policy as a given initialization. We instead study O2O adaptation from the perspective of network plasticity, asking whether the offline-trained policy remains sufficiently adaptable for online learning. Controlled experiments show that prolonged optimization on static offline data progressively reduces network plasticity even after offline performance has largely saturated, and that lower plasticity is associated with weaker subsequent online improvement. Motivated by these observations, we propose REstoring plasticity via Fresh Initialization and policy Transfer (REFIT), a lightweight model-level method for the O2O transition. Before online fine-tuning, REFIT distills the offline policy into a freshly initialized student while temporarily freezing a random subset of student units, transferring the learned offline behavior to a more plastic policy initialization. Extensive experiments on D4RL and OGBench demonstrate that REFIT consistently achieves higher aggregate performance than existing O2O plug-in methods across both Cal-QL and IQL backbones, while plasticity diagnostics and ablations provide further evidence of restored network plasticity.

</details>


### [240] [Self-Confirming Superposition Traps in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32966)
*Dai Shi,Andi Han,Feng Chen,Yiqun Duan,Junbin Gao,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示强化学习中的一种自我强化表示陷阱：策略回避的动作可能因训练数据不足而形成不利表示，进而持续被回避；增加对被忽略状态的训练访问可缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习依据当前策略采集的数据学习表示，再用回报调整策略。研究关注这一闭环为何会使低回报策略持续存在，即使表示在当前数据上已达到全局最优。

Method: 在 tied 双步模型中刻画陷阱出现的维度条件，并分别分析特征频率、持续访问和控制器独立学习的影响；在有限动作模型中界定访问频率造成的回报失真，并推导基于回放权重的排序保持条件。另通过神经网络 PPO 实验，以及 MiniGrid、DMControl 和 DreamerV3--Crafter 评估状态访问、回放权重、特征重叠和受保护拟合等干预。

Result: 当特征在当前策略下很少共同出现时，最优编码仍可能让它们共享表示方向；替代动作会因此产生干扰、回报下降，并强化对该动作的回避。等频特征、持续访问和独立学习控制器均不足以排除此陷阱。实验显示，不同初始偏好会形成不同干扰模式及最终策略；保留被忽略状态可降低干扰、提升序贯回报，即使编码器被冻结也有收益。相关干预还改善了 MiniGrid、DMControl 的控制表现，并在不增加模型容量的情况下提高 DreamerV3--Crafter 的累计训练分数。

Conclusion: 按访问频率加权的表示学习可能低估被回避动作上的误差，使其潜在回报优势消失。通过增加这些状态的训练权重或保护其表示拟合，可减轻自我确认式退化并改善控制；有限动作情形下，充分回放最佳动作可在存在残余误差时保持其回报排序。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) trains representations on data selected by the agent's policy, which then uses the resulting returns to guide its next choices. We show that this loop can sustain a lower-return policy even when representation fitting is globally optimal on those data. In a self-confirming superposition trap, every optimal code assigns overlapping directions to features that rarely occur together under the current policy. An alternative action brings them together, causing interference that lowers its return and reinforces avoidance, although refitting to that action would yield more return at the same capacity. We characterize the dimensions admitting a trap in a tied two-step model and show separately that equal feature frequencies, continued visitation, and independent controller learning need not prevent it. Because fitting weights errors by visitation, an avoided action can lose its return advantage at little cost to the objective. In a finite-action model, we bound this distortion and derive a replay condition: sufficient training weight on the best separately adapted action preserves its ranking despite residual error. Neural PPO experiments show how the feedback develops during learning: agents initialized toward different actions develop different interference patterns, opposite mean return rankings, and different final policies at the same capacity. We therefore test whether retaining access to neglected states can improve control. Keeping these states in training reduces measured interference and improves sequential return, with gains even when the encoder is frozen. Related interventions on state access, replay weights, and feature overlap improve control on MiniGrid and DMControl. For agents that learn through a world model, protected fitting improves DreamerV3--Crafter's cumulative training scores at unchanged capacity.

</details>


### [241] [Retimed Bellman Flows: Escaping the Impossible Triangle of Velocity Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32828)
*Boyang Xu,Shengzhe Chen,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Retimed Bellman Flows（ReBF），通过调整教师 critic 的查询时间并使用独立的新噪声，改进流式回报分布学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有速度自举方法在直线路径上存在结构性限制：无法同时实现无残差的同时间仿射映射、保持初始高斯噪声并构造无偏目标，影响训练稳定性与目标可靠性。

Method: ReBF 在动态提前的流时间查询教师 critic，使学生与教师的中间轨迹对齐；同时生成新鲜且解耦的噪声，据此构造条件无偏的速度目标。理论上，该方法保持 Bellman 固定点，并在 Wasserstein 距离下具有收缩性质。

Result: 在合成马尔可夫奖励过程上，ReBF 将预测回报分布与真实分布之间的 W₁ 距离最多降低至原来的约七分之一点七；在 38 个具有挑战性的 OGBench 和 D4RL 离线强化学习任务中，其表现优于已有流式 critic。

Conclusion: 通过重定时教师查询并解耦噪声，ReBF 缓解了现有流式 critic 的目标构造限制，在理论保证与实验表现方面均展现出优势。

Abstract: Flow critics learn return distributions by transporting Gaussian noise to Bellman endpoints via continuous velocity fields. While velocity bootstrapping stabilizes training by querying a successor teacher, existing methods face a structural dilemma: on straight paths, no residual-free same-time affine mapping can preserve Gaussian initial noise while maintaining an unbiased target. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Retimed Bellman Flows (ReBF). ReBF queries the teacher critic at a dynamically shifted earlier flow time, aligning intermediate student and teacher trajectories. By combining this retimed clock with fresh, decoupled noise generation, ReBF constructs a provably conditionally unbiased velocity target that preserves the Bellman fixed point and contracts under Wasserstein distances. Empirically, ReBF reduces $W_1$ distance to ground-truth return distributions by up to $7.7\times$ on synthetic MRPs and outperforms existing flow critics across 38 challenging OGBench and D4RL offline reinforcement learning tasks.

</details>


### [242] [What Do Latent Predictive Vehicle Representations Retain? Measuring State, Geometry, and Local Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32512)
*Enzo Nicolás Spotorno,Josafat Leal Filho,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套评估车辆控制用动作条件潜在预测模型的测量方案，分别检验物理量保留、预测能力及对控制指令微小变化的局部响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型可用于规划和训练控制器，但车辆控制依赖位置、航向角、速度和横摆角速度等物理量，也需要模型准确反映指令变化的影响。仅用潜在目标上的预测误差，无法判断这些关键能力。

Method: 为带有物理量读出的动作条件潜在预测器设计评估协议，分别测量物理量保留、物理邻域组织、预测表现和指令扰动下的局部响应；以未训练编码器作参照，并通过三条匹配的响应路径定位表示或预测器中的误差。在 IPG CarMaker 日志数据上评估时序联合嵌入预测模型。

Result: 模型表示保留了所测平面物理量，但同架构的未训练编码器略优。输入未来指令可改善一秒预测，且物理量保留基本不变。小幅指令脉冲引发的模型响应从潜在空间起就偏离模拟器，并导致在近邻指令选择中产生更高遗憾；针对响应更新预测器可改善局部响应，但会牺牲预测精度。

Conclusion: 潜在预测模型是否适用于控制，不能仅由预测精度判定；必须分别评估物理量保留、预测能力和指令局部响应。该协议为后续闭环评估提供基础。

Abstract: Models of vehicle dynamics learned from logged states and commands complement physics-based models, and latent world models, which predict in a learned representation, are used to plan and train controllers in other domains. Vehicle controllers are usually specified in physical terms: costs, limits, and references depend on position, yaw angle, speed, and yaw rate, and the optimizer compares or differentiates predicted outcomes across nearby commands. A latent model placed in such a controller must therefore let these quantities be recovered and must change its predictions with commands as the vehicle does, and prediction error on its own latent targets measures neither. We contribute a measurement protocol for action-conditioned latent predictors with a physical readout that separately tests retention, physical-neighborhood organization, forecasting, and local response to command perturbations, using an untrained-encoder reference and three matched response paths that locate errors in the representation or the predictor. In a case study of a temporal joint-embedding predictive model trained on signals logged in IPG CarMaker, the representations retain the measured planar outputs, though an untrained encoder of the same architecture retains them slightly better; future-command input improves one-second forecasts with retention nearly unchanged; and responses to small command pulses diverge from the simulator already in latent coordinates, raising regret when choosing among nearby commands in all comparisons. Updating the predictor on responses corrects them locally at a cost in forecast accuracy. Measuring retention, forecasting, and local response separately is thus what qualifies a predictive latent as a candidate model for control, and the protocol provides the basis for its closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [243] [Uncertainty-Aware Selection of Online Algorithms with Simulator Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32170)
*Yongyi Guo,Zifan Xu,Ziping Xu,Kelly W. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，基于多个模拟器评估算法的“不确定性感知选择”能缓解模拟器数据有限带来的选择风险，并提升在线强化学习表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的插件式选择只依据单个拟合模拟器的表现挑选算法；当离线数据不足时，模拟器评估可能不可靠，进而导致部署效果不佳。

Method: 构建模拟器集成（例如通过自助重采样获得），按算法在集成中的平均表现进行选择；通过多臂老虎机理论分析，并在机器人控制深度强化学习实验中测试奖励塑形超参数选择。

Result: 理论分析显示，该方法能缓解拟合模拟器时数据有限的影响，并在多臂老虎机中相较插件式选择显著降低遗憾。机器人控制实验也显示，其选择更可靠，在线表现有所改善。

Conclusion: 将模拟器不确定性纳入算法选择，有望提升有限离线数据条件下的选择可靠性与在线强化学习性能。

Abstract: The performance of online reinforcement learning depends critically on design choices, especially those that affect exploration. These choices are often selected by fitting a simulator to offline data, evaluating candidate algorithms in that simulator, and deploying the best-performing one. The simplest Plug-In selection rule simply selects the best performing algorithm on the fitted simulator, making evaluations unreliable when the offline data used to fit the simulator are limited. We investigate Uncertainty-Aware selection, which forms an ensemble of simulators---for example, obtained by bootstrap resampling---and selects the online algorithm with the best average performance across the ensemble. While ensemble-based approaches have been used to mitigate distribution shift and facilitate sim-to-real transfer, we formally show that this approach can mitigate the effects of limited data when fitting the simulator and theoretically has significant regret gains compared to Plug-In selection in multi-armed bandits. We also empirically investigate the Uncertainty-Aware selection approach in deep RL experiments on robotic control tasks that involve selecting reward-shaping hyperparameters, and show that it leads to more reliable selection and improved online performance.

</details>


### [244] [Timestep Weighting: A Hidden Key to Effective ELBO-Based Flow-Matching RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32665)
*Qinwei Ma,Jingzhe Shi,Simin Fan,Ling Li,Mengdi Wang,Alex Lamb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于 ELBO 的强化学习中，流匹配模型的时间步加权如何影响奖励驱动的微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间步加权会显著影响性能，也为理解既有预测损失提供统一视角，但相关研究不足，实践中常沿用预训练配置。

Method: 作者在受控 CIFAR 图像生成任务及机器人任务中考察不同噪声水平下的奖励驱动更新；分析梯度以揭示跨噪声协调模式及其训练过程中的变化；进一步评估静态加权、预算约束的加权配置选择和动态调度。

Result: 有效的时间步加权取决于奖励景观和学习阶段。不同任务呈现不同的跨噪声梯度协调模式，且这些模式会随训练演进。基于分析设计的加权策略可优于常规目标配置。

Conclusion: 时间步加权是流匹配强化学习中的重要设计选择。研究结果提示，加权策略应考虑当前策略行为与奖励偏好行为之间的差距，并推动进一步探索训练过程中自适应选择和调整加权的方法。

Abstract: ELBO-based reinforcement learning offers a sampler-agnostic approach to fine-tuning flow matching models with reward feedback. Timestep weighting in ELBO-based RL has large impact on performance, and it also provides a unified view (as we show in this work) to understand prediction losses heuristically chosen in prior work, yet it remains under-researched and is often chosen to inherit pretrain configs. We investigate impacts and dynamics of timestep weighting in ELBO-based RL. We show that effective weighting depends on both the reward landscape and stage of learning. (1) Through experiments on controlled CIFAR image generation, complemented by robotics, we investigate how weighting impacts reward-driven updates across noise levels. (2) Through gradient analysis, we reveal distinct patterns of cross-noise coordination across tasks and their evolution during training. These findings motivate the hypothesis that useful weighting depends on the gap between the policy's current behavior and the behavior favored by the reward. (3) Guided by this analysis, we study simple static weighting, budgeted profile selection, and dynamic schedules that improve performance beyond conventional target choices. Our results establish timestep weighting as an important design choice for flow-matching RL and motivate further research into methods that choose and adapt it throughout learning.

</details>


### [245] [Manifold-Stable Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35454)
*Amirhossein Nazerian,Ali Pezeshki,Jianguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流形稳定流匹配（MSFM），通过学习切向输运并显式施加法向收缩，使生成过程能够从任意环境先验出发，同时增强样本对目标流形的几何遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见几何流匹配方法假设先验已位于数据流形上，这需要实际中往往不可得的几何信息；即使回归误差较低，也不能保证生成样本贴合流形，而偏离流形可能影响任务表现。

Method: MSFM借助收缩理论，将学习到的切向输运与预先设定的法向收缩结合。已知流形时使用解析投影算子；未知数据几何时通过主成分分析估计局部仿射近似。方法构造兼容的概率路径，并将训练损失分解为可学习的切向项与法向残差，从而保证流形不变性，并使样本在设定时间窗内向流形横向收敛。

Result: 椭圆实验的终端平均流形外误差约为10⁻⁶。Push-T机器人实验成功率由74%升至82%；Robomimic Square任务成功率由60%升至72%，旋转流形偏差由约10⁻²降至10⁻⁷。终端几何误差受所选数值容差控制。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式法向收缩可补充学习式生成输运，提升生成样本的几何贴合度，并在所测机器人任务中带来更高的观察成功率。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) learns generative dynamics through velocity regression. Geometric FM variants commonly assume a prior supported on the data manifold, requiring geometric knowledge that is often unavailable. Without such knowledge, low regression error alone does not guarantee manifold adherence. Adherence keeps generated samples within valid configurations and is empirically associated with better task performance. We introduce manifold-stable flow matching (MSFM), which can start from an arbitrary ambient prior, not necessarily supported on the manifold. Using tools from nonlinear dynamics, namely contraction theory, MSFM combines learned tangential transport with prescribed normal contraction. The construction uses analytical projectors for known manifolds and local affine proxies estimated by principal component analysis for unknown data geometry. By implementing contraction theory in both cases of known and unknown manifolds, we guarantee manifold invariance and transverse convergence to the manifold within a desired time window (e.g., one second). We derive a family of compatible probability paths and decompose the training loss into a learnable tangential term and a normal residual. An ellipse experiment attains a mean terminal off-manifold error of order $10^{-6}$. In Push-T robotic experiments, MSFM raises success from $74\%$ to $82\%$. In the Robomimic Square task, success increases from $60\%$ to $72\%$, while rotation-manifold deviation decreases from order $10^{-2}$ to $10^{-7}$. The MSFM terminal geometric errors are controlled by the chosen numerical tolerance. These results demonstrate stronger geometric adherence and higher observed task performance, supporting prescribed normal contraction as a complement to learned generative transport.

</details>


### [246] [Adjoint Guidance Flow: Amortized Critic Guidance for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34944)
*Jeongsol Kim,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Jangho Park,Kwanyoung Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AGF 将轨迹感知的价值引导蒸馏到轻量网络，在保留预训练 VLA 策略的同时提升任务表现，并降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于行为克隆训练的流式 VLA 策略不会显式优化长期任务回报。现有 critic 引导方法依赖单步采样器近似，并在每个流步骤通过 critic 集成进行反向传播，计算与推理成本较高。

Method: 将 critic 引导的流生成建模为确定性最优控制问题。最优引导对应一个 costate，它将终端 critic 梯度沿剩余流过程反向传递。训练时冻结 VLA 与 critic，并让轻量引导网络拟合该 costate；推理时每步仅需一次引导网络前向计算。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboCasa 和 LIBERO-Pro 上，AGF 持续提升预训练 VLA 的表现，并与 critic 引导及策略微调基线相比具有竞争力；在跨任务统一使用单一引导强度时鲁棒性最佳。相较 QGF，AGF 每个引导步骤快 3.6 倍、参数量少 7.0 倍，性能相当或更优。

Conclusion: AGF 表明，critic 引导可以兼具轨迹感知能力与轻量化特性，在减少训练和推理开销的同时有效增强预训练 VLA 策略。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically trained by behavior cloning and thus do not explicitly optimize long-term task return. Critic guidance steers generation toward higher-value actions, but existing methods differentiate the critic through a one-step surrogate of the sampler and back-propagate a critic ensemble at every flow step. In contrast, here we propose Adjoint Guidance Flow (AGF), which amortizes trajectory-aware critic guidance into a lightweight guidance network while preserving the pretrained VLA policy. Specifically, we formulate critic-guided flow generation as a deterministic optimal control problem, whose optimal guidance is a costate that carries the terminal critic gradient back through the remaining flow, and regress the guidance network onto this costate while keeping both the VLA and critic frozen. This design provides favorable memory and throughput scaling during training, and inference needs one guidance-network forward pass per step, without the critic ensemble, back-propagation, or adjoint computation. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and LIBERO-Pro, AGF consistently improves pretrained VLAs, remains competitive with critic-guidance and policy-fine-tuning baselines, and is the most robust method when a single guidance strength is deployed across tasks. Compared with QGF, AGF runs $3.6\times$ faster per guidance step with $7.0\times$ fewer parameters, with comparable and even better performance, showing that critic guidance can be trajectory-aware and lightweight.

</details>


### [247] [Learning High-Risk High-Precision Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34851)
*Nam Hee Kim,Markus Kirjonen,Perttu Hämäläinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 State-Conditioned Shooting（SCOOT），用于需要高精度且动作后果不可逆的运动控制任务，并以计算机台球为例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见深度强化学习基准允许后续动作弥补先前误差，未能充分反映高精度控制中动作失误可能造成不可逆后果、奖励景观呈现尖峰与脊线的挑战。

Method: SCOOT 基于优势加权回归（AWR），包含三项改进：仅用精英样本优化策略；采用混合专家策略，以适应不同状态下的奖励模式；加入距离正则化与学习课程，先鼓励探索多样策略，再逐步适应优势样本。

Result: 在基于物理的台球击球任务中，SCOOT 学会了高精度击球，并能针对同一球局配置发现多种击球策略。

Conclusion: 精英样本优化、状态相关的混合专家策略以及分阶段探索，有助于强化学习应对高精度、动作后果不可逆的控制任务。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms for movement control are typically evaluated and benchmarked on sequential decision tasks where imprecise actions may be corrected with later actions, thus allowing high returns with noisy actions. In contrast, we focus on an under-researched class of high-risk, high-precision motion control problems where actions carry irreversible outcomes, driving sharp peaks and ridges to plague the state-action reward landscape. Using computational pool as a representative example of such problems, we propose and evaluate State-Conditioned Shooting (SCOOT), a novel DRL algorithm that builds on advantage-weighted regression (AWR) with three key modifications: 1) Performing policy optimization only using elite samples, allowing the policy to better latch on to the rare high-reward action samples; 2) Utilizing a mixture-of-experts (MoE) policy, to allow switching between reward landscape modes depending on the state; 3) Adding a distance regularization term and a learning curriculum to encourage exploring diverse strategies before adapting to the most advantageous samples. We showcase our features' performance in learning physically-based billiard shots demonstrating high action precision and discovering multiple shot strategies for a given ball configuration.

</details>


### [248] [LRC-JEPA: Disentangling Dynamics and Residual Context for Efficient World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34375)
*Luzhe Huang,Lei Chu,Jingyi Liang,Yuhuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LRC-JEPA 将可规划的动态信息与持续性视觉背景信息分离，在较小模型规模下提升规划表现并加快规划速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型 JEPA 世界模型需在低维潜变量中同时编码动作相关动力学与可预测视觉信息；复杂场景中的高维外观干扰可能与可控状态纠缠，进而损害规划效果。

Method: 提出轻量端到端模型 LRC-JEPA，将信息分流至紧凑预测潜变量 z 和基于学习查询的残差上下文嵌入 u。动力学模型仅传播 z，规划也仅使用 z；u 通过交叉注意力重建捕获时间上持续的信息，并以可微残差连接鼓励 z 保留互补的动态内容。论文在明确假设下证明该表征具备充分性、最小性、干扰不变性与解耦性。

Result: 在四种模拟控制环境中，LRC-JEPA 相较参数量匹配的 JEPA 基线，平均规划成功率提高 9 个百分点，并达到或超过规模大得多的预训练模型。在真实世界 Bridge-v2 数据集上，5.5M 参数的主动编码器优于 DINO-WM（22.1M）和 V-JEPA2（303.9M）编码器，且规划速度更快。物理状态探测、重建干预和消融实验支持其表征解耦效果。

Conclusion: 将动态状态与持续性视觉上下文分路表示，有助于降低外观干扰对紧凑规划潜变量的影响。实验结果表明，LRC-JEPA 能以较少参数实现有竞争力甚至更优的规划表现。

Abstract: Compact JEPA world models enable efficient latent-space planning, but low-dimensional representation trained under reward-free self-supervision must encode both action-conditioned dynamics and predictable visual context. This competition can entangle controllable state with high-rank nuisance appearance and degrade planning as scenes become more complex. We introduce LRC-JEPA, a lightweight end-to-end world model that routes information into a compact predictive latent $\mathbf{z}$ and learned-query residual-context embeddings $\mathbf{u}$. Only $\mathbf{z}$ is propagated by the dynamics model and used for planning, while $\mathbf{u}$ captures temporally persistent information for cross-attention reconstruction; a differentiable residual connection encourages the latent to retain complementary dynamic content. Under explicit assumptions, we show that the resulting representation is sufficient, minimal, nuisance-invariant, and disentangled. Across four simulated control environments, LRC-JEPA improves average planning success over a parameter-matched JEPA baseline by 9 percentage points and matches or exceeds substantially larger pretrained models. On the real-world Bridge-v2 set, its 5.5M-parameter active encoder outperforms DINO-WM (22.1M) and V-JEPA2 (303.9M) encoders while also enabling faster planning. Physical-state probes, reconstruction interventions, and ablations confirm the effectiveness of LRC-JEPA's representation disentanglement.

</details>


### [249] [Emergence, Not Bandwidth: Physical Coupling and the Limits of Learned Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34373)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究受通信速率限制的多智能体团队，理论上刻画最优消息内容、长期压缩代价及协议可读性，并通过三个MuJoCo场景比较工程化通信器与强化学习通信器。结果表明，通信收益取决于物理耦合；在部分耦合场景中，强化学习未能发现有效协议，且不同随机种子学到的协议难以互通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涌现通信研究主要依赖经验结果，尚未充分回答受限通信下消息应编码什么、长期压缩的代价，以及学习协议能否被不同队友可靠理解。本文旨在区分信息论限制与强化学习优化失败，并明确物理耦合和侧信道对通信价值的影响。

Method: 在速率受限的Dec-POMDP框架下建立关于可实现通信能力的理论结果，并将其与强化学习表现进行对比。实验覆盖三个具有零耦合、部分耦合和刚性耦合的MuJoCo场景，每次决策固定使用2比特；通过关闭一个物理侧信道构造可区分条件，同时保持身体、任务和奖励不变。比较工程化发送器、学习发送器、静默基线及七个匹配通信速率的已发表基线，并采用冷启动、工程化接收器预热和跨种子对战进行诊断。所有主要结果均基于每个场景25个随机种子。

Result: 刚性共享物体耦合下，通信相较静默几乎没有收益（+0.001±0.001，p=0.982），说明本体感觉已提供所需信息。无耦合时各条件均能完成任务。部分耦合时，工程化2比特发送器的四分位均值为1.000，而学习发送器仅为0.482，与静默条件无显著差异（p=0.400）。在共享字母和相同带宽下，该差距不能归因于表示能力或通信容量。使用工程化接收器预热后性能提升至0.857，冷启动为0.562（p<0.001），表明主要失败源于强化学习未发现协议。跨种子测试显示协议在自博弈中性能为0.980，但跨种子对战降至0.144；最佳构造对齐仍保留至少77%的性能差距。

Conclusion: 通信价值由智能体间的物理耦合及可用侧信道决定，而非由带宽单独决定。在部分耦合任务中，理论上可行且工程化验证有效的协议并未被强化学习发现，因此学习系统与最优方案之间的差距主要是优化问题，而非信息论或表示能力限制。此外，学习协议具有个体内语义，却缺乏跨训练过程的互操作性，协议发现与协议对齐仍是关键挑战。

Abstract: Rate-limited multi-agent teams raise three questions the emergent-communication literature has answered only empirically: what an optimal message should encode, what compression costs over a horizon, and when a learned protocol is unique enough for a teammate to read. We answer them for rate-limited Dec-POMDPs, then measure how far reinforcement learning falls short of the optimum. Our theorems fix what is achievable independently of any learner, so a gap between an engineered and a learned sender at the same bit budget is an optimization fact, not an information-theoretic one. We instantiate this on three MuJoCo arenas spanning zero, partial and rigid physical coupling, charging every condition exactly 2 bits per decision, and create the discriminating regime by closing a physical side channel within one arena, holding bodies, task and reward fixed. Communication value is governed by coupling: under rigid coupling through a shared object, no channel beats silence (+0.001 +/- 0.001, p = 0.982, n = 25), since proprioception already carries that information; without coupling, every condition solves the task; under partial coupling, the engineered 2-bit sender reaches an interquartile mean of 1.000 but the learned one reaches 0.482, indistinguishable from silence (p = 0.400, n = 25). With a shared alphabet, bandwidth cannot explain the gap. Warm-starting from an engineered receiver localizes the failure: the same channel reaches 0.857 versus 0.562 cold-started (p < 0.001), so it is neither representational nor one of maintenance; reinforcement learning fails to discover the protocol. Cross-play shows learned protocols are individually meaningful but mutually unintelligible: self-play 0.980 collapses to 0.144 across seeds, and our best constructed alignment leaves at least 77% of that gap. All headline results use 25 seeds per arena and seven published baselines at matched rate.

</details>


### [250] [Safe Score Matching: Diffusion Policies with Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Online Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33337)
*Boyang Li,Matthew Kim,Sylvia Lee Herbert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出安全分数匹配（SSM），将扩散策略与 Hamilton–Jacobi 可达性结合，用于满足硬状态约束的在线安全强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见原始-对偶方法只约束期望成本，无法保证状态级安全。基于可达性的硬约束方法则要求策略在可行区域优化奖励、在不可行区域进行恢复，目标动作分布可能呈多峰，现有高斯或确定性策略容易错失优良模式。

Method: SSM 是一种离策略演员—评论家方法，利用 HJ 可达性对双分支分数目标进行门控：在可行区域，对 HJ 评论家判定为可行的动作执行 Q 分数匹配；在不可行区域，通过恢复分支引导去噪过程转向最坏违规程度较低的区域。扩散策略用于表达复杂的多峰动作分布。

Result: 在四旋翼与固定翼轨迹跟踪及稳定—避障基准上，SSM 的任务表现最佳或接近最佳，且误判为安全的比例较低。相比之下，原始-对偶基线出现更多不安全行为，可达性基线则往往更保守。在 Safety-Gymnasium 速度任务中，SSM 成本最低，同时保持有竞争力的奖励。

Conclusion: SSM 将 Q 分数匹配扩展至带硬状态约束的安全强化学习，通过按可达性切换奖励优化与安全恢复，提升了策略对多峰目标的表达能力，并在实验中兼顾任务性能与安全性。

Abstract: Online safe reinforcement learning (RL) seeks policies that maximize reward while satisfying safety constraints. A popular line of research in safe RL relaxes safety to a soft expected-cost constraint and solves the resulting Constrained Markov Decision Process via primal-dual Lagrangian updates that only enforce safety on average. To address this limitation, hard, state-wise constraints are introduced and often imposed through Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability. Yet such constraints require solving different objectives in the feasible and infeasible regions: reward maximization in the former, recovery toward the feasible regions in the latter. The resulting target action distributions are inherently multimodal, and this structure poses a fundamental challenge for the Gaussian or deterministic actors used in existing HJ-based safe RL, which often collapse onto suboptimal modes. Diffusion policies provide the expressiveness needed to represent such distributions, and recent work on Q-score matching offers a route to training them for online RL by score regression -- but has been applied only to reward maximization. We propose Safe Score Matching (SSM), an off-policy actor-critic method that adapts Q-score matching to hard-constrained safe RL by gating a two-branch score target with HJ reachability: inside the feasible set, the denoising process degenerates to Q-score matching on actions classified as viable by the HJ critic; outside, a recovery branch biases denoising toward regions with lower worst-case violation. On quadrotor and fixed-wing trajectory-tracking and stabilize-and-avoid benchmarks, SSM attains the best or near-best task performance with low false-safe rates, whereas the primal-dual baseline admits more unsafe behavior and reachability-based baselines tend to be more conservative; on Safety-Gymnasium velocity tasks, SSM attains the lowest cost with competitive reward.

</details>


### [251] [When an Evaluation Rule Writes Training Labels: Measuring Human-Reference Forgiveness in NAVSIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33189)
*Jiaxuan Guo,Jingxin Yang,Jiaqi Ye,Youran Sun,Shuo Xin,Kejia Zhang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NAVSIM 的人工参考宽免规则会将某些场景中整列候选轨迹标签改为通过，抹去训练所需的候选差异；实验显示这与车道保持预测能力及评测表现相关，但短期再训练结果和不同指标的表现并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究该宽免规则对 GTRS-Dense 标签监督及模型表现的实际影响。该规则原本旨在避免因单条轨迹评分而惩罚情境合理的动作，但其作者也指出，这可能掩盖重要失败。

Method: 在 103,288 个 navtrain 场景上重新运行标签生成器，关闭宽免覆盖，并将覆盖前标签与发布标签比较；随后在 navtest 上评估已发布模型对覆盖前结果的区分能力，与失败候选数量相匹配的未宽免场景作比较。另以三个配对随机种子继续训练发布的 GTRS-Dense 检查点 300 步，并在关闭宽免的条件下比较评测结果。

Result: 宽免规则在 11,237 个 navtrain 场景（10.8793%）中抹去了损失函数可读取的候选差异；13,042 个被宽免的损失列中，9,982 列与车道保持有关，典型列在覆盖前有 16,384 个候选中的 14,391 个未通过。车道保持宽免场景上，GTRS-Dense 与 Hydra-MDP 的中位 AUC 分别为 0.7095 和 0.6627；匹配的未宽免场景分别为 0.9807 和 0.9761。短期继续训练后，使用覆盖前标签相较发布标签，宽免场景 AUC 高出 0.1086–0.1251，匹配组间差距有所缩小但仍存在。关闭宽免评测时，受影响场景的车道保持得分提高 2.478–3.524 分，但相邻帧规划一致性降低；两项变化均大于其他场景。EPDMS 未显示两种标签集之间的区分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，宽免规则可能造成广泛的标签信息损失，并与模型在相关场景中的车道保持识别及评测行为差异有关。覆盖前标签在短期再训练实验中带来更高的宽免场景 AUC，但规划一致性有所下降；EPDMS 未呈现相同差异，因此影响取决于评测指标。

Abstract: When the human reference scores zero on a metric, the released GTRS-Dense label generator for NAVSIM marks every candidate trajectory in the scene as passing it. NAVSIM's authors introduced this human-reference forgiveness to avoid penalizing contextually justified maneuvers when scoring one trajectory, and warned that it could overlook important failures. In label generation it sets a whole column of 16,384 candidate targets to passing. To measure the consequences for supervision, we re-run the generator with the overwrite disabled and compare the pre-overwrite targets with the released labels on all 103,288 navtrain scenes. The rule erases a candidate distinction that the training loss reads on 11,237 of them (10.8793%). Firing usually changes most of a column: lane keeping carries 9,982 of the 13,042 forgiven loss columns, and its median forgiven column had 14,391 of 16,384 candidates failing before the overwrite. On held-out navtest scenes forgiven on lane keeping, the released lane-keeping head's median AUC against the pre-overwrite outcome is 0.7095; on unforgiven scenes matched on failing-candidate count it is 0.9807. For the Hydra-MDP checkpoint released with GTRS, whose configuration takes the same label file, the two values are 0.6627 and 0.9761.
